ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Open%20Technology%20Fund%3A%20Internet%20Freedom%20Fund.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.opentech.fund%2Ffunds%2Finternet-freedom-fund%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.opentech.fund%2Ffunds%2Finternet-freedom-fund%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Open%20Technology%20Fund%3A%20Internet%20Freedom%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20USD%2010%2C000%20-%20900%2C000%20for%20up%20to%2024%20months%3B%20ideal%20requests%20USD%2050%2C000%20-%20200%2C000%20for%206-12%20months%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Individuals%20aged%2018%2B%20and%20for-profit%20or%20nonprofit%20organizations%20of%20any%20nationality%20working%20on%20technology%20that%20advances%20internet%20freedom%20for%20people%20facing%20censorship%20and%20surveillance.%20Applicants%20in%20countries%20under%20U.S.%20trade%20restrictions%20or%20OFAC%20sanctions%20cannot%20be%20supported.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28except%20U.S.-sanctioned%20countries%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%2C%20nonprofit%2C%20individuals.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.opentech.fund%2Ffunds%2Finternet-freedom-fund%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.opentech.fund%2Ffunds%2Finternet-freedom-fund%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20an%20account%20in%20OTF%27s%20online%20application%20system.%202.%20Submit%20a%20Concept%20Note%20%28Stage%201%29.%203.%20OTF%20responds%20in%20about%206-8%20weeks%3B%20competitive%20Concept%20Notes%20are%20invited%20to%20submit%20a%20full%20Proposal%20%28Stage%202%29.%204.%20Proposal%20reviewed%20by%20OTF%20staff%20and%20the%20Advisory%20Council.%205.%20Performance-based%20contract%20negotiated%20and%20signed.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Concept%20Note%3A%20project%20title%0A-%20Project%20description%20in%201-3%20sentences%20%28problem%20and%20expected%20outcome%29%0A-%20Problem%20statement%3A%20context%2C%20causes%2C%20consequences%2C%20supporting%20sources%0A-%20Form%20of%20project%20%28Technical%20Development%2C%20Applied%20Research%2C%20Digital%20Security%2C%20or%20Community%20Convenings%29%0A-%20Overview%20of%20objectives%20and%20activities%2C%20who%20carries%20them%20out%2C%20optional%20deliverables%20and%20timeline%0A-%20Similar%20existing%20projects%20and%20how%20this%20differs%20or%20complements%20them%20%28with%20links%29%0A-%20Estimated%20duration%0A-%20Estimated%20total%20funding%20in%20USD%20%28rough%20estimate%3B%20no%20itemised%20budget%20at%20concept%20stage%29%0A-%20Intended%20beneficiaries%20and%20how%20their%20needs%20are%20known%0A-%20Location%20of%20intended%20users%20by%20UN%20M49%20region%0A-%20Applicant%20name%20%28pseudonym%20allowed%29%20and%20contact%20email%0A-%20Why%20the%20team%20is%20right%20for%20the%20work%20%28expertise%2C%20past%20work%2C%20partner%20connections%29%0A-%20If%20invited%3A%20full%20Proposal%20%28project%20description%20up%20to%203%2C000%20words%2C%20800%20recommended%3B%20objectives%20and%20activities%20up%20to%202%2C000%20words%3B%20other%20questions%20mostly%20400%20recommended%20%2F%201%2C000%20max%29%20plus%20detailed%20budget%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%206-8%20weeks%20for%20the%20Concept%20Note%20decision%3B%20full%20Proposal%20review%20adds%20further%20months%20%28unconfirmed%20exact%29%0AObligations%3A%20Performance-based%20contract%3A%20payment%20in%20USD%20only%20after%20agreed%20objectives%2C%20activities%20and%20deliverables%20are%20completed%3B%20monitoring%20and%20evaluation%20with%20an%20OTF%20program%20manager%20against%20agreed%20metrics%3B%20possible%20security%20audit%20of%20funded%20software%3B%20open-source%20release%20expected.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirror%20fleet%20as%20censorship-resistant%20Git%20hosting%3A%20repositories%20%28including%20circumvention%20tools%29%20stay%20clonable%20when%20a%20source%20host%20is%20blocked%20or%20taken%20down%2C%20plus%20portable%20signed%20issues%2FPRs%20so%20collaboration%20survives%20deplatforming.%20OTF%20funds%20exactly%20this%20kind%20of%20resilience%20infrastructure%20for%20internet%20freedom%20tool%20developers%20in%20repressive%20contexts.%0ARisks%3A%20Highly%20competitive%3B%20must%20show%20concrete%20users%20in%20repressive%20environments%20rather%20than%20general%20developer%20resilience.%20OTF%27s%20U.S.%20government%20funding%20was%20disrupted%20in%202025%2C%20so%20award%20volume%20and%20timelines%20may%20vary.%20Crypto%20payout%2Fwallet%20features%20are%20out%20of%20scope%20and%20should%20not%20be%20in%20the%20proposal.%20Work-product%20and%20publicity%20clauses%20in%20OTF%20contracts%20need%20review.)** · [Program page](https://www.opentech.fund/funds/internet-freedom-fund/) · [Apply](https://www.opentech.fund/funds/internet-freedom-fund/)

- **Funder:** Open Technology Fund
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** USD 10,000 - 900,000 for up to 24 months; ideal requests USD 50,000 - 200,000 for 6-12 months (about $10,000 - $900,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 85/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Individuals aged 18+ and for-profit or nonprofit organizations of any nationality working on technology that advances internet freedom for people facing censorship and surveillance. Applicants in countries under U.S. trade restrictions or OFAC sanctions cannot be supported.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (except U.S.-sanctioned countries) · **Entity types:** For-profit, nonprofit, individuals
- **Decision time:** About 6-8 weeks for the Concept Note decision; full Proposal review adds further months (unconfirmed exact)
- **Obligations:** Performance-based contract: payment in USD only after agreed objectives, activities and deliverables are completed; monitoring and evaluation with an OTF program manager against agreed metrics; possible security audit of funded software; open-source release expected.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose ForkMesh's signed, integrity-checked mirror fleet as censorship-resistant Git hosting: repositories (including circumvention tools) stay clonable when a source host is blocked or taken down, plus portable signed issues/PRs so collaboration survives deplatforming. OTF funds exactly this kind of resilience infrastructure for internet freedom tool developers in repressive contexts.

**How to apply:**

1. Create an account in OTF's online application system.
2. Submit a Concept Note (Stage 1).
3. OTF responds in about 6-8 weeks; competitive Concept Notes are invited to submit a full Proposal (Stage 2).
4. Proposal reviewed by OTF staff and the Advisory Council.
5. Performance-based contract negotiated and signed.

**What the application asks for:**

- Concept Note: project title
- Project description in 1-3 sentences (problem and expected outcome)
- Problem statement: context, causes, consequences, supporting sources
- Form of project (Technical Development, Applied Research, Digital Security, or Community Convenings)
- Overview of objectives and activities, who carries them out, optional deliverables and timeline
- Similar existing projects and how this differs or complements them (with links)
- Estimated duration
- Estimated total funding in USD (rough estimate; no itemised budget at concept stage)
- Intended beneficiaries and how their needs are known
- Location of intended users by UN M49 region
- Applicant name (pseudonym allowed) and contact email
- Why the team is right for the work (expertise, past work, partner connections)
- If invited: full Proposal (project description up to 3,000 words, 800 recommended; objectives and activities up to 2,000 words; other questions mostly 400 recommended / 1,000 max) plus detailed budget

**Risks:** Highly competitive; must show concrete users in repressive environments rather than general developer resilience. OTF's U.S. government funding was disrupted in 2025, so award volume and timelines may vary. Crypto payout/wallet features are out of scope and should not be in the proposal. Work-product and publicity clauses in OTF contracts need review.

**Sources:** <https://www.opentech.fund/funds/internet-freedom-fund/>, <https://www.opentech.fund/funds/>, <https://docs.opentech.fund/otf-application-guidebook/appendix-i-concept-note-guide>, <https://docs.opentech.fund/otf-application-guidebook/appendix-ii-proposal-guide>

20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20NLnet%20Foundation%20for%20the%20European%20Commission%3A%20Restack%3A%20Open%20Internet%20Stack%20open%20call%0AAward%3A%20EUR%205%2C000%20-%2050%2C000%20for%20a%20first%20proposal%3B%20larger%20amounts%20only%20after%20successfully%20finished%20smaller%20projects%2C%20capped%20at%20EUR%20150%2C000%20per%20proposal%20and%20EUR%20500%2C000%20per%20third%20party%20over%20the%20programme%3B%20about%20EUR%207%20million%20in%20total%20to%202030%0ADeadline%3A%202026-11-03%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20No%20categorical%20exclusion%20of%20persons%3B%20individuals%20and%20organisations%20can%20apply.%20The%20project%20needs%20a%20strong%20European%20dimension%2C%20normally%20team%20members%20in%20Horizon%20Europe-eligible%20countries%20receiving%20at%20least%2060%25%20of%20the%20funds%3B%20others%20are%20considered%20only%20for%20exceptional%20quality%20and%20unique%20expertise.%20All%20results%20must%20be%20free%2Fopen%20source%2C%20and%20projects%20with%20AI%20at%20their%20core%20are%20out%20of%20scope.%20Geography%3A%20Horizon%20Europe-eligible%20countries%20%2860%25%20of%20funds%29%3B%20worldwide%20only%20by%20exception.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20for-profit%2C%20non-profit%2C%20research%20institutions.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fnlnet.nl%2Frestack%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fnlnet.nl%2Fpropose%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Check%20the%20scope%20%28core%20internet%20infrastructure%2C%20open%20alternatives%20to%20closed%20services%2C%20supply-chain%20security%2C%20reproducibility%2C%20standards%29%20and%20the%20European-dimension%20rule.%202.%20Submit%20an%20English%20proposal%20at%20nlnet.nl%2Fpropose%20before%203%20Nov%202026%2012%3A00%20CET.%203.%20Eligibility%20%28knock-out%29%20check.%204.%20First-stage%20scoring%3A%20technical%20excellence%2030%25%2C%20relevance%2Fimpact%2040%25%2C%20cost-effectiveness%2030%25%3B%20must%20score%20above%205.0%2F7.%205.%20Second-stage%20review%20with%20clarifying%20questions%20and%20ranking.%206.%20Independent%20review%20committee%20check.%207.%20Sign%20a%20Memorandum%20of%20Understanding%20with%20NLnet%3B%20paid%20against%20deliverables.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Proposal%20in%20English%20via%20the%20NLnet%20form%20%28exact%20fields%20and%20character%20limits%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Requested%20amount%20and%20a%20budget%20justified%20as%20value%20for%20money%0A-%20Description%20of%20the%20project%27s%20link%20to%20Restack%20topics%20and%20the%20Open%20Internet%20Stack%0A-%20Team%20location%20details%20showing%20the%20European%20dimension%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20timeline%20stated%3B%20projects%20not%20answered%20in%20time%20move%20to%20the%20next%20call%0AObligations%3A%20Deliverables%20under%20recognised%20free%2Fopen%20licences.%20Software%20must%20be%20WCAG-compliant.%20Independent%20security%20audit%20findings%20must%20be%20addressed%2C%20and%20larger%20grants%20can%20depend%20on%20the%20audit.%20Scientific%20output%20must%20be%20open%20access.%20Payment%20terms%20are%20set%20in%20the%20MoU.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20scoped%20EUR%2050k%20project%20making%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed-mirror%20and%20signed%20in-repo%20issue%2FPR%20protocol%20a%20reusable%20Open%20Internet%20Stack%20building%20block%3A%20an%20independently%20auditable%20specification%20plus%20reference%20implementation%20that%20any%20forge%20can%20adopt.%20Restack%20explicitly%20funds%20open%20alternatives%20to%20closed%20services%20with%20large%20user%20bases%2C%20software%20supply-chain%20security%20and%20reproducibility.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20European%20team%20members%20to%20receive%20most%20of%20the%20funds.%20AI-agent%20features%20are%20out%20of%20scope.%20Leave%20the%20Solana%20payouts%20out.%20Grants%20are%20staged%2C%20so%20start%20at%20EUR%2050k%20or%20less.%20Another%20list%20may%20already%20include%20NLnet%27s%20NGI0%20Commons%20Fund.%20The%20direct%20Horizon%20Europe%20Open%20Internet%20Stack%20topics%20%28HORIZON-CL4-2026-04-DATA-02%2F03%29%20closed%20on%2015%20April%202026%2C%20and%20the%202027%20Cluster%204%20topics%20are%20about%20AI%20compute.%20The%20parallel%20NLnet%20CodeSupply%20call%20%28same%20deadline%2C%20EUR%20400k%20pool%29%20suits%20supply-chain%20metadata%20work.)** · [Program page](https://nlnet.nl/restack/) · [Apply](https://nlnet.nl/propose/)

- **Funder:** NLnet Foundation for the European Commission (DG CNECT, Horizon Europe grant agreement No. 101299072, Next Generation Internet / Open Internet Stack)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** EUR 5,000 - 50,000 for a first proposal; larger amounts only after successfully finished smaller projects, capped at EUR 150,000 per proposal and EUR 500,000 per third party over the programme; about EUR 7 million in total to 2030 (about $5,750 - $172,500)
- **Deadline:** 2026-11-03 (open)
- **Fit score:** 85/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** No categorical exclusion of persons; individuals and organisations can apply. The project needs a strong European dimension, normally team members in Horizon Europe-eligible countries receiving at least 60% of the funds; others are considered only for exceptional quality and unique expertise. All results must be free/open source, and projects with AI at their core are out of scope.
- **Geography:** Horizon Europe-eligible countries (60% of funds); worldwide only by exception · **Entity types:** Individuals, for-profit, non-profit, research institutions
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: no timeline stated; projects not answered in time move to the next call
- **Obligations:** Deliverables under recognised free/open licences. Software must be WCAG-compliant. Independent security audit findings must be addressed, and larger grants can depend on the audit. Scientific output must be open access. Payment terms are set in the MoU.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a scoped EUR 50k project making ForkMesh's signed-mirror and signed in-repo issue/PR protocol a reusable Open Internet Stack building block: an independently auditable specification plus reference implementation that any forge can adopt. Restack explicitly funds open alternatives to closed services with large user bases, software supply-chain security and reproducibility.

**How to apply:**

1. Check the scope (core internet infrastructure, open alternatives to closed services, supply-chain security, reproducibility, standards) and the European-dimension rule.
2. Submit an English proposal at nlnet.nl/propose before 3 Nov 2026 12:00 CET.
3. Eligibility (knock-out) check.
4. First-stage scoring: technical excellence 30%, relevance/impact 40%, cost-effectiveness 30%; must score above 5.0/7.
5. Second-stage review with clarifying questions and ranking.
6. Independent review committee check.
7. Sign a Memorandum of Understanding with NLnet; paid against deliverables.

**What the application asks for:**

- Proposal in English via the NLnet form (exact fields and character limits unconfirmed)
- Requested amount and a budget justified as value for money
- Description of the project's link to Restack topics and the Open Internet Stack
- Team location details showing the European dimension

**Risks:** Needs European team members to receive most of the funds. AI-agent features are out of scope. Leave the Solana payouts out. Grants are staged, so start at EUR 50k or less. Another list may already include NLnet's NGI0 Commons Fund. The direct Horizon Europe Open Internet Stack topics (HORIZON-CL4-2026-04-DATA-02/03) closed on 15 April 2026, and the 2027 Cluster 4 topics are about AI compute. The parallel NLnet CodeSupply call (same deadline, EUR 400k pool) suits supply-chain metadata work.

**Sources:** <https://ngi.eu/opencalls/>, <https://nlnet.nl/restack/>, <https://nlnet.nl/restack/eligibility/>, <https://nlnet.nl/restack/guideforapplicants/>, <https://nlnet.nl/codesupply/>, <https://nlnet.nl/events/20260211/OIS-matchmaking/>, <https://ec.europa.eu/info/funding-tenders/opportunities/docs/2021-2027/horizon/wp-call/2026-2027/wp-7-digital-industry-and-space_horizon-2026-2027_en.pdf>

<a id="nlnet-codesupply"></a>
## 3. NLnet Foundation: CodeSupply Fund

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20NLnet%20Foundation%3A%20CodeSupply%20Fund.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fnlnet.nl%2Fcodesupply%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fnlnet.nl%2Fpropose%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20NLnet%20Foundation%3A%20CodeSupply%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20between%205.000%20and%2050.000%20EUR%20per%20project%3B%20about%20400%2C000%20EUR%20reserved%20for%20open%20calls%0ADeadline%3A%202026-11-03%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Independent%20researchers%2C%20developers%20and%20organisations%20proposing%20free%20and%20open%20source%20work%20auxiliary%20to%20CodeSupply%27s%20goal%20of%20current%2C%20correct%2C%20openly%20published%20software%20package%20metadata%20%28origin%2C%20licensing%2C%20vulnerabilities%2C%20quality%29.%20As%20an%20EU-funded%20NLnet%20fund%20it%20requires%20a%20European%20dimension%2C%20usually%20meaning%20an%20applicant%20from%20a%20Horizon%20Europe%20eligible%20country%3B%20applicants%20elsewhere%20must%20explain%20how%20the%20project%20meets%20it.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20in%20principle%2C%20European%20dimension%20required%20%28normally%20Horizon%20Europe%20eligible%20countries%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20collectives%2C%20associations%2C%20foundations%2C%20NGOs%2C%20universities%2C%20public%20sector%2C%20cooperatives%2C%20SMEs%2C%20corporations.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fnlnet.nl%2Fcodesupply%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fnlnet.nl%2Fpropose%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Check%20the%20project%20fits%20CodeSupply%20and%20NLnet%20general%20requirements.%202.%20Fill%20in%20the%20NLnet%20online%20form%20at%20nlnet.nl%2Fpropose%2C%20selecting%20the%20CodeSupply%20fund%2C%20in%20plain%20English%20text%20in%20your%20own%20words.%203.%20Submit%20before%202026-11-03%2012%3A00%20CET.%204.%20NLnet%20reviews%20in%20rounds%20and%20may%20send%20follow-up%20questions.%205.%20Selected%20projects%20agree%20a%20milestone%20plan%3B%20grants%20are%20paid%20against%20completed%20milestones.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Proposal%20title%0A-%20Project%20website%28s%29%20%2F%20repositories%0A-%20Project%20summary%20and%20expected%20results%20%28max%201%2C000%20characters%29%0A-%20Requested%20amount%20in%20EUR%20%285%2C000-50%2C000%20for%20a%20first%20grant%29%0A-%20Budget%20breakdown%3A%20tasks%2C%20effort%2C%20hourly%2Fdaily%2Fmonthly%20rate%2C%20expenses%20%28max%204%2C000%20characters%29%0A-%20Comparison%20with%20existing%20efforts%20and%20what%20is%20new%20%28max%204%2C000%20characters%29%0A-%20Expected%20technical%20challenges%20%28max%204%2C000%20characters%29%0A-%20Ecosystem%2C%20dependencies%2C%20engagement%20and%20main%20users%20%28max%202%2C000%20characters%29%0A-%20Relevant%20experience%20%28max%202%2C000%20characters%29%0A-%20Other%20funding%20sources%20%28max%201%2C000%20characters%29%0A-%20Optional%20attachments%20%28HTML%2C%20PDF%2C%20ODF%2C%20images%2C%20plain%20text%3B%2050%20MB%20total%29%0A-%20Generative%20AI%20disclosure%3B%20if%20used%2C%20prompts%20and%20AI%20output%20%28max%208%2C000%20characters%20or%20files%20up%20to%2050%20MB%29%0A-%20Contact%3A%20name%20or%20pseudonym%2C%20email%2C%20phone%20with%20country%20code%2C%20optional%20OpenPGP%20key%0A-%20Applicant%20type%2C%20organisation%20name%20and%20country%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20NLnet%20does%20not%20state%20it%20on%20the%20fund%20page%3B%20past%20NLnet%20calls%20typically%20announced%20first-round%20selection%20within%20about%202-3%20months%20of%20the%20deadline%0AObligations%3A%20All%20project%20results%20must%20be%20released%20under%20a%20recognised%20free%20or%20open%20source%20licence%20and%20fit%20CodeSupply%27s%20goals%20and%20the%20Open%20Internet%20Stack%3B%20work%20is%20delivered%20and%20paid%20per%20agreed%20milestone%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20FOSS%20component%20that%20publishes%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20provenance%20metadata%20for%20mirrored%20Git%20repositories%20%28source%20origin%2C%20mirror%20attestations%2C%20licence%20detection%29%20as%20open%20data%20that%20CodeSupply%27s%20federated%20catalog%20can%20ingest.%20CodeSupply%20exists%20to%20curate%20package%20origin%20and%20supply-chain%20metadata%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed%20mirrors%20already%20produce%20exactly%20this%20kind%20of%20origin%20evidence.%0ARisks%3A%20CodeSupply%20is%20a%20separate%20fund%20from%20NLnet%20Restack%20%28also%20on%20this%20list%29%3A%20both%20are%20open%20in%20the%20same%20NLnet%20call%20%28deadline%202026-11-03%29%20and%20use%20the%20same%20nlnet.nl%2Fpropose%20form%2C%20where%20the%20applicant%20selects%20the%20fund%3B%20this%20entry%20is%20only%20the%20CodeSupply%20option%2C%20and%20the%20same%20work%20must%20not%20be%20submitted%20to%20both.%20Small%20pot%20%28about%20400%2C000%20EUR%20total%29%20and%20a%20narrow%20scope%3A%20the%20proposal%20must%20be%20auxiliary%20to%20package-metadata%20curation%2C%20not%20general%20ForkMesh%20development.%20European%20dimension%20needed%20if%20the%20entity%20is%20outside%20Horizon%20Europe%20countries.%20NLnet%20says%20work%20%28mostly%29%20generated%20by%20LLMs%20is%20ineligible%20under%20its%20current%20GenAI%20policy%2C%20which%20is%20being%20revised%3B%20ForkMesh%27s%20AI-agent%20workflow%20must%20be%20disclosed%20and%20could%20be%20an%20obstacle.%20Results%E2%80%A6)** · [Program page](https://nlnet.nl/codesupply/) · [Apply](https://nlnet.nl/propose/)

- **Funder:** NLnet Foundation (European Commission DG CNECT funding)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** between 5.000 and 50.000 EUR per project; about 400,000 EUR reserved for open calls (about $5,800 - $58,000)
- **Deadline:** 2026-11-03 (open)
- **Fit score:** 78/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Independent researchers, developers and organisations proposing free and open source work auxiliary to CodeSupply's goal of current, correct, openly published software package metadata (origin, licensing, vulnerabilities, quality). As an EU-funded NLnet fund it requires a European dimension, usually meaning an applicant from a Horizon Europe eligible country; applicants elsewhere must explain how the project meets it.
- **Geography:** Worldwide in principle, European dimension required (normally Horizon Europe eligible countries) · **Entity types:** Individuals, collectives, associations, foundations, NGOs, universities, public sector, cooperatives, SMEs, corporations
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: NLnet does not state it on the fund page; past NLnet calls typically announced first-round selection within about 2-3 months of the deadline
- **Obligations:** All project results must be released under a recognised free or open source licence and fit CodeSupply's goals and the Open Internet Stack; work is delivered and paid per agreed milestone; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a FOSS component that publishes signed, integrity-checked provenance metadata for mirrored Git repositories (source origin, mirror attestations, licence detection) as open data that CodeSupply's federated catalog can ingest. CodeSupply exists to curate package origin and supply-chain metadata, and ForkMesh's signed mirrors already produce exactly this kind of origin evidence.

**How to apply:**

1. Check the project fits CodeSupply and NLnet general requirements.
2. Fill in the NLnet online form at nlnet.nl/propose, selecting the CodeSupply fund, in plain English text in your own words.
3. Submit before 2026-11-03 12:00 CET.
4. NLnet reviews in rounds and may send follow-up questions.
5. Selected projects agree a milestone plan; grants are paid against completed milestones.

**What the application asks for:**

- Proposal title
- Project website(s) / repositories
- Project summary and expected results (max 1,000 characters)
- Requested amount in EUR (5,000-50,000 for a first grant)
- Budget breakdown: tasks, effort, hourly/daily/monthly rate, expenses (max 4,000 characters)
- Comparison with existing efforts and what is new (max 4,000 characters)
- Expected technical challenges (max 4,000 characters)
- Ecosystem, dependencies, engagement and main users (max 2,000 characters)
- Relevant experience (max 2,000 characters)
- Other funding sources (max 1,000 characters)
- Optional attachments (HTML, PDF, ODF, images, plain text; 50 MB total)
- Generative AI disclosure; if used, prompts and AI output (max 8,000 characters or files up to 50 MB)
- Contact: name or pseudonym, email, phone with country code, optional OpenPGP key
- Applicant type, organisation name and country

**Risks:** CodeSupply is a separate fund from NLnet Restack (also on this list): both are open in the same NLnet call (deadline 2026-11-03) and use the same nlnet.nl/propose form, where the applicant selects the fund; this entry is only the CodeSupply option, and the same work must not be submitted to both. Small pot (about 400,000 EUR total) and a narrow scope: the proposal must be auxiliary to package-metadata curation, not general ForkMesh development. European dimension needed if the entity is outside Horizon Europe countries. NLnet says work (mostly) generated by LLMs is ineligible under its current GenAI policy, which is being revised; ForkMesh's AI-agent workflow must be disclosed and could be an obstacle. Results must be FOSS (MIT is fine).

**Sources:** <https://nlnet.nl/codesupply/>, <https://nlnet.nl/propose/>, <https://nlnet.nl/news/2026/20260903-call.html>, <https://nlnet.nl/commonsfund/>

adically%20Open%20Security%2C%20SRLabs%29.%204.%20Audit%20or%20design%20review%20is%20performed.%205.%20Findings%20are%20fixed%20during%20a%20responsible%20disclosure%20period%2C%20then%20the%20report%20is%20published.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Security%20Lab%20application%20form%20%28fields%20not%20published%20on%20the%20page%29%0A-%20Description%20of%20the%20tool%2C%20its%20users%20and%20internet%20freedom%20relevance%0A-%20Repository%20and%20documentation%20links%0A-%20Scope%20of%20the%20requested%20review%20%28architecture%2C%20crypto%20design%2C%20code%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20Audit%20results%20are%20published%20openly%20after%20a%20responsible%20disclosure%20period%3B%20project%20must%20fix%20findings.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Request%20a%20cryptographic%20design%20review%20and%20code%20audit%20of%20ForkMesh%27s%20signing%2C%20mirror%20integrity%20verification%20and%20node%20identity%20keys.%20An%20independent%20published%20audit%20directly%20supports%20the%20supply-chain%20integrity%20claim%20and%20is%20a%20strong%20credential%20for%20later%20OTF%20or%20Alpha-Omega%20funding.%0ARisks%3A%20Must%20still%20argue%20internet%20freedom%20relevance%3B%20capacity%20is%20limited%20and%20timeline%20unpublished.)** · [Program page](https://www.opentech.fund/labs/security-lab/) · [Apply](https://www.opentech.fund/labs/security-lab/)

- **Funder:** Open Technology Fund
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** In-kind: free independent security audit, cryptographic design review or code review by an OTF partner firm (no cash amount stated) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 75/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Any project whose tool contributes to internet freedom anywhere in the world, OTF-funded or not. Applicants must be 18 or older.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (except U.S.-sanctioned countries, per OTF general rules) · **Entity types:** For-profit, nonprofit, individuals
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** Audit results are published openly after a responsible disclosure period; project must fix findings.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Request a cryptographic design review and code audit of ForkMesh's signing, mirror integrity verification and node identity keys. An independent published audit directly supports the supply-chain integrity claim and is a strong credential for later OTF or Alpha-Omega funding.

**How to apply:**

1. Complete the Security Lab online application form.
2. OTF reviews mission fit.
3. Project is matched with a security partner firm (e.g. 7ASecurity, Include Security, Radically Open Security, SRLabs).
4. Audit or design review is performed.
5. Findings are fixed during a responsible disclosure period, then the report is published.

**What the application asks for:**

- Security Lab application form (fields not published on the page)
- Description of the tool, its users and internet freedom relevance
- Repository and documentation links
- Scope of the requested review (architecture, crypto design, code)

**Risks:** Must still argue internet freedom relevance; capacity is limited and timeline unpublished.

**Sources:** <https://www.opentech.fund/labs/security-lab/>, <https://docs.opentech.fund/otf-application-guidebook/our-labs/security-lab>, <https://www.opentech.fund/funds/rapid-response-fund/>

<a id="nsf-pesose-26-506"></a>
## 5. U.S. National Science Foundation: Pathways to Enable Secure Open-Source Ecosystems

**[Draft this application in 0developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20U.S.%20National%20Science%20Foundation%3A%20Pathways%20to%20Enable%20Secure%20Open-Source%20Ecosystems%0AAward%3A%20Track%201%20%28scoping%20and%20planning%29%3A%20up%20to%20%24300%2C000%20for%20up%20to%201%20year%2C%20about%2030%20awards%3B%20Track%202%20%28establishing%20and%20expanding%29%20and%20Track%203%20%28improving%20safety%2C%20security%20and%20privacy%29%3A%20up%20to%20%241%2C500%2C000%20for%20up%20to%202%20years%2C%20about%2010%20awards%20each%3B%20about%20%2440%2C000%2C000%20total%2C%2040-60%20awards%0ADeadline%3A%202027-03-02%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20U.S.-based%20for-profit%20organisations%20%28including%20small%20businesses%29%2C%20U.S.%20non-profits%2C%20accredited%20U.S.%20IHEs%2C%20state%2Flocal%20governments%20and%20Tribal%20Nations%3B%20non-profits%20and%20for-profits%20must%20be%20more%20than%2050%25%20owned%20and%20controlled%20by%20U.S.%20citizens%20or%20permanent%20residents.%20For%20non-academic%20organisations%20the%20PI%20must%20be%20an%20employee%20normally%20resident%20in%20the%20U.S.%2C%20and%20all%20funded%20staff%20need%20a%20legal%20right%20to%20work%20in%20the%20U.S.%20The%20proposal%20must%20build%20on%20an%20existing%20open-source%20product%20with%20users%20and%20contributors.%20Geography%3A%20US%20only.%20Entity%20types%3A%20U.S.%20for-profit%20%28incl.%20small%20business%29%2C%20U.S.%20non-profit%2C%20U.S.%20universities%2C%20state%2Flocal%2Ftribal%20governments%3B%20no%20individuals.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.nsf.gov%2Ffunding%2Fopportunities%2Fpesose-pathways-enable-secure-open-source-ecosystems%2Fnsf26-506%2Fsolicitation%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.research.gov%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Register%20the%20organisation%20in%20SAM.gov%20%28active%20UEI%29%20and%20Research.gov.%202.%20Pick%20a%20track%20%281%20scoping%2C%202%20establishing%2Fexpanding%2C%203%20safety%2Fsecurity%2Fprivacy%29.%203.%20Collect%203-5%20letters%20of%20collaboration%20from%20independent%20third-party%20users%20or%20contributors.%204.%20Submit%20the%20full%20proposal%20in%20Research.gov%20or%20Grants.gov%20by%205%20p.m.%20submitter%20local%20time%20on%20the%20deadline%20%28first%20Tuesday%20of%20March%20and%20September%3B%20next%202%20March%202027%29.%205.%20NSF%20merit%20review.%206.%20Track%201%20and%20Track%202%20awardees%20complete%20the%20mandatory%20I-Corps%20for%20PESOSE%20training.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Title%20beginning%20%27PESOSE%3A%27%20followed%20by%20the%20track%20and%20project%20title%0A-%20Project%20Summary%20whose%20last%20line%20is%20a%20prioritised%20list%20of%202-5%20keywords%20starting%20%27Keywords%3A%27%20separated%20by%20semicolons%0A-%20Project%20Description%3A%20max%207%20pages%20%28Track%201%29%20or%2015%20pages%20%28Tracks%202%20and%203%29%2C%20no%20URLs%20allowed%20in%20it%0A-%20In-line%20citation%20plus%20References%20Cited%20entry%20for%20the%20existing%20open-source%20product%0A-%20Current%20status%20of%20development%2Ftesting%2C%20dissemination%2C%20user%20base%20and%20contributor%20base%3B%20problem%20description%3B%20justification%20of%20team%20qualifications%0A-%20Track-specific%20content%3A%20Track%201%20ecosystem%20discovery%2C%20governance%2C%20risk%2Fsecurity%20planning%2C%20community%20building%3B%20Track%202%20growth%2C%20governance%2C%20continuous%20development%2C%20risk%20and%20security%2C%20sustainability%2C%20evaluation%20plan%3B%20Track%203%20societal%20impact%2C%20targeted%20vulnerabilities%2C%20year-by-year%20development%20plan%2C%20evaluation%20plan%0A-%203-5%20letters%20of%20collaboration%20%28max%202%20pages%20each%29%20from%20current%20third-party%20users%20or%20contributors%20not%20directly%20related%20to%20the%20team%2C%20each%20stating%20writer%20name%2C%20affiliation%20and%20relationship%3B%20Track%203%20letters%20must%20address%20the%20targeted%20vulnerabilities%0A-%20Facilities%20letter%20if%20the%20ecosystem%20will%20depend%20on%20an%20organisation%27s%20facilities%20after%20the%20award%0A-%20Personnel%20and%20Partner%20List%20table%20%28full%20name%2C%20organisation%28s%29%2C%20role%29%20including%20letter%20writers%0A-%20Budget%20within%20track%20caps%3B%20voluntary%20committed%20cost%20sharing%20prohibited%0A-%20Track%202%20applicants%20who%20already%20did%20I-Corps%20for%20PESOSE%3A%20I-Corps%20dates%20on%20the%20first%20line%20of%20the%20Project%20Description%20to%20waive%20it%0A-%20Standard%20PAPPG%20documents%20%28biosketches%2C%20current%20and%20pending%20support%2C%20budget%20justification%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20NSF%20generally%20aims%20to%20notify%20applicants%20within%20about%206%20months%20of%20the%20deadline%0AObligations%3A%20Annual%20project%20reports%20and%20a%20public%20project%20outcomes%20report%3B%20mandatory%20I-Corps%20for%20PESOSE%20for%20Track%201%20and%202%20awardees%3B%20no%20voluntary%20cost%20sharing%3B%20CHIPS%20and%20Science%20Act%20section%2010636%20restricti%E2%80%A6)** · [Program page](https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/pesose-pathways-enable-secure-open-source-ecosystems/nsf26-506/solicitation) · [Apply](https://www.research.gov/)

- **Funder:** U.S. National Science Foundation (Directorate for Technology, Innovation and Partnerships)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Track 1 (scoping and planning): up to $300,000 for up to 1 year, about 30 awards; Track 2 (establishing and expanding) and Track 3 (improving safety, security and privacy): up to $1,500,000 for up to 2 years, about 10 awards each; about $40,000,000 total, 40-60 awards (about $1,500,000)
- **Deadline:** 2027-03-02 (open)
- **Fit score:** 72/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** U.S.-based for-profit organisations (including small businesses), U.S. non-profits, accredited U.S. IHEs, state/local governments and Tribal Nations; non-profits and for-profits must be more than 50% owned and controlled by U.S. citizens or permanent residents. For non-academic organisations the PI must be an employee normally resident in the U.S., and all funded staff need a legal right to work in the U.S. The proposal must build on an existing open-source product with users and contributors.
- **Geography:** US only · **Entity types:** U.S. for-profit (incl. small business), U.S. non-profit, U.S. universities, state/local/tribal governments; no individuals
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: NSF generally aims to notify applicants within about 6 months of the deadline
- **Obligations:** Annual project reports and a public project outcomes report; mandatory I-Corps for PESOSE for Track 1 and 2 awardees; no voluntary cost sharing; CHIPS and Science Act section 10636 restrictions on listed entities.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a Track 3 project hardening ForkMesh's signed, integrity-checked mirror protocol and signed in-repo issues/PR records against supply-chain attacks (tampered mirrors, forged reviews, origin takedown), or a Track 1 award to set up open governance and a contributor community around the protocol. PESOSE exists to fund secure, sustainable ecosystems around an open-source product that already has users, which is what the protocol and mirror fleet are.

**How to apply:**

1. Register the organisation in SAM.gov (active UEI) and Research.gov.
2. Pick a track (1 scoping, 2 establishing/expanding, 3 safety/security/privacy).
3. Collect 3-5 letters of collaboration from independent third-party users or contributors.
4. Submit the full proposal in Research.gov or Grants.gov by 5 p.m. submitter local time on the deadline (first Tuesday of March and September; next 2 March 2027).
5. NSF merit review.
6. Track 1 and Track 2 awardees complete the mandatory I-Corps for PESOSE training.

**What the application asks for:**

- Title beginning 'PESOSE:' followed by the track and project title
- Project Summary whose last line is a prioritised list of 2-5 keywords starting 'Keywords:' separated by semicolons
- Project Description: max 7 pages (Track 1) or 15 pages (Tracks 2 and 3), no URLs allowed in it
- In-line citation plus References Cited entry for the existing open-source product
- Current status of development/testing, dissemination, user base and contributor base; problem description; justification of team qualifications
- Track-specific content: Track 1 ecosystem discovery, governance, risk/security planning, community building; Track 2 growth, governance, continuous development, risk and security, sustainability, evaluation plan; Track 3 societal impact, targeted vulnerabilities, year-by-year development plan, evaluation plan
- 3-5 letters of collaboration (max 2 pages each) from current third-party users or contributors not directly related to the team, each stating writer name, affiliation and relationship; Track 3 letters must address the targeted vulnerabilities
- Facilities letter if the ecosystem will depend on an organisation's facilities after the award
- Personnel and Partner List table (full name, organisation(s), role) including letter writers
- Budget within track caps; voluntary committed cost sharing prohibited
- Track 2 applicants who already did I-Corps for PESOSE: I-Corps dates on the first line of the Project Description to waive it
- Standard PAPPG documents (biosketches, current and pending support, budget justification)

**Risks:** Needs a U.S.-based entity that is more than 50% U.S.-owned and a U.S.-resident PI. You must show an existing user and contributor base, backed by 3-5 independent letters. Tracks 2 and 3 have only about 10 awards each. Track 1 pays for planning, not product development. Keep the Solana payout and marketplace pieces out of scope. The 1 September 2026 deadline has passed, so the next one is 2 March 2027.

**Sources:** <https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/pose-pathways-enable-open-source-ecosystems>, <https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/pesose-pathways-enable-secure-open-source-ecosystems/nsf26-506/solicitation>, <https://nsf-gov-resources.nsf.gov/files/PESOSE_Technical_Webinar_8_2026_508c.pdf>

o%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Filecoin%20Foundation%3A%20Filecoin%20Dev%20Grants%20-%20Open%20Grants%0AAward%3A%20Open%20Grants%20applications%20for%20funding%20up%20to%20%2450%2C000%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Individuals%20or%20organisations%20proposing%20novel%20work%20that%20advances%20the%20Filecoin%20network%20%28developer%20and%20data%20tooling%2C%20integrations%2C%20research%20and%20protocols%2C%20storage%2C%20retrieval%2C%20FVM%29.%20The%20program%20prioritises%20Filecoin-network%20functionality%20and%20de-emphasises%20IPFS%2Flibp2p-only%20projects.%20The%20agreement%20and%20payments%20must%20be%20with%20the%20same%20individual%20or%20entity%20that%20applies.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20sanctions%2FKYC%20restrictions%20apply%20at%20contracting%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20for-profit%2C%20nonprofit.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Ffil.org%2Fgrants%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Ffilecoin-project%2Fdevgrants%2Fissues%2Fnew%3Flabels%3DOpen%2BGrant%26template%3Dopen-grant-appolication.md%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Open%20a%20GitHub%20issue%20in%20filecoin-project%2Fdevgrants%20using%20the%20Open%20Grant%20Application%20template.%202.%20Continuous%20review%3B%20the%20committee%20comments%20with%20questions%20or%20change%20requests%20on%20the%20issue.%203.%20Preliminary%20review%20update%20within%20about%202%20weeks.%204.%20Final%20decision%20generally%20within%204%20weeks%20of%20submission.%205.%20Sign%20the%20Open%20Source%20Software%20Grant%20Agreement%20with%20the%20work%20plan%20and%20funding%20milestones%20%28no%20work%20before%20signature%29.%206.%20Deliver%20milestones%20and%20get%20paid%20per%20milestone.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20name%20and%20proposal%20category%20%28Developer%20and%20data%20tooling%2C%20Integrations%2C%20Research%20%26%20protocols%2C%20Storage%2C%20Retrieval%2C%20FVM%2C%20Other%29%0A-%20Individual%20or%20entity%20name%20%28must%20match%20contracting%2Fpayment%20party%29%0A-%20Proposer%20GitHub%20username%20and%20project%20repo%28s%29%0A-%20Optional%3A%20Filecoin%2FProtocol%20Labs%20network%20affiliations%20and%20technical%20sponsor%0A-%20Yes%2FNo%20agreement%20to%20open-source%20all%20work%20under%20MIT%2FApache-2%20dual%20licence%0A-%20Project%20Summary%20%282-3%20paragraphs%29%0A-%20Impact%20%282-3%20paragraphs%3A%20pain%20points%2C%20benefits%2C%20risks%2C%20success%29%0A-%20Outcomes%20and%20deliverables%20with%20success%20metrics%0A-%20Data%20onboarding%20projections%20at%20months%201%2C%203%2C%206%2C%2012%20%28if%20onboarding%20data%20to%20Filecoin%29%0A-%20Adoption%2C%20reach%20and%20growth%20strategy%20%28first%2010%20%2F%20100%20users%29%0A-%20Development%20roadmap%20of%202-4%20milestones%20%28aim%20for%201-2%20pages%29%3A%20functionality%2C%20people%20and%20roles%2C%20funding%20and%20real%20dates%20per%20milestone%0A-%20Total%20budget%20table%20%28milestone%2C%20description%2C%20deliverables%2C%20completion%20date%2C%20funding%29%0A-%20Maintenance%20and%20upgrade%20plans%0A-%20Team%20members%2C%20LinkedIn%20profiles%2C%20team%20website%20%28https%29%2C%20relevant%20experience%2C%20prior%20code%20repositories%0A-%20How%20you%20heard%20of%20the%20program%20and%20contact%20email%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Preliminary%20update%20within%20~2%20weeks%3B%20final%20decision%20generally%20within%204%20weeks%20of%20submission%0AObligations%3A%20Signed%20Open%20Source%20Software%20Grant%20Agreement%3B%20milestone-based%20funding%3B%20all%20funded%20work%20open-sourced%20under%20MIT%2FApache-2%20dual%20licence%3B%20no%20work%20before%20both%20parties%20sign.%20No%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20Filecoin%20archival%20backend%20for%20ForkMesh%3A%20mirror%20nodes%20periodically%20seal%20signed%20repository%20snapshots%20%28Git%20packs%20plus%20signed%20issue%2FPR%20records%29%20into%20Filecoin%20deals%20and%20can%20restore%20a%20repo%20from%20Filecoin%20when%20every%20live%20mirror%20is%20gone%2C%20under%20the%20Developer%20and%20data%20tooling%20or%20Integrations%20category.%20This%20adds%20steady%20real%20data%20onboarding%20%28source-code%20preservation%29%20and%20a%20Web2%20developer-tool%20integration%2C%20both%20of%20which%20the%20program%20asks%20for.%0ARisks%3A%20Proposal%20is%20public%20as%20a%20GitHub%20issue.%20ForkMesh%27s%20code%20is%20MIT%3B%20the%20grant%20asks%20for%20MIT%2FApache-2%20dual%20licence%20on%20funded%20work%20%28compatible%20but%20needs%20a%20decision%29.%20Must%20show%20Filecoin-network%20usage%20%28data%20onboarding%29%2C%20not%20just%20IPFS.%20Heavy%20volume%20of%20AI-generated%20proposals%20in%20the%20queue%20%28several%20per%20day%20in%20Oct%202026%29%20raises%20competition.%20Payment%20currency%20%28FIL%20or%20fiat%29%20unconfirmed.)** · [Program page](https://fil.org/grants) · [Apply](https://github.com/filecoin-project/devgrants/issues/new?labels=Open+Grant&template=open-grant-appolication.md)

- **Funder:** Filecoin Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Open Grants applications for funding up to $50,000 (about $50,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 72/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Individuals or organisations proposing novel work that advances the Filecoin network (developer and data tooling, integrations, research and protocols, storage, retrieval, FVM). The program prioritises Filecoin-network functionality and de-emphasises IPFS/libp2p-only projects. The agreement and payments must be with the same individual or entity that applies.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: sanctions/KYC restrictions apply at contracting) · **Entity types:** Individuals, for-profit, nonprofit
- **Decision time:** Preliminary update within ~2 weeks; final decision generally within 4 weeks of submission
- **Obligations:** Signed Open Source Software Grant Agreement; milestone-based funding; all funded work open-sourced under MIT/Apache-2 dual licence; no work before both parties sign. No equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a Filecoin archival backend for ForkMesh: mirror nodes periodically seal signed repository snapshots (Git packs plus signed issue/PR records) into Filecoin deals and can restore a repo from Filecoin when every live mirror is gone, under the Developer and data tooling or Integrations category. This adds steady real data onboarding (source-code preservation) and a Web2 developer-tool integration, both of which the program asks for.

**How to apply:**

1. Open a GitHub issue in filecoin-project/devgrants using the Open Grant Application template.
2. Continuous review; the committee comments with questions or change requests on the issue.
3. Preliminary review update within about 2 weeks.
4. Final decision generally within 4 weeks of submission.
5. Sign the Open Source Software Grant Agreement with the work plan and funding milestones (no work before signature).
6. Deliver milestones and get paid per milestone.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project name and proposal category (Developer and data tooling, Integrations, Research & protocols, Storage, Retrieval, FVM, Other)
- Individual or entity name (must match contracting/payment party)
- Proposer GitHub username and project repo(s)
- Optional: Filecoin/Protocol Labs network affiliations and technical sponsor
- Yes/No agreement to open-source all work under MIT/Apache-2 dual licence
- Project Summary (2-3 paragraphs)
- Impact (2-3 paragraphs: pain points, benefits, risks, success)
- Outcomes and deliverables with success metrics
- Data onboarding projections at months 1, 3, 6, 12 (if onboarding data to Filecoin)
- Adoption, reach and growth strategy (first 10 / 100 users)
- Development roadmap of 2-4 milestones (aim for 1-2 pages): functionality, people and roles, funding and real dates per milestone
- Total budget table (milestone, description, deliverables, completion date, funding)
- Maintenance and upgrade plans
- Team members, LinkedIn profiles, team website (https), relevant experience, prior code repositories
- How you heard of the program and contact email

**Risks:** Proposal is public as a GitHub issue. ForkMesh's code is MIT; the grant asks for MIT/Apache-2 dual licence on funded work (compatible but needs a decision). Must show Filecoin-network usage (data onboarding), not just IPFS. Heavy volume of AI-generated proposals in the queue (several per day in Oct 2026) raises competition. Payment currency (FIL or fiat) unconfirmed.

**Sources:** <https://fil.org/grants>, <https://github.com/filecoin-project/devgrants>, <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/filecoin-project/devgrants/master/Program%20Resources/Open%20Grants%20README.md>, <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/filecoin-project/devgrants/master/.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/open-grant-appolication.md>, <https://api.github.com/repos/filecoin-project/devgrants/issues?state=all&per_page=5>

## 7. Solana Foundation Grants

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Solana%20Foundation%20Grants.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fsolana.org%2Fgrants-funding%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fshare.hsforms.com%2F1GE1hYdApQGaDiCgaiWMXHA5lohw%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Solana%20Foundation%20Grants%0AAward%3A%20No%20per-grant%20amount%20stated%3B%20the%20Foundation%20cites%20%24100M%2B%20committed%20across%20500%2B%20projects.%20Milestone-based.%20%28Superteam%20microgrants%2C%20a%20separate%20track%2C%20are%20up%20to%20%2410k.%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Anyone%20%28individuals%2C%20teams%2C%20companies%2C%20nonprofits%2C%20universities%2C%20governments%29%20building%20a%20public%20good%20for%20the%20Solana%20ecosystem%3A%20a%20significant%20open-source%20contribution%2C%20a%20meaningful%20free%20community%20offering%2C%20or%20a%20novel%20proof%20of%20concept%2C%20and%20able%20to%20explain%20why%20it%20is%20only%20possible%20on%20Solana.%20Projects%20with%20a%20commercial%20component%20are%20steered%20to%20convertible%20grants.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20sanctioned%20jurisdictions%20presumably%20excluded%20at%20contracting%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20for-profit%2C%20nonprofit%2C%20universities%2C%20governments.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsolana.org%2Fgrants-funding%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fshare.hsforms.com%2F1GE1hYdApQGaDiCgaiWMXHA5lohw%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Optionally%20browse%20the%20Foundation%20RFP%20list%20%28Airtable%29%20for%20a%20matching%20request.%202.%20Submit%20the%20Foundation%20Grants%20form%20%28HubSpot%29%20with%20overview%2C%20public-good%20case%2C%20budget%20and%20milestones.%203.%20Rolling%20review%20%28about%201%20week%29%3B%20subject-matter%20experts%20may%20schedule%20a%20call%20for%20technical%20due%20diligence%20and%20market%20research.%204.%20Decision%20by%20email%20%28about%203%20weeks%29.%205.%20Negotiate%20and%20sign%20the%20grant%20agreement%20with%20the%20Foundation%20legal%20team%20%28standard%20or%20convertible%29.%206.%20Deliver%20milestones%3B%20funds%20released%20per%20milestone.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20overview%0A-%20Explanation%20of%20how%20the%20project%20is%20a%20public%20good%20for%20Solana%20and%20why%20it%20is%20only%20possible%20on%20Solana%0A-%20Structured%20budget%0A-%20Clear%2C%20measurable%20funding%20milestones%20tied%20to%20community%20impact%0A-%20Open-source%20repository%20%2F%20plan%20to%20open-source%20learnings%0A-%20Contact%20email%20for%20decision%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20HubSpot%20form%20fields%20%28not%20shown%20on%20the%20official%20page%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%201%20week%20review%20plus%20about%203%20weeks%20to%20decision%20%28official%20page%29%3B%20rolling%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone-based%20payments%20under%20a%20signed%20grant%20agreement%3B%20open-source%20%2F%20public-good%20deliverables.%20Convertible%20grants%20convert%20into%20an%20investment%20once%20agreed%20milestones%20are%20met%20%28equity%2Ftoken-like%20obligation%20for%20commercial%20projects%29.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20open-sourcing%20ForkMesh%27s%20Solana-based%20community%20payout%20pool%20as%20a%20reusable%20public%20good%3A%20self-custodial%20contributor%20wallets%2C%20on-chain%20payouts%20for%20merged%20pull%20requests%20and%20mirror%20hosting%2C%20all%20keyed%20to%20signed%20Git%20records%2C%20usable%20by%20any%20open-source%20project.%20ForkMesh%20already%20references%20Solana%20for%20payouts%2C%20so%20the%20%27only%20possible%20on%20Solana%27%20case%20%28cheap%2C%20fast%20micro-payouts%20to%20many%20contributors%29%20is%20natural%20and%20the%20Foundation%20funds%20developer%20public%20goods.%0ARisks%3A%20Funding%20is%20only%20for%20the%20Solana-specific%20public-good%20part%3B%20the%20hosted%20paid%20tier%20would%20push%20the%20Foundation%20toward%20a%20convertible%20grant%20%28investment-like%20obligation%29.%20No%20stated%20grant%20size%3B%20third-party%20guides%20%28unverified%29%20suggest%20typical%20grants%20of%20USD%2010k-50k.%20Crypto%20payouts%20may%20raise%20regulatory%2FKYC%20questions%20depending%20on%20ForkMesh%27s%20jurisdiction.)** · [Program page](https://solana.org/grants-funding) · [Apply](https://share.hsforms.com/1GE1hYdApQGaDiCgaiWMXHA5lohw)

- **Funder:** Solana Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** No per-grant amount stated; the Foundation cites $100M+ committed across 500+ projects. Milestone-based. (Superteam microgrants, a separate track, are up to $10k.) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 68/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Anyone (individuals, teams, companies, nonprofits, universities, governments) building a public good for the Solana ecosystem: a significant open-source contribution, a meaningful free community offering, or a novel proof of concept, and able to explain why it is only possible on Solana. Projects with a commercial component are steered to convertible grants.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: sanctioned jurisdictions presumably excluded at contracting) · **Entity types:** Individuals, for-profit, nonprofit, universities, governments
- **Decision time:** About 1 week review plus about 3 weeks to decision (official page); rolling
- **Obligations:** Milestone-based payments under a signed grant agreement; open-source / public-good deliverables. Convertible grants convert into an investment once agreed milestones are met (equity/token-like obligation for commercial projects).

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose open-sourcing ForkMesh's Solana-based community payout pool as a reusable public good: self-custodial contributor wallets, on-chain payouts for merged pull requests and mirror hosting, all keyed to signed Git records, usable by any open-source project. ForkMesh already references Solana for payouts, so the 'only possible on Solana' case (cheap, fast micro-payouts to many contributors) is natural and the Foundation funds developer public goods.

**How to apply:**

1. Optionally browse the Foundation RFP list (Airtable) for a matching request.
2. Submit the Foundation Grants form (HubSpot) with overview, public-good case, budget and milestones.
3. Rolling review (about 1 week); subject-matter experts may schedule a call for technical due diligence and market research.
4. Decision by email (about 3 weeks).
5. Negotiate and sign the grant agreement with the Foundation legal team (standard or convertible).
6. Deliver milestones; funds released per milestone.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project overview
- Explanation of how the project is a public good for Solana and why it is only possible on Solana
- Structured budget
- Clear, measurable funding milestones tied to community impact
- Open-source repository / plan to open-source learnings
- Contact email for decision
- unconfirmed: exact HubSpot form fields (not shown on the official page)

**Risks:** Funding is only for the Solana-specific public-good part; the hosted paid tier would push the Foundation toward a convertible grant (investment-like obligation). No stated grant size; third-party guides (unverified) suggest typical grants of USD 10k-50k. Crypto payouts may raise regulatory/KYC questions depending on ForkMesh's jurisdiction.

**Sources:** <https://solana.org/grants-funding>, <https://solana.com/grants>, <https://airtable.com/apppDmK2Pin9WX8jV/shrR0uMKu4N57TGW7/tbli2ERM3sdhyHJYB>, <https://share.hsforms.com/1GE1hYdApQGaDiCgaiWMXHA5lohw>

<a id="scaleway-startup-program"></a>
## 8. Scaleway Startup Program

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Scaleway%20Startup%20Program.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.scaleway.com%2Fen%2Fstartup-program%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.scaleway.com%2Fen%2Fstartup-program%2Fearly-stage-program%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Scaleway%20Startup%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Founders%20Program%3A%20up%20to%20EUR%201%2C000%20credits%20over%20one%20year%3B%20Early%20Stage%3A%20EUR%201%2C500%2Fmonth%20for%206%20months%20%28EUR%209%2C000%20total%2C%20non-renewable%29%3B%20Growth%20Stage%3A%20EUR%203%2C000%2Fmonth%20for%2012%20months%20%28EUR%2036%2C000%20total%29.%20Page%20FAQ%20also%20quotes%20lower%20monthly%20starting%20figures%20%28EUR%20500%20%2F%20EUR%201%2C500%29%2C%20so%20confirm.%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Startups%20less%20than%205%20years%20old%20with%20fewer%20than%2050%20employees%20that%20are%20not%20yet%20Scaleway%20customers%20%28or%20are%20moving%20up%20from%20the%20Founders%20stage%29.%20Early%20Stage%20requires%20a%20minimum%20cloud%20consumption%20of%20about%20EUR%20500%2Fmonth%3B%20navigation%20describes%20Early%20Stage%20as%20pre-seed%20to%20seed%20and%20Growth%20as%20seed%20to%20Series%20A.%20Focus%20on%20startups%20going%20to%20market%2C%20especially%20in%20the%20EU.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20geographic%20restriction%20stated%3B%20EU-market%20focus.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20startups%20%28company%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.scaleway.com%2Fen%2Fstartup-program%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.scaleway.com%2Fen%2Fstartup-program%2Fearly-stage-program%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Start%20with%20the%20Founders%20Program%20%28self-serve%20credits%29%20or%20apply%20directly%20to%20Early%2FGrowth%20Stage.%202.%20Submit%20the%20single%20application%20form%20linked%20from%20the%20stage%20page%20%28%27Apply%20to%20Early%20Stage%20Program%21%27%29.%203.%20The%20admission%20committee%20reviews%20applications%20on%20a%20rolling%20%28weekly%2Fmonthly%29%20basis%3B%20decision%20within%2021%20business%20days.%204.%20Collective%20onboarding%20session%20and%20on-demand%20CSM%20access%3B%20credits%20activated%20for%206%20or%2012%20months.%205.%20Scaleway%20periodically%20reviews%20consumption%20and%20plan%20adjustments.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Company%20details%20%28age%2C%20headcount%2C%20website%29%0A-%20Scaleway%20account%20%28not%20an%20existing%20paying%20customer%29%0A-%20Expected%20cloud%20consumption%20%2F%20architecture%20plan%20%28minimum%20EUR%20500%2Fmonth%20for%20Early%20Stage%29%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20funding%20stage%20and%20investors%3B%20full%20form%20fields%20not%20visible%20without%20opening%20the%20form%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Within%2021%20business%20days%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20time-limited%20%286%20or%2012%20months%29%20and%20non-renewable%20for%20Early%20Stage%3B%20consumption%20beyond%20credits%20is%20billed.%20Expected%20willingness%20to%20share%20a%20customer%20story%20%2F%20co-marketing.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Run%20European%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20nodes%20and%20runner%20capacity%20on%20Scaleway%20%28Paris%2FAmsterdam%2FWarsaw%29%2C%20adding%20a%20sovereign%20EU%20provider%20to%20the%20mirror%20fleet%20so%20no%20single%20hyperscaler%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Scaleway%20markets%20European%20digital%20sovereignty%2C%20which%20matches%20ForkMesh%27s%20resilient%2C%20provider-independent%20Git%20hosting%20story.%0ARisks%3A%20Amounts%20on%20the%20page%20are%20internally%20inconsistent%3B%20must%20not%20already%20be%20a%20Scaleway%20customer%3B%20Early%20Stage%20needs%20real%20spend%20of%20EUR%20500%2Fmonth.%20Entity%20country%20unknown%2C%20though%20no%20geography%20rule%20is%20stated.%20Larger%20Growth%20tier%20implies%20seed-to-Series%20A%20funding.)** · [Program page](https://www.scaleway.com/en/startup-program/) · [Apply](https://www.scaleway.com/en/startup-program/early-stage-program/)

- **Funder:** Scaleway (Iliad group)
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Founders Program: up to EUR 1,000 credits over one year; Early Stage: EUR 1,500/month for 6 months (EUR 9,000 total, non-renewable); Growth Stage: EUR 3,000/month for 12 months (EUR 36,000 total). Page FAQ also quotes lower monthly starting figures (EUR 500 / EUR 1,500), so confirm. (about $1,100 - $39,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 66/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Startups less than 5 years old with fewer than 50 employees that are not yet Scaleway customers (or are moving up from the Founders stage). Early Stage requires a minimum cloud consumption of about EUR 500/month; navigation describes Early Stage as pre-seed to seed and Growth as seed to Series A. Focus on startups going to market, especially in the EU.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: no geographic restriction stated; EU-market focus · **Entity types:** For-profit startups (company)
- **Decision time:** Within 21 business days
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits time-limited (6 or 12 months) and non-renewable for Early Stage; consumption beyond credits is billed. Expected willingness to share a customer story / co-marketing.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Run European ForkMesh mirror nodes and runner capacity on Scaleway (Paris/Amsterdam/Warsaw), adding a sovereign EU provider to the mirror fleet so no single hyperscaler is a point of failure. Scaleway markets European digital sovereignty, which matches ForkMesh's resilient, provider-independent Git hosting story.

**How to apply:**

1. Start with the Founders Program (self-serve credits) or apply directly to Early/Growth Stage.
2. Submit the single application form linked from the stage page ('Apply to Early Stage Program!').
3. The admission committee reviews applications on a rolling (weekly/monthly) basis; decision within 21 business days.
4. Collective onboarding session and on-demand CSM access; credits activated for 6 or 12 months.
5. Scaleway periodically reviews consumption and plan adjustments.

**What the application asks for:**

- Company details (age, headcount, website)
- Scaleway account (not an existing paying customer)
- Expected cloud consumption / architecture plan (minimum EUR 500/month for Early Stage)
- unconfirmed: funding stage and investors; full form fields not visible without opening the form

**Risks:** Amounts on the page are internally inconsistent; must not already be a Scaleway customer; Early Stage needs real spend of EUR 500/month. Entity country unknown, though no geography rule is stated. Larger Growth tier implies seed-to-Series A funding.

**Sources:** <https://www.scaleway.com/en/startup-program/>, <https://www.scaleway.com/en/startup-program/early-stage-program/>
