ure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Akamai%20Technologies%3A%20Rise%20Start-Up%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Receive%20between%20USD%20500%20and%20USD%20120%2C000%20in%20cloud%20credits%20during%20your%20first%20year%3B%20discounted%20cloud%20services%20in%20years%20two%20and%20three%20%28third-party%20sources%20report%2050%25%20off%20in%20year%202%20and%2025%25%20off%20in%20year%203%20-%20unconfirmed%20on%20official%20page%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Early-stage%20for-profit%20company%20under%20seven%20years%20old%2C%20new%20to%20Rise%20and%20not%20an%20existing%20Akamai%20Cloud%20customer%2C%20with%20a%20plan%20to%20scale%20infrastructure%20using%20Akamai%20Cloud%20as%20a%20primary%20provider.%20Must%20show%20at%20least%20one%20of%3A%20paid%20staff%20beyond%20founders%2C%20active%20non-friends%2Ffamily%20users%2C%20initial%20revenue%2C%20or%20VC%20funding.%20Managed%20hosting%20providers%2C%20MSPs%2C%20system%20integrators%20and%20resellers%20are%20not%20eligible%3B%20SaaS%2C%20media%20and%20gaming%20are%20prioritised.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20geographic%20restriction%20stated.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20companies%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.akamai.com%2Fcloud%2Fstart-ups%2Frise%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.akamai.com%2Fcloud%2Fstart-ups%2Frise%23rise%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20a%20new%20Akamai%20Cloud%20account%20for%20the%20startup%20with%20a%20corporate-domain%20email%20and%20valid%20credit%20card.%202.%20Submit%20the%20%27Apply%20to%20Rise%20program%27%20intake%20form%20on%20the%20Rise%20page%20about%20the%20startup%20and%20its%20cloud%20needs.%203.%20Review%20with%20a%20status%20update%20typically%20within%201-2%20weeks.%204.%20Discovery%20call%20with%20a%20team%20member%20for%20qualified%20applicants.%205.%20Onboarding%3A%20credits%20added%20to%20the%20account%20and%20next%20steps%20set%20with%20the%20program%20coordinator.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20New%20Akamai%20Cloud%20account%20using%20a%20corporate%20email%20on%20the%20company%20domain%0A-%20Valid%20credit%20card%20on%20the%20account%0A-%20Working%20company%20website%20matching%20the%20email%20domain%0A-%20Corporate%20entity%20name%20in%20the%20Company%20field%0A-%20Intake%20form%20answers%20on%20the%20startup%2C%20traction%20signal%20%28staff%2C%20users%2C%20revenue%20or%20VC%20funding%29%20and%20cloud%20infrastructure%20needs%20%2F%20scaling%20plan%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Status%20update%20typically%20within%201-2%20weeks%2C%20then%20a%20discovery%20call%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20do%20not%20roll%20over%20month%20to%20month%20or%20between%20years%3B%20usage%20above%20the%20monthly%20credit%20limit%20is%20billed.%20Expected%20to%20use%20Akamai%20Cloud%20as%20a%20primary%20provider%20and%20scale%20on%20it%20over%20three%20years.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Run%20a%20set%20of%20ForkMesh%20mirror-fleet%20nodes%20and%20runner-tier%20workers%20on%20Akamai%20Cloud%20regions%20worldwide%2C%20giving%20the%20fleet%20an%20additional%20independent%20provider%20with%20distributed%20edge%20presence.%20Akamai%20prioritises%20SaaS%20startups%20with%20real%20users%20that%20will%20grow%20workloads%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%27s%20paid%20bounded%20hosting%20and%20mirror%20fleet%20are%20exactly%20that%20kind%20of%20compute%20and%20egress%20consumer.%0ARisks%3A%20Exclusion%20of%20%27managed%20hosting%20providers%27%20could%20be%20read%20to%20cover%20ForkMesh%27s%20hosted%20repository%20service%3B%20frame%20it%20as%20a%20SaaS%20developer%20platform.%20Requires%20a%20traction%20signal%20and%20treating%20Akamai%20as%20a%20primary%20provider%2C%20which%20conflicts%20with%20the%20existing%20Vultr-based%20fleet.%20Existing%20Akamai%20customers%20are%20ineligible.%20Credit%20amount%20within%20the%20range%20is%20discretionary.)** · [Program page](https://www.akamai.com/cloud/start-ups/rise) · [Apply](https://www.akamai.com/cloud/start-ups/rise#rise)

- **Funder:** Akamai Technologies (Akamai Cloud, formerly Linode)
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Receive between USD 500 and USD 120,000 in cloud credits during your first year; discounted cloud services in years two and three (third-party sources report 50% off in year 2 and 25% off in year 3 - unconfirmed on official page) (about $500 - $120,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 64/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Early-stage for-profit company under seven years old, new to Rise and not an existing Akamai Cloud customer, with a plan to scale infrastructure using Akamai Cloud as a primary provider. Must show at least one of: paid staff beyond founders, active non-friends/family users, initial revenue, or VC funding. Managed hosting providers, MSPs, system integrators and resellers are not eligible; SaaS, media and gaming are prioritised.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: no geographic restriction stated · **Entity types:** For-profit companies only
- **Decision time:** Status update typically within 1-2 weeks, then a discovery call
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits do not roll over month to month or between years; usage above the monthly credit limit is billed. Expected to use Akamai Cloud as a primary provider and scale on it over three years.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Run a set of ForkMesh mirror-fleet nodes and runner-tier workers on Akamai Cloud regions worldwide, giving the fleet an additional independent provider with distributed edge presence. Akamai prioritises SaaS startups with real users that will grow workloads, and ForkMesh's paid bounded hosting and mirror fleet are exactly that kind of compute and egress consumer.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a new Akamai Cloud account for the startup with a corporate-domain email and valid credit card.
2. Submit the 'Apply to Rise program' intake form on the Rise page about the startup and its cloud needs.
3. Review with a status update typically within 1-2 weeks.
4. Discovery call with a team member for qualified applicants.
5. Onboarding: credits added to the account and next steps set with the program coordinator.

**What the application asks for:**

- New Akamai Cloud account using a corporate email on the company domain
- Valid credit card on the account
- Working company website matching the email domain
- Corporate entity name in the Company field
- Intake form answers on the startup, traction signal (staff, users, revenue or VC funding) and cloud infrastructure needs / scaling plan

**Risks:** Exclusion of 'managed hosting providers' could be read to cover ForkMesh's hosted repository service; frame it as a SaaS developer platform. Requires a traction signal and treating Akamai as a primary provider, which conflicts with the existing Vultr-based fleet. Existing Akamai customers are ineligible. Credit amount within the range is discretionary.

**Sources:** <https://www.akamai.com/cloud/start-ups/rise>, <https://linode.com/rise>, <https://betterstack.com/community/comparisons/akamai-rise-vs-ovhcloud-startups/>

<a id="nsf-sbir-sttr-phase1"></a>
## 10. U.S. National Science Foundation: America's Seed Fund: NSF SBIR/STTR Phase I

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20U.S.%20National%20Science%20Foundation%3A%20America%27s%20Seed%20Fund%3A%20NSF%20SBIR%2FSTTR%20Phase%20I.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.nsf.gov%2Ffunding%2Fopportunities%2Fsmall-business-innovation-research-small-business-technology%2Fnsf26-510%2Fsolicitation%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fseedfund.nsf.gov%2Fproject-pitch%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20U.S.%20National%20Science%20Foundation%3A%20America%27s%20Seed%20Fund%3A%20NSF%20SBIR%2FSTTR%20Phase%20I%0AAward%3A%20Phase%20I%3A%20up%20to%20%24305%2C000%20for%206-18%20months%2C%20covering%20direct%20and%20indirect%20costs%2C%20the%20small%20business%20fee%2C%20Technical%20and%20Business%20Assistance%20%28TABA%29%20and%20the%20optional%20I-Corps%20component%3B%20%27up%20to%20%242%20million%20in%20seed%20funding%27%20across%20phases%3B%20zero%20equity%0ADeadline%3A%202027-03-04%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Small%20business%20concerns%20with%20500%20or%20fewer%20employees%20including%20affiliates%3B%20the%20PI%20must%20be%20at%20least%2051%25%20employed%20by%20the%20company%20and%20legally%20authorised%20to%20work%20in%20the%20U.S.%20A%20Project%20Pitch%20invitation%20is%20required%20before%20a%20full%20proposal.%20Majority%20U.S.%20ownership%20applies%20under%20SBIR%20rules%20%28stated%20for%20DoW%20SBIR%20on%20the%20AFWERX%20page%3B%20unconfirmed%20in%20the%20NSF%20text%20fetched%29.%20Geography%3A%20US%20only.%20Entity%20types%3A%20U.S.%20for-profit%20small%20businesses%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.nsf.gov%2Ffunding%2Fopportunities%2Fsmall-business-innovation-research-small-business-technology%2Fnsf26-510%2Fsolicitation%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fseedfund.nsf.gov%2Fproject-pitch%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Register%20in%20SAM.gov%20and%20Research.gov.%202.%20Submit%20a%20Project%20Pitch%20in%20NSF%20MyWork%3B%20one%20pending%20pitch%20at%20a%20time%2C%20max%20two%20per%2012%20months%2C%20max%20three%20for%20the%20same%20technology.%203.%20Get%20an%20official%20invitation%2C%20which%20is%20valid%20for%20the%20next%20two%20deadlines.%204.%20Submit%20the%20full%20Phase%20I%20proposal%20in%20Research.gov%20by%205%20p.m.%20local%20time%20%284%20Nov%202026%20only%20for%20existing%20invitations%3B%20then%204%20March%202027%2C%207%20July%202027%29.%205.%20Merit%20review%20and%20award%3B%20Phase%20II%20later.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20Pitch%3A%20Technology%20Innovation%20%28max%203%2C500%20characters%29%0A-%20Project%20Pitch%3A%20Technical%20Objectives%20and%20Challenges%20%28max%203%2C500%20characters%29%0A-%20Project%20Pitch%3A%20Market%20Opportunity%20%28max%201%2C750%20characters%29%0A-%20Project%20Pitch%3A%20Company%20and%20Team%20%28max%201%2C750%20characters%29%0A-%20Full%20proposal%20per%20PAPPG%20Chapter%20II.D.2%20plus%20NSF%2026-510%20supplemental%20instructions%20%28project%20description%20and%20commercialisation%20sections%3B%20exact%20page%20limits%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Budget%20including%20TABA%20and%20optional%20I-Corps%20component%2C%20total%20max%20%24305%2C000%0A-%20Active%20SAM.gov%20UEI%20registration%0A-%20Foreign-risk%20due%20diligence%20answers%20if%20asked%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20pitch%20response%20time%20and%20full-proposal%20decision%20time%20are%20not%20stated%20on%20the%20pages%20fetched%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity%20taken%3B%20the%20company%20keeps%20its%20IP.%20PI%20must%20stay%20at%20least%2051%25%20employed%20by%20the%20company%20and%20give%20at%20least%20one%20calendar%20month%20per%20six%20months.%20Phase%20I%20has%20its%20own%20reporting%20requirements.%20Voluntary%20cost%20sharing%20is%20prohibited.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20the%20technical%20risk%20in%20making%20Git%20hosting%20independent%20of%20any%20one%20provider%3A%20a%20signed%2C%20verifiable%20mirror%20network%20that%20keeps%20clones%20available%20and%20tamper-evident%20when%20the%20origin%20fails%2C%20plus%20signed%20collaboration%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20NSF%20SBIR%20funds%20unproven%2C%20high-impact%20technology%20with%20a%20commercial%20path%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%27s%20paid%20hosted%20services%20are%20that%20path.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20a%20U.S.%20small%20business.%20The%20PI%20must%20be%20primarily%20employed%20by%20the%20company.%20NSF%20screens%20out%20incremental%20work%20or%20simple%20integration%2C%20so%20the%20R%26D%20risk%20has%20to%20be%20clear.%20Reviewers%20may%20question%20an%20MIT-licensed%20core%20business%20model%2C%20and%20the%20crypto%20payouts%20could%20put%20them%20off.%20SBIR%20was%20paused%20from%20October%202025%20until%20reauthorisation%20in%202026%2C%20so%20check%20that%20the%20dates%20do%20not%20move%20again.)** · [Program page](https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/small-business-innovation-research-small-business-technology/nsf26-510/solicitation) · [Apply](https://seedfund.nsf.gov/project-pitch/)

- **Funder:** U.S. National Science Foundation
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Phase I: up to $305,000 for 6-18 months, covering direct and indirect costs, the small business fee, Technical and Business Assistance (TABA) and the optional I-Corps component; 'up to $2 million in seed funding' across phases; zero equity (about $305,000)
- **Deadline:** 2027-03-04 (open)
- **Fit score:** 62/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Small business concerns with 500 or fewer employees including affiliates; the PI must be at least 51% employed by the company and legally authorised to work in the U.S. A Project Pitch invitation is required before a full proposal. Majority U.S. ownership applies under SBIR rules (stated for DoW SBIR on the AFWERX page; unconfirmed in the NSF text fetched).
- **Geography:** US only · **Entity types:** U.S. for-profit small businesses only
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: pitch response time and full-proposal decision time are not stated on the pages fetched
- **Obligations:** No equity taken; the company keeps its IP. PI must stay at least 51% employed by the company and give at least one calendar month per six months. Phase I has its own reporting requirements. Voluntary cost sharing is prohibited.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch the technical risk in making Git hosting independent of any one provider: a signed, verifiable mirror network that keeps clones available and tamper-evident when the origin fails, plus signed collaboration records stored in the repository. NSF SBIR funds unproven, high-impact technology with a commercial path, and ForkMesh's paid hosted services are that path.

**How to apply:**

1. Register in SAM.gov and Research.gov.
2. Submit a Project Pitch in NSF MyWork; one pending pitch at a time, max two per 12 months, max three for the same technology.
3. Get an official invitation, which is valid for the next two deadlines.
4. Submit the full Phase I proposal in Research.gov by 5 p.m. local time (4 Nov 2026 only for existing invitations; then 4 March 2027, 7 July 2027).
5. Merit review and award; Phase II later.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project Pitch: Technology Innovation (max 3,500 characters)
- Project Pitch: Technical Objectives and Challenges (max 3,500 characters)
- Project Pitch: Market Opportunity (max 1,750 characters)
- Project Pitch: Company and Team (max 1,750 characters)
- Full proposal per PAPPG Chapter II.D.2 plus NSF 26-510 supplemental instructions (project description and commercialisation sections; exact page limits unconfirmed)
- Budget including TABA and optional I-Corps component, total max $305,000
- Active SAM.gov UEI registration
- Foreign-risk due diligence answers if asked

**Risks:** Needs a U.S. small business. The PI must be primarily employed by the company. NSF screens out incremental work or simple integration, so the R&D risk has to be clear. Reviewers may question an MIT-licensed core business model, and the crypto payouts could put them off. SBIR was paused from October 2025 until reauthorisation in 2026, so check that the dates do not move again.

**Sources:** <https://seedfund.nsf.gov/>, <https://seedfund.nsf.gov/solicitations/>, <https://seedfund.nsf.gov/project-pitch/>, <https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/small-business-innovation-research-small-business-technology/nsf26-510/solicitation>

20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Anthropic%3A%20Claude%20for%20Startups%0AAward%3A%20USD%201%2C000%20Claude%20Console%20API%20credit%20%286-month%20expiry%29%20%2B%201%20year%20of%20Claude%20Team%20%28up%20to%205%20seats%29%20%2B%20Startup%20Stack%20partner%20offers%20worth%20up%20to%20USD%2045%2C000%3B%20up%20to%20USD%20100K%20additional%20API%20credits%20if%20backed%20by%20a%20VC%20in%20Anthropic%27s%20partner%20network%0ADeadline%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20next%20cohort%20date%20not%20announced%20%28page%20says%20current%20cohort%20is%20full%3B%20subscribe%20to%20the%20Claude%20Startup%20newsletter%20for%20reopening%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Early-stage%20startups%20building%20on%20Claude%3B%20per%20October%202026%20coverage%2C%20bootstrapped%2C%20pre-seed%20and%20venture-backed%20companies%20founded%20within%20the%20last%20five%20years%20or%20that%20raised%20funding%20within%20the%20last%20two%20years.%20Requires%20an%20active%20Claude%20Console%20account%20and%20a%20company-domain%20email.%20The%20up-to-USD%20100K%20tier%20is%20only%20claimable%20through%20a%20partner%20VC. tion%20in%20the%20agent%20workflow.%20Anthropic%20cares%20about%20startups%20building%20agentic%20developer%20products%20on%20Claude%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%20ships%20Claude%20Code%20as%20a%20supported%20agent%20runtime.%0ARisks%3A%20Applications%20currently%20closed%20%28cohort%20full%29%20with%20no%20reopening%20date.%20Base%20credit%20is%20small%20%28USD%201%2C000%29%3B%20the%20large%20tier%20needs%20a%20partner-VC%20backer%2C%20which%20ForkMesh%20may%20not%20have.%20Entity%20must%20be%20a%20company%20with%20domain%20email%3B%20legal%20entity%20status%20unknown.%20Distinct%20from%20Claude%20for%20Open%20Source%2C%20which%20is%20covered%20elsewhere.)** · [Program page](https://claude.com/programs/startups) · [Apply](https://claude.com/programs/startups)

- **Funder:** Anthropic
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** USD 1,000 Claude Console API credit (6-month expiry) + 1 year of Claude Team (up to 5 seats) + Startup Stack partner offers worth up to USD 45,000; up to USD 100K additional API credits if backed by a VC in Anthropic's partner network (about $1,000 - $100,000)
- **Deadline:** unconfirmed: next cohort date not announced (page says current cohort is full; subscribe to the Claude Startup newsletter for reopening) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 62/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Early-stage startups building on Claude; per October 2026 coverage, bootstrapped, pre-seed and venture-backed companies founded within the last five years or that raised funding within the last two years. Requires an active Claude Console account and a company-domain email. The up-to-USD 100K tier is only claimable through a partner VC.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: no geographic restriction stated on the official page · **Entity types:** For-profit startups (company); individuals and nonprofits not addressed
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits restricted to the first-party Claude API in the Claude Console (not Bedrock, Vertex AI or Azure); USD 1,000 credit expires after 6 months; one-time per company.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Use credits to run Claude Code agents in ForkMesh desktop worktrees and the runner tier (agent-opened pull requests, review assistance on signed PR records), and to build first-class Claude integration in the agent workflow. Anthropic cares about startups building agentic developer products on Claude, and ForkMesh ships Claude Code as a supported agent runtime.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a Claude Console account using a company-domain email.
2. Subscribe to the Claude Startup newsletter to be notified when the next cohort opens (applications currently closed).
3. Submit the application on claude.com/programs/startups when it reopens.
4. Once accepted, sign in at platform.claude.com/claude-for-startups and redeem credits and Startup Stack codes in the Console.
5. If backed by a partner VC, request the additional API credits through that VC.

**What the application asks for:**

- Active Claude Console account / organization ID
- Company-domain email address
- unconfirmed: company details (name, website, founding date, funding stage) - form not visible while applications are closed
- Partner-VC referral for the up-to-USD 100K tier

**Risks:** Applications currently closed (cohort full) with no reopening date. Base credit is small (USD 1,000); the large tier needs a partner-VC backer, which ForkMesh may not have. Entity must be a company with domain email; legal entity status unknown. Distinct from Claude for Open Source, which is covered elsewhere.

**Sources:** <https://claude.com/programs/startups>, <https://androidheadlines.com/2026/10/anthropic-expands-claude-startups-program.html>, <https://guptadeepak.com/anthropic-claude-for-startups-the-complete-guide-to-credits-tiers-and-eligibility-2026/>

<a id="open-source-collective-fiscal-host"></a>
## 12. Open Source Collective: Fiscal hosting for open source projects

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Open%20Source%20Collective%3A%20Fiscal%20hosting%20for%20open%20source%20projects.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fdocs.oscollective.org%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fopencollective.com%2Fopensource%2Fapply%2Fintro%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Open%20Source%20Collective%3A%20Fiscal%20hosting%20for%20open%20source%20projects%0AAward%3A%20No%20grant%20itself%3B%20a%20funding%20channel.%20Fixed%2010%25%20host%20fee%20on%20incoming%20funds%2C%20no%20setup%20fees%20or%20minimum%20balances%3B%20payment%20processor%20fees%20extra.%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Original%20or%20substantially%20forked%20open%20source%20project%20with%20recent%20activity%20or%20significant%20usage%2C%20OSI%2FFSF%2FDebian%2FFedora-recognised%20license%2C%20hosted%20in%20an%20organisation%20repository%20%28not%20a%20personal%20account%29%2C%20applicant%20must%20be%20a%20maintainer%3B%20two%20or%20more%20admins%20preferred.%20Not%20for%20business%20transactions%20benefiting%20an%20individual%20or%20customer.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28except%20sanctioned%20countries%29%3B%20funds%20held%20in%20USD%3B%20can%20pay%20non-US%20maintainers.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Unincorporated%20open%20source%20projects%20%2F%20communities%20%28the%20project%2C%20not%20a%20company%2C%20is%20hosted%3B%20for-profit%20status%20not%20addressed%20by%20OSC%20docs%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdocs.oscollective.org%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopencollective.com%2Fopensource%2Fapply%2Fintro%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20the%20Terms%20of%20Fiscal%20Sponsorship%20and%20fee%20page%3B%20confirm%20eligibility.%202.%20Line%20up%20at%20least%20two%20community%20members%20as%20admins.%203.%20Create%20the%20collective%20and%20apply%20at%20opencollective.com%2Fopensource%2Fapply%2Fintro.%204.%20Verify%20via%20GitHub%20OAuth%20%28clear%20cases%20auto-approved%29%20or%20request%20manual%20verification%20with%20project%20description%20and%20links.%205.%20OSC%20reviews%20%28aims%20for%20weekly%20review%3B%20a%20few%20business%20days%29.%206.%20Collective%20goes%20live%3B%20set%20up%20tiers%2C%20link%20GitHub%20Sponsors%20%2F%20thanks.dev%20%2F%20company%20funders.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Agreement%20to%20Terms%20of%20Fiscal%20Sponsorship%0A-%20GitHub%20authentication%20of%20an%20organisation%20repository%20%28or%20manual%20verification%20request%29%0A-%20Manual%20path%3A%20description%20of%20the%20project%20and%20its%20goals%2C%20links%20to%20repositories%2C%20website%20and%20social%20accounts%0A-%20Collective%20profile%3A%20name%2C%20slug%2C%20description%2C%20admins%20%282%2B%20preferred%29%0A-%20Encouraged%3A%20CONTRIBUTING%20guide%2C%20code%20of%20conduct%2C%20documented%20decision-making%20process%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Automatic%20to%20a%20few%20business%20days%20%28weekly%20review%20target%29%0AObligations%3A%2010%25%20host%20fee%3B%20expenses%20must%20be%20submitted%20publicly%20with%20receipts%2Finvoices%20on%20the%20transparent%20ledger%3B%20funds%20must%20serve%20the%20open%20source%20project%2C%20not%20private%20business%20benefit%3B%20crypto%20only%20case-by-case%20via%20Drips%3B%20payments%20to%20sanctioned%20countries%20not%20possible.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Open%20a%20%27forkmesh%27%20collective%20for%20the%20MIT-licensed%20protocol%20and%20public%20mirror%20network%20so%20individual%20sponsors%2C%20companies%20%28Open%20Source%20Pledge%20members%29%20and%20thanks.dev%20dependency-funders%20can%20pay%20into%20a%20transparent%20ledger%20that%20covers%20mirror-fleet%20hosting%20and%20contributor%20bounties.%20It%20is%20the%20lowest-effort%20way%20to%20make%20ForkMesh%20fundable%20by%20the%20wider%20open%20source%20funding%20ecosystem.%0ARisks%3A%20The%20repo%20must%20sit%20under%20an%20organisation%20account%20and%20be%20open%20source%3B%20funds%20cannot%20pay%20for%20the%20commercial%20hosted%20service%20or%20benefit%20the%20startup%20as%20a%20business.%20Crypto%2FSolana%20payout%20pool%20would%20not%20be%20supported%20%28crypto%20case-by-case%20only%20via%20Drips%29.%20Money%20raised%20is%20donor-driven%2C%20not%20guaranteed.)** · [Program page](https://docs.oscollective.org/) · [Apply](https://opencollective.com/opensource/apply/intro)

- **Funder:** Open Source Collective (fiscal host on the Open Collective platform)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** No grant itself; a funding channel. Fixed 10% host fee on incoming funds, no setup fees or minimum balances; payment processor fees extra. (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 62/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Original or substantially forked open source project with recent activity or significant usage, OSI/FSF/Debian/Fedora-recognised license, hosted in an organisation repository (not a personal account), applicant must be a maintainer; two or more admins preferred. Not for business transactions benefiting an individual or customer.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (except sanctioned countries); funds held in USD; can pay non-US maintainers · **Entity types:** Unincorporated open source projects / communities (the project, not a company, is hosted; for-profit status not addressed by OSC docs)
- **Decision time:** Automatic to a few business days (weekly review target)
- **Obligations:** 10% host fee; expenses must be submitted publicly with receipts/invoices on the transparent ledger; funds must serve the open source project, not private business benefit; crypto only case-by-case via Drips; payments to sanctioned countries not possible.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Open a 'forkmesh' collective for the MIT-licensed protocol and public mirror network so individual sponsors, companies (Open Source Pledge members) and thanks.dev dependency-funders can pay into a transparent ledger that covers mirror-fleet hosting and contributor bounties. It is the lowest-effort way to make ForkMesh fundable by the wider open source funding ecosystem.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the Terms of Fiscal Sponsorship and fee page; confirm eligibility.
2. Line up at least two community members as admins.
3. Create the collective and apply at opencollective.com/opensource/apply/intro.
4. Verify via GitHub OAuth (clear cases auto-approved) or request manual verification with project description and links.
5. OSC reviews (aims for weekly review; a few business days).
6. Collective goes live; set up tiers, link GitHub Sponsors / thanks.dev / company funders.

**What the application asks for:**

- Agreement to Terms of Fiscal Sponsorship
- GitHub authentication of an organisation repository (or manual verification request)
- Manual path: description of the project and its goals, links to repositories, website and social accounts
- Collective profile: name, slug, description, admins (2+ preferred)
- Encouraged: CONTRIBUTING guide, code of conduct, documented decision-making process

**Risks:** The repo must sit under an organisation account and be open source; funds cannot pay for the commercial hosted service or benefit the startup as a business. Crypto/Solana payout pool would not be supported (crypto case-by-case only via Drips). Money raised is donor-driven, not guaranteed.

**Sources:** <https://docs.oscollective.org/llms-full.txt>, <https://opencollective.com/opensource/apply/intro>

%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20OVHcloud%20Startup%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Start%20level%20%2812%20months%29%3A%20EUR%2010%2C000%20free%20cloud%20credits%20%2B%206%20hours%201-on-1%20engineer%20time%3B%20Scale%20level%20%2812%20months%29%3A%20up%20to%20EUR%20100%2C000%20free%20cloud%20credits%20%2B%20up%20to%2020%20hours%20engineer%20time%20%28US%20site%20cites%20up%20to%20USD%20120%2C000%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Technical%20startups%20with%20growth%20potential%20and%20at%20least%20a%20proof%20of%20concept%20or%20MVP%20deployable%20on%20OVHcloud.%20Start%20suits%20early-stage%20startups%20with%20monthly%20cloud%20spend%20in%20the%20hundreds%3B%20Scale%20suits%20companies%20with%20a%20closed%20Series%20A.%20Excludes%20NGOs%2C%20crypto%20mining%2C%20agencies%2C%20e-commerce%20sites%20and%20existing%20members%3B%20admission%20is%20more%20likely%20with%20a%20launched%20product%2C%20incubator%2Faccelerator%20support%2C%20a%20website%20and%20at%20least%20one%20fundraise.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20via%20regional%20programs%20%28one%20membership%2C%20generally%20in%20country%20of%20incorporation%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20startups%20%28NGOs%20excluded%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fstartup.ovhcloud.com%2Fen%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fstartup.ovhcloud.com%2Fen%2Flogin%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Apply%20via%20the%20Apply%20button%20%2F%20web%20form%20on%20startup.ovhcloud.com%20%28OVHcloud%20account%20not%20required%20first%29.%202.%20Describe%20the%20solution%2C%20business%20model%2C%20team%20and%20funding.%203.%20OVHcloud%20reviews%20and%20assigns%20the%20tier%20%28Start%20or%20Scale%29.%204.%20On%20acceptance%2C%20receive%20credit%20voucher%20and%20engineer-time%20sessions%20for%2012%20months.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Company%20details%20and%20country%20of%20incorporation%0A-%20Website%0A-%20Detailed%20solution%20outline%20and%20business%20model%0A-%20Proof%20of%20concept%20or%20MVP%20description%0A-%20Funding%20history%20%28at%20least%20one%20fundraise%20strengthens%20the%20case%29%0A-%20Incubator%2Faccelerator%20affiliation%20if%20any%0A-%20Expected%20monthly%20cloud%20spend%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity%20and%20no%20exclusivity%20%28other%20cloud%20startup%20programs%20allowed%29.%20Credits%20usable%20on%20listed%20eligible%20products%20only%20over%20the%2012-month%20program.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Run%20European%20ForkMesh%20core%20and%20mirror%20nodes%20on%20OVHcloud%20bare%20metal%20and%20public%20cloud%2C%20giving%20EU-sovereign%2C%20non-hyperscaler%20hosting%20for%20signed%20repository%20mirrors.%20OVHcloud%20positions%20itself%20on%20European%20digital%20sovereignty%2C%20which%20matches%20ForkMesh%27s%20no-single-provider%2C%20sovereign%20source-code%20hosting%20story.%0ARisks%3A%20Selective%20admission%20favours%20funded%20or%20accelerator-backed%20startups.%20NGO%2Fnonprofit%20entities%20excluded.%20Crypto%20mining%20excluded%20%28payout%20pool%20is%20not%20mining%20but%20should%20be%20explained%29.%20Credits%20expire%20after%2012%20months.)** · [Program page](https://startup.ovhcloud.com/en/) · [Apply](https://startup.ovhcloud.com/en/login/)

- **Funder:** OVHcloud
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Start level (12 months): EUR 10,000 free cloud credits + 6 hours 1-on-1 engineer time; Scale level (12 months): up to EUR 100,000 free cloud credits + up to 20 hours engineer time (US site cites up to USD 120,000) (about $11,000 - $116,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 60/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Technical startups with growth potential and at least a proof of concept or MVP deployable on OVHcloud. Start suits early-stage startups with monthly cloud spend in the hundreds; Scale suits companies with a closed Series A. Excludes NGOs, crypto mining, agencies, e-commerce sites and existing members; admission is more likely with a launched product, incubator/accelerator support, a website and at least one fundraise.
- **Geography:** Worldwide via regional programs (one membership, generally in country of incorporation) · **Entity types:** For-profit startups (NGOs excluded)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** No equity and no exclusivity (other cloud startup programs allowed). Credits usable on listed eligible products only over the 12-month program.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Run European ForkMesh core and mirror nodes on OVHcloud bare metal and public cloud, giving EU-sovereign, non-hyperscaler hosting for signed repository mirrors. OVHcloud positions itself on European digital sovereignty, which matches ForkMesh's no-single-provider, sovereign source-code hosting story.

**How to apply:**

1. Apply via the Apply button / web form on startup.ovhcloud.com (OVHcloud account not required first).
2. Describe the solution, business model, team and funding.
3. OVHcloud reviews and assigns the tier (Start or Scale).
4. On acceptance, receive credit voucher and engineer-time sessions for 12 months.

**What the application asks for:**

- Company details and country of incorporation
- Website
- Detailed solution outline and business model
- Proof of concept or MVP description
- Funding history (at least one fundraise strengthens the case)
- Incubator/accelerator affiliation if any
- Expected monthly cloud spend

**Risks:** Selective admission favours funded or accelerator-backed startups. NGO/nonprofit entities excluded. Crypto mining excluded (payout pool is not mining but should be explained). Credits expire after 12 months.

**Sources:** <https://startup.ovhcloud.com/en/>, <https://us.ovhcloud.com/startup-program/faq-support/>, <https://docs.ovhcloud.com/en/guides/account-and-service-management/startup-program/available-products>

## 14. GitHub Secure Open Source Fund

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20GitHub%20Secure%20Open%20Source%20Fund.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fopen-source%2Fgithub-secure-open-source-fund%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fopen-source%2Fgithub-secure-open-source-fund%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20GitHub%20Secure%20Open%20Source%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20USD%2010%2C000%20per%20project%20%28USD%206%2C000%20during%20the%20program%2C%20USD%202%2C000%20at%206-month%20and%20USD%202%2C000%20at%2012-month%20check-ins%29%20plus%20security%20training%20and%20Azure%2FGitHub%20security%20tooling%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28considered%20for%20all%20program%20sessions%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Current%20maintainers%20%28individual%20or%20team%20of%20up%20to%202%29%20of%20an%20open-source-first%20project%20with%20a%20clear%20OSI%20licence%2C%20demonstrated%20community%20traction%2C%20clear%20governance%2C%20and%20willingness%20to%20improve%20security.%20Must%20be%2018%2B%2C%20have%20an%20active%20GitHub%20profile%2C%20and%20live%20in%20a%20region%20supported%20by%20GitHub%20Sponsors%3B%20GitHub%2FMicrosoft%20employees%20excluded.%20Geography%3A%20Regions%20supported%20by%20GitHub%20Sponsors.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individual%20maintainers%20%2F%20project%20teams%20%28paid%20via%20GitHub%20Sponsors%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fopen-source%2Fgithub-secure-open-source-fund%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fopen-source%2Fgithub-secure-open-source-fund%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20the%20external%20application%20form%20linked%20from%20the%20fund%20page.%202.%20Rolling%20review%20%28projects%20may%20also%20be%20referred%20by%20funding%20partners%20or%20invited%20by%20GitHub%29.%203.%20Virtual%20interview.%204.%20Join%20a%203-week%20security%20education%20session.%205.%20Complete%20project%20security%20milestones%20and%206-%20and%2012-month%20check-ins.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Application%20form%20%28questions%20not%20published%20on%20the%20page%29%0A-%20Project%20repository%20and%20licence%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20adoption%2Ftraction%0A-%20Governance%20structure%20description%0A-%20Commitment%20of%20core%20leaders%20to%20~20%20hours%20of%20programming%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20rolling%3B%20sessions%20run%20several%20times%20a%20year%20%28session%204%20ran%20May%202026%29%0AObligations%3A%20About%2015%20hours%20over%203%20weeks%20plus%202.5%20hours%20at%20each%20of%20the%206-%20and%2012-month%20check-ins%20%28meetings%20on%20Pacific%20time%29%3B%20complete%20security%20milestones%3B%20tranche%20payments%20depend%20on%20check-ins%3B%20agree%20to%20Code%20of%20Conduct.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Use%20the%20session%20to%20harden%20ForkMesh%27s%20release%20signing%2C%20SBOM%2Fprovenance%20for%20desktop%20builds%2C%20secret%20scanning%20and%20incident%20response%20for%20the%20mirror%20fleet.%20GitHub%20cares%20because%20ForkMesh%20mirrors%20are%20a%20supply-chain%20integrity%20layer%20for%20GitHub-hosted%20repositories.%0ARisks%3A%20GitHub%20SOSF%20requires%20%27demonstrated%20community%20traction%27%3B%20ForkMesh%20positions%20itself%20as%20a%20GitHub%20alternative%2C%20which%20may%20weigh%20against%20it.)** · [Program page](https://github.com/open-source/github-secure-open-source-fund) · [Apply](https://github.com/open-source/github-secure-open-source-fund)

- **Funder:** GitHub
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** USD 10,000 per project (USD 6,000 during the program, USD 2,000 at 6-month and USD 2,000 at 12-month check-ins) plus security training and Azure/GitHub security tooling (about $10,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (considered for all program sessions) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 60/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Current maintainers (individual or team of up to 2) of an open-source-first project with a clear OSI licence, demonstrated community traction, clear governance, and willingness to improve security. Must be 18+, have an active GitHub profile, and live in a region supported by GitHub Sponsors; GitHub/Microsoft employees excluded.
- **Geography:** Regions supported by GitHub Sponsors · **Entity types:** Individual maintainers / project teams (paid via GitHub Sponsors)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: rolling; sessions run several times a year (session 4 ran May 2026)
- **Obligations:** About 15 hours over 3 weeks plus 2.5 hours at each of the 6- and 12-month check-ins (meetings on Pacific time); complete security milestones; tranche payments depend on check-ins; agree to Code of Conduct.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Use the session to harden ForkMesh's release signing, SBOM/provenance for desktop builds, secret scanning and incident response for the mirror fleet. GitHub cares because ForkMesh mirrors are a supply-chain integrity layer for GitHub-hosted repositories.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit the external application form linked from the fund page.
2. Rolling review (projects may also be referred by funding partners or invited by GitHub).
3. Virtual interview.
4. Join a 3-week security education session.
5. Complete project security milestones and 6- and 12-month check-ins.

**What the application asks for:**

- Application form (questions not published on the page)
- Project repository and licence
- Evidence of adoption/traction
- Governance structure description
- Commitment of core leaders to ~20 hours of programming

**Risks:** GitHub SOSF requires 'demonstrated community traction'; ForkMesh positions itself as a GitHub alternative, which may weigh against it.

**Sources:** <https://github.com/open-source/github-secure-open-source-fund>, <https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/announcing-github-secure-open-source-fund/>, <https://docs.evcc.io/en/blog/2026/02/18/github-secure-open-source-fund.md>

community%2Fcodex-for-oss%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopenai.com%2Fform%2Fcodex-for-oss%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Apply%20via%20the%20Codex%20for%20OSS%20form.%202.%20Agree%20to%20the%20Codex%20for%20Open%20Source%20Program%20Terms.%203.%20OpenAI%20reviews%20%28rolling%29.%204.%20Pro%20access%2C%20Codex%20Security%20and%2For%20API%20credits%20granted.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20form%20%28fields%20not%20published%29%0A-%20Project%20and%20repository%20links%0A-%20Maintainer%20role%20evidence%0A-%20Description%20of%20how%20Codex%2FAPI%20credits%20will%20be%20used%20%28PR%20review%2C%20maintainer%20automation%2C%20releases%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20published%0AObligations%3A%20Codex%20for%20Open%20Source%20Program%20Terms%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20reporting%20stated.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20ForkMesh%20nodes%20already%20run%20Codex%20agents%20that%20open%20pull%20requests%20from%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20API%20credits%20would%20fund%20agent-driven%20review%20and%20maintenance%20on%20the%20public%20mirror%20fleet%20and%20showcase%20Codex%20on%20a%20decentralized%20forge.%0ARisks%3A%20Third-party%20blogs%20cite%20up%20to%20USD%2025%2C000%20in%20API%20credits%20per%20project%2C%20unconfirmed%20on%20the%20official%20page.%20Preference%20for%20widely%20used%20projects.)** · [Program page](https://developers.openai.com/codex/community/codex-for-oss) · [Apply](https://openai.com/form/codex-for-oss/)

- **Funder:** OpenAI
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** Six months of ChatGPT Pro with Codex, conditional Codex Security access, and API credits from the USD 1 million Codex Open Source Fund (credit amount per project not stated; approx. USD 1,200 value for Pro at list price) (about $1,200)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (open)
- **Fit score:** 60/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Core maintainers and people running widely used public open-source projects; projects outside those criteria that play an important ecosystem role may apply and explain why. Codex Security limited to core maintainers with write access, case by case.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: countries where OpenAI services are available · **Entity types:** Individual maintainers / projects
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not published
- **Obligations:** Codex for Open Source Program Terms; unconfirmed: no reporting stated.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** ForkMesh nodes already run Codex agents that open pull requests from isolated worktrees; API credits would fund agent-driven review and maintenance on the public mirror fleet and showcase Codex on a decentralized forge.

**How to apply:**

1. Apply via the Codex for OSS form.
2. Agree to the Codex for Open Source Program Terms.
3. OpenAI reviews (rolling).
4. Pro access, Codex Security and/or API credits granted.

**What the application asks for:**

- Online form (fields not published)
- Project and repository links
- Maintainer role evidence
- Description of how Codex/API credits will be used (PR review, maintainer automation, releases)

**Risks:** Third-party blogs cite up to USD 25,000 in API credits per project, unconfirmed on the official page. Preference for widely used projects.

**Sources:** <https://developers.openai.com/codex/community/codex-for-oss>, <https://the-decoder.com/openai-offers-open-source-maintainers-six-months-of-free-chatgpt-pro-and-codex-access/>, <https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Grants:Programs/Wikimedia_Research_%26_Technology_Fund>

elopers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20U.S.%20Department%20of%20Homeland%20Security%2C%20Science%20and%20Technology%20Directorate%3A%20Long%20Range%20Broad%20Agency%20Announcement%2C%20topics%20CYBCI%2003%20Software%20and%20Hardware%20Supply%20Chain%20Assurance%20%2F%20CYBCI%2002%20Shared%20Cyber%20Resilience%0AAward%3A%20No%20predefined%20funding%20level%3B%20availability%20of%20funds%20is%20considered%20during%20evaluation%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Businesses%20of%20all%20sizes%2C%20universities%2C%20national%20laboratories%20and%20other%20R%26D%20organisations%2C%20domestic%20and%20international.%20The%20work%20must%20be%20original%2C%20state-of-the-art%20research%20or%20a%20unique%20prototype%20that%20needs%20proof%20of%20concept.%20Commercially%20available%20products%20and%20support%20services%20cannot%20be%20bought%20through%20the%20LRBAA.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28domestic%20and%20international%20submitters%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20%28any%20size%29%2C%20universities%2C%20national%20laboratories%2C%20other%20R%26D%20organisations.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.dhs.gov%2Fscience-and-technology%2Fst-lrbaa%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Foip.dhs.gov%2Fbaa%2Fpublic%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20the%20current%20LRBAA%20topics%20%28nine%20open%2C%20including%20CYBCI%2002%2C%2003%2C%2004%2C%2005%29.%202.%20Submit%20research%20concept%20material%20in%20the%20LRBAA%20Portal.%203.%20DHS%20normally%20says%20within%2010%20business%20days%20whether%20it%20is%20interested.%204.%20If%20it%20is%2C%20talk%20with%20the%20topic%20program%20manager%2C%20give%20a%20virtual%20pitch%2C%20and%20optionally%20send%20a%20video.%205.%20Later%20proposal%20and%20negotiation%20follow%20the%20LRBAA%2024-01%20solicitation%20on%20SAM.gov%20%28steps%20after%20the%20pitch%20unconfirmed%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Research%20concept%20material%20submitted%20through%20the%20LRBAA%20Portal%20%28format%20and%20page%20limits%20unconfirmed%3B%20see%20the%20LRBAA%2024-01%20solicitation%20on%20SAM.gov%29%0A-%20Virtual%20pitch%20to%20the%20topic%20program%20manager%20if%20invited%0A-%20Optional%20video%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20full%20proposal%20and%20SAM.gov%20registration%20at%20the%20award%20stage%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Interest%20notification%20usually%20within%2010%20business%20days%20of%20the%20concept%20submission%3B%20full%20award%20timing%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20set%20by%20the%20resulting%20contract%20or%20agreement%3B%20the%20LRBAA%20funds%20R%26D%20and%20prototypes%2C%20not%20commercial%20products.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Send%20a%20CYBCI%2003%20concept%20for%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20Git%20mirrors%20that%20let%20critical-infrastructure%20operators%20verify%2C%20and%20keep%20fetching%2C%20the%20source%20they%20depend%20on%20when%20upstream%20hosts%20are%20compromised%20or%20offline.%20CYBCI%2002%20%28Shared%20Cyber%20Resilience%29%20is%20a%20fallback.%20DHS%20S%26T%20is%20asking%20for%20proof-of-concept%20prototypes%20that%20harden%20the%20software%20supply%20chain.%0ARisks%3A%20These%20are%20research%20contracts%2C%20not%20grants%2C%20and%20no%20funding%20level%20is%20stated.%20Topics%20change%20every%20year%3B%20an%20August%202025%20amendment%20removed%2014.%20Check%20the%20closing%20date%20on%20SAM.gov.%20Replaced%20DHS%20SVIP%3A%20its%20page%20is%20archived%20and%20its%205-year%20solicitation%2070RSAT21R00000006%20closed%20in%20May%202026%20with%20no%20new%20call%20found.)** · [Program page](https://www.dhs.gov/science-and-technology/st-lrbaa) · [Apply](https://oip.dhs.gov/baa/public)

- **Funder:** U.S. Department of Homeland Security, Science and Technology Directorate
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** No predefined funding level; availability of funds is considered during evaluation (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 58/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Businesses of all sizes, universities, national laboratories and other R&D organisations, domestic and international. The work must be original, state-of-the-art research or a unique prototype that needs proof of concept. Commercially available products and support services cannot be bought through the LRBAA.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (domestic and international submitters) · **Entity types:** For-profit (any size), universities, national laboratories, other R&D organisations
- **Decision time:** Interest notification usually within 10 business days of the concept submission; full award timing unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: set by the resulting contract or agreement; the LRBAA funds R&D and prototypes, not commercial products.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Send a CYBCI 03 concept for signed, integrity-checked Git mirrors that let critical-infrastructure operators verify, and keep fetching, the source they depend on when upstream hosts are compromised or offline. CYBCI 02 (Shared Cyber Resilience) is a fallback. DHS S&T is asking for proof-of-concept prototypes that harden the software supply chain.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the current LRBAA topics (nine open, including CYBCI 02, 03, 04, 05).
2. Submit research concept material in the LRBAA Portal.
3. DHS normally says within 10 business days whether it is interested.
4. If it is, talk with the topic program manager, give a virtual pitch, and optionally send a video.
5. Later proposal and negotiation follow the LRBAA 24-01 solicitation on SAM.gov (steps after the pitch unconfirmed).

**What the application asks for:**

- Research concept material submitted through the LRBAA Portal (format and page limits unconfirmed; see the LRBAA 24-01 solicitation on SAM.gov)
- Virtual pitch to the topic program manager if invited
- Optional video
- unconfirmed: full proposal and SAM.gov registration at the award stage

**Risks:** These are research contracts, not grants, and no funding level is stated. Topics change every year; an August 2025 amendment removed 14. Check the closing date on SAM.gov. Replaced DHS SVIP: its page is archived and its 5-year solicitation 70RSAT21R00000006 closed in May 2026 with no new call found.

**Sources:** <https://www.dhs.gov/science-and-technology/st-lrbaa>, <https://www.dhs.gov/current-lrbaa-topics>, <https://www.dhs.gov/science-and-technology/svip>, <https://www.highergov.com/contract-opportunity/silicon-valley-innovation-program-5-year-other-tra-70rsat21r00000006-k-955e6/>

o%2Ffor%2Fopen-source%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsentry.io%2Ffor%2Fopen-source%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20a%20Sentry%20organization%20for%20the%20project.%202.%20Fill%20in%20the%20application%20form%20on%20the%20Sentry%20for%20Open%20Source%20page.%203.%20Sentry%20reviews%20the%20project%20and%20licence.%204.%20On%20approval%20the%20organization%20is%20moved%20to%20the%20Open-Source%20Sponsorship%20Plan.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Name%0A-%20Email%0A-%20Website%20URL%0A-%20Company%20type%20%28Open-Source%20%2F%20Non-Profit%20%2F%20Education%20%2F%20Startup%29%0A-%20Sentry%20organization%20name%0A-%20Sentry%20organization%20URL%0A-%20Project%20URL%0A-%20GitHub%20URL%20%28if%20available%29%20linking%20directly%20to%20the%20licence%0A-%20Brief%20project%20description%20%2F%20details%20to%20help%20the%20application%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20none%20stated%20on%20the%20page%3B%20no%20term%20limit%20on%20the%20plan.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Instrument%20the%20ForkMesh%20desktop%20node%20%28C%2B%2B%2FQt%29%2C%20mobile%20clients%20and%20Python%2FGo%20services%20with%20Sentry%20error%2C%20crash%20and%20performance%20monitoring%2C%20and%20use%20Seer%20to%20triage%20crashes%20from%20agent-run%20worktrees.%20Sentry%20supports%20permissively%20licensed%20OSS%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%20is%20MIT-licensed%20developer%20infrastructure%20with%20a%20large%20cross-platform%20surface%20to%20observe.%0ARisks%3A%20Sentry%20may%20treat%20the%20commercial%20hosted%20service%20as%20out%20of%20scope%20for%20the%20sponsored%20org%3B%20keep%20the%20sponsored%20org%20for%20the%20open%20source%20clients%2Fprotocol.%20Shipping%20crash%20telemetry%20from%20a%20privacy-focused%2C%20self-hosted%20desktop%20app%20needs%20opt-in%20and%20clear%20docs.%20In-kind%20only.%20Datadog%20for%20Startups%20was%20the%20alternative%20named%20in%20the%20assignment%3B%20Sentry%20chosen%20as%20it%20has%20no%20funding%20requirement.)** · [Program page](https://sentry.io/for/open-source/) · [Apply](https://sentry.io/for/open-source/)

- **Funder:** Sentry (Functional Software, Inc.)
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** Free Sentry account: 5M errors, 5,000GB logs, 1B spans, 100K replays, 500 cron monitors, 25 uptime monitors, 3K continuous profile hours, 150 UI profile hours, 10GB attachments, 1,000 size analysis uploads, Seer (AI debugging) for 5 active contributors, Business features, no term limit; option to enable an on-demand budget. No monetary value stated. (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 58/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source projects released under a friendly (permissive) license such as Apache or MIT. The form accepts company types Open-Source, Non-Profit, Education and Startup; no explicit non-commercial restriction is stated.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (no restriction stated) · **Entity types:** Open source projects, nonprofits, education, startups
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: none stated on the page; no term limit on the plan.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Instrument the ForkMesh desktop node (C++/Qt), mobile clients and Python/Go services with Sentry error, crash and performance monitoring, and use Seer to triage crashes from agent-run worktrees. Sentry supports permissively licensed OSS, and ForkMesh is MIT-licensed developer infrastructure with a large cross-platform surface to observe.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a Sentry organization for the project.
2. Fill in the application form on the Sentry for Open Source page.
3. Sentry reviews the project and licence.
4. On approval the organization is moved to the Open-Source Sponsorship Plan.

**What the application asks for:**

- Name
- Email
- Website URL
- Company type (Open-Source / Non-Profit / Education / Startup)
- Sentry organization name
- Sentry organization URL
- Project URL
- GitHub URL (if available) linking directly to the licence
- Brief project description / details to help the application

**Risks:** Sentry may treat the commercial hosted service as out of scope for the sponsored org; keep the sponsored org for the open source clients/protocol. Shipping crash telemetry from a privacy-focused, self-hosted desktop app needs opt-in and clear docs. In-kind only. Datadog for Startups was the alternative named in the assignment; Sentry chosen as it has no funding requirement.

**Sources:** <https://sentry.io/for/open-source/>

ource%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Amazon%20Web%20Services%3A%20AWS%20Activate%0AAward%3A%20Activate%20Founders%3A%20up%20to%20USD%205%2C000%20%28start%20with%20USD%201%2C000%2C%20select%20participants%20up%20to%20USD%205%2C000%29%3B%20Activate%20Portfolio%3A%20up%20to%20USD%20200%2C000%20%28requires%20Activate%20Provider%20Org%20ID%29%3B%20AWS%20Credits%20for%20AI%20Startups%3A%20USD%20200%2C000%2B%20%28invite-only%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Startups%20that%20are%20pre-Series%20B%2C%20founded%20in%20the%20last%2010%20years%2C%20with%20an%20AWS%20account%20on%20a%20Paid%20Tier%20Plan%2C%20and%20new%20to%20Activate%20Credits%20%28or%20requesting%20more%20than%20previously%20received%29.%20Portfolio%20tier%20requires%20an%20Organization%20ID%20from%20an%20Activate%20Provider%20%28accelerator%2C%20angel%2C%20VC%29.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20country%20exclusions%20per%20AWS%20Promotional%20Credit%20Terms%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20startups%20%28self-funded%20for%20Founders%3B%20investor%2Faccelerator-backed%20for%20Portfolio%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Faws.amazon.com%2Fstartups%2Fcredits%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Faws.amazon.com%2Fstartups%2Fcredits%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%2Fverify%20an%20AWS%20Builder%20ID%20with%20a%20professional%20email%20and%20complete%20the%20profile.%202.%20Choose%20tier%3A%20Activate%20Founders%20%28self-funded%29%20or%20Activate%20Portfolio%20%28enter%20Activate%20Provider%20Org%20ID%29.%203.%20Provide%20product%2C%20funding%20stage%20and%20company%20profile%20details.%204.%20Create%20or%20link%20an%20AWS%20account%20%28Paid%20Tier%20Plan%29%20to%20the%20Builder%20ID%20and%20submit.%205.%20Hear%20back%20within%205-10%20business%20days.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20AWS%20Builder%20ID%20with%20professional%20%28company-domain%29%20email%0A-%20AWS%20account%20on%20a%20Paid%20Tier%20Plan%0A-%20Company%20profile%20%28name%2C%20founding%20date%2C%20website%2C%20stage%29%0A-%20Product%20description%0A-%20Funding%20stage%20details%0A-%20Activate%20Provider%20Organization%20ID%20%28Portfolio%20tier%20only%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%205-10%20business%20days%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20governed%20by%20AWS%20Promotional%20Credit%20Terms%20%26%20Conditions%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20expiry%20period%20and%20eligible%20services%29%3B%20credits%20apply%20only%20to%20the%20linked%20AWS%20account.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Use%20credits%20to%20run%20additional%20ForkMesh%20mirror-fleet%20nodes%20and%20runner-tier%20CI%20compute%20in%20AWS%20regions%2C%20adding%20cross-provider%20redundancy%20to%20the%20existing%20Vultr-hosted%20core%20so%20repositories%20survive%20a%20single%20cloud%20outage.%20AWS%20cares%20about%20startups%20building%20developer%20tooling%20that%20grows%20on%20its%20infrastructure%3B%20Portfolio%20tier%20needs%20an%20accelerator%2Finvestor%20Org%20ID.%0ARisks%3A%20Founders%20tier%20is%20small%20%28USD%201%2C000-5%2C000%29%3B%20the%20USD%20200%2C000%20Portfolio%20tier%20needs%20backing%20from%20an%20Activate%20Provider.%20ForkMesh%20runs%20on%20Vultr%2C%20so%20credits%20only%20help%20if%20workloads%20move%20to%20or%20add%20AWS.%20Credits%20expire%20%28unconfirmed%20term%29.%20Requires%20a%20legal%20company%20with%20a%20professional%20email%20domain.)** · [Program page](https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits) · [Apply](https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits)

- **Funder:** Amazon Web Services
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Activate Founders: up to USD 5,000 (start with USD 1,000, select participants up to USD 5,000); Activate Portfolio: up to USD 200,000 (requires Activate Provider Org ID); AWS Credits for AI Startups: USD 200,000+ (invite-only) (about $1,000 - $200,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 55/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Startups that are pre-Series B, founded in the last 10 years, with an AWS account on a Paid Tier Plan, and new to Activate Credits (or requesting more than previously received). Portfolio tier requires an Organization ID from an Activate Provider (accelerator, angel, VC).
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: country exclusions per AWS Promotional Credit Terms) · **Entity types:** For-profit startups (self-funded for Founders; investor/accelerator-backed for Portfolio)
- **Decision time:** 5-10 business days
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits governed by AWS Promotional Credit Terms & Conditions (unconfirmed: expiry period and eligible services); credits apply only to the linked AWS account.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Use credits to run additional ForkMesh mirror-fleet nodes and runner-tier CI compute in AWS regions, adding cross-provider redundancy to the existing Vultr-hosted core so repositories survive a single cloud outage. AWS cares about startups building developer tooling that grows on its infrastructure; Portfolio tier needs an accelerator/investor Org ID.

**How to apply:**

1. Create/verify an AWS Builder ID with a professional email and complete the profile.
2. Choose tier: Activate Founders (self-funded) or Activate Portfolio (enter Activate Provider Org ID).
3. Provide product, funding stage and company profile details.
4. Create or link an AWS account (Paid Tier Plan) to the Builder ID and submit.
5. Hear back within 5-10 business days.

**What the application asks for:**

- AWS Builder ID with professional (company-domain) email
- AWS account on a Paid Tier Plan
- Company profile (name, founding date, website, stage)
- Product description
- Funding stage details
- Activate Provider Organization ID (Portfolio tier only)

**Risks:** Founders tier is small (USD 1,000-5,000); the USD 200,000 Portfolio tier needs backing from an Activate Provider. ForkMesh runs on Vultr, so credits only help if workloads move to or add AWS. Credits expire (unconfirmed term). Requires a legal company with a professional email domain.

**Sources:** <https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits>

%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20OpenSSF%20Alpha-Omega%3A%20Alpha-Omega%20grants%0AAward%3A%20Typical%20grants%20about%20USD%2050%2C000%20-%20100%2C000%2C%20varying%20by%20proposal%20needs%0ADeadline%3A%20Intake%20open%20during%20the%20first%20month%20of%20each%20quarter%3A%20October%202026%20window%20now%2C%20next%20January%202027%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Maintainers%20of%20critical%20open-source%20software%2C%20standalone%20projects%2C%20foundations%20covering%20many%20projects%2C%20or%20core%20ecosystem%20services%2C%20under%20an%20OSI-approved%20licence%2C%20seeking%20to%20improve%20security.%20No%20unpatched%20or%20sensitive%20vulnerabilities%20may%20be%20disclosed%20in%20the%20proposal.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Projects%2C%20maintainers%2C%20foundations%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20entity%20type%20restrictions.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Falpha-omega.dev%2Fgrants%2Fhow-to-apply%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Falpha-omega.dev%2Fgrants%2Fhow-to-apply%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Month%201%3A%20submit%20the%20lightweight%20intake%20form%20%28only%20form%20submissions%20considered%29.%202.%20Month%202%3A%20qualifying%20projects%20invited%20to%20a%20co-design%20consultation%20to%20shape%20the%20proposal%20and%20Statement%20of%20Work.%203.%20Month%203%3A%20final%20approval.%204.%20Funds%20disbursed%20the%20following%20quarter.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%2Forganization%20receiving%20the%20grant%20with%20proof%20of%20OSI-approved%20licence%0A-%20Why%20the%20project%20is%20critical%20infrastructure%0A-%20Current%20security%20posture%20and%20challenges%0A-%20Expected%20security%20outcomes%2C%20success%20metrics%20and%20end-user%20benefit%0A-%20Execution%20plan%3A%20activities%2C%20any%20hiring%2C%20start%20time%20after%20funding%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%203%20months%20%28one%2012-week%20cycle%29%0AObligations%3A%20Monthly%20public%20reports%3B%20monthly%20strategy%20roundtables%3B%20participation%20in%20Alpha-Omega%20community%20efforts%3B%20kickoff%2C%20mid-point%20and%20final%20blog%20reports%3B%20future%20funding%20depends%20on%20progress.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed%20mirrors%20as%20supply-chain%20hardening%20for%20package%20and%20source%20distribution%3A%20verifiable%20clones%20that%20keep%20critical%20repositories%20available%20and%20tamper-evident%20when%20a%20forge%20is%20compromised%20or%20offline%2C%20plus%20a%20security%20review%20of%20the%20mirror%20protocol.%20Alpha-Omega%20funds%20work%20that%20measurably%20improves%20security%20of%20critical%20OSS.%0ARisks%3A%20Alpha-Omega%20prioritises%20already-critical%2C%20widely%20used%20projects%3B%20ForkMesh%20must%20show%20adoption.%20October%20window%20closes%20at%20month%20end%2C%20so%20act%20immediately%20or%20aim%20for%20January%202027.)** · [Program page](https://alpha-omega.dev/grants/how-to-apply/) · [Apply](https://alpha-omega.dev/grants/how-to-apply/)

- **Funder:** OpenSSF Alpha-Omega (Linux Foundation)
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** Typical grants about USD 50,000 - 100,000, varying by proposal needs (about $50,000 - $100,000)
- **Deadline:** Intake open during the first month of each quarter: October 2026 window now, next January 2027 (open)
- **Fit score:** 55/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Maintainers of critical open-source software, standalone projects, foundations covering many projects, or core ecosystem services, under an OSI-approved licence, seeking to improve security. No unpatched or sensitive vulnerabilities may be disclosed in the proposal.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Projects, maintainers, foundations; unconfirmed: entity type restrictions
- **Decision time:** About 3 months (one 12-week cycle)
- **Obligations:** Monthly public reports; monthly strategy roundtables; participation in Alpha-Omega community efforts; kickoff, mid-point and final blog reports; future funding depends on progress.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose ForkMesh's signed mirrors as supply-chain hardening for package and source distribution: verifiable clones that keep critical repositories available and tamper-evident when a forge is compromised or offline, plus a security review of the mirror protocol. Alpha-Omega funds work that measurably improves security of critical OSS.

**How to apply:**

1. Month 1: submit the lightweight intake form (only form submissions considered).
2. Month 2: qualifying projects invited to a co-design consultation to shape the proposal and Statement of Work.
3. Month 3: final approval.
4. Funds disbursed the following quarter.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project/organization receiving the grant with proof of OSI-approved licence
- Why the project is critical infrastructure
- Current security posture and challenges
- Expected security outcomes, success metrics and end-user benefit
- Execution plan: activities, any hiring, start time after funding

**Risks:** Alpha-Omega prioritises already-critical, widely used projects; ForkMesh must show adoption. October window closes at month end, so act immediately or aim for January 2027.

**Sources:** <https://alpha-omega.dev/grants/how-to-apply/>, <https://www.ripe.net/support/cpf/>
