clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20DigitalOcean%20Startups%0AAward%3A%20Credit%20amount%20varies%20by%20award%20level%20and%20is%20set%20through%20approved%20partners%3B%20program%20term%20generally%2012%20months%3B%20USD%2010%2C000%20monthly%20spending%20limit%3B%20plus%2015%20months%20free%20Standard%20Tier%20support.%20Unconfirmed%3A%20maximum%20%28third-party%20listings%20cite%20up%20to%20USD%20100%2C000%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Startups%20that%20have%20raised%20USD%2010M%20or%20less%2C%20with%20a%20company%20website%20and%20matching%20corporate%20email%2C%20that%20have%20not%20used%20DigitalOcean%20credits%20before.%20AI-native%20startups%20are%20prioritised.%20No%20VC%2Faccelerator%20partner%20required%20%28select%20Other%29.%20Consultancies%2C%20agencies%20and%20web%2Fapp%20development%20shops%20are%20ineligible.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20sanctioned-country%20exclusions%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Registered%20startup%20companies%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20whether%20nonprofits%20qualify%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.digitalocean.com%2Fhatch%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsammydigitalocean.typeform.com%2Fhatch%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20a%20new%20DigitalOcean%20team%20account%20with%20a%20corporate%20email%20and%20a%20valid%20credit%20card.%202.%20Submit%20the%20Hatch%20application%20Typeform%20%28select%20Other%20if%20no%20affiliated%20partner%29.%203.%20On%20acceptance%2C%20receive%20onboarding%20email%20and%20Welcome%20Kit%20with%20credits%20and%20perks.%204.%20Selected%20startups%20may%20later%20be%20invited%20to%20apply%20for%20separate%20GPU%20credits.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20New%20DigitalOcean%20team%20account%0A-%20Corporate%20email%20matching%20company%20website%20domain%0A-%20Valid%20credit%20card%0A-%20Company%20website%0A-%20Funding%20raised%20%28USD%2010M%20or%20less%29%20and%20partner%20affiliation%20%28or%20Other%29%0A-%20Company%20and%20product%20description%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20form%20fields%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20non-transferable%2C%20no%20extensions%2C%20unused%20credits%20forfeited%20at%20term%20end.%20Exclusions%3A%20GPU%20Droplets%2C%20H100%20GPU%20Kubernetes%2C%20bare-metal%20GPUs%2C%20dedicated%2Fserverless%20inference%2C%20third-party%20models%2C%20Cloudways%2C%20crypto%20mining%20and%20excessive%20network%2FCPU%2Fdisk%20I%2FO%20usage.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Run%20additional%20mirror-fleet%20and%20runner-tier%20droplets%20on%20DigitalOcean%20%28simple%20VMs%2C%20Spaces%20object%20storage%29%20to%20add%20a%20second%20independent%20provider%20next%20to%20Vultr%20for%20signed%20mirrors.%20DigitalOcean%20targets%20developer-focused%20startups%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%27s%20self-hosted%20VM%20architecture%20maps%20directly%20onto%20droplets.%0ARisks%3A%20Exact%20credit%20amount%20not%20published%20and%20may%20be%20small%20without%20a%20partner.%20Mirror%20sync%20and%20clone%20serving%20could%20trip%20the%20excessive-network-usage%20exclusion.%20AI-native%20startups%20are%20prioritised.%20Credits%20expire%20in%20about%2012%20months.)** · [Program page](https://www.digitalocean.com/hatch) · [Apply](https://sammydigitalocean.typeform.com/hatch)

- **Funder:** DigitalOcean
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Credit amount varies by award level and is set through approved partners; program term generally 12 months; USD 10,000 monthly spending limit; plus 15 months free Standard Tier support. Unconfirmed: maximum (third-party listings cite up to USD 100,000) (about $100,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 55/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Startups that have raised USD 10M or less, with a company website and matching corporate email, that have not used DigitalOcean credits before. AI-native startups are prioritised. No VC/accelerator partner required (select Other). Consultancies, agencies and web/app development shops are ineligible.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: sanctioned-country exclusions) · **Entity types:** Registered startup companies (unconfirmed: whether nonprofits qualify)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits non-transferable, no extensions, unused credits forfeited at term end. Exclusions: GPU Droplets, H100 GPU Kubernetes, bare-metal GPUs, dedicated/serverless inference, third-party models, Cloudways, crypto mining and excessive network/CPU/disk I/O usage.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Run additional mirror-fleet and runner-tier droplets on DigitalOcean (simple VMs, Spaces object storage) to add a second independent provider next to Vultr for signed mirrors. DigitalOcean targets developer-focused startups, and ForkMesh's self-hosted VM architecture maps directly onto droplets.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a new DigitalOcean team account with a corporate email and a valid credit card.
2. Submit the Hatch application Typeform (select Other if no affiliated partner).
3. On acceptance, receive onboarding email and Welcome Kit with credits and perks.
4. Selected startups may later be invited to apply for separate GPU credits.

**What the application asks for:**

- New DigitalOcean team account
- Corporate email matching company website domain
- Valid credit card
- Company website
- Funding raised (USD 10M or less) and partner affiliation (or Other)
- Company and product description (unconfirmed: exact form fields)

**Risks:** Exact credit amount not published and may be small without a partner. Mirror sync and clone serving could trip the excessive-network-usage exclusion. AI-native startups are prioritised. Credits expire in about 12 months.

**Sources:** <https://www.digitalocean.com/hatch>, <https://innmind.com/startup-deals/digitalocean-hatch-startup-credits/>, <https://guptadeepak.com/startup-offers/programs/digitalocean-hatch>

e%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20FUTO%20Grants%0AAward%3A%20Microgrants%3A%20one-time%20USD%201%2C000%20-%205%2C000%3B%20Legendary%20Grants%3A%20large-scale%20grants%20for%20established%20projects%20%28no%20amount%20stated%3B%20over%20USD%205M%20given%20in%20total%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Projects%20and%20people%20working%20to%20challenge%20the%20tech%20oligopoly%2C%20including%20open-source%20projects.%20Microgrants%20target%20small%20or%20early-stage%20projects%3B%20Legendary%20Grants%20are%20generally%20for%20established%20entities%20FUTO%20already%20trusts.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20no%20restriction%20stated%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20open-source%20projects%2C%20nonprofits%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20companies.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Ffuto.tech%2Fgrants%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Ffuto.tech%2Fgrants%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Email%20an%20application%20to%20the%20FUTO%20grants%20address%20listed%20on%20the%20page.%202.%20FUTO%20reviews%20%28many%20microgrants%20are%20also%20employee-recommended%29.%203.%20One-time%20grant%20paid.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Email%20describing%20the%20project%2C%20its%20open-source%20status%20and%20how%20it%20reduces%20dependence%20on%20large%20tech%20platforms%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20links%20to%20repository%20and%20funding%20ask%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20none%20stated%20for%20microgrants%3B%20ongoing%20relationship%20for%20Legendary%20Grants.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20ForkMesh%20is%20a%20direct%20answer%20to%20code-hosting%20centralisation%3A%20developers%20keep%20repositories%2C%20issues%20and%20reviews%20on%20hosts%20they%20control.%20A%20microgrant%20could%20fund%20self-hosting%20documentation%20or%20a%20one-command%20node%20installer%2C%20opening%20the%20door%20to%20a%20larger%20grant%20later.%0ARisks%3A%20Knight%20Foundation%20was%20dropped%3A%20its%20open%20calls%20%28Knight%20Cities%20Challenge%2C%20closed%2030%20Apr%202026%29%20fund%20local%20news%2C%20arts%20and%20Knight%20communities%2C%20unsuitable%20for%20ForkMesh.%20FUTO%20microgrants%20are%20small%3B%20most%20recent%20listed%20awards%20are%20from%202025%3B%20paid%20hosted%20tier%20may%20reduce%20appeal.)** · [Program page](https://futo.tech/grants/) · [Apply](https://futo.tech/grants/)

- **Funder:** FUTO
- **Category:** Preservation and digital commons
- **Amount:** Microgrants: one-time USD 1,000 - 5,000; Legendary Grants: large-scale grants for established projects (no amount stated; over USD 5M given in total) (about $1,000 - $5,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (open)
- **Fit score:** 55/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Projects and people working to challenge the tech oligopoly, including open-source projects. Microgrants target small or early-stage projects; Legendary Grants are generally for established entities FUTO already trusts.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: no restriction stated) · **Entity types:** Individuals, open-source projects, nonprofits; unconfirmed: companies
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: none stated for microgrants; ongoing relationship for Legendary Grants.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** ForkMesh is a direct answer to code-hosting centralisation: developers keep repositories, issues and reviews on hosts they control. A microgrant could fund self-hosting documentation or a one-command node installer, opening the door to a larger grant later.

**How to apply:**

1. Email an application to the FUTO grants address listed on the page.
2. FUTO reviews (many microgrants are also employee-recommended).
3. One-time grant paid.

**What the application asks for:**

- Email describing the project, its open-source status and how it reduces dependence on large tech platforms
- unconfirmed: links to repository and funding ask

**Risks:** Knight Foundation was dropped: its open calls (Knight Cities Challenge, closed 30 Apr 2026) fund local news, arts and Knight communities, unsuitable for ForkMesh. FUTO microgrants are small; most recent listed awards are from 2025; paid hosted tier may reduce appeal.

**Sources:** <https://futo.tech/grants/>, <https://knightfoundation.org/knight-cities-challenge/>

ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20JetBrains%3A%20Licenses%20for%20Open%20Source%20Development.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.jetbrains.com%2Fcommunity%2Fopensource%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.jetbrains.com%2Fshop%2Feform%2Fopensource%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20JetBrains%3A%20Licenses%20for%20Open%20Source%20Development%0AAward%3A%20Free%20All%20Products%20Pack%20and%20Team%20Tools%20licences%20%28free%20versions%20of%20TeamCity%2C%20YouTrack%2C%20Datalore%20Enterprise%20and%20Qodana%20Ultimate%20Plus%3B%20full%20versions%20on%20request%29%20for%20active%20contributors%3B%20in-kind%2C%20no%20monetary%20value%20stated%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Project%20leads%20or%20core%20contributors%20of%20an%20open%20source%20project%20may%20request%20licences%3B%20licences%20go%20only%20to%20active%20contributors%20whose%20code%20contributions%20are%20regular%20and%20visible%20in%20the%20repository%20%28non-code%20commits%20do%20not%20count%29.%20Licences%20are%20for%20non-commercial%20open%20source%20development%20only.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28form%20asks%20for%20country%2Fregion%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%20via%20their%20leads%2Fcore%20contributors%20%28individuals%29%3B%20no%20legal%20entity%20required.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.jetbrains.com%2Fcommunity%2Fopensource%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.jetbrains.com%2Fshop%2Feform%2Fopensource%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Project%20lead%20or%20core%20contributor%20fills%20in%20the%20open%20source%20licence%20request%20form.%202.%20If%20the%20project%20is%20on%20GitHub%2C%20repository%20activity%20is%20checked%20automatically%20on%20submission.%203.%20JetBrains%20reviews%20and%20issues%20All%20Products%20Pack%20%2F%20Team%20Tools%20licences%20to%20the%20listed%20active%20contributors.%204.%20Licence%20is%20issued%20for%20one%20year%20and%20renewed%20on%20request%20%28supply%20the%20previous%20License%20ID%20to%20speed%20up%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Whether%20you%20have%20had%20JetBrains%20OSS%20licences%20before%20%28License%20ID%20if%20yes%29%0A-%20Project%20name%0A-%20Project%20website%0A-%20Repository%20URL%0A-%20License%20URL%20%28copy%20of%20the%20software%27s%20license%20terms%29%0A-%20Country%20%2F%20region%0A-%20Number%20of%20required%20licences%0A-%20Project%20description%20%28optional%3B%20request%20full-version%20Team%20Tools%20here%29%0A-%20Email%20address%20with%20ownership%20confirmation%0A-%20Name%0A-%20Profile%20link%20%28GitHub%20or%20link%20showing%20project%20contributions%29%0A-%20Confirmation%20checkboxes%3A%20licence%20use%20limits%2C%20sharing%20limits%2C%20JetBrains%20Account%20Agreement%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20Licences%20used%20only%20for%20non-commercial%20open%20source%20development%20of%20the%20project%20and%20shared%20only%20with%20active%20contributors%3B%20one-year%20term%2C%20renewable%20on%20request.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Request%20IDE%20licences%20%28CLion%20for%20the%20C%2B%2B%2FQt%20desktop%20node%2C%20GoLand%2C%20PyCharm%2C%20WebStorm%29%20plus%20Qodana%20for%20static%20analysis%20of%20the%20MIT-licensed%20ForkMesh%20protocol%2C%20desktop%20and%20tooling%20repositories.%20Low-effort%20in-kind%20support%20that%20directly%20speeds%20up%20core%20open%20source%20development%20and%20code-quality%20checks%20on%20supply-chain-sensitive%20code.%0ARisks%3A%20%27Non-commercial%20open%20source%20development%20only%27%3A%20licences%20may%20not%20be%20used%20for%20ForkMesh%27s%20paid%20hosted%20services%20or%20proprietary%20work%3B%20contributors%20employed%20on%20the%20commercial%20side%20may%20make%20eligibility%20ambiguous.%20Activity%20is%20auto-checked%20on%20GitHub%3B%20if%20the%20canonical%20repository%20is%20hosted%20on%20ForkMesh%20itself%20rather%20than%20GitHub%2C%20activity%20must%20be%20shown%20another%20way.%20Small%20monetary%20value.)** · [Program page](https://www.jetbrains.com/community/opensource/) · [Apply](https://www.jetbrains.com/shop/eform/opensource)

- **Funder:** JetBrains
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** Free All Products Pack and Team Tools licences (free versions of TeamCity, YouTrack, Datalore Enterprise and Qodana Ultimate Plus; full versions on request) for active contributors; in-kind, no monetary value stated (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 55/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Project leads or core contributors of an open source project may request licences; licences go only to active contributors whose code contributions are regular and visible in the repository (non-code commits do not count). Licences are for non-commercial open source development only.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (form asks for country/region) · **Entity types:** Open source projects via their leads/core contributors (individuals); no legal entity required
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** Licences used only for non-commercial open source development of the project and shared only with active contributors; one-year term, renewable on request.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Request IDE licences (CLion for the C++/Qt desktop node, GoLand, PyCharm, WebStorm) plus Qodana for static analysis of the MIT-licensed ForkMesh protocol, desktop and tooling repositories. Low-effort in-kind support that directly speeds up core open source development and code-quality checks on supply-chain-sensitive code.

**How to apply:**

1. Project lead or core contributor fills in the open source licence request form.
2. If the project is on GitHub, repository activity is checked automatically on submission.
3. JetBrains reviews and issues All Products Pack / Team Tools licences to the listed active contributors.
4. Licence is issued for one year and renewed on request (supply the previous License ID to speed up).

**What the application asks for:**

- Whether you have had JetBrains OSS licences before (License ID if yes)
- Project name
- Project website
- Repository URL
- License URL (copy of the software's license terms)
- Country / region
- Number of required licences
- Project description (optional; request full-version Team Tools here)
- Email address with ownership confirmation
- Name
- Profile link (GitHub or link showing project contributions)
- Confirmation checkboxes: licence use limits, sharing limits, JetBrains Account Agreement

**Risks:** 'Non-commercial open source development only': licences may not be used for ForkMesh's paid hosted services or proprietary work; contributors employed on the commercial side may make eligibility ambiguous. Activity is auto-checked on GitHub; if the canonical repository is hosted on ForkMesh itself rather than GitHub, activity must be shown another way. Small monetary value.

**Sources:** <https://www.jetbrains.com/shop/eform/opensource>, <https://www.jetbrains.com/community/opensource/>, <https://blog.jetbrains.com/platform/2023/04/open-source-software-oss-licenses-explained/>

ce%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Agency%3A%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20The%20cost%20of%20the%20work%20described%20in%20the%20application%20must%20exceed%20EUR%2050%2C000%20%28current%20minimum%29%3B%20no%20maximum%20stated%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Maintainers%2C%20organisations%20or%20communities%20behind%20open%20digital%20base%20technologies%20that%20other%20software%20depends%20on%20%28libraries%2C%20package%20managers%2C%20protocol%20implementations%2C%20developer%2Fadmin%20tools%2C%20encryption%29.%20Code%20must%20be%20under%20an%20OSI-approved%20or%20FSF%20free%20licence%3B%20activities%20already%20funded%20by%20another%20public%20entity%20are%20excluded%3B%20prototypes%20and%20user-facing%20applications%20%28e.g.%20messaging%20apps%2C%20file%20storage%20services%29%20are%20not%20currently%20sought.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28the%20Agency%20says%20it%20invests%20globally%20and%20works%20with%20people%2C%20companies%20and%20FOSS%20communities%20everywhere%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20page%20names%20no%20restriction%3B%20FAQ%20refers%20to%20people%2C%20companies%20and%20FOSS%20communities.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sovereign.tech%2Fprograms%2Ffund%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fapply.sovereign.tech%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20an%20account%20on%20apply.sovereign.tech%20and%20submit%20the%20application%20%28German%20or%20English%29%20through%20the%20portal%20only.%202.%20Basic%20requirements%20check%2C%20then%20review%20against%20criteria%20%28prevalence%2C%20relevance%2C%20vulnerability%2C%20public%20interest%2C%20activities%2C%20expertise%29%2C%20up%20to%2010%20weeks.%203.%20Scoping%20phase%20with%20a%20program%20manager%20to%20define%20the%20project%20description.%204.%20External%20expert%20consultation%20for%20final%20selection%2C%20up%20to%208%20weeks.%205.%20Legal%20compliance%20review%2C%20supporting%20documents%20and%20contracting%2C%20up%20to%208%20weeks%3B%20work%20starts%20only%20after%20the%20contract%20is%20signed.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20portal%20application%20in%20English%20or%20German%20%28form%20preview%3A%20Application-Form-Sovereign-Tech-Fund-1-November-2024.pdf%29%0A-%20Description%20of%20the%20technology%20and%20its%20role%20as%20a%20dependency%20for%20other%20software%20%28prevalence%2C%20relevance%29%0A-%20Vulnerability%20%2F%20risk%20the%20investment%20addresses%20and%20public%20interest%20argument%0A-%20Planned%20activities%20with%20cost%20exceeding%20EUR%2050%2C000%0A-%20Team%20expertise%20and%20maintainer%20information%0A-%20Licence%20information%20%28OSI%20or%20FSF%20approved%3B%20docs%20not%20CC%20NC%2FND%29%0A-%20Declaration%20of%20other%20public%20funding%20for%20the%20same%20activities%0A-%20Supporting%20legal%2Fcompliance%20documentation%20during%20contracting%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%206%20months%20from%20submission%20to%20potential%20contract%20start%20%28up%20to%2010%20weeks%20review%2C%20up%20to%208%20weeks%20expert%20consultation%2C%20up%20to%208%20weeks%20legal%20and%20contracting%29%0AObligations%3A%20Contract%20with%20scope%20and%20milestones%20agreed%20in%20scoping%3B%20regular%20progress%20reports%20tied%20to%20invoicing%20and%20a%20final%20report%3B%20payment%20within%20up%20to%2030%20days%20of%20approved%20invoice%20and%20report%3B%20work%20must%20stay%20under%20an%20OSI%2FFSF%20licence%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20the%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20protocol%20and%20its%20library%20layer%20%28signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20Git%20mirroring%20and%20the%20signed%20issue%2FPR%20record%20format%29%20as%20base%20infrastructure%20that%20keeps%20any%20Git-hosted%20dependency%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20fails%2C%20not%20the%20desktop%20app.%20The%20fund%27s%20criteria%20reward%20reducing%20vulnerability%20of%20technologies%20many%20projects%20depend%20on%2C%20which%20matches%20source-availability%20resilience%20for%20the%20open%20source%20supply%20chain.%0ARisks%3A%20Funds%20established%2C%20widely%20depended-on%20technology%3B%20prototypes%20and%20user-facing%20apps%20are%20explicitly%20not%20sought%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%27s%20adoption%20is%20early%2C%20so%20%27prevalence%27%20will%20be%20weak.%20Large%20competition%20and%20a%20~6%20month%20process.%20Entity-type%20rules%20and%20contract%20type%20%28likely%20a%20service%20contract%20under%20German%20public%20procurement%20rules%29%20are%20not%20stated%20on%20the%20page.%20Commercial%20hosted%20tier%20must%20be%20kept%20separate%20from%20the%20funded%20open%20source%20work.)** · [Program page](https://www.sovereign.tech/programs/fund) · [Apply](https://apply.sovereign.tech)

- **Funder:** Sovereign Tech Agency (German Federal Ministry funded)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** The cost of the work described in the application must exceed EUR 50,000 (current minimum); no maximum stated (about $58,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 55/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Maintainers, organisations or communities behind open digital base technologies that other software depends on (libraries, package managers, protocol implementations, developer/admin tools, encryption). Code must be under an OSI-approved or FSF free licence; activities already funded by another public entity are excluded; prototypes and user-facing applications (e.g. messaging apps, file storage services) are not currently sought.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (the Agency says it invests globally and works with people, companies and FOSS communities everywhere) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: page names no restriction; FAQ refers to people, companies and FOSS communities
- **Decision time:** About 6 months from submission to potential contract start (up to 10 weeks review, up to 8 weeks expert consultation, up to 8 weeks legal and contracting)
- **Obligations:** Contract with scope and milestones agreed in scoping; regular progress reports tied to invoicing and a final report; payment within up to 30 days of approved invoice and report; work must stay under an OSI/FSF licence; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch the ForkMesh mirror protocol and its library layer (signed, integrity-checked Git mirroring and the signed issue/PR record format) as base infrastructure that keeps any Git-hosted dependency reachable when its source host fails, not the desktop app. The fund's criteria reward reducing vulnerability of technologies many projects depend on, which matches source-availability resilience for the open source supply chain.

**How to apply:**

1. Create an account on apply.sovereign.tech and submit the application (German or English) through the portal only.
2. Basic requirements check, then review against criteria (prevalence, relevance, vulnerability, public interest, activities, expertise), up to 10 weeks.
3. Scoping phase with a program manager to define the project description.
4. External expert consultation for final selection, up to 8 weeks.
5. Legal compliance review, supporting documents and contracting, up to 8 weeks; work starts only after the contract is signed.

**What the application asks for:**

- Online portal application in English or German (form preview: Application-Form-Sovereign-Tech-Fund-1-November-2024.pdf)
- Description of the technology and its role as a dependency for other software (prevalence, relevance)
- Vulnerability / risk the investment addresses and public interest argument
- Planned activities with cost exceeding EUR 50,000
- Team expertise and maintainer information
- Licence information (OSI or FSF approved; docs not CC NC/ND)
- Declaration of other public funding for the same activities
- Supporting legal/compliance documentation during contracting

**Risks:** Funds established, widely depended-on technology; prototypes and user-facing apps are explicitly not sought, and ForkMesh's adoption is early, so 'prevalence' will be weak. Large competition and a ~6 month process. Entity-type rules and contract type (likely a service contract under German public procurement rules) are not stated on the page. Commercial hosted tier must be kept separate from the funded open source work.

**Sources:** <https://www.sovereign.tech/programs/fund>, <https://www.sovereign.tech/faq>, <https://apply.sovereign.tech>

## 24. Ethereum Foundation: Ecosystem Support Program - Wishlist and RFP grants

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Ethereum%20Foundation%3A%20Ecosystem%20Support%20Program%20-%20Wishlist%20and%20RFP%20grants.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fesp.ethereum.foundation%2Fapplicants%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fesp.ethereum.foundation%2Fapplicants%2Fwishlist%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Ethereum%20Foundation%3A%20Ecosystem%20Support%20Program%20-%20Wishlist%20and%20RFP%20grants%0AAward%3A%20No%20fixed%20range%3A%20funding%20depends%20on%20scope%20and%20complexity%20unless%20the%20Wishlist%2FRFP%20item%20states%20a%20budget%3B%20ESP%20expects%20rates%20somewhat%20below%20market.%20Paid%20on-chain%20in%20ETH%20by%20default.%0ADeadline%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20active%20Wishlist%20or%20RFP%20items%20listed%20on%202026-10-16%3B%20new%20items%20are%20posted%20periodically%20%28next%20ones%20not%20dated%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Teams%20and%20individuals%20building%20free%2C%20open-source%2C%20non-commercial%20work%20aligned%20with%20CROPS%20%28censorship%20resistance%2C%20open%20source%2C%20privacy%2C%20security%29%20that%20enables%20Ethereum%20builders.%20For-profit%20companies%20may%20apply%20if%20the%20grant-funded%20work%20is%20open%20source%20and%20accessible%20to%20the%20community.%20Must%20apply%20against%20a%20specific%20posted%20Wishlist%20or%20RFP%20item%20%28general%20open%20applications%20were%20discontinued%20in%20late%202025%29.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28subject%20to%20identity%20verification%20and%20sanctions%20screening%20at%20onboarding%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%2C%20nonprofit%2C%20individuals%2C%20academic%20teams.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fesp.ethereum.foundation%2Fapplicants%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fesp.ethereum.foundation%2Fapplicants%2Fwishlist%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Browse%20the%20posted%20Wishlist%20items%20%28problem%20areas%29%20or%20RFPs%20%28scoped%2C%20time-bound%20deliverables%29.%202.%20Submit%20one%20application%20per%20item%20via%20the%20item%27s%20form%20with%20methodology%2C%20timeline%20and%20deliverables%3B%20a%20confirmation%20email%20follows.%203.%20Review%20by%20the%20grants-management%20team%20with%20the%20relevant%20EF%20team%2C%20possibly%20an%20interview%2C%20rescoping%20or%20budget%20negotiation.%204.%20Decision%20by%20email.%205.%20Onboarding%20%28identity%20verification%2C%20formal%20grant%20letter%29%2C%20then%20execution%20with%20a%20Grant%20Evaluator%20doing%20check-ins%20and%20milestone%20reviews.%206.%20Publish%20results%20in%20a%20public%20report%20or%20post.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Selection%20of%20the%20specific%20Wishlist%20or%20RFP%20item%20being%20addressed%20%28separate%20application%20per%20item%29%0A-%20Team%20%2F%20applicant%20background%20and%20relevant%20experience%0A-%20Proposed%20approach%20and%20methodology%0A-%20Timeline%20with%20milestones%0A-%20Concrete%20deliverables%0A-%20Budget%20request%20%28negotiable%20with%20the%20GM%20team%3B%20RFPs%20may%20give%20budget%20guidance%29%0A-%20Identity%20verification%20documents%20and%20signed%20grant%20letter%20at%20onboarding%0A-%20ETH-capable%20wallet%20address%20for%20on-chain%20payment%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Most%20applications%20reviewed%20within%203-6%20weeks%20of%20submission%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone-based%20payments%20%28ETH%20on-chain%20by%20default%29%3B%20check-ins%20with%20a%20Grant%20Evaluator%3B%20outputs%20must%20remain%20open%20source%3B%20public%20report%20or%20post%20on%20completion.%20No%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20Git%20mirror%20network%20as%20censorship-resistant%20source%20hosting%20for%20Ethereum%20client%20and%20tooling%20repositories%2C%20so%20that%20client%20code%20stays%20clonable%20if%20a%20central%20forge%20removes%20or%20loses%20it.%20This%20matches%20ESP%27s%20CROPS%20mandate%20%28censorship%20resistance%2C%20open%20source%2C%20security%29%20and%20the%20EF%27s%20concern%20for%20client%20diversity%20and%20supply-chain%20integrity%3B%20the%20pitch%20should%20target%20a%20matching%20Wishlist%2FRFP%20item%20when%20one%20is%20posted.%0ARisks%3A%20No%20open%20Wishlist%20or%20RFP%20items%20on%202026-10-16%2C%20so%20ForkMesh%20must%20wait%20for%20a%20fitting%20item%20and%20cannot%20pitch%20unsolicited.%20Funded%20work%20must%20be%20non-commercial%2C%20which%20conflicts%20with%20ForkMesh%27s%20paid%20hosted%20tier%20unless%20the%20proposal%20is%20scoped%20to%20the%20MIT%20protocol%2Fmirror%20code.%20Ethereum-specific%20relevance%20must%20be%20argued%20since%20ForkMesh%20references%20Solana%20for%20payouts.%20Payment%20in%20ETH%20carries%20price%20risk.)** · [Program page](https://esp.ethereum.foundation/applicants) · [Apply](https://esp.ethereum.foundation/applicants/wishlist)

- **Funder:** Ethereum Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** No fixed range: funding depends on scope and complexity unless the Wishlist/RFP item states a budget; ESP expects rates somewhat below market. Paid on-chain in ETH by default. (about varies)
- **Deadline:** unconfirmed: no active Wishlist or RFP items listed on 2026-10-16; new items are posted periodically (next ones not dated) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 52/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Teams and individuals building free, open-source, non-commercial work aligned with CROPS (censorship resistance, open source, privacy, security) that enables Ethereum builders. For-profit companies may apply if the grant-funded work is open source and accessible to the community. Must apply against a specific posted Wishlist or RFP item (general open applications were discontinued in late 2025).
- **Geography:** Worldwide (subject to identity verification and sanctions screening at onboarding) · **Entity types:** For-profit, nonprofit, individuals, academic teams
- **Decision time:** Most applications reviewed within 3-6 weeks of submission
- **Obligations:** Milestone-based payments (ETH on-chain by default); check-ins with a Grant Evaluator; outputs must remain open source; public report or post on completion. No equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose ForkMesh's signed, integrity-checked Git mirror network as censorship-resistant source hosting for Ethereum client and tooling repositories, so that client code stays clonable if a central forge removes or loses it. This matches ESP's CROPS mandate (censorship resistance, open source, security) and the EF's concern for client diversity and supply-chain integrity; the pitch should target a matching Wishlist/RFP item when one is posted.

**How to apply:**

1. Browse the posted Wishlist items (problem areas) or RFPs (scoped, time-bound deliverables).
2. Submit one application per item via the item's form with methodology, timeline and deliverables; a confirmation email follows.
3. Review by the grants-management team with the relevant EF team, possibly an interview, rescoping or budget negotiation.
4. Decision by email.
5. Onboarding (identity verification, formal grant letter), then execution with a Grant Evaluator doing check-ins and milestone reviews.
6. Publish results in a public report or post.

**What the application asks for:**

- Selection of the specific Wishlist or RFP item being addressed (separate application per item)
- Team / applicant background and relevant experience
- Proposed approach and methodology
- Timeline with milestones
- Concrete deliverables
- Budget request (negotiable with the GM team; RFPs may give budget guidance)
- Identity verification documents and signed grant letter at onboarding
- ETH-capable wallet address for on-chain payment

**Risks:** No open Wishlist or RFP items on 2026-10-16, so ForkMesh must wait for a fitting item and cannot pitch unsolicited. Funded work must be non-commercial, which conflicts with ForkMesh's paid hosted tier unless the proposal is scoped to the MIT protocol/mirror code. Ethereum-specific relevance must be argued since ForkMesh references Solana for payouts. Payment in ETH carries price risk.

**Sources:** <https://esp.ethereum.foundation/applicants>, <https://esp.ethereum.foundation/applicants/wishlist>, <https://esp.ethereum.foundation/applicants/rfp>, <https://blog.ethereum.org/2025/11/03/new-esp-grants>

0the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Google%20Cloud%3A%20Google%20for%20Startups%20Cloud%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Google%20Cloud%20credits%20up%20to%20USD%20200%2C000%2C%20or%20up%20to%20USD%20350%2C000%20for%20Scale%20tier%20AI%20startups%20%28over%20two%20years%29%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20Start%20tier%20amount%20%28partner%20material%20cites%20USD%202%2C000%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Start%20tier%3A%20digital-native%20startups%20with%20a%20working%20MVP%2C%20clear%20business%20model%20and%20plans%20to%20seek%20venture%20funding%20soon%20%28angel%2C%20crowdfunding%20and%20friends-and-family%20funding%20are%20evaluated%20for%20Start%20only%29.%20Scale%20tier%3A%20VC-funded%20seed%20to%20Series%20A%2C%20founded%20within%20the%20last%2010%20years.%20Google%20may%20not%20accept%20IPO%27d%2Facquired%20companies%2C%20nonprofits%2C%20government%20or%20educational%20entities%2C%20dev%20shops%2Fagencies%2C%20crypto-mining%20companies%2C%20or%20companies%20distributing%20tokens%20contrary%20to%20regulatory%20guidance.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20sanctioned-country%20exclusions%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20startups%20only%20%28nonprofits%20excluded%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcloud.google.com%2Fstartup%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcloud.google.com%2Fstartup%2Fapply%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20a%20Google%20Cloud%20account%20and%20obtain%20the%2018-character%20billing%20account%20ID.%202.%20Use%20a%20business%20email%20matching%20the%20startup%27s%20public%20website%20domain.%203.%20Submit%20the%20application%20at%20cloud.google.com%2Fstartup%2Fapply%20%28choose%20tier%3B%20for%20Scale%20provide%20links%20to%20public%20sources%20documenting%20equity%20funding%29.%204.%20Decision%20usually%20within%20a%20few%20business%20days%3B%20manual%20review%20can%20take%2010%2B%20days.%205.%20Credits%20deposited%20to%20the%20billing%20account.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%2018-character%20Google%20Cloud%20billing%20account%20ID%0A-%20Business%20email%20on%20the%20startup%27s%20public%20website%20domain%0A-%20Public%20company%20website%0A-%20Company%20and%20product%20description%20%28working%20MVP%29%0A-%20Funding%20stage%20details%3B%20for%20Scale%20tier%2C%20links%20to%20publicly%20available%20sources%20showing%20equity%20funding%20%28seed%20to%20Series%20A%29%0A-%20Founding%20date%20%28within%20last%2010%20years%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20A%20few%20business%20days%3B%2010%2B%20days%20if%20manual%20review%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20cannot%20be%20used%20for%20third-party%20offerings%20including%20Google%20Cloud%20Marketplace%3B%20credits%20expire%20within%20the%20program%20period%20%28up%20to%20two%20years%29.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Run%20a%20set%20of%20ForkMesh%20mirror-fleet%20and%20runner-tier%20nodes%20on%20Google%20Cloud%20to%20add%20a%20second%20provider%20behind%20the%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20and%20use%20credits%20%28plus%20Gemini%2FVertex%20access%29%20for%20the%20AI%20coding-agent%20features%20in%20the%20desktop%20app.%20Google%20wants%20startups%20building%20developer%20tooling%20and%20AI%20agents%20on%20its%20platform.%0ARisks%3A%20Bootstrapped%20ForkMesh%20likely%20only%20qualifies%20for%20the%20small%20Start%20tier%3B%20Scale%20needs%20institutional%20VC.%20The%20Solana-referenced%20payout%20pool%20could%20draw%20scrutiny%20under%20the%20token-distribution%20exclusion.%20Nonprofits%20are%20excluded.%20Credits%20only%20useful%20if%20workloads%20run%20on%20GCP%20rather%20than%20Vultr.)** · [Program page](https://cloud.google.com/startup) · [Apply](https://cloud.google.com/startup/apply)

- **Funder:** Google Cloud
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Google Cloud credits up to USD 200,000, or up to USD 350,000 for Scale tier AI startups (over two years); unconfirmed: Start tier amount (partner material cites USD 2,000) (about $2,000 - $200,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 50/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Start tier: digital-native startups with a working MVP, clear business model and plans to seek venture funding soon (angel, crowdfunding and friends-and-family funding are evaluated for Start only). Scale tier: VC-funded seed to Series A, founded within the last 10 years. Google may not accept IPO'd/acquired companies, nonprofits, government or educational entities, dev shops/agencies, crypto-mining companies, or companies distributing tokens contrary to regulatory guidance.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: sanctioned-country exclusions) · **Entity types:** For-profit startups only (nonprofits excluded)
- **Decision time:** A few business days; 10+ days if manual review
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits cannot be used for third-party offerings including Google Cloud Marketplace; credits expire within the program period (up to two years).

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Run a set of ForkMesh mirror-fleet and runner-tier nodes on Google Cloud to add a second provider behind the signed, integrity-checked mirrors, and use credits (plus Gemini/Vertex access) for the AI coding-agent features in the desktop app. Google wants startups building developer tooling and AI agents on its platform.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a Google Cloud account and obtain the 18-character billing account ID.
2. Use a business email matching the startup's public website domain.
3. Submit the application at cloud.google.com/startup/apply (choose tier; for Scale provide links to public sources documenting equity funding).
4. Decision usually within a few business days; manual review can take 10+ days.
5. Credits deposited to the billing account.

**What the application asks for:**

- 18-character Google Cloud billing account ID
- Business email on the startup's public website domain
- Public company website
- Company and product description (working MVP)
- Funding stage details; for Scale tier, links to publicly available sources showing equity funding (seed to Series A)
- Founding date (within last 10 years)

**Risks:** Bootstrapped ForkMesh likely only qualifies for the small Start tier; Scale needs institutional VC. The Solana-referenced payout pool could draw scrutiny under the token-distribution exclusion. Nonprofits are excluded. Credits only useful if workloads run on GCP rather than Vultr.

**Sources:** <https://cloud.google.com/startup/faq?hl=en>, <https://cloud.google.com/startup/ai?hl=en>, <https://services.google.com/fh/files/misc/antler_googlecloud_2026.pdf>

<a id="microsoft-for-startups"></a>
## 26. Microsoft for Startups - Azure Startup credits

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Microsoft%20for%20Startups%20-%20Azure%20Startup%20credits.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Flearn.microsoft.com%2Fen-us%2Fstartups%2Fmicrosoft-for-startups%2Foverview%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fstartups.microsoft.com%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Microsoft%20for%20Startups%20-%20Azure%20Startup%20credits%0AAward%3A%20Startup%20credits%20right%20away%20while%20eligibility%20is%20verified%3B%20unlock%20up%20to%20USD%20150K%20in%20Startup%20credits%20over%20time%20with%20verified%20progress%2C%20service%20adoption%20and%20sustained%20Azure%20usage%3B%20Investor%20Network-backed%20startups%20may%20unlock%20more.%20Unconfirmed%3A%20entry%20amounts%20%28third-party%20guides%20cite%20USD%201%2C000%20then%20USD%204%2C000%20after%20business%20verification%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Company%20must%20develop%20a%20software%20product%20or%20service%20it%20owns%2C%20be%20privately%20held%20and%20for-profit%2C%20headquartered%20in%20a%20country%20where%20Azure%20is%20available%2C%20have%20under%20USD%20350%2C000%20lifetime%20free%20Azure%20credits%2C%20and%20not%20have%20raised%20Series%20C%20or%20later.%20Excludes%20educational%20institutions%2C%20government%20organisations%2C%20consultancies%2C%20agencies%20and%20crypto-mining%20businesses.%20Geography%3A%20Countries%20where%20Azure%20services%20are%20available%20%28near-worldwide%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Privately%20held%20for-profit%20companies%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Flearn.microsoft.com%2Fen-us%2Fstartups%2Fmicrosoft-for-startups%2Foverview%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fstartups.microsoft.com%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Go%20to%20startups.microsoft.com%20and%20select%20Get%20started.%202.%20Submit%20the%20application%20%28enter%20an%20Investor%20Network%20referral%20code%20if%20available%29.%203.%20Set%20up%20the%20Azure%20account%3B%20starter%20credits%20are%20available%20while%20eligibility%2Fbusiness%20verification%20runs.%204.%20Decision%20by%20email%2C%20typically%20within%20three%20business%20days.%205.%20Activate%20and%20manage%20benefits%20in%20the%20Azure%20Portal%3B%20additional%20credits%20unlock%20with%20verified%20progress%20and%20Azure%20usage.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Microsoft%2FAzure%20account%0A-%20Company%20name%2C%20headquarters%20country%20and%20website%0A-%20Description%20of%20the%20software%20product%20the%20company%20owns%0A-%20Funding%20stage%20%28must%20be%20pre-Series%20C%29%0A-%20Business%20verification%20details%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20documents%29%0A-%20Investor%20Network%20referral%20code%20%28optional%2C%20for%20higher%20tiers%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Typically%20within%20three%20business%20days%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20usable%20only%20on%20eligible%20Azure%20services%3B%20unused%20credits%20forfeit%20on%20expiry%20%28expiry%20cannot%20be%20extended%20per%20Microsoft%20Q%26A%29.%20Higher%20tiers%20depend%20on%20demonstrated%20Azure%20usage.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Host%20a%20slice%20of%20the%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20fleet%20and%20runner%20tier%20on%20Azure%20%28and%20Azure%20OpenAI%20capacity%20for%20the%20desktop%27s%20AI%20coding%20agents%29%2C%20giving%20signed%20mirrors%20a%20further%20independent%20provider.%20Microsoft%20wants%20developer-tooling%20startups%20that%20adopt%20Azure%20and%20may%20list%20on%20Microsoft%20Marketplace.%0ARisks%3A%20Credits%20beyond%20the%20small%20starter%20amount%20require%20sustained%20Azure%20spend%20or%20an%20Investor%20Network%20partner.%20Must%20be%20a%20for-profit%20company%3B%20nonprofit%20or%20individual%20applicants%20ineligible.%20Crypto-mining%20excluded%20%28Solana%20payout%20pool%20should%20be%20framed%20as%20payments%2C%20not%20mining%29.%20Workloads%20currently%20on%20Vultr%20would%20need%20to%20move%20to%20Azure.)** · [Program page](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/startups/microsoft-for-startups/overview) · [Apply](https://startups.microsoft.com)

- **Funder:** Microsoft
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Startup credits right away while eligibility is verified; unlock up to USD 150K in Startup credits over time with verified progress, service adoption and sustained Azure usage; Investor Network-backed startups may unlock more. Unconfirmed: entry amounts (third-party guides cite USD 1,000 then USD 4,000 after business verification) (about $1,000 - $150,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 50/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Company must develop a software product or service it owns, be privately held and for-profit, headquartered in a country where Azure is available, have under USD 350,000 lifetime free Azure credits, and not have raised Series C or later. Excludes educational institutions, government organisations, consultancies, agencies and crypto-mining businesses.
- **Geography:** Countries where Azure services are available (near-worldwide) · **Entity types:** Privately held for-profit companies only
- **Decision time:** Typically within three business days
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits usable only on eligible Azure services; unused credits forfeit on expiry (expiry cannot be extended per Microsoft Q&A). Higher tiers depend on demonstrated Azure usage.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Host a slice of the ForkMesh mirror fleet and runner tier on Azure (and Azure OpenAI capacity for the desktop's AI coding agents), giving signed mirrors a further independent provider. Microsoft wants developer-tooling startups that adopt Azure and may list on Microsoft Marketplace.

**How to apply:**

1. Go to startups.microsoft.com and select Get started.
2. Submit the application (enter an Investor Network referral code if available).
3. Set up the Azure account; starter credits are available while eligibility/business verification runs.
4. Decision by email, typically within three business days.
5. Activate and manage benefits in the Azure Portal; additional credits unlock with verified progress and Azure usage.

**What the application asks for:**

- Microsoft/Azure account
- Company name, headquarters country and website
- Description of the software product the company owns
- Funding stage (must be pre-Series C)
- Business verification details (unconfirmed: exact documents)
- Investor Network referral code (optional, for higher tiers)

**Risks:** Credits beyond the small starter amount require sustained Azure spend or an Investor Network partner. Must be a for-profit company; nonprofit or individual applicants ineligible. Crypto-mining excluded (Solana payout pool should be framed as payments, not mining). Workloads currently on Vultr would need to move to Azure.

**Sources:** <https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-for-startups/>, <https://guptadeepak.com/startup-offers/guides/microsoft-for-startups>, <https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/answers/a/12813777>

rce%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Anthropic%3A%20Claude%20for%20Open%20Source%0AAward%3A%20Six%20months%20of%20complimentary%20Claude%20Max%2020x%20per%20eligible%20maintainer%20%28approx.%20USD%201%2C200%20at%20list%20price%3B%20overages%20billed%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28open%20until%20Anthropic%20closes%20it%3B%20capped%20at%2010%2C000%20recipients%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Natural%20persons%2018%2B%20with%20a%20GitHub%20account%20at%20least%202%20years%20old%2C%20public%20OSS%20activity%20in%20the%20last%2090%20days%2C%20maintaining%20or%20contributing%20to%20an%20OSI-licensed%20project%2C%20resident%20where%20Claude.ai%20is%20offered.%20Maintainer%20track%20needs%20one%20of%3A%20500%2B%20dependent%20repos%20or%20100%2B%20dependent%20packages%2C%20200%2C000%2B%20monthly%20downloads%2C%20foundation%2Flanguage-project%20committer%2C%20100%2B%20merged%20external%20PRs%20in%2012%20months%2C%2020%2B%20external%20contributors%20in%2012%20months%2C%20or%20OpenSSF%20criticality%20score%20%3E%3D%200.4%3B%20otherwise%20a%20discretionary%20Ecosystem%20Impact%20track.%20Geography%3A%20Countries%20where%20Claude.ai%20is%20available%20%28excluding%20sanctioned%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.anthropic.com%2Fclaude-for-oss-terms%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fclaude.com%2Fopen-source-max%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Sign%20in%20with%20GitHub%20on%20the%20application%20form.%202.%20Describe%20planned%20use%20of%20the%20subscription.%203.%20Explain%20eligibility%20in%20up%20to%20500%20words%20%28Ecosystem%20Impact%20applicants%20describe%20ecosystem%20significance%29.%204.%20Rolling%20review%3B%20only%20approved%20applicants%20are%20contacted.%205.%20Activate%20within%2090%20days%20of%20the%20link.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20GitHub%20account%20%282%2B%20years%20old%29%20for%20sign-in%20and%20verification%0A-%20Brief%20description%20of%20planned%20use%0A-%20Eligibility%20statement%20up%20to%20500%20words%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20rolling%2C%20no%20committed%20timeline%0AObligations%3A%20Personal%2C%20non-transferable%20use%3B%20comply%20with%20Anthropic%20Terms%20and%20Usage%20Policy%3B%20Anthropic%20may%20publicly%20name%20recipients%20and%20their%20projects%3B%20activate%20within%2090%20days%3B%20recipient%20handles%20own%20taxes.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Each%20ForkMesh%20core%20maintainer%20applies%20to%20cover%20Claude%20Code%20usage%20for%20the%20agents%20ForkMesh%20already%20runs%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%2C%20dogfooding%20and%20improving%20the%20Claude%20Code%20integration.%20Anthropic%20benefits%20from%20a%20decentralized%20forge%20that%20showcases%20agent-driven%20pull%20requests.%0ARisks%3A%20Value%20is%20in-kind%20and%20per%20person%3B%20thresholds%20%28dependents%2Fdownloads%29%20may%20not%20be%20met%20yet.)** · [Program page](https://www.anthropic.com/claude-for-oss-terms) · [Apply](https://claude.com/open-source-max)

- **Funder:** Anthropic
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** Six months of complimentary Claude Max 20x per eligible maintainer (approx. USD 1,200 at list price; overages billed) (about $1,200)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (open until Anthropic closes it; capped at 10,000 recipients) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 50/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Natural persons 18+ with a GitHub account at least 2 years old, public OSS activity in the last 90 days, maintaining or contributing to an OSI-licensed project, resident where Claude.ai is offered. Maintainer track needs one of: 500+ dependent repos or 100+ dependent packages, 200,000+ monthly downloads, foundation/language-project committer, 100+ merged external PRs in 12 months, 20+ external contributors in 12 months, or OpenSSF criticality score >= 0.4; otherwise a discretionary Ecosystem Impact track.
- **Geography:** Countries where Claude.ai is available (excluding sanctioned) · **Entity types:** Individuals only
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: rolling, no committed timeline
- **Obligations:** Personal, non-transferable use; comply with Anthropic Terms and Usage Policy; Anthropic may publicly name recipients and their projects; activate within 90 days; recipient handles own taxes.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Each ForkMesh core maintainer applies to cover Claude Code usage for the agents ForkMesh already runs in isolated worktrees, dogfooding and improving the Claude Code integration. Anthropic benefits from a decentralized forge that showcases agent-driven pull requests.

**How to apply:**

1. Sign in with GitHub on the application form.
2. Describe planned use of the subscription.
3. Explain eligibility in up to 500 words (Ecosystem Impact applicants describe ecosystem significance).
4. Rolling review; only approved applicants are contacted.
5. Activate within 90 days of the link.

**What the application asks for:**

- GitHub account (2+ years old) for sign-in and verification
- Brief description of planned use
- Eligibility statement up to 500 words

**Risks:** Value is in-kind and per person; thresholds (dependents/downloads) may not be met yet.

**Sources:** <https://www.anthropic.com/claude-for-oss-terms>, <https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shuttleworth_Foundation>, <https://omidyar.com/>

f%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Sia%20Foundation%20Grants%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Small%2C%20Standard%20and%20Large%20grants%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20limits%20not%20shown%20on%20the%20page%20fetched%20today%20%28earlier%20program%20documentation%20listed%20Small%20up%20to%20%2410%2C000%20and%20Standard%20up%20to%20%2450%2C000%2C%20Large%20above%20that%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20once%20reopened%3A%20the%20program%20page%20says%20it%20is%20%27on%20pause%20until%20September%202026%27%20%28unconfirmed%20whether%20it%20has%20formally%20reopened%3B%20September%202026%20proposals%20are%20on%20the%20forum%29%3B%20committee%20meets%20every%20two%20weeks%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open-source%20projects%20that%20build%20on%20Sia%20decentralized%20storage%3B%20since%20April%202026%20proposals%20must%20focus%20on%20building%20with%20the%20Sia%20SDKs%20or%20building%20on%20indexd.%20Scored%20on%20mission%20alignment%2C%20organisation%2C%20impact%2C%20technical%20feasibility%2C%20team%20capability%20and%20budget%20justification.%20The%20page%20has%20an%20international-proposals%20FAQ%20%28answer%20not%20captured%29.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20international%20proposals%20addressed%20in%20the%20program%20FAQ%3B%20likely%20worldwide%20subject%20to%20sanctions%20screening.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20individuals%20and%20teams%2Fcompanies%20have%20both%20been%20grantees.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsia.tech%2Fgrants%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforum.sia.tech%2Ft%2Fabout-the-grants-category%2F228%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20%27How%20to%20submit%20a%20grant%20proposal%27%20on%20the%20Sia%20forum.%202.%20Complete%20the%20grant%20application%20form%20and%20choose%20Small%2C%20Standard%20or%20Large.%203.%20Post%20the%20proposal%20in%20the%20forum%20%27Grants%20%2F%20Proposed%27%20category%20with%20all%20linked%20material.%204.%20Grants%20Committee%20reviews%20at%20its%20bi-weekly%20meeting%20%28may%20ask%20questions%20on%20the%20forum%20thread%29.%205.%20On%20approval%2C%20sign%20the%20grant%20agreement%20and%20deliver%20milestones%20with%20progress%20reports.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Grant%20application%20form%0A-%20Public%20forum%20proposal%20post%20with%20all%20linked%20material%20copied%20in%0A-%20Project%20description%20focused%20on%20Sia%20SDKs%20or%20indexd%0A-%20Technical%20plan%20%2F%20feasibility%0A-%20Team%20background%0A-%20Budget%20with%20justification%20and%20milestones%2Ftimeline%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20format%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20committee%20meets%20every%20two%20weeks%2C%20so%20typically%20a%20few%20weeks%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%20on%20the%20page%20fetched%20today%3A%20open-source%20licensing%2C%20milestone%20progress%20reports%20on%20the%20forum%20and%20payment%20method%20are%20not%20stated%20there.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20Sia%20storage%20backend%20for%20ForkMesh%20mirrors%20using%20the%20Sia%20SDK%20%2F%20indexd%3A%20Git%20packfiles%2C%20release%20artifacts%20and%20signed%20collaboration%20records%20are%20archived%20to%20Sia%20so%20any%20repository%20can%20be%20restored%20and%20integrity-checked%20even%20if%20every%20live%20mirror%20host%20disappears.%20This%20combines%20ForkMesh%27s%20resilience%2Fpreservation%20mission%20with%20a%20real%2C%20high-volume%20storage%20use%20case%20for%20Sia.%0ARisks%3A%20Official%20page%20still%20says%20the%20program%20is%20paused%20until%20September%202026%20and%20does%20not%20confirm%20reopening%3B%20amount%20limits%20unconfirmed%20today%3B%20proposals%20must%20be%20scoped%20to%20Sia%20SDKs%2Findexd%3B%20all%20proposals%20are%20public%20on%20the%20forum%3B%20payment%20currency%20unconfirmed.)** · [Program page](https://sia.tech/grants) · [Apply](https://forum.sia.tech/t/about-the-grants-category/228)

- **Funder:** Sia Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Small, Standard and Large grants; unconfirmed: limits not shown on the page fetched today (earlier program documentation listed Small up to $10,000 and Standard up to $50,000, Large above that) (about $50,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling once reopened: the program page says it is 'on pause until September 2026' (unconfirmed whether it has formally reopened; September 2026 proposals are on the forum); committee meets every two weeks (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 50/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Open-source projects that build on Sia decentralized storage; since April 2026 proposals must focus on building with the Sia SDKs or building on indexd. Scored on mission alignment, organisation, impact, technical feasibility, team capability and budget justification. The page has an international-proposals FAQ (answer not captured).
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: international proposals addressed in the program FAQ; likely worldwide subject to sanctions screening · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: individuals and teams/companies have both been grantees
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: committee meets every two weeks, so typically a few weeks
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed on the page fetched today: open-source licensing, milestone progress reports on the forum and payment method are not stated there.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a Sia storage backend for ForkMesh mirrors using the Sia SDK / indexd: Git packfiles, release artifacts and signed collaboration records are archived to Sia so any repository can be restored and integrity-checked even if every live mirror host disappears. This combines ForkMesh's resilience/preservation mission with a real, high-volume storage use case for Sia.

**How to apply:**

1. Read 'How to submit a grant proposal' on the Sia forum.
2. Complete the grant application form and choose Small, Standard or Large.
3. Post the proposal in the forum 'Grants / Proposed' category with all linked material.
4. Grants Committee reviews at its bi-weekly meeting (may ask questions on the forum thread).
5. On approval, sign the grant agreement and deliver milestones with progress reports.

**What the application asks for:**

- Grant application form
- Public forum proposal post with all linked material copied in
- Project description focused on Sia SDKs or indexd
- Technical plan / feasibility
- Team background
- Budget with justification and milestones/timeline (unconfirmed: exact format)

**Risks:** Official page still says the program is paused until September 2026 and does not confirm reopening; amount limits unconfirmed today; proposals must be scoped to Sia SDKs/indexd; all proposals are public on the forum; payment currency unconfirmed.

**Sources:** <https://sia.tech/grants>, <https://forum.sia.tech/c/grants/proposed/>, <https://forum.sia.tech/t/about-the-grants-category/228>, <https://arweave.org/funding>, <https://dha.arweave.net/>, <https://fwd.arweave.net/>
