0failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Human%20Rights%20Foundation%3A%20Bitcoin%20Development%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20No%20amount%20stated%3B%20grant%20sizes%20vary%20by%20project%20scope%20and%20impact%20and%20are%20paid%20in%20bitcoin%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Software%20developers%2C%20activists%2C%20educators%2C%20community%20builders%2C%20translators%20and%20UX%20designers%20working%20on%20Bitcoin%20and%20complementary%20freedom%20technology%20%28Bitcoin%20Core%2C%20Nostr%2C%20Ecash%2C%20Bitcoin%20utility%20apps%29%20that%20serves%20human-rights%20defenders%20in%20repressive%20settings.%20Non-Bitcoin%20cryptocurrencies%20and%20projects%20outside%20HRF%27s%20mission%20are%20not%20funded.%20Unsuccessful%20applicants%20may%20reapply%20in%20later%20rounds.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20nonprofits%2C%20organisations%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20for-profit%20eligibility%20not%20stated%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fhrf.org%2Fprogram%2Ffinancial-freedom%2Fbitcoin-development-fund%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fhrf.org%2Fbdfapply%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20a%20brief%20project%20description%20through%20the%20online%20BDF%20application%20form%20%28HRF.org%2Fbdfapply%2C%20a%20monday.com%20form%29.%202.%20Rolling%20review%20by%20the%20BDF%20team.%203.%20Expect%20further%20information%20within%204-6%20weeks.%204.%20Grantees%20are%20announced%20in%20an%20end-of-quarter%20press%20release.%205.%20HRF%20finalises%20administrative%20details%20and%20delivers%20the%20grant%20in%20bitcoin%3B%20the%20team%20supports%20the%20project%20during%20the%20work.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Brief%20project%20description%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20individual%20form%20fields%20%28not%20listed%20on%20the%20official%20page%29%0A-%20Bitcoin%20receiving%20address%20for%20payment%20%28grants%20are%20delivered%20in%20bitcoin%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Further%20information%20within%204-6%20weeks%3B%20grantees%20announced%20quarterly%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20formal%20reporting%20not%20described%3B%20public%20announcement%20of%20grantees%20each%20quarter.%20No%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20nodes%20as%20censorship-resistant%20hosting%20for%20Bitcoin%2C%20Nostr%20and%20Ecash%20source%20code%3A%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%20keep%20these%20repositories%20clonable%20if%20a%20central%20forge%20takes%20them%20down%20or%20a%20government%20blocks%20it%2C%20and%20contributors%20in%20repressive%20countries%20can%20collaborate%20through%20signed%20in-repo%20issues%20and%20pull%20requests.%20Code-hosting%20resilience%20for%20freedom%20tech%20is%20squarely%20within%20HRF%27s%20freedom-tech%20remit%3B%20ForkMesh%27s%20own%20Nostr%20or%20Bitcoin%20integration%20would%20strengthen%20the%20case.%0ARisks%3A%20BDF%20funds%20Bitcoin-specific%20freedom%20tech%3B%20ForkMesh%20is%20chain-agnostic%20and%20its%20payout%20pool%20references%20Solana%2C%20which%20HRF%20does%20not%20fund%2C%20so%20the%20proposal%20must%20stay%20strictly%20about%20Bitcoin%2FNostr%20ecosystem%20resilience.%20Grant%20sizes%20are%20typically%20modest%20and%20paid%20in%20BTC.%20The%20program%20favours%20work%20serving%20human-rights%20defenders%2C%20so%20the%20impact%20case%20must%20be%20concrete.)** · [Program page](https://hrf.org/program/financial-freedom/bitcoin-development-fund/) · [Apply](https://hrf.org/bdfapply)

- **Funder:** Human Rights Foundation (HRF)
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** No amount stated; grant sizes vary by project scope and impact and are paid in bitcoin (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 48/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Software developers, activists, educators, community builders, translators and UX designers working on Bitcoin and complementary freedom technology (Bitcoin Core, Nostr, Ecash, Bitcoin utility apps) that serves human-rights defenders in repressive settings. Non-Bitcoin cryptocurrencies and projects outside HRF's mission are not funded. Unsuccessful applicants may reapply in later rounds.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Individuals, nonprofits, organisations (unconfirmed: for-profit eligibility not stated)
- **Decision time:** Further information within 4-6 weeks; grantees announced quarterly
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: formal reporting not described; public announcement of grantees each quarter. No equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose ForkMesh mirror nodes as censorship-resistant hosting for Bitcoin, Nostr and Ecash source code: signed, integrity-checked mirrors keep these repositories clonable if a central forge takes them down or a government blocks it, and contributors in repressive countries can collaborate through signed in-repo issues and pull requests. Code-hosting resilience for freedom tech is squarely within HRF's freedom-tech remit; ForkMesh's own Nostr or Bitcoin integration would strengthen the case.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit a brief project description through the online BDF application form (HRF.org/bdfapply, a monday.com form).
2. Rolling review by the BDF team.
3. Expect further information within 4-6 weeks.
4. Grantees are announced in an end-of-quarter press release.
5. HRF finalises administrative details and delivers the grant in bitcoin; the team supports the project during the work.

**What the application asks for:**

- Brief project description
- unconfirmed: individual form fields (not listed on the official page)
- Bitcoin receiving address for payment (grants are delivered in bitcoin)

**Risks:** BDF funds Bitcoin-specific freedom tech; ForkMesh is chain-agnostic and its payout pool references Solana, which HRF does not fund, so the proposal must stay strictly about Bitcoin/Nostr ecosystem resilience. Grant sizes are typically modest and paid in BTC. The program favours work serving human-rights defenders, so the impact case must be concrete.

**Sources:** <https://hrf.org/program/financial-freedom/bitcoin-development-fund/>

ing%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20European%20Innovation%20Council%3A%20EIC%20Accelerator%0AAward%3A%20Grant%20%28lump%20sum%29%20below%20EUR%202.5%20million%20for%20innovation%20activities%20%28TRL%206-8%29%20within%2024%20months%3B%20plus%20optional%20investment%20of%20EUR%201-10%20million%20%28equity%20or%20quasi-equity%29%20through%20the%20EIC%20Fund%3B%202026%20budgets%20EUR%20414%20million%20%28Open%29%20and%20EUR%20220%20million%20%28Challenges%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%3A%20short%20applications%20batched%20on%20the%20first%20Tuesday%20of%20each%20month%3B%20next%20full-proposal%20cut-off%202026-11-04%3B%202027%20cut-offs%20unconfirmed%20%28draft%20Work%20Programme%202027%20reportedly%203%20Feb%2C%201%20Apr%2C%201%20Sep%2C%203%20Nov%202027%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Single%20start-ups%20and%20SMEs%20%28including%20spin-outs%29%20in%20EU%20Member%20States%20or%20Horizon%20Europe%20associated%20countries.%20Natural%20persons%20or%20entities%20planning%20to%20set%20up%20an%20SME%20can%20also%20apply.%20Small%20mid-caps%20up%20to%20499%20employees%20can%20apply%20for%20investment%20only.%20Third-country%20applicants%20may%20relocate%20before%20Step%202.%20UK%20applicants%20can%20only%20get%20the%20grant.%20One%20application%20at%20a%20time.%20Geography%3A%20EU%20Member%20States%20and%20Horizon%20Europe%20associated%20countries.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20SMEs%2Fstart-ups%3B%20natural%20persons%20intending%20to%20found%20an%20SME%3B%20small%20mid-caps%20%28investment%20only%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Feic.ec.europa.eu%2Feic-funding-opportunities%2Feic-accelerator_en%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Feic.ec.europa.eu%2Feic-funding-opportunities%2Feic-accelerator_en%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Step%201%20short%20proposal%2C%20assessed%20remotely%20by%20four%20experts%3B%20feedback%20usually%20in%204-6%20weeks.%202.%20Step%202%20full%20proposal%20at%20a%20cut-off%2C%20with%20remote%20evaluation%20and%20an%20online%20interview%20with%20a%20technology%20expert%3B%20results%20in%208-9%20weeks.%203.%20Top-ranked%20proposals%20%282.5x%20the%20grant%20budget%29%20go%20to%20an%20EIC%20Jury%20interview%3B%20results%202-3%20weeks%20later.%204.%20Grant%20signature%20and%2For%20EIC%20Fund%20due%20diligence.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Step%201%3A%2012-page%20short%20application%20form%20covering%20innovation%2C%20market%20and%20team%0A-%20Step%201%3A%20pitch%20deck%20of%20up%20to%2010%20slides%20%28PDF%29%0A-%20Step%201%3A%20video%20pitch%20up%20to%203%20minutes%20by%20up%20to%20three%20core%20team%20members%0A-%20Step%202%3A%20full%20proposal%20and%20business%20plan%20in%20the%20EIC%20template%20%28sections%20and%20page%20limits%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Step%203%3A%20EIC%20Jury%20interview%20pitch%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Step%201%20feedback%204-6%20weeks%3B%20Step%202%20results%208-9%20weeks%3B%20Jury%20results%202-3%20weeks%20after%20the%20interview%20week%3B%20investment%20decision%202-6%20months%20after%20Step%204%0AObligations%3A%20Lump-sum%20grant%20with%20milestones%20and%20reporting%20under%20the%20Horizon%20Europe%20grant%20agreement.%20Blended%20finance%20means%20the%20EIC%20Fund%20takes%20equity%20or%20quasi-equity.%20Companies%20not%20yet%20ready%20for%20investment%20start%20with%20the%20grant%20and%20are%20reassessed%20at%20milestones.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20the%20hosted%20resilient-forge%20product%20%28mirror%20fleet%2C%20runner%20tier%2C%20desktop%20full%20node%2C%20paid%20bounded%20hosting%29%20as%20a%20European%20digital-sovereignty%20deep-tech%20scale-up%20at%20TRL%206-8.%20The%20EIC%20wants%20high-risk%2C%20high-growth%20companies%20addressing%20big%20markets%2C%20so%20lead%20with%20the%20commercial%20hosted%20offering%20and%20the%20supply-chain-security%20market%20rather%20than%20the%20free%20protocol.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20an%20SME%20in%20an%20EU%20or%20associated%20country%20%28or%20a%20commitment%20to%20relocate%29.%20Very%20competitive%2C%20with%20heavy%20multi-stage%20effort.%20The%20equity%20component%20dilutes%20ownership.%20An%20MIT-licensed%20core%20may%20weaken%20the%20defensibility%20case.%20The%20crypto%20and%20payout%20pieces%20may%20complicate%20investment%20due%20diligence.%20The%202027%20rules%20are%20still%20in%20draft.)** · [Program page](https://eic.ec.europa.eu/eic-funding-opportunities/eic-accelerator_en) · [Apply](https://eic.ec.europa.eu/eic-funding-opportunities/eic-accelerator_en)

- **Funder:** European Innovation Council (European Commission, Horizon Europe)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Grant (lump sum) below EUR 2.5 million for innovation activities (TRL 6-8) within 24 months; plus optional investment of EUR 1-10 million (equity or quasi-equity) through the EIC Fund; 2026 budgets EUR 414 million (Open) and EUR 220 million (Challenges) (about $2,875,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling: short applications batched on the first Tuesday of each month; next full-proposal cut-off 2026-11-04; 2027 cut-offs unconfirmed (draft Work Programme 2027 reportedly 3 Feb, 1 Apr, 1 Sep, 3 Nov 2027) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Single start-ups and SMEs (including spin-outs) in EU Member States or Horizon Europe associated countries. Natural persons or entities planning to set up an SME can also apply. Small mid-caps up to 499 employees can apply for investment only. Third-country applicants may relocate before Step 2. UK applicants can only get the grant. One application at a time.
- **Geography:** EU Member States and Horizon Europe associated countries · **Entity types:** For-profit SMEs/start-ups; natural persons intending to found an SME; small mid-caps (investment only)
- **Decision time:** Step 1 feedback 4-6 weeks; Step 2 results 8-9 weeks; Jury results 2-3 weeks after the interview week; investment decision 2-6 months after Step 4
- **Obligations:** Lump-sum grant with milestones and reporting under the Horizon Europe grant agreement. Blended finance means the EIC Fund takes equity or quasi-equity. Companies not yet ready for investment start with the grant and are reassessed at milestones.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch the hosted resilient-forge product (mirror fleet, runner tier, desktop full node, paid bounded hosting) as a European digital-sovereignty deep-tech scale-up at TRL 6-8. The EIC wants high-risk, high-growth companies addressing big markets, so lead with the commercial hosted offering and the supply-chain-security market rather than the free protocol.

**How to apply:**

1. Step 1 short proposal, assessed remotely by four experts; feedback usually in 4-6 weeks.
2. Step 2 full proposal at a cut-off, with remote evaluation and an online interview with a technology expert; results in 8-9 weeks.
3. Top-ranked proposals (2.5x the grant budget) go to an EIC Jury interview; results 2-3 weeks later.
4. Grant signature and/or EIC Fund due diligence.

**What the application asks for:**

- Step 1: 12-page short application form covering innovation, market and team
- Step 1: pitch deck of up to 10 slides (PDF)
- Step 1: video pitch up to 3 minutes by up to three core team members
- Step 2: full proposal and business plan in the EIC template (sections and page limits unconfirmed)
- Step 3: EIC Jury interview pitch

**Risks:** Needs an SME in an EU or associated country (or a commitment to relocate). Very competitive, with heavy multi-stage effort. The equity component dilutes ownership. An MIT-licensed core may weaken the defensibility case. The crypto and payout pieces may complicate investment due diligence. The 2027 rules are still in draft.

**Sources:** <https://eic.ec.europa.eu/eic-funding-opportunities/eic-accelerator_en>, <https://www.fundingprogrammesportal.gov.cy/en/call/eic-accelerator-2026-short-application-en-2025/>, <https://www.rasph.com/eic-work-programme-2027-drafts-pathfinder-transition-accelerator-changes>

rovider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Open%20Source%20Technology%20Improvement%20Fund%3A%20OSTIF%20security%20audits%0AAward%3A%20In-kind%20managed%20security%20audit%3B%20initial%20audits%20typically%20cost%20USD%2030k%20-%20200k%2C%20which%20OSTIF%20helps%20cover%20by%20raising%20funds%20and%20finding%20sponsors%20%28not%20free%20by%20default%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open-source%20projects%20seeking%20an%20independent%20security%20audit%3B%20no%20formal%20criteria%20published%20and%20OSTIF%20invites%20any%20project%20to%20reach%20out.%20Funding%20depends%20on%20OSTIF%20finding%20sponsors%20%28e.g.%20via%20its%20sponsor%20and%20foundation%20partnerships%29%20for%20the%20project.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open-source%20projects%20%28any%20owner%20type%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20prioritisation%20criteria%20not%20published%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fostif.org%2Fget-an-audit%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforms.clickup.com%2F90132124106%2Ff%2F2ky4p4ea-3833%2FO6UZRESBTKJLR0VB72%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Review%20OSTIF%27s%20security%20best-practices%20guide%20on%20GitHub.%202.%20Submit%20the%20intake%20form%20%28ClickUp%29.%203.%20Initial%20meeting%20and%20questionnaire%20to%20scope%20the%20audit%3B%20OSTIF%20seeks%20sponsor%20funding.%204.%20OSTIF%20runs%20an%20RfP%2C%20collects%20auditor%20bids%2C%20and%20signs%20a%20SOW.%205.%20Audit%20with%20kickoff%2C%20sync%20and%20closing%20meetings.%206.%20Fix%20findings%20with%20auditor%20help.%207.%20Coordinated%20public%20report%20publication.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Intake%20form%20with%20project%20information%20%28GitHub%20link%20or%20source%20code%2C%20documentation%29%0A-%20Documentation%20of%20development%20practices%20and%20processes%0A-%20Previous%20security%20reviews%2C%20including%20of%20dependencies%0A-%20Security%20policy%20%28e.g.%20SECURITY.md%29%0A-%20List%20of%20any%20components%20that%20are%20not%20open%20source%0A-%20Fuzzing%2C%20dynamic%20analysis%20and%20CI%2FCD%20tooling%20in%20use%0A-%20Cryptographic%20libraries%20used%0A-%20Completed%20OSTIF%20scoping%20questionnaire%20%28feeds%20the%20RfP%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20depends%20on%20securing%20sponsor%20funding%3B%20audit%20phases%20listed%20without%20durations%0AObligations%3A%20Publish%20the%20audit%20report%20publicly%20after%20fixes%3B%20resolve%20security-impacting%20findings%3B%20attend%20meetings%20and%20answer%20auditor%20questions%3B%20follow%2090-day%20disclosure%20%28extendable%2030%20days%20on%20request%29%3B%20maintainer%20feedback%20form.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Request%20an%20audit%20of%20ForkMesh%27s%20signing%20and%20integrity-verification%20path%20%28signed%20issue%2FPR%20records%2C%20mirror%20integrity%20checks%2C%20node%20identity%20keys%29%20and%20the%20agent%20worktree%20isolation.%20A%20published%20audit%20makes%20ForkMesh%27s%20supply-chain-integrity%20claims%20credible%2C%20and%20OSTIF%27s%20sponsors%20fund%20exactly%20this%20kind%20of%20supply-chain%20security%20work.%0ARisks%3A%20Not%20a%20guaranteed%20grant%3A%20the%20audit%20happens%20only%20if%20OSTIF%20or%20the%20project%20finds%20sponsors%20to%20pay%20USD%2030k-200k.%20Sponsors%20favour%20widely%20used%20critical%20projects%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%20is%20early-stage.%20Findings%20become%20public%20after%20the%20disclosure%20window.)** · [Program page](https://ostif.org/get-an-audit/) · [Apply](https://forms.clickup.com/90132124106/f/2ky4p4ea-3833/O6UZRESBTKJLR0VB72)

- **Funder:** Open Source Technology Improvement Fund (OSTIF)
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** In-kind managed security audit; initial audits typically cost USD 30k - 200k, which OSTIF helps cover by raising funds and finding sponsors (not free by default) (about $200,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open-source projects seeking an independent security audit; no formal criteria published and OSTIF invites any project to reach out. Funding depends on OSTIF finding sponsors (e.g. via its sponsor and foundation partnerships) for the project.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Open-source projects (any owner type; unconfirmed: prioritisation criteria not published)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: depends on securing sponsor funding; audit phases listed without durations
- **Obligations:** Publish the audit report publicly after fixes; resolve security-impacting findings; attend meetings and answer auditor questions; follow 90-day disclosure (extendable 30 days on request); maintainer feedback form.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Request an audit of ForkMesh's signing and integrity-verification path (signed issue/PR records, mirror integrity checks, node identity keys) and the agent worktree isolation. A published audit makes ForkMesh's supply-chain-integrity claims credible, and OSTIF's sponsors fund exactly this kind of supply-chain security work.

**How to apply:**

1. Review OSTIF's security best-practices guide on GitHub.
2. Submit the intake form (ClickUp).
3. Initial meeting and questionnaire to scope the audit; OSTIF seeks sponsor funding.
4. OSTIF runs an RfP, collects auditor bids, and signs a SOW.
5. Audit with kickoff, sync and closing meetings.
6. Fix findings with auditor help.
7. Coordinated public report publication.

**What the application asks for:**

- Intake form with project information (GitHub link or source code, documentation)
- Documentation of development practices and processes
- Previous security reviews, including of dependencies
- Security policy (e.g. SECURITY.md)
- List of any components that are not open source
- Fuzzing, dynamic analysis and CI/CD tooling in use
- Cryptographic libraries used
- Completed OSTIF scoping questionnaire (feeds the RfP)

**Risks:** Not a guaranteed grant: the audit happens only if OSTIF or the project finds sponsors to pay USD 30k-200k. Sponsors favour widely used critical projects, and ForkMesh is early-stage. Findings become public after the disclosure window.

**Sources:** <https://ostif.org/>, <https://ostif.org/get-an-audit/>

## 32. Stellar Development Foundation: Stellar Community Fund Build Award

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Stellar%20Development%20Foundation%3A%20Stellar%20Community%20Fund%20Build%20Award.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fcommunityfund.stellar.org%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fcommunityfund.stellar.org%2Fawards%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Stellar%20Development%20Foundation%3A%20Stellar%20Community%20Fund%20Build%20Award%0AAward%3A%20Up%20to%20%24150%2C000%20in%20XLM%20per%20Build%20Award%20%28lifetime%20cap%20%24150%2C000%2C%20case-by-case%20up%20to%20%24300%2C000%29%3B%20paid%20in%20four%20tranches%2010%25%20%2F%2020%25%20%2F%2030%25%20%2F%2040%25%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20interest%20form%3B%20invited%20teams%20submit%20in%20the%20next%20quarterly%20SCF%20round%20%28SCF%20%2343%2F%2344%20windows%3B%20exact%20dates%20unconfirmed%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Teams%20building%20on%20the%20Stellar%20network%20%28Soroban%20smart%20contracts%20or%20Stellar%20integrations%29%20who%20pass%20an%20interest-form%20eligibility%20review%20and%20are%20invited%20to%20a%20round.%20Awarded%20teams%20must%20pass%20KYC%2FKYB%3B%20jurisdiction-specific%20conditions%20may%20restrict%20tranche%20structure%20or%20deployment.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%2C%20subject%20to%20SDF%20KYC%2FKYB%20and%20sanctions%20screening%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20excluded%20jurisdictions%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20teams%2Fcompanies%3B%20individuals%20appear%20allowed%20via%20KYC%2C%20entity%20type%20not%20stated%20on%20handbook%20welcome%20page.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcommunityfund.stellar.org%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcommunityfund.stellar.org%2Fawards%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20the%20open%20Interest%20Form%20on%20communityfund.stellar.org.%202.%20Review%20panel%20scores%20ecosystem%20value%2C%20eligibility%2Falignment%20and%20team%20readiness.%203.%20If%20invited%2C%20pick%20a%20track%20%28Open%2C%20Integration%2C%20or%20RFP%20for%20dev%20tools%20answering%20an%20active%20SCF%20RFP%29.%204.%20Optionally%20secure%20referrals%20from%20Stellar%20ecosystem%20stakeholders%20%28weighed%20heavily%29.%205.%20Submit%20the%20round%27s%20Build%20Award%20form%3B%20possible%20reviewer%20interview.%206.%20KYC%2FKYB%2C%20then%20tranche%20%230%20payment%20and%20milestone-based%20tranches.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Interest%20Form%20%28project%20description%2C%20team%2C%20ecosystem%20value%29%0A-%20Build%20Award%20submission%20with%20technical%20architecture%20document%0A-%20Budget%20broken%20into%20four%20tranches%20with%20deliverables%20per%20tranche%0A-%20Threat%20model%20and%20monitoring%20plan%20%28required%20for%20tranche%20%232%29%0A-%20For%20Integration%20Track%3A%20committed%20on-chain%20metric%20approved%20by%20panel%0A-%20Ecosystem%20referrals%20%28optional%20but%20weighted%29%0A-%20KYC%2FKYB%20documents%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20roughly%20one%20quarterly%20round%20cycle%20%28about%201-3%20months%20from%20invitation%29%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone%20delivery%20per%20tranche%20%2810%2F20%2F30%2F40%25%29%3B%20missing%20a%20deadline%20without%20notice%20forfeits%20the%20remainder%3B%20threat%20model%20and%20monitoring%20plan%3B%20Integration%20Track%20final%20tranche%20tied%20to%20an%20on-chain%20metric%3B%20paid%20in%20XLM%20%28USD%20valued%20at%20CF%20Benchmarks%20settlement%20price%20on%20payment%20date%29%3B%20KYC%2FKYB%20may%20be%20re-requested.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20Stellar%20integration%20for%20ForkMesh%27s%20community%20payout%20%2F%20contribution%20pool%3A%20paying%20contributors%20and%20bounty%20hunters%20in%20XLM%2FUSDC%20on%20Stellar%20alongside%20Solana%2C%20with%20signed%20ForkMesh%20pull-request%20records%20as%20the%20payout%20trigger.%20Alternatively%20respond%20to%20an%20SCF%20RFP%20for%20developer%20tooling%20by%20offering%20decentralized%2C%20signed%20Git%20mirroring%20for%20Soroban%20ecosystem%20repositories.%0ARisks%3A%20Requires%20a%20real%20Stellar%20integration%20%28ForkMesh%20currently%20references%20Solana%29%3B%20payment%20in%20volatile%20XLM%3B%20invitation-gated%20after%20interest%20form%3B%20referrals%20weigh%20heavily%20and%20ForkMesh%20has%20no%20Stellar%20ecosystem%20ties%20yet%3B%20KYC%2FKYB%20jurisdiction%20limits.)** · [Program page](https://communityfund.stellar.org/) · [Apply](https://communityfund.stellar.org/awards)

- **Funder:** Stellar Development Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Up to $150,000 in XLM per Build Award (lifetime cap $150,000, case-by-case up to $300,000); paid in four tranches 10% / 20% / 30% / 40% (about $150,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling interest form; invited teams submit in the next quarterly SCF round (SCF #43/#44 windows; exact dates unconfirmed) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Teams building on the Stellar network (Soroban smart contracts or Stellar integrations) who pass an interest-form eligibility review and are invited to a round. Awarded teams must pass KYC/KYB; jurisdiction-specific conditions may restrict tranche structure or deployment.
- **Geography:** Worldwide, subject to SDF KYC/KYB and sanctions screening (unconfirmed: exact excluded jurisdictions) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: teams/companies; individuals appear allowed via KYC, entity type not stated on handbook welcome page
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: roughly one quarterly round cycle (about 1-3 months from invitation)
- **Obligations:** Milestone delivery per tranche (10/20/30/40%); missing a deadline without notice forfeits the remainder; threat model and monitoring plan; Integration Track final tranche tied to an on-chain metric; paid in XLM (USD valued at CF Benchmarks settlement price on payment date); KYC/KYB may be re-requested.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a Stellar integration for ForkMesh's community payout / contribution pool: paying contributors and bounty hunters in XLM/USDC on Stellar alongside Solana, with signed ForkMesh pull-request records as the payout trigger. Alternatively respond to an SCF RFP for developer tooling by offering decentralized, signed Git mirroring for Soroban ecosystem repositories.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit the open Interest Form on communityfund.stellar.org.
2. Review panel scores ecosystem value, eligibility/alignment and team readiness.
3. If invited, pick a track (Open, Integration, or RFP for dev tools answering an active SCF RFP).
4. Optionally secure referrals from Stellar ecosystem stakeholders (weighed heavily).
5. Submit the round's Build Award form; possible reviewer interview.
6. KYC/KYB, then tranche #0 payment and milestone-based tranches.

**What the application asks for:**

- Interest Form (project description, team, ecosystem value)
- Build Award submission with technical architecture document
- Budget broken into four tranches with deliverables per tranche
- Threat model and monitoring plan (required for tranche #2)
- For Integration Track: committed on-chain metric approved by panel
- Ecosystem referrals (optional but weighted)
- KYC/KYB documents

**Risks:** Requires a real Stellar integration (ForkMesh currently references Solana); payment in volatile XLM; invitation-gated after interest form; referrals weigh heavily and ForkMesh has no Stellar ecosystem ties yet; KYC/KYB jurisdiction limits.

**Sources:** <https://communityfund.stellar.org/>, <https://stellar.gitbook.io/scf-handbook>, <https://stellar.org/blog/ecosystem/introducing-scf-v7>

live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Zerodha%3A%20FLOSS%2Ffund%0AAward%3A%20up%20to%20%241%20million%20per%20year%20in%20total%3B%20a%20project%20can%20apply%20for%20up%20to%20%24100%2C000%20in%20one%20year%2C%20minimum%20%2410%2C000%20and%20multiples%20of%20%2425%2C000%20thereafter%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28reviewed%20at%20the%20end%20of%20each%20quarter%3B%20next%20review%20end%20of%20Dec%202026%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Individuals%2C%20projects%2C%20groups%2C%20communities%20or%20organisations%20maintaining%20existing%2C%20widely%20used%20and%20impactful%20Free%2FLibre%20and%20Open%20Source%20projects%3B%20very%20new%20projects%20or%20those%20with%20minimal%20usage%20are%20not%20considered%20for%20now.%20The%20recipient%20needs%20a%20bank%20account%20and%20the%20tax%20documents%20its%20jurisdiction%20requires.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20no%20restriction%20stated%3B%20Indian%20tax%20paperwork%20applies%20for%20disbursement%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20groups%2C%20communities%2C%20organisations%3B%20for-profit%20eligibility%20unconfirmed.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Ffloss.fund%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdir.floss.fund%2Fsubmit%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Write%20a%20funding.json%20manifest%20following%20the%20fundingjson.org%20schema.%202.%20Publish%20it%20on%20the%20project%20website%20or%20repository%20%28with%20the%20domain%2Fwell-known%20verification%20the%20schema%20provides%29.%203.%20Submit%20its%20URL%20at%20dir.floss.fund%2Fsubmit.%204.%20The%20internal%20investment%20committee%20reviews%20submissions%20at%20the%20end%20of%20each%20quarter%20and%20emails%20the%20decision.%205.%20If%20accepted%2C%20tax%20and%20residency%20paperwork%2C%20up%20to%204%20weeks%2C%20then%20disbursement.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20funding.json%20manifest%20%28fundingjson.org%20schema%29%3A%20entity%20block%20%28type%2C%20role%2C%20name%2C%20email%2C%20description%2C%20webpage%20URL%29%0A-%20projects%20block%3A%20name%2C%20description%2C%20webpage%20and%20repository%20URLs%2C%20licences%2C%20tags%0A-%20funding%20block%3A%20payment%20channels%2C%20funding%20plans%20%28amount%2C%20currency%2C%20frequency%29%2C%20funding%20history%0A-%20Domain%20%2F%20repository%20ownership%20verification%20for%20the%20listed%20URLs%0A-%20Bank%20account%20and%20jurisdiction%20tax%20documents%20%28after%20acceptance%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Quarterly%3A%20decision%20at%20the%20end%20of%20the%20quarter%20of%20submission%2C%20then%20up%20to%204%20weeks%20of%20paperwork%0AObligations%3A%20Tax%20and%20residency%20paperwork%20before%20payment%3B%20public%20acknowledgement%20with%20a%20link%2Fbadge%20requested%20but%20not%20required%3B%20no%20reporting%20conditions%20stated%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20List%20ForkMesh%27s%20open%20mirror%20protocol%20and%20node%20software%20in%20funding.json%20and%20request%20USD%2035%2C000-60%2C000%20for%20maintenance%20of%20the%20signed%20mirroring%20and%20in-repo%20collaboration%20record%20format.%20Low%20effort%20since%20the%20manifest%20is%20reusable%20for%20other%20funders%2C%20and%20FLOSS%2Ffund%20backs%20FOSS%20that%20the%20wider%20ecosystem%20relies%20on.%0ARisks%3A%20Explicitly%20favours%20existing%2C%20widely%20used%20projects%20and%20skips%20very%20new%20or%20minimally%20used%20ones%2C%20which%20hurts%20an%20early-stage%20project.%20Most%20recent%20update%20on%20the%20official%20site%20is%20October%202025%2C%20so%202026%20tranche%20activity%20is%20unconfirmed.%20Company-backed%20%2F%20commercial%20projects%20not%20addressed.%20Low%20effort%20makes%20it%20worth%20submitting%20anyway.)** · [Program page](https://floss.fund) · [Apply](https://dir.floss.fund/submit)

- **Funder:** Zerodha
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** up to $1 million per year in total; a project can apply for up to $100,000 in one year, minimum $10,000 and multiples of $25,000 thereafter (about $10,000 - $100,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (reviewed at the end of each quarter; next review end of Dec 2026) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Individuals, projects, groups, communities or organisations maintaining existing, widely used and impactful Free/Libre and Open Source projects; very new projects or those with minimal usage are not considered for now. The recipient needs a bank account and the tax documents its jurisdiction requires.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: no restriction stated; Indian tax paperwork applies for disbursement) · **Entity types:** Individuals, groups, communities, organisations; for-profit eligibility unconfirmed
- **Decision time:** Quarterly: decision at the end of the quarter of submission, then up to 4 weeks of paperwork
- **Obligations:** Tax and residency paperwork before payment; public acknowledgement with a link/badge requested but not required; no reporting conditions stated; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** List ForkMesh's open mirror protocol and node software in funding.json and request USD 35,000-60,000 for maintenance of the signed mirroring and in-repo collaboration record format. Low effort since the manifest is reusable for other funders, and FLOSS/fund backs FOSS that the wider ecosystem relies on.

**How to apply:**

1. Write a funding.json manifest following the fundingjson.org schema.
2. Publish it on the project website or repository (with the domain/well-known verification the schema provides).
3. Submit its URL at dir.floss.fund/submit.
4. The internal investment committee reviews submissions at the end of each quarter and emails the decision.
5. If accepted, tax and residency paperwork, up to 4 weeks, then disbursement.

**What the application asks for:**

- funding.json manifest (fundingjson.org schema): entity block (type, role, name, email, description, webpage URL)
- projects block: name, description, webpage and repository URLs, licences, tags
- funding block: payment channels, funding plans (amount, currency, frequency), funding history
- Domain / repository ownership verification for the listed URLs
- Bank account and jurisdiction tax documents (after acceptance)

**Risks:** Explicitly favours existing, widely used projects and skips very new or minimally used ones, which hurts an early-stage project. Most recent update on the official site is October 2025, so 2026 tranche activity is unconfirmed. Company-backed / commercial projects not addressed. Low effort makes it worth submitting anyway.

**Sources:** <https://floss.fund>, <https://floss.fund/faq/>, <https://dir.floss.fund/submit>

## 34. Google Summer of Code

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Google%20Summer%20of%20Code.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fsummerofcode.withgoogle.com%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fsummerofcode.withgoogle.com%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Google%20Summer%20of%20Code%0AAward%3A%20Contributor%20stipends%20paid%20by%20Google%3A%20Small%20USD%20750-1%2C650%2C%20Medium%20USD%201%2C500-3%2C300%2C%20Large%20USD%203%2C000-6%2C600%20%28PPP-adjusted%29%3B%20mentoring%20orgs%20receive%20a%20%27small%20stipend%27%20per%20accepted%20contributor%20%28amount%20not%20stated%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20org%20application%20window%20expected%20late%20Jan%20-%20early%20Feb%202027%20%282026%3A%2019%20Jan%20-%203%20Feb%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%202027%20dates%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Active%20open-source%20projects%20with%20a%20solid%20community%2C%20software%20released%20under%20an%20OSI-approved%20licence%2C%20and%20at%20least%20two%20committed%20mentors%3B%20Google-led%20and%20government-affiliated%20orgs%20receive%20no%20org%20stipend.%20Search%20results%20cite%20at%20least%2018%20months%20of%20project%20history%20%28unconfirmed%20on%20fetched%20pages%29.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28contributors%20paid%20via%20Payoneer%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open-source%20projects%2Forganizations%20%28any%20legal%20form%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsummerofcode.withgoogle.com%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsummerofcode.withgoogle.com%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Prepare%20an%20ideas%20list%20and%20recruit%202%2B%20mentors%20per%20contributor%20slot.%202.%20Submit%20the%20organization%20application%20in%20the%20Jan-Feb%20window.%203.%20Accepted%20orgs%20announced%20%28~19%20Feb%29.%204.%20Contributors%20apply%20%28March%29%3B%20org%20ranks%20proposals%20and%20requests%20slots.%205.%20Community%20bonding%20then%2012%2B%20weeks%20of%20coding%20with%20evaluations.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Organization%20application%20%28profile%2C%20description%2C%20licence%2C%20contribution%20guidelines%29%0A-%20Public%20ideas%20list%20with%20scoped%20projects%20%2890%20%2F%20175%20%2F%20350%20hours%29%0A-%20At%20least%20two%20mentors%20per%20requested%20slot%0A-%20Contributor%20guidance%20%2F%20application%20template%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%202-3%20weeks%20from%20org%20deadline%20to%20acceptance%0AObligations%3A%20Mentors%20supervise%20and%20evaluate%20contributors%3B%20org%20administrators%20manage%20slots%20and%20evaluations%3B%20follow%20GSoC%20program%20rules.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Offer%20GSoC%20projects%20on%20mirror%20protocol%2C%20signed%20issue%2FPR%20records%2C%20Git-native%20review%20tooling%20and%20agent%20sandboxing%3B%20Google%20pays%20contributor%20stipends%20while%20ForkMesh%20gains%20contributors%20and%20visibility.%0ARisks%3A%20Competitive%20%28about%20185%20orgs%20accepted%20in%202026%29%3B%20first-year%20orgs%20are%20often%20rejected%3B%20project%20age%20requirement%20may%20exclude%20a%20young%20project%3B%20money%20goes%20to%20contributors%2C%20not%20ForkMesh%3B%20mentoring%20time%20cost.)** · [Program page](https://summerofcode.withgoogle.com/) · [Apply](https://summerofcode.withgoogle.com/)

- **Funder:** Google
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** Contributor stipends paid by Google: Small USD 750-1,650, Medium USD 1,500-3,300, Large USD 3,000-6,600 (PPP-adjusted); mentoring orgs receive a 'small stipend' per accepted contributor (amount not stated) (about $6,600)
- **Deadline:** Next org application window expected late Jan - early Feb 2027 (2026: 19 Jan - 3 Feb; unconfirmed: 2027 dates) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Active open-source projects with a solid community, software released under an OSI-approved licence, and at least two committed mentors; Google-led and government-affiliated orgs receive no org stipend. Search results cite at least 18 months of project history (unconfirmed on fetched pages).
- **Geography:** Worldwide (contributors paid via Payoneer) · **Entity types:** Open-source projects/organizations (any legal form)
- **Decision time:** About 2-3 weeks from org deadline to acceptance
- **Obligations:** Mentors supervise and evaluate contributors; org administrators manage slots and evaluations; follow GSoC program rules.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Offer GSoC projects on mirror protocol, signed issue/PR records, Git-native review tooling and agent sandboxing; Google pays contributor stipends while ForkMesh gains contributors and visibility.

**How to apply:**

1. Prepare an ideas list and recruit 2+ mentors per contributor slot.
2. Submit the organization application in the Jan-Feb window.
3. Accepted orgs announced (~19 Feb).
4. Contributors apply (March); org ranks proposals and requests slots.
5. Community bonding then 12+ weeks of coding with evaluations.

**What the application asks for:**

- Organization application (profile, description, licence, contribution guidelines)
- Public ideas list with scoped projects (90 / 175 / 350 hours)
- At least two mentors per requested slot
- Contributor guidance / application template

**Risks:** Competitive (about 185 orgs accepted in 2026); first-year orgs are often rejected; project age requirement may exclude a young project; money goes to contributors, not ForkMesh; mentoring time cost.

**Sources:** <https://developers.google.com/open-source/gsoc/timeline>, <https://developers.google.com/open-source/gsoc/faq>, <https://developers.google.com/open-source/gsoc/help/student-stipends>, <https://summerofcode.withgoogle.com/how-it-works>

20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Code%20for%20Science%20%26%20Society%3A%20Fiscal%20Sponsorship%20Program%0AAward%3A%20No%20direct%20grant%3B%20fiscal%20sponsorship%20lets%20the%20project%20receive%20tax-deductible%20donations%20and%20foundation%2Fgovernment%20grants.%20Fee%20per%20third-party%20directory%3A%2015%25%20on%20non-governmental%20funds%2C%2020%25%20on%20governmental%20funds%20%28unconfirmed%20on%20official%20page%29.%20Past%20Incubator%20cohorts%20paid%20a%20USD%205%2C000%20stipend%20%28no%20current%20cohort%20announced%29.%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28New%20Project%20Inquiry%20Form%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open%20source%20%2F%20open%20science%20%2F%20public-interest%20technology%20projects%20whose%20mission%20aligns%20with%20CS%26S%3B%20the%20project%20joins%20CS%26S%2C%20a%20US%20501%28c%29%283%29%20public%20charity%2C%20which%20holds%20funds%20on%20its%20behalf.%20Directory%20listing%20mentions%20an%20advisory%20group%20and%20a%20minimum%20budget%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20threshold%29.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20US-based%20sponsor%3B%20non-US%20projects%20not%20explicitly%20excluded%20on%20the%20official%20page.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Unincorporated%20open%20source%20projects%20%28charitable%20activity%29%3B%20not%20a%20for-profit%20company%20itself%20%28the%20project%20would%20be%20a%20charitable%20program%20under%20CS%26S%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.codeforsociety.org%2Ffsp%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.codeforsociety.org%2Fbecome-a-fiscally-sponsored-project%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20the%20Fiscally%20Sponsored%20Projects%20FAQ%20and%20the%20%27Timeline%20and%20Application%20Requirements%27%20document.%202.%20Submit%20the%20New%20Project%20Inquiry%20Form.%203.%20Discovery%20conversation%28s%29%20with%20CS%26S%20staff%20on%20mission%20fit%2C%20budget%20and%20governance.%204.%20Formal%20application%20and%20review.%205.%20Sign%20fiscal%20sponsorship%20agreement%3B%20funds%20and%20grants%20then%20flow%20through%20CS%26S.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20New%20Project%20Inquiry%20Form%20%28project%20description%2C%20mission%2C%20team%29%0A-%20Items%20listed%20in%20the%20CS%26S%20%27Timeline%20and%20Application%20Requirements%27%20document%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20list%29%0A-%20Project%20budget%20%2F%20expected%20funding%20sources%20%28directory%20notes%20a%20minimum%20budget%29%0A-%20Advisory%20group%20%2F%20governance%20description%20%28per%20directory%20listing%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20multi-step%20onboarding%2C%20typically%20weeks%20to%20a%20few%20months%0AObligations%3A%20Administrative%20fee%20on%20incoming%20funds%20%2815%25%2F20%25%20per%20directory%2C%20unconfirmed%29%3B%20funds%20must%20be%20spent%20on%20charitable%20purposes%20under%20CS%26S%20oversight%3B%20grant%20reporting%20and%20tax%20compliance%20handled%20with%20CS%26S%3B%20project%20governance%20expectations.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Place%20the%20open%20protocol%20and%20public-clone%2Fmirror%20network%20%28the%20free%2C%20MIT-licensed%20commons%20part%20of%20ForkMesh%29%20under%20CS%26S%20as%20a%20charitable%20open%20infrastructure%20project%2C%20so%20ForkMesh%20can%20take%20foundation%20money%20%28e.g.%20Sloan%2C%20Ford%2C%20Sovereign%20Tech-type%20funds%29%20that%20requires%20a%20501%28c%29%283%29.%20CS%26S%20specialises%20in%20open%20research%20and%20digital%20infrastructure%20and%20its%20Incubator%20focuses%20on%20governance%20and%20community%20health%20for%20such%20projects.%0ARisks%3A%20Fiscal%20sponsorship%20requires%20separating%20a%20charitable%20open-source%20project%20from%20the%20commercial%20ForkMesh%20hosted%20service%20and%20crypto%20payout%20pool%3B%20a%20for-profit%20startup%20cannot%20itself%20be%20sponsored.%20Fees%2015-20%25.%20The%20Digital%20Infrastructure%20Incubator%20%28Event%20Fund%20also%20not%20listed%29%20has%20no%20announced%202026%20cohort%3B%20CS%26S%20says%20it%20is%20seeking%20funders%20for%20future%20cohorts.)** · [Program page](https://www.codeforsociety.org/fsp) · [Apply](https://www.codeforsociety.org/become-a-fiscally-sponsored-project)

- **Funder:** Code for Science & Society (CS&S)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** No direct grant; fiscal sponsorship lets the project receive tax-deductible donations and foundation/government grants. Fee per third-party directory: 15% on non-governmental funds, 20% on governmental funds (unconfirmed on official page). Past Incubator cohorts paid a USD 5,000 stipend (no current cohort announced). (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (New Project Inquiry Form) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source / open science / public-interest technology projects whose mission aligns with CS&S; the project joins CS&S, a US 501(c)(3) public charity, which holds funds on its behalf. Directory listing mentions an advisory group and a minimum budget (unconfirmed: exact threshold).
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: US-based sponsor; non-US projects not explicitly excluded on the official page · **Entity types:** Unincorporated open source projects (charitable activity); not a for-profit company itself (the project would be a charitable program under CS&S)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: multi-step onboarding, typically weeks to a few months
- **Obligations:** Administrative fee on incoming funds (15%/20% per directory, unconfirmed); funds must be spent on charitable purposes under CS&S oversight; grant reporting and tax compliance handled with CS&S; project governance expectations.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Place the open protocol and public-clone/mirror network (the free, MIT-licensed commons part of ForkMesh) under CS&S as a charitable open infrastructure project, so ForkMesh can take foundation money (e.g. Sloan, Ford, Sovereign Tech-type funds) that requires a 501(c)(3). CS&S specialises in open research and digital infrastructure and its Incubator focuses on governance and community health for such projects.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the Fiscally Sponsored Projects FAQ and the 'Timeline and Application Requirements' document.
2. Submit the New Project Inquiry Form.
3. Discovery conversation(s) with CS&S staff on mission fit, budget and governance.
4. Formal application and review.
5. Sign fiscal sponsorship agreement; funds and grants then flow through CS&S.

**What the application asks for:**

- New Project Inquiry Form (project description, mission, team)
- Items listed in the CS&S 'Timeline and Application Requirements' document (unconfirmed: exact list)
- Project budget / expected funding sources (directory notes a minimum budget)
- Advisory group / governance description (per directory listing)

**Risks:** Fiscal sponsorship requires separating a charitable open-source project from the commercial ForkMesh hosted service and crypto payout pool; a for-profit startup cannot itself be sponsored. Fees 15-20%. The Digital Infrastructure Incubator (Event Fund also not listed) has no announced 2026 cohort; CS&S says it is seeking funders for future cohorts.

**Sources:** <https://www.codeforsociety.org/>, <https://www.codeforsociety.org/become-a-fiscally-sponsored-project>, <https://www.codeforsociety.org/incubator>, <https://fiscalsponsordirectory.org/code-for-science-and-society/>, <https://codeforsociety.org/news/the-digital-infrastructure-incubator-is-live>

## 36. Filecoin Foundation for the Decentralized Web: FFDW grants and partnerships

**[Draft this application in 0the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Filecoin%20Foundation%20for%20the%20Decentralized%20Web%3A%20FFDW%20grants%20and%20partnerships%0AAward%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20amounts%20published%3B%20FFDW%20funds%20selected%20organisations%20and%20programs%20%28e.g.%20digital-library%2C%20archive%20and%20fellowship%20programs%29%0ADeadline%3A%20No%20open%20call%3B%20approach%20by%20email%20%28impact%40ffdweb.org%29%20%28invite-only%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20No%20public%20application%20process%3B%20third-party%20grant%20databases%20describe%20FFDW%20as%20funding%20pre-selected%20organisations.%20Focus%20areas%3A%20decentralized%20web%20research%20and%20development%2C%20education%2C%20human%20rights%2C%20cultural%20preservation%2C%20government%20datasets%20and%20policy%2C%20science%20and%20environment.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20worldwide%20%28US-based%20private%20foundation%3B%20past%20grantees%20are%20mostly%20US%20nonprofits%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20mainly%20nonprofits%20and%20charitable%20programs%20%28FFDW%20is%20a%20charitable%20foundation%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fffdweb.org%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fffdweb.org%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Introduce%20ForkMesh%20and%20a%20concrete%20preservation%2FDWeb%20proposal%20to%20FFDW%20via%20impact%40ffdweb.org.%202.%20If%20invited%2C%20FFDW%20scopes%20the%20grant%20or%20partnership.%203.%20unconfirmed%3A%20grant%20agreement%20and%20reporting%20terms.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Short%20introduction%20email%20with%20project%20summary%20and%20fit%20to%20FFDW%20focus%20areas%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20full%20proposal%20and%20budget%20if%20invited%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3B%20as%20a%20charitable%20funder%20FFDW%20is%20likely%20to%20require%20charitable-purpose%20use%2C%20public%20outputs%20and%20reporting.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20ForkMesh%20as%20decentralized-web%20preservation%20infrastructure%20for%20source%20code%3A%20signed%20mirrors%20plus%20a%20Filecoin%2FIPFS%20archival%20tier%20so%20public-interest%20software%20%28government%2C%20archive%20and%20human-rights%20tooling%29%20survives%20forge%20shutdowns%20or%20takedowns.%20This%20sits%20between%20FFDW%27s%20DWeb%20R%26D%2C%20cultural%20preservation%20and%20human-rights%20priorities.%0ARisks%3A%20Protocol%20Labs%20%2F%20IPFS%20open%20grants%20have%20ended%20%28the%20ipfs%2Fdevgrants%20repo%20is%20archived%29.%20FFDW%20has%20no%20open%20call%20and%20appears%20to%20fund%20pre-selected%2C%20mostly%20nonprofit%20organisations%2C%20so%20a%20for-profit%20ForkMesh%20entity%20may%20need%20a%20nonprofit%20partner%20or%20fiscal%20sponsor.%20FFDW%20is%20active%20%282025%20annual%20report%20published%20Feb%202026%29.)** · [Program page](https://ffdweb.org/) · [Apply](https://ffdweb.org/)

- **Funder:** Filecoin Foundation for the Decentralized Web (FFDW)
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** unconfirmed: no amounts published; FFDW funds selected organisations and programs (e.g. digital-library, archive and fellowship programs) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** No open call; approach by email (impact@ffdweb.org) (invite-only)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** No public application process; third-party grant databases describe FFDW as funding pre-selected organisations. Focus areas: decentralized web research and development, education, human rights, cultural preservation, government datasets and policy, science and environment.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: worldwide (US-based private foundation; past grantees are mostly US nonprofits) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: mainly nonprofits and charitable programs (FFDW is a charitable foundation)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed; as a charitable funder FFDW is likely to require charitable-purpose use, public outputs and reporting.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch ForkMesh as decentralized-web preservation infrastructure for source code: signed mirrors plus a Filecoin/IPFS archival tier so public-interest software (government, archive and human-rights tooling) survives forge shutdowns or takedowns. This sits between FFDW's DWeb R&D, cultural preservation and human-rights priorities.

**How to apply:**

1. Introduce ForkMesh and a concrete preservation/DWeb proposal to FFDW via impact@ffdweb.org.
2. If invited, FFDW scopes the grant or partnership.
3. unconfirmed: grant agreement and reporting terms.

**What the application asks for:**

- Short introduction email with project summary and fit to FFDW focus areas
- unconfirmed: full proposal and budget if invited

**Risks:** Protocol Labs / IPFS open grants have ended (the ipfs/devgrants repo is archived). FFDW has no open call and appears to fund pre-selected, mostly nonprofit organisations, so a for-profit ForkMesh entity may need a nonprofit partner or fiscal sponsor. FFDW is active (2025 annual report published Feb 2026).

**Sources:** <https://ffdweb.org/>, <https://www.guidestar.org/profile/85-2066977>, <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ipfs/devgrants/master/README.md>

0provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Oregon%20State%20University%20Open%20Source%20Lab%3A%20OSUOSL%20free%20hosting%20for%20open%20source%20projects%0AAward%3A%20In-kind%20hosting%20%28no%20stated%20dollar%20value%29%3A%20VMs%20on%20OpenStack%2FGaneti%2C%20colocation%2C%20CI%2FCD%20runners%2C%20FTP%20mirroring%2C%20S3-compatible%20object%20storage%2C%20DNS%2C%20databases%2C%20hosted%20GitLab%2FDiscourse%2FMatrix%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20whether%20any%20fee%20applies%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%20under%20an%20OSI-approved%20license%20with%20an%20open%20development%20model%2C%20a%20significant%20positive%20impact%20on%20the%20open%20source%20community%2C%20an%20active%20development%20or%20user%20community%2C%20and%20needs%20that%20have%20outgrown%20free%20hosting%20services.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28hosting%20located%20in%20Oregon%2C%20USA%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20whether%20a%20for-profit%20company%27s%20project%20qualifies%3B%20project-based%20rather%20than%20entity-based%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fosuosl.org%2Fservices%2Fhosting%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fosuosl.org%2Fservices%2Fhosting%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20the%20OSUOSL%20Hosting%20Policy%20to%20confirm%20eligibility.%202.%20Complete%20the%20hosting%20request%20form%2C%20selecting%20each%20needed%20service%20%28VMs%2C%20mirroring%2C%20CI%20runners%2C%20object%20storage%2C%20etc.%29%20and%20answering%20its%20questions.%203.%20OSUOSL%20reviews%20and%20replies%20within%20a%20few%20days%20to%20a%20week%2C%20often%20with%20follow-up%20questions.%204.%20After%20approval%2C%20OSUOSL%20provisions%20the%20services%20and%20sends%20access%20details.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20name%2C%20description%20and%20URL%0A-%20OSI-approved%20license%20%28MIT%20qualifies%29%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20open%20development%20model%20and%20active%20community%0A-%20Per-service%20answers%20on%20the%20hosting%20request%20form%20%28resource%20sizes%20for%20VMs%2C%20mirroring%20needs%2C%20CI%20runner%20needs%2C%20storage%29%0A-%20Explanation%20of%20why%20free%20hosting%20services%20are%20insufficient%0A-%20Project%20contact%20details%0ADecision%20time%3A%20A%20few%20days%20to%20a%20week%20for%20first%20response%0AObligations%3A%20Comply%20with%20the%20OSUOSL%20Hosting%20Policy%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20acknowledgement%2Fsponsor%20credit%20expectations%29.%20OSUOSL%20is%20donation-funded%20and%20asks%20hosted%20projects%2Fcompanies%20to%20consider%20supporting%20it.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Request%20VMs%20and%20FTP-style%20mirroring%20capacity%20to%20run%20an%20independent%2C%20university-hosted%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20node%20and%20CI%20runners%20for%20the%20MIT-licensed%20protocol%2C%20adding%20a%20non-commercial%2C%20non-Vultr%20host%20to%20the%20mirror%20fleet.%20OSUOSL%20already%20mirrors%20major%20FOSS%20projects%2C%20so%20a%20signed%20Git%20mirror%20network%20that%20preserves%20source%20code%20aligns%20with%20its%20mission.%0ARisks%3A%20OSUOSL%20had%20a%202025%20funding%20crisis%20%28one-year%20emergency%20lifeline%29%2C%20so%20long-term%20availability%20is%20uncertain.%20ForkMesh%20must%20show%20community%20impact%3B%20an%20early%20commercial%20project%20may%20be%20seen%20as%20not%20yet%20outgrowing%20free%20hosting.)** · [Program page](https://osuosl.org/services/hosting/) · [Apply](https://osuosl.org/services/hosting/)

- **Funder:** Oregon State University Open Source Lab (OSUOSL)
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** In-kind hosting (no stated dollar value): VMs on OpenStack/Ganeti, colocation, CI/CD runners, FTP mirroring, S3-compatible object storage, DNS, databases, hosted GitLab/Discourse/Matrix; unconfirmed: whether any fee applies (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source projects under an OSI-approved license with an open development model, a significant positive impact on the open source community, an active development or user community, and needs that have outgrown free hosting services.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (hosting located in Oregon, USA) · **Entity types:** Open source projects (unconfirmed: whether a for-profit company's project qualifies; project-based rather than entity-based)
- **Decision time:** A few days to a week for first response
- **Obligations:** Comply with the OSUOSL Hosting Policy (unconfirmed: acknowledgement/sponsor credit expectations). OSUOSL is donation-funded and asks hosted projects/companies to consider supporting it.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Request VMs and FTP-style mirroring capacity to run an independent, university-hosted ForkMesh mirror node and CI runners for the MIT-licensed protocol, adding a non-commercial, non-Vultr host to the mirror fleet. OSUOSL already mirrors major FOSS projects, so a signed Git mirror network that preserves source code aligns with its mission.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the OSUOSL Hosting Policy to confirm eligibility.
2. Complete the hosting request form, selecting each needed service (VMs, mirroring, CI runners, object storage, etc.) and answering its questions.
3. OSUOSL reviews and replies within a few days to a week, often with follow-up questions.
4. After approval, OSUOSL provisions the services and sends access details.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project name, description and URL
- OSI-approved license (MIT qualifies)
- Evidence of open development model and active community
- Per-service answers on the hosting request form (resource sizes for VMs, mirroring needs, CI runner needs, storage)
- Explanation of why free hosting services are insufficient
- Project contact details

**Risks:** OSUOSL had a 2025 funding crisis (one-year emergency lifeline), so long-term availability is uncertain. ForkMesh must show community impact; an early commercial project may be seen as not yet outgrowing free hosting.

**Sources:** <https://osuosl.org/services/hosting/>, <https://www.thedroptimes.com/node/48647>, <https://itsfoss.com/osu-open-source-lab-future/>

entralised%20ecosystems%29%20line.%20unconfirmed%3A%20past%20rounds%20required%20applicants%20to%20be%20resident%2Ftax-liable%20in%20Germany%20and%20funded%20natural%20persons%2C%20not%20companies.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20Germany%20%28residence%20or%20tax%20liability%20in%20Germany%20required%20in%20past%20rounds%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20and%20small%20teams%20%28max%204%20people%29%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20companies%20and%20organisations%20cannot%20be%20the%20grantee.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.prototypefund.de%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.prototypefund.de%2Fbewerbung%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Optional%20info%20sessions%20%2819%20Oct%202026%20general%2C%2022%20Oct%202026%20FLINTA%2A%29.%202.%20Submit%20the%20application%20in%20German%20or%20English%20via%20the%20application%20platform%20%28bewerben.prototypefund.de%20%2F%20prototypefund.de%2Fbewerbung%29%20by%202026-11-30%2015%3A00%20CET.%203.%20Jury%20review%20and%20selection.%204.%20Funding%20starts%202027-06-01%3A%206-month%20base%20phase%20with%20coaching%20and%20networking.%205.%20Optional%204-month%20Second%20Stage%20application.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20application%20in%20German%20or%20English%20via%20the%20application%20platform%0A-%20Choice%20of%20funding%20line%20%28Innovation%2C%20Up%20and%20Coming%2C%20Resilience%29%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20project%20idea%20and%20problem%20statement%2C%20target%20group%2C%20prior%20work%2C%20planned%20milestones%20for%206%20months%2C%20team%20members%20and%20roles%2C%20time%20commitment%20and%20budget%20per%20person%20%28per-field%20limits%20not%20confirmed%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20about%206%20months%20%28deadline%202026-11-30%2C%20funding%20start%202027-06-01%29%0AObligations%3A%20Open%20source%20licence%20for%20results%3B%20participation%20in%20coaching%20and%20networking%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20interim%20and%20final%20reporting%2C%20demo%20day%2C%20and%20the%20grant%20is%20personal%20funding%20for%20the%20team%20members%27%20work.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Apply%20in%20the%20Resilience%20line%20with%20ForkMesh%27s%20decentralised%20Git%20collaboration%20%28signed%20in-repo%20issues%20and%20PRs%20plus%20live%20signed%20mirrors%29%20as%20an%20open%20source%20alternative%20to%20centralised%20Big%20Tech%20code%20hosting.%20The%20line%20was%20created%20for%20exactly%20this%20kind%20of%20de-centralising%20alternative.%0ARisks%3A%20Official%20prototypefund.de%20pages%20returned%20403%20%2F%20did%20not%20resolve%2C%20so%20details%20come%20from%20the%20OSB%20Alliance%20news%20item%20and%20third-party%20listings%3B%20amounts%20conflict%20between%20sources.%20Funds%20individuals%2C%20likely%20only%20those%20living%20or%20taxed%20in%20Germany%3B%20a%20ForkMesh%20company%20cannot%20be%20the%20grantee%2C%20so%20team%20members%20would%20need%20to%20qualify%20personally.%20Prototype%20stage%20focus%3B%20long%20gap%20to%20funding%20start%20%28June%202027%29.)** · [Program page](https://www.prototypefund.de) · [Apply](https://www.prototypefund.de/bewerbung)

- **Funder:** Open Knowledge Foundation Deutschland (funded by the German Federal Ministry of Research)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** bis zu 79.000 EUR fuer Einzelentwickler*innen und bis zu 158.000 EUR fuer Teams (press release); unconfirmed: other listings state up to EUR 47,500 per person / EUR 95,000 per team for the 6-month base phase, and EUR 158,333 per team for 10 months including the Second Stage (about $184,000)
- **Deadline:** 2026-11-30 (open)
- **Fit score:** 45/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Individual developers or teams of up to four adults building software (not hardware) under an open source licence, in the Innovation (data security and software infrastructure), Up and Coming (early-career) or Resilience (open source alternatives to Big Tech centralised ecosystems) line. unconfirmed: past rounds required applicants to be resident/tax-liable in Germany and funded natural persons, not companies.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: Germany (residence or tax liability in Germany required in past rounds) · **Entity types:** Individuals and small teams (max 4 people); unconfirmed: companies and organisations cannot be the grantee
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: about 6 months (deadline 2026-11-30, funding start 2027-06-01)
- **Obligations:** Open source licence for results; participation in coaching and networking; unconfirmed: interim and final reporting, demo day, and the grant is personal funding for the team members' work.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Apply in the Resilience line with ForkMesh's decentralised Git collaboration (signed in-repo issues and PRs plus live signed mirrors) as an open source alternative to centralised Big Tech code hosting. The line was created for exactly this kind of de-centralising alternative.

**How to apply:**

1. Optional info sessions (19 Oct 2026 general, 22 Oct 2026 FLINTA*).
2. Submit the application in German or English via the application platform (bewerben.prototypefund.de / prototypefund.de/bewerbung) by 2026-11-30 15:00 CET.
3. Jury review and selection.
4. Funding starts 2027-06-01: 6-month base phase with coaching and networking.
5. Optional 4-month Second Stage application.

**What the application asks for:**

- Online application in German or English via the application platform
- Choice of funding line (Innovation, Up and Coming, Resilience)
- unconfirmed: project idea and problem statement, target group, prior work, planned milestones for 6 months, team members and roles, time commitment and budget per person (per-field limits not confirmed)

**Risks:** Official prototypefund.de pages returned 403 / did not resolve, so details come from the OSB Alliance news item and third-party listings; amounts conflict between sources. Funds individuals, likely only those living or taxed in Germany; a ForkMesh company cannot be the grantee, so team members would need to qualify personally. Prototype stage focus; long gap to funding start (June 2027).

**Sources:** <https://osb-alliance.de/news/prototype-fund-bewerbung-fuer-drei-open-source-foerderlinien-gestartet>, <https://www.starthub-hessen.de/de/services/navigator/prototype-fund-bewerbung-ab-01-oktober-2026-moglich/>, <https://foerderdatenbank.d-s-e-e.de/foerderprogramme/prototype-fund>
