phy%3A%20Worldwide%20%28subject%20to%20export%20control%20and%20security%20regulations%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%2C%20universities%2C%20non-profits%2C%20individuals%20as%20part%20of%20teams%3B%20FFRDC%2FUARC%20with%20extra%20documentation.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsimpler.grants.gov%2Fopportunity%2F4ec76651-17d7-47fd-ac5e-377e8673b3a4%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsimpler.grants.gov%2Fopportunity%2F4ec76651-17d7-47fd-ac5e-377e8673b3a4%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Optionally%20email%20the%20BAA%20coordinator%20to%20test%20interest.%202.%20Submit%20an%20abstract%20on%20the%20required%20IPTO%20Office%20Wide%20Abstract%20Template%20through%20the%20DARPA%20BAA%20Tool%20%28BAAT%29%20by%2022%20June%202027%2C%202%3A00%20PM%20ET.%203.%20Two-step%20review%3A%20technical%20conformance%2C%20then%20technical%20comprehension%2C%20ability%20and%20cost.%204.%20If%20encouraged%2C%20get%20an%20Invitation%20to%20Proceed%20with%20a%20proposal%20due%20date.%205.%20Submit%20the%20full%20proposal.%206.%20Award%20negotiation.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Abstract%20on%20the%20mandatory%20IPTO%20Office%20Wide%20Abstract%20Template%20%28content%20and%20format%20rules%20in%20the%20template%29%0A-%20Full%20proposal%20%28by%20invitation%20only%29%3A%20IPTO%20OW%20Volume%20I%20%28Technical%20and%20Management%29%0A-%20IPTO%20OW%20Volume%20II%20%28Cost%29%3B%20DARPA%20Standard%20Cost%20Spreadsheet%20strongly%20encouraged%0A-%20IPTO%20OW%20Proposal%20Summary%20Slide%0A-%20Redlined%20model%20contract%2C%20cooperative%20agreement%20or%20OT%20agreement%20%28encouraged%29%0A-%20Common%20Disclosure%20Forms%20for%20fundamental-research%20risk%20review%20when%20requesting%20a%20grant%2C%20cooperative%20agreement%20or%20OT%0A-%20Security%20plan%20if%20classified%20work%20is%20anticipated%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20abstract%20feedback%20letters%20are%20sent%20but%20no%20timeline%20is%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20Terms%20depend%20on%20the%20instrument.%20Cost%20sharing%20may%20be%20required%20for%20OTs%20%2810%20U.S.C.%204022%29.%20Foreign-influence%20risk%20review%20for%20fundamental%20research.%20Evaluation%20includes%20%27Plans%20to%20Implement%20Resilient%20Software%27%2C%20with%20formal%20methods%20preferred.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20an%20Inherent%20Security%20and%20Privacy%20or%20Resilient%20Megasystems%20abstract%3A%20a%20decentralised%2C%20cryptographically%20verified%20software%20distribution%20layer%20that%20keeps%20source%20and%20build%20inputs%20available%20and%20tamper-evident%20under%20host%20loss%20or%20attack%2C%20treating%20the%20open-source%20supply%20chain%20as%20a%20resilient%20megasystem%20to%20model%20and%20protect.%20DARPA%20wants%20radical%2C%20not%20incremental%2C%20approaches%20and%20favours%20formal%20verification.%0ARisks%3A%20The%20BAA%20explicitly%20excludes%20evolutionary%20improvements%2C%20and%20generic%20or%20boilerplate%20abstracts%20are%20rejected.%20It%20has%20a%20national-security%20framing%20and%20prefers%20formal%20methods.%20No%20award%20size%20is%20stated.%20The%20former%20I2O%20office-wide%20BAA%20%28HR001126S0001%29%20closed%20on%2024%20June%202026%20and%20points%20to%20this%20IPTO%20BAA.)** · [Program page](https://simpler.grants.gov/opportunity/4ec76651-17d7-47fd-ac5e-377e8673b3a4) · [Apply](https://simpler.grants.gov/opportunity/4ec76651-17d7-47fd-ac5e-377e8673b3a4)

- **Funder:** U.S. Defense Advanced Research Projects Agency (DARPA), Information Processing Techniques Office (IPTO)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Not stated; multiple awards anticipated as procurement contracts, grants, cooperative agreements, or OT for Prototype / OT for Research agreements (about varies)
- **Deadline:** 2027-06-22 (open)
- **Fit score:** 42/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** All responsible sources, U.S. and non-U.S. Non-U.S. organisations and individuals must comply with NDAs, security rules and export controls. FFRDCs, UARCs and government entities must document their eligibility at the abstract stage. Proposers cannot propose work that is already done or already funded.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (subject to export control and security regulations) · **Entity types:** For-profit, universities, non-profits, individuals as part of teams; FFRDC/UARC with extra documentation
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: abstract feedback letters are sent but no timeline is stated
- **Obligations:** Terms depend on the instrument. Cost sharing may be required for OTs (10 U.S.C. 4022). Foreign-influence risk review for fundamental research. Evaluation includes 'Plans to Implement Resilient Software', with formal methods preferred.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch an Inherent Security and Privacy or Resilient Megasystems abstract: a decentralised, cryptographically verified software distribution layer that keeps source and build inputs available and tamper-evident under host loss or attack, treating the open-source supply chain as a resilient megasystem to model and protect. DARPA wants radical, not incremental, approaches and favours formal verification.

**How to apply:**

1. Optionally email the BAA coordinator to test interest.
2. Submit an abstract on the required IPTO Office Wide Abstract Template through the DARPA BAA Tool (BAAT) by 22 June 2027, 2:00 PM ET.
3. Two-step review: technical conformance, then technical comprehension, ability and cost.
4. If encouraged, get an Invitation to Proceed with a proposal due date.
5. Submit the full proposal.
6. Award negotiation.

**What the application asks for:**

- Abstract on the mandatory IPTO Office Wide Abstract Template (content and format rules in the template)
- Full proposal (by invitation only): IPTO OW Volume I (Technical and Management)
- IPTO OW Volume II (Cost); DARPA Standard Cost Spreadsheet strongly encouraged
- IPTO OW Proposal Summary Slide
- Redlined model contract, cooperative agreement or OT agreement (encouraged)
- Common Disclosure Forms for fundamental-research risk review when requesting a grant, cooperative agreement or OT
- Security plan if classified work is anticipated

**Risks:** The BAA explicitly excludes evolutionary improvements, and generic or boilerplate abstracts are rejected. It has a national-security framing and prefers formal methods. No award size is stated. The former I2O office-wide BAA (HR001126S0001) closed on 24 June 2026 and points to this IPTO BAA.

**Sources:** <https://simpler.grants.gov/opportunity/4ec76651-17d7-47fd-ac5e-377e8673b3a4>, <https://simpler.grants.gov/opportunity/45c68802-ecf4-463f-a244-26d6a7f2974d>, <https://research-authority.tau.ac.il/sites/resauth.tau.ac.il/files/DARPA%20IPTO%202026%20HR001126S0011.pdf>, <https://www.bwcoconsulting.com/fod/darpa-ipto-office-wide-baa-hr001126s0011>

## 40. Golem Foundation: Octant - recurring public goods funding epochs

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Golem%20Foundation%3A%20Octant%20-%20recurring%20public%20goods%20funding%20epochs.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Foctant.build%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fdocs.octant.app%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Golem%20Foundation%3A%20Octant%20-%20recurring%20public%20goods%20funding%20epochs%0AAward%3A%20Epoch%2013%20%28allocation%20window%2014-21%20October%202026%29%3A%20100%20ETH%20matching%20pool%20funded%20by%20the%20Golem%20Foundation%2C%20distributed%20by%20proper%20quadratic%20funding%20%28pQF%29%20on%20top%20of%20GLM-locker%20reward%20allocations%20and%20ETH%20contributions%3B%20per-project%20amount%20depends%20on%20community%20allocations%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20epoch%20unconfirmed%3A%20epochs%20have%20run%20on%20a%20~90-day%20cycle%2C%20so%20the%20next%20allocation%20window%20would%20be%20about%20January%202027%3B%20application%20window%20not%20announced%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Public%20goods%20projects%20selected%20for%20each%20epoch%27s%20cohort%20from%20a%20competitive%20application%20pool%20after%20multiple%20review%20rounds%2C%20prioritising%20innovation%2C%20strategic%20importance%20and%20traction.%20Epoch%2013%20was%20themed%20on%20privacy%20with%20two%20tracks%3A%20Ethereum%20and%20Open%20Internet.%20unconfirmed%3A%20formal%20eligibility%20criteria%20and%20entity%20requirements%20are%20not%20published%20on%20the%20pages%20fetched.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20worldwide%20%28no%20restriction%20stated%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20open-source%20projects%2C%20nonprofits%20and%20teams%20%28not%20stated%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Foctant.build%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdocs.octant.app%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Watch%20Octant%20channels%20for%20the%20next%20epoch%27s%20call%20and%20theme.%202.%20Apply%20%2F%20onboard%20the%20project%20%28Octant%20v2%20docs%20reference%20project%20onboarding%29%20during%20the%20application%20window.%203.%20Octant%20team%20vets%20applicants%20over%20multiple%20review%20rounds%20and%20selects%20the%20cohort.%204.%20During%20the%20allocation%20window%2C%20GLM%20lockers%20allocate%20their%20rewards%20and%20ETH%20holders%20contribute%3B%20matching%20is%20computed%20by%20pQF.%205.%20Claim%20allocated%20ETH%20after%20the%20epoch.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20project%20application%2Fonboarding%20profile%20%28description%2C%20team%2C%20links%2C%20payout%20address%29%20via%20the%20Octant%20onboarding%20flow%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20traction%20and%20public-goods%20impact%20%28selection%20criteria%29%0A-%20ETH%20payout%20address%0A-%20Community%20outreach%20material%20to%20attract%20allocations%20during%20the%20window%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20cohort%20selection%20precedes%20each%20epoch%27s%20allocation%20window%3B%20funds%20follow%20the%20window%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20none%20formally%20stated%20beyond%20public-goods%20profile%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Enter%20ForkMesh%20in%20an%20Open%20Internet%20track%20as%20a%20public%20good%3A%20signed%2C%20decentralised%20Git%20mirroring%20keeps%20open-source%20code%20%28including%20privacy%20tooling%29%20available%20when%20a%20central%20forge%20censors%20or%20loses%20it%2C%20and%20in-repo%20signed%20issues%2FPRs%20make%20collaboration%20data%20portable.%20Octant%27s%20recent%20theme%20%28privacy%20for%20Ethereum%20and%20the%20open%20internet%29%20and%20its%20preference%20for%20traction%20mean%20the%20pitch%20should%20lead%20with%20live%20mirror-fleet%20usage.%0ARisks%3A%20Gitcoin%20has%20scheduled%20no%20GG25%20round%20%28its%20October%202026%20reboot%20folds%20quadratic%20funding%20into%20a%20local-money%20pilot%29%2C%20so%20Octant%20is%20the%20main%20recurring%20public-goods%20round.%20Selection%20is%20competitive%2C%20and%20Epoch%2013%20had%20a%20narrow%20theme%20with%20only%20about%2015%20projects.%20A%20Golem%20Foundation%20post%20of%2031%20July%202026%20signals%20the%20model%20may%20change%2C%20so%20future%20epochs%20are%20not%20guaranteed.%20Payout%20depends%20on%20community%20allocations%2C%20so%20amounts%20are%20unpredictable%20and%20modest.)** · [Program page](https://octant.build/) · [Apply](https://docs.octant.app/)

- **Funder:** Golem Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Epoch 13 (allocation window 14-21 October 2026): 100 ETH matching pool funded by the Golem Foundation, distributed by proper quadratic funding (pQF) on top of GLM-locker reward allocations and ETH contributions; per-project amount depends on community allocations (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Next epoch unconfirmed: epochs have run on a ~90-day cycle, so the next allocation window would be about January 2027; application window not announced (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 42/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Public goods projects selected for each epoch's cohort from a competitive application pool after multiple review rounds, prioritising innovation, strategic importance and traction. Epoch 13 was themed on privacy with two tracks: Ethereum and Open Internet. unconfirmed: formal eligibility criteria and entity requirements are not published on the pages fetched.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: worldwide (no restriction stated) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: open-source projects, nonprofits and teams (not stated)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: cohort selection precedes each epoch's allocation window; funds follow the window
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: none formally stated beyond public-goods profile; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Enter ForkMesh in an Open Internet track as a public good: signed, decentralised Git mirroring keeps open-source code (including privacy tooling) available when a central forge censors or loses it, and in-repo signed issues/PRs make collaboration data portable. Octant's recent theme (privacy for Ethereum and the open internet) and its preference for traction mean the pitch should lead with live mirror-fleet usage.

**How to apply:**

1. Watch Octant channels for the next epoch's call and theme.
2. Apply / onboard the project (Octant v2 docs reference project onboarding) during the application window.
3. Octant team vets applicants over multiple review rounds and selects the cohort.
4. During the allocation window, GLM lockers allocate their rewards and ETH holders contribute; matching is computed by pQF.
5. Claim allocated ETH after the epoch.

**What the application asks for:**

- unconfirmed: project application/onboarding profile (description, team, links, payout address) via the Octant onboarding flow
- Evidence of traction and public-goods impact (selection criteria)
- ETH payout address
- Community outreach material to attract allocations during the window

**Risks:** Gitcoin has scheduled no GG25 round (its October 2026 reboot folds quadratic funding into a local-money pilot), so Octant is the main recurring public-goods round. Selection is competitive, and Epoch 13 had a narrow theme with only about 15 projects. A Golem Foundation post of 31 July 2026 signals the model may change, so future epochs are not guaranteed. Payout depends on community allocations, so amounts are unpredictable and modest.

**Sources:** <https://octant.build/>, <https://octant.substack.com/p/epoch-13-meet-the-13-teams-advancing>, <https://docs.octant.app/>, <https://golem.foundation/blog>

%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Vultr%20Digital%20Start-up%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Up%20to%20USD%20100K%20in%20cloud%20credits%20and%2035%25%20long-term%20discounts%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Startups%20that%20have%20recently%20closed%20Series%20A%2C%20B%2C%20C%2C%20D%20or%20E%20funding%20and%20meet%20Vultr%27s%20%28unpublished%29%20qualification%20criteria.%20No%20Vultr%20open-source%20credit%20program%20could%20be%20found.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Venture-funded%20for-profit%20startups%20%28Series%20A-E%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdiscover.vultr.com%2Fstartup-program%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdiscover.vultr.com%2Fstartup-program%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Complete%20the%20application%20form%20on%20discover.vultr.com%2Fstartup-program.%202.%20Provide%20contacts%20for%20the%20senior-most%20technical%20executive%20and%20the%20senior-most%20cloud%20infrastructure%20leader.%203.%20Share%20the%20last%20six%20months%20of%20cloud%20compute%20invoices.%204.%20Agree%20to%20be%20publicly%20referenceable%20as%20a%20program%20member.%205.%20Vultr%20qualifies%20the%20startup%20and%20sizes%20credits%2Fdiscounts%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20sizing%20based%20on%20submitted%20invoices%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Senior-most%20technical%20executive%20%28CTO%20or%20equivalent%29%20contact%20details%0A-%20Senior-most%20cloud%20infrastructure%20leader%20contact%20details%0A-%20Last%20six%20months%20of%20cloud%20compute%20invoices%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20recent%20Series%20A-E%20funding%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20format%29%0A-%20Agreement%20to%20be%20publicly%20referenceable%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20Must%20agree%20to%20be%20publicly%20referenceable%20as%20a%20program%20member%3B%20long-term%20discounts%20tied%20to%20commitment%20terms%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20commit%20schedule%29%3B%20credit%20duration%20and%20draw-down%20not%20stated.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20ForkMesh%20Next%20already%20runs%20its%20three%20core%20nodes%2C%20runner%20tier%20and%20mirror%20fleet%20on%20Vultr%20%28fmnext-%2A%20instances%29%2C%20so%20credits%20and%20long-term%20discounts%20directly%20cut%20existing%20hosting%20cost%20and%20fund%20more%20mirror%20regions.%20Vultr%20gains%20a%20referenceable%20open-source%20developer-infrastructure%20customer%20built%20on%20its%20independent%20cloud%3B%20worth%20also%20asking%20the%20Vultr%20account%20team%20about%20open-source%20sponsorship%20or%20the%20partner%20program%27s%20Digital%20Start-up%20tier.%0ARisks%3A%20Hard%20eligibility%20gate%3A%20requires%20a%20recently%20closed%20Series%20A-E%20round%2C%20which%20an%20early-stage%20ForkMesh%20likely%20lacks%3B%20kept%20because%20ForkMesh%20already%20runs%20on%20Vultr%20and%20the%20gate%20may%20be%20negotiable%20via%20the%20account%20team.%20No%20Vultr%20open-source%20credits%20program%20found.%20Main%20pages%20%28vultr.com%29%20block%20automated%20fetch%3B%20terms%20partly%20from%20third-party%20listings.)** · [Program page](https://discover.vultr.com/startup-program) · [Apply](https://discover.vultr.com/startup-program)

- **Funder:** Vultr
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Up to USD 100K in cloud credits and 35% long-term discounts (about $100,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 40/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Startups that have recently closed Series A, B, C, D or E funding and meet Vultr's (unpublished) qualification criteria. No Vultr open-source credit program could be found.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed) · **Entity types:** Venture-funded for-profit startups (Series A-E)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** Must agree to be publicly referenceable as a program member; long-term discounts tied to commitment terms (unconfirmed: exact commit schedule); credit duration and draw-down not stated.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** ForkMesh Next already runs its three core nodes, runner tier and mirror fleet on Vultr (fmnext-* instances), so credits and long-term discounts directly cut existing hosting cost and fund more mirror regions. Vultr gains a referenceable open-source developer-infrastructure customer built on its independent cloud; worth also asking the Vultr account team about open-source sponsorship or the partner program's Digital Start-up tier.

**How to apply:**

1. Complete the application form on discover.vultr.com/startup-program.
2. Provide contacts for the senior-most technical executive and the senior-most cloud infrastructure leader.
3. Share the last six months of cloud compute invoices.
4. Agree to be publicly referenceable as a program member.
5. Vultr qualifies the startup and sizes credits/discounts (unconfirmed: sizing based on submitted invoices).

**What the application asks for:**

- Senior-most technical executive (CTO or equivalent) contact details
- Senior-most cloud infrastructure leader contact details
- Last six months of cloud compute invoices
- Evidence of recent Series A-E funding (unconfirmed: format)
- Agreement to be publicly referenceable

**Risks:** Hard eligibility gate: requires a recently closed Series A-E round, which an early-stage ForkMesh likely lacks; kept because ForkMesh already runs on Vultr and the gate may be negotiable via the account team. No Vultr open-source credits program found. Main pages (vultr.com) block automated fetch; terms partly from third-party listings.

**Sources:** <https://discover.vultr.com/startup-program>, <https://betterstack.com/community/comparisons/vultr-vs-cloudflare-startups.md>, <https://grove.builtfirst.com/vultr-vultr-digital-start-up-program>

## 42. Vercel Open Source Program

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Vercel%20Open%20Source%20Program.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fvercel.com%2Fopen-source-program%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fvercel.com%2Fopen-source-program%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Vercel%20Open%20Source%20Program%0AAward%3A%20USD%203%2C600%20in%20Vercel%20platform%20credits%20over%20three%20years%20%28earlier%20Vercel%20material%20said%20over%2012%20months%29%2C%20plus%20an%20OSS%20Starter%20Pack%20of%20third-party%20service%20credits%20and%20priority%20community%20support%0ADeadline%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20applications%20currently%20closed%3B%20Vercel%20runs%20new%20cohorts%20four%20times%20a%20year%20%28Spring%202026%20was%20the%20latest%20listed%29%2C%20so%20the%20next%20window%20is%20expected%20within%20a%20few%20months%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%20that%20are%20actively%20developed%20and%20maintained%2C%20hosted%20on%20%28or%20intending%20to%20host%20on%29%20Vercel%2C%20showing%20measurable%20impact%20or%20growth%20potential%2C%20and%20following%20a%20Code%20of%20Conduct.%20Fully%20open%20source%20nonprofits%2C%20new%20projects%20and%20startups%20with%20open%20source%20projects%20can%20apply%3B%20funded%20open-source%20companies%20are%20directed%20to%20the%20Vercel%20Startups%20Program%20instead.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28no%20restriction%20stated%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%2C%20nonprofits%2C%20unfunded%20startups%20with%20open%20source%20projects.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fvercel.com%2Fopen-source-program%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fvercel.com%2Fopen-source-program%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Watch%20the%20program%20page%20%2F%20Vercel%20Community%20open-source%20category%20for%20the%20next%20quarterly%20cohort%20opening.%202.%20Submit%20the%20application%20when%20open.%203.%20Vercel%20reviews%20on%20impact%2C%20community%20engagement%20and%20adherence%20to%20criteria.%204.%20Selected%20projects%20receive%20credits%20and%20the%20OSS%20Starter%20Pack%3B%20projects%20graduate%20after%20three%20years.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20application%20form%20not%20visible%20while%20closed%0A-%20Public%20open%20source%20repository%20with%20active%20development%0A-%20Code%20of%20Conduct%20in%20the%20project%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20impact%20or%20growth%20potential%20%28users%2C%20stars%2C%20contributors%29%0A-%20Vercel%20team%2Faccount%20hosting%20or%20intending%20to%20host%20the%20project%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20per-cohort%2C%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20Credits%20used%20only%20for%20open%20source%20work%20and%20the%20project%20itself%3B%20follow%20a%20Code%20of%20Conduct%3B%20graduation%20after%20three%20years.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Host%20the%20forkmesh.com%20marketing%20site%2C%20documentation%20and%20public%20dashboard%20front-ends%20on%20Vercel%2C%20keeping%20them%20off%20the%20core%20nodes.%20Vercel%20explicitly%20admits%20startups%20with%20open%20source%20projects%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%27s%20MIT-licensed%20decentralized%20Git%20collaboration%20tooling%20is%20the%20kind%20of%20developer-infrastructure%20OSS%20it%20showcases.%0ARisks%3A%20Applications%20closed%20today%3B%20next%20cohort%20date%20not%20published.%20Credits%20only%20cover%20Vercel%20hosting%2C%20and%20only%20matter%20if%20ForkMesh%20moves%20web%20front-ends%20to%20Vercel%20%28the%20served%20stack%20is%20self-hosted%29.%20If%20ForkMesh%20raises%20funding%20it%20would%20be%20redirected%20to%20the%20Startups%20Program.%20Netlify%27s%20Open%20Source%20plan%20was%20checked%20as%20the%20alternative%20and%20rejected%3A%20it%20excludes%20commercial%20projects%20including%20commercial%20hosting%20services.)** · [Program page](https://vercel.com/open-source-program) · [Apply](https://vercel.com/open-source-program)

- **Funder:** Vercel
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** USD 3,600 in Vercel platform credits over three years (earlier Vercel material said over 12 months), plus an OSS Starter Pack of third-party service credits and priority community support (about $3,600)
- **Deadline:** unconfirmed: applications currently closed; Vercel runs new cohorts four times a year (Spring 2026 was the latest listed), so the next window is expected within a few months (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 40/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source projects that are actively developed and maintained, hosted on (or intending to host on) Vercel, showing measurable impact or growth potential, and following a Code of Conduct. Fully open source nonprofits, new projects and startups with open source projects can apply; funded open-source companies are directed to the Vercel Startups Program instead.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (no restriction stated) · **Entity types:** Open source projects, nonprofits, unfunded startups with open source projects
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: per-cohort, not stated
- **Obligations:** Credits used only for open source work and the project itself; follow a Code of Conduct; graduation after three years.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Host the forkmesh.com marketing site, documentation and public dashboard front-ends on Vercel, keeping them off the core nodes. Vercel explicitly admits startups with open source projects, and ForkMesh's MIT-licensed decentralized Git collaboration tooling is the kind of developer-infrastructure OSS it showcases.

**How to apply:**

1. Watch the program page / Vercel Community open-source category for the next quarterly cohort opening.
2. Submit the application when open.
3. Vercel reviews on impact, community engagement and adherence to criteria.
4. Selected projects receive credits and the OSS Starter Pack; projects graduate after three years.

**What the application asks for:**

- unconfirmed: application form not visible while closed
- Public open source repository with active development
- Code of Conduct in the project
- Evidence of impact or growth potential (users, stars, contributors)
- Vercel team/account hosting or intending to host the project

**Risks:** Applications closed today; next cohort date not published. Credits only cover Vercel hosting, and only matter if ForkMesh moves web front-ends to Vercel (the served stack is self-hosted). If ForkMesh raises funding it would be redirected to the Startups Program. Netlify's Open Source plan was checked as the alternative and rejected: it excludes commercial projects including commercial hosting services.

**Sources:** <https://vercel.com/open-source-program>, <https://vercel.com/blog/vercel-open-source-program-spring-2026-cohort>, <https://www.netlify.com/legal/open-source-policy/>

gle%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Akash%20Network%3A%20Akash%20Community%20Pool%20Spend%20Proposal%0AAward%3A%20No%20fixed%20cap%3B%20amount%20requested%20in%20AKT%20and%20approved%20by%20on-chain%20vote%20%28recent%202026%20proposals%20ranged%20roughly%20%24290%2C000%20-%20%24725%2C000%2C%20mostly%20from%20the%20core%20team%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28submit%20an%20on-chain%20governance%20proposal%20any%20time%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Anyone%20may%20draft%20a%20community%20pool%20spend%20proposal%3B%20it%20needs%20a%20governance%20discussion%2C%20an%20on-chain%20deposit%20and%20a%20passing%20vote%20of%20AKT%20stakers%2Fvalidators.%20Proposals%20must%20benefit%20the%20Akash%20ecosystem%20%28e.g.%20workloads%2C%20tooling%2C%20integrations%20driving%20compute%20usage%29.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28permissionless%20on-chain%20governance%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20any%20sanctions%20screening%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Any%20%28individuals%2C%20companies%2C%20nonprofits%29%3B%20payment%20goes%20to%20a%20public%20AKT%20wallet.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Forgs%2Fakash-network%2Fdiscussions%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Forgs%2Fakash-network%2Fdiscussions%2Fcategories%2Fgovernance-proposals%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Draft%20the%20proposal%20as%20a%20GitHub%20Discussion%20in%20the%20akash-network%20%27Governance%20Proposals%27%20category%20and%20gather%20community%2Fvalidator%20feedback%20%28draft%20then%20final%20version%29.%202.%20Present%20in%20Akash%20community%20calls%20%2F%20special%20interest%20groups%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20optional%29.%203.%20Submit%20the%20community-pool-spend%20proposal%20on-chain%20with%20the%20minimum%20deposit%20%28observed%201%2C000%20AKT%3B%20deposit%20period%20about%202%20weeks%29.%204.%20Seven-day%20validator%2Fdelegator%20vote%20%28observed%20quorum%20about%2033.4%25%29.%205.%20If%20passed%2C%20AKT%20is%20disbursed%20to%20the%20stated%20public%20wallet.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20GitHub%20Discussion%20proposal%3A%20Summary%2C%20Motivation%2C%20Total%20Request%2C%20Budget%20Breakdown%2C%20deliverables%2Fprograms%2C%20Conclusion%0A-%20Requested%20amount%20in%20AKT%20with%20USD%20basis%20and%20price%20assumption%20%28volatility%20buffer%20if%20any%29%0A-%20Public%20receiving%20wallet%20address%0A-%20Explanation%20of%20what%20Yes%20%2F%20No%20%2F%20Abstain%20votes%20mean%0A-%20Reporting%20%2F%20market-impact%20plan%20for%20liquidating%20AKT%0A-%20On-chain%20deposit%20%28observed%201%2C000%20AKT%3B%20verify%20current%20chain%20parameter%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%203-5%20weeks%20%28discussion%20period%2C%20~2-week%20deposit%20window%2C%207-day%20vote%29%0AObligations%3A%20Public%20accountability%20to%20the%20Akash%20community%3A%20progress%20reporting%20on%20the%20GitHub%20discussion%2C%20transparent%20use%20and%20liquidation%20of%20AKT%2C%20return%20of%20unused%20buffer%20customary%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20running%20the%20ForkMesh%20runner%20tier%20%28CI-like%20compute%29%20and%20public%20mirror-fleet%20nodes%20on%20Akash%2C%20plus%20a%20one-click%20Akash%20SDL%20deployment%20template%20so%20any%20developer%20can%20launch%20a%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20node%20on%20Akash.%20This%20drives%20sustained%20compute%20leases%20to%20Akash%20providers%20and%20gives%20Akash%20a%20decentralized%20Git%20hosting%20showcase.%0ARisks%3A%20Outcome%20decided%20by%20token-holder%20vote%3B%20most%202026%20pool%20spend%20went%20to%20the%20core%20team%2C%20so%20third-party%20proposals%20face%20scrutiny%3B%20requires%20AKT%20deposit%20%28lost%20if%20vetoed%29%3B%20payment%20in%20volatile%20AKT%3B%20needs%20real%20Akash%20deployment%20commitment.%20Official%20docs%20page%20for%20the%20proposal%20template%20was%20not%20reachable%20%28404%29%3B%20deposit%20and%20quorum%20figures%20come%20from%20third-party%20governance%20mirrors.)** · [Program page](https://github.com/orgs/akash-network/discussions) · [Apply](https://github.com/orgs/akash-network/discussions/categories/governance-proposals)

- **Funder:** Akash Network (on-chain governance, Akash Community Pool)
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** No fixed cap; amount requested in AKT and approved by on-chain vote (recent 2026 proposals ranged roughly $290,000 - $725,000, mostly from the core team) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (submit an on-chain governance proposal any time) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 40/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Anyone may draft a community pool spend proposal; it needs a governance discussion, an on-chain deposit and a passing vote of AKT stakers/validators. Proposals must benefit the Akash ecosystem (e.g. workloads, tooling, integrations driving compute usage).
- **Geography:** Worldwide (permissionless on-chain governance; unconfirmed: any sanctions screening) · **Entity types:** Any (individuals, companies, nonprofits); payment goes to a public AKT wallet
- **Decision time:** About 3-5 weeks (discussion period, ~2-week deposit window, 7-day vote)
- **Obligations:** Public accountability to the Akash community: progress reporting on the GitHub discussion, transparent use and liquidation of AKT, return of unused buffer customary; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose running the ForkMesh runner tier (CI-like compute) and public mirror-fleet nodes on Akash, plus a one-click Akash SDL deployment template so any developer can launch a ForkMesh mirror node on Akash. This drives sustained compute leases to Akash providers and gives Akash a decentralized Git hosting showcase.

**How to apply:**

1. Draft the proposal as a GitHub Discussion in the akash-network 'Governance Proposals' category and gather community/validator feedback (draft then final version).
2. Present in Akash community calls / special interest groups (unconfirmed: optional).
3. Submit the community-pool-spend proposal on-chain with the minimum deposit (observed 1,000 AKT; deposit period about 2 weeks).
4. Seven-day validator/delegator vote (observed quorum about 33.4%).
5. If passed, AKT is disbursed to the stated public wallet.

**What the application asks for:**

- GitHub Discussion proposal: Summary, Motivation, Total Request, Budget Breakdown, deliverables/programs, Conclusion
- Requested amount in AKT with USD basis and price assumption (volatility buffer if any)
- Public receiving wallet address
- Explanation of what Yes / No / Abstain votes mean
- Reporting / market-impact plan for liquidating AKT
- On-chain deposit (observed 1,000 AKT; verify current chain parameter)

**Risks:** Outcome decided by token-holder vote; most 2026 pool spend went to the core team, so third-party proposals face scrutiny; requires AKT deposit (lost if vetoed); payment in volatile AKT; needs real Akash deployment commitment. Official docs page for the proposal template was not reachable (404); deposit and quorum figures come from third-party governance mirrors.

**Sources:** <https://github.com/orgs/akash-network/discussions/1464>, <https://akash.valopers.com/proposals/331>, <https://akash.valopers.com/proposals/270>, <https://akash.network/community/>

## 44. Fastly: Fast Forward

**[Draft this application in s%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Fastly%3A%20Fast%20Forward%0AAward%3A%20Free%20and%20discounted%20Fastly%20services%20%28CDN%2C%20edge%20compute%2C%20security%29%3B%20no%20dollar%20value%20stated%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%2C%20and%20the%20nonprofits%2Ffoundations%20that%20support%20them%2C%20judged%20on%20being%20open%20%28licence%20and%20collaboration%20values%29%2C%20community-centred%2C%20and%20built%20on%20trust%20%28secure%2C%20trustworthy%20processes%29.%20Commercial%20companies%20are%20not%20mentioned%20as%20eligible.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20geographic%20restriction%20stated%20%28worldwide%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%20and%20supporting%20nonprofits%3B%20for-profit%20eligibility%20unconfirmed.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.fastly.com%2Ffast-forward%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.fastly.com%2Ffast-forward%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Fill%20in%20the%20eligibility%20form%20on%20the%20Fast%20Forward%20page.%202.%20Fastly%20reviews%20eligibility%20against%20the%20open%20%2F%20community-centred%20%2F%20built-on-trust%20criteria.%203.%20Fastly%20contacts%20the%20project%20about%20its%20technical%20needs.%204.%20Services%20are%20provisioned%20free%20or%20discounted.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Fast%20Forward%20eligibility%20form%20%28fields%20not%20shown%20on%20the%20public%20page%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20project%20name%2C%20repository%2C%20licence%2C%20contact%2C%20description%20of%20needs%29%0A-%20Description%20of%20technical%20needs%20%28traffic%2C%20CDN%20%2F%20edge%20compute%20requirements%29%20on%20follow-up%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20none%20stated%20on%20the%20page%20%28past%20participants%20typically%20acknowledge%20Fastly%29%3B%20services%20only%2C%20no%20cash%2C%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Ask%20for%20CDN%20and%20edge%20capacity%20to%20serve%20public%20clones%2C%20release%20artifacts%20and%20the%20signed%20mirror%20index%20of%20the%20open%20ForkMesh%20network%2C%20so%20the%20free%20public%20clone%20tier%20scales%20without%20the%20fleet%27s%20own%20bandwidth%20bill.%20Fastly%27s%20program%20exists%20to%20back%20open%20source%20projects%20with%20an%20open-internet%20mission%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%27s%20mirror%20network%20is%20open%20infrastructure.%0ARisks%3A%20In-kind%20services%20only%2C%20value%20unstated.%20Eligibility%20targets%20open%20source%20projects%20and%20nonprofits%3B%20ForkMesh%27s%20paid%20hosted%20tier%20may%20make%20a%20for-profit%20applicant%20ineligible%2C%20so%20apply%20for%20the%20open%20protocol%2Fpublic%20clone%20project.%20Git%20smart-HTTP%20traffic%20may%20not%20cache%20well%20on%20a%20CDN.)** · [Program page](https://www.fastly.com/fast-forward) · [Apply](https://www.fastly.com/fast-forward)

- **Funder:** Fastly
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** Free and discounted Fastly services (CDN, edge compute, security); no dollar value stated (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 40/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source projects, and the nonprofits/foundations that support them, judged on being open (licence and collaboration values), community-centred, and built on trust (secure, trustworthy processes). Commercial companies are not mentioned as eligible.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: no geographic restriction stated (worldwide) · **Entity types:** Open source projects and supporting nonprofits; for-profit eligibility unconfirmed
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: none stated on the page (past participants typically acknowledge Fastly); services only, no cash, no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Ask for CDN and edge capacity to serve public clones, release artifacts and the signed mirror index of the open ForkMesh network, so the free public clone tier scales without the fleet's own bandwidth bill. Fastly's program exists to back open source projects with an open-internet mission, and ForkMesh's mirror network is open infrastructure.

**How to apply:**

1. Fill in the eligibility form on the Fast Forward page.
2. Fastly reviews eligibility against the open / community-centred / built-on-trust criteria.
3. Fastly contacts the project about its technical needs.
4. Services are provisioned free or discounted.

**What the application asks for:**

- Fast Forward eligibility form (fields not shown on the public page; unconfirmed: project name, repository, licence, contact, description of needs)
- Description of technical needs (traffic, CDN / edge compute requirements) on follow-up

**Risks:** In-kind services only, value unstated. Eligibility targets open source projects and nonprofits; ForkMesh's paid hosted tier may make a for-profit applicant ineligible, so apply for the open protocol/public clone project. Git smart-HTTP traffic may not cache well on a CDN.

**Sources:** <https://www.fastly.com/fast-forward>, <https://nlnet.nl/core/>, <https://nlnet.nl/themes/>, <https://ngi.eu/opencalls/>

erated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Open%20Source%20Security%20Foundation%3A%20OpenSSF%20Technical%20Initiative%20project%20lifecycle%20with%20project%20budget%20requests%0AAward%3A%20No%20fixed%20grant.%20Sandbox%3A%20in-kind%20support%20%28sponsor%20guidance%2C%20infrastructure%20on%20request%2C%20events%2C%20newsletter%29.%20Incubating%3A%20can%20request%20budget%20for%20security%20audits%20or%20time-limited%20contracting%20and%20fundraise%20with%20TAC%2FWG%20approval.%20Graduated%3A%20can%20request%20budget%20for%20sustained%20maintainer%20stipends.%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28TAC%20reviews%20proposals%20at%20its%20regular%20meetings%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open%20source%20security%20project%20aligned%20with%20the%20OpenSSF%20mission%20%28novel%20approach%20or%20unmet%20need%29%20with%20at%20least%20one%20maintainer%20%28two%20or%20more%20encouraged%29%20and%20a%20sponsoring%20OpenSSF%20Working%20Group%3B%20repository%20must%20move%20to%20the%20OpenSSF%20GitHub%20organisation%3B%20existing%20code%20needs%20Linux%20Foundation%20IP%2Flicense%20review.%20Code%20typically%20Apache-2.0%20or%20MIT.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open%20source%20projects%20%2F%20maintainers%20from%20any%20organisation%20%28for-profit%20contributors%20are%20common%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fossf%2Ftac%2Fblob%2Fmain%2Fprocess%2Fproject-lifecycle.md%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fossf%2Ftac%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Engage%20a%20relevant%20Working%20Group%20%28e.g.%20Securing%20Software%20Repositories%20WG%20or%20Supply%20Chain%20Integrity%20WG%29%20and%20find%20a%20WG%20sponsor.%202.%20Open%20an%20issue%20in%20ossf%2Ftac%20for%20LF%20IP%20and%20license%20review%20of%20the%20existing%20code.%203.%20Open%20a%20PR%20adding%20a%20project%20lifecycle%20document%20from%20the%20stage%20template%20and%20update%20the%20README%20project%20table.%204.%20TAC%20reviews%20and%20votes%3B%20project%20exists%20when%20the%20PR%20merges.%205.%20Move%20the%20repository%20under%20the%20OpenSSF%20GitHub%20organisation%3B%20later%20apply%20for%20Incubating%20%283%2B%20maintainers%20from%202%2B%20orgs%2C%20Silver%20Best%20Practices%20badge%2C%20governance%20docs%2C%20technical%20due%20diligence%2C%20two-week%20public%20comment%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20lifecycle%20document%20from%20the%20OpenSSF%20stage%20template%20%28PR%20to%20ossf%2Ftac%29%0A-%20Sponsoring%20Working%20Group%20and%20sponsor%20name%0A-%20IP%20and%20license%20review%20issue%20for%20existing%20code%0A-%20Maintainer%20list%0A-%20OpenSSF%20Best%20Practices%20badge%20%28Passing%20for%20Sandbox%3B%20Silver%20for%20Incubating%29%0A-%20For%20Incubating%3A%20contributor%20guide%2C%20governance%20document%2C%20adoption%20evidence%2C%20vulnerability%20reporting%20contact%2C%20Security%20Baseline%20requirements%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20weeks%2C%20depending%20on%20WG%20sponsorship%20and%20TAC%20meeting%20cadence%0AObligations%3A%20Repository%20hosted%20in%20the%20OpenSSF%20GitHub%20org%3B%20OpenSSF%20Code%20of%20Conduct%3B%20OpenSSF%20Best%20Practices%20criteria%3B%20charter%20licensing%20%28Apache-2.0%2FMIT%20code%2C%20CC%20BY%204.0%20docs%29%3B%20bi-annual%20TAC%20updates%20at%20Incubating%3B%20neutral%20multi-organisation%20governance%20as%20the%20project%20matures.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Contribute%20a%20separable%2C%20vendor-neutral%20component%20rather%20than%20the%20whole%20product%3A%20e.g.%20the%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20Git%20mirror%20verification%20spec%20and%20client%20%28verifying%20that%20a%20clone%20from%20any%20mirror%20matches%20the%20signed%20source%20state%29%2C%20which%20fits%20the%20Securing%20Software%20Repositories%20and%20supply-chain%20integrity%20work.%20Incubating%20status%20unlocks%20OpenSSF%20budget%20for%20a%20security%20audit%20and%20visibility%20with%20package-registry%20and%20repository%20operators.%0ARisks%3A%20Not%20a%20cash%20grant%20at%20entry%3B%20money%20only%20via%20budget%20requests%20at%20Incubating%2FGraduated.%20Requires%20transferring%20the%20contributed%20component%20to%20the%20OpenSSF%20GitHub%20org%20and%20neutral%20governance%2C%20which%20conflicts%20with%20keeping%20ForkMesh%27s%20core%20under%20the%20startup%27s%20control.%20Incubating%20needs%20maintainers%20from%20two%20or%20more%20organisations.%20Alpha-Omega%20%28OpenSSF%27s%20grant%20arm%29%20is%20covered%20in%20another%20batch.)** · [Program page](https://github.com/ossf/tac/blob/main/process/project-lifecycle.md) · [Apply](https://github.com/ossf/tac)

- **Funder:** Open Source Security Foundation (OpenSSF, a Linux Foundation project)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** No fixed grant. Sandbox: in-kind support (sponsor guidance, infrastructure on request, events, newsletter). Incubating: can request budget for security audits or time-limited contracting and fundraise with TAC/WG approval. Graduated: can request budget for sustained maintainer stipends. (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (TAC reviews proposals at its regular meetings) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 40/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source security project aligned with the OpenSSF mission (novel approach or unmet need) with at least one maintainer (two or more encouraged) and a sponsoring OpenSSF Working Group; repository must move to the OpenSSF GitHub organisation; existing code needs Linux Foundation IP/license review. Code typically Apache-2.0 or MIT.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Open source projects / maintainers from any organisation (for-profit contributors are common)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: weeks, depending on WG sponsorship and TAC meeting cadence
- **Obligations:** Repository hosted in the OpenSSF GitHub org; OpenSSF Code of Conduct; OpenSSF Best Practices criteria; charter licensing (Apache-2.0/MIT code, CC BY 4.0 docs); bi-annual TAC updates at Incubating; neutral multi-organisation governance as the project matures.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Contribute a separable, vendor-neutral component rather than the whole product: e.g. the signed, integrity-checked Git mirror verification spec and client (verifying that a clone from any mirror matches the signed source state), which fits the Securing Software Repositories and supply-chain integrity work. Incubating status unlocks OpenSSF budget for a security audit and visibility with package-registry and repository operators.

**How to apply:**

1. Engage a relevant Working Group (e.g. Securing Software Repositories WG or Supply Chain Integrity WG) and find a WG sponsor.
2. Open an issue in ossf/tac for LF IP and license review of the existing code.
3. Open a PR adding a project lifecycle document from the stage template and update the README project table.
4. TAC reviews and votes; project exists when the PR merges.
5. Move the repository under the OpenSSF GitHub organisation; later apply for Incubating (3+ maintainers from 2+ orgs, Silver Best Practices badge, governance docs, technical due diligence, two-week public comment).

**What the application asks for:**

- Project lifecycle document from the OpenSSF stage template (PR to ossf/tac)
- Sponsoring Working Group and sponsor name
- IP and license review issue for existing code
- Maintainer list
- OpenSSF Best Practices badge (Passing for Sandbox; Silver for Incubating)
- For Incubating: contributor guide, governance document, adoption evidence, vulnerability reporting contact, Security Baseline requirements

**Risks:** Not a cash grant at entry; money only via budget requests at Incubating/Graduated. Requires transferring the contributed component to the OpenSSF GitHub org and neutral governance, which conflicts with keeping ForkMesh's core under the startup's control. Incubating needs maintainers from two or more organisations. Alpha-Omega (OpenSSF's grant arm) is covered in another batch.

**Sources:** <https://github.com/ossf/tac/blob/main/process/project-lifecycle.md>, <https://github.com/ossf/tac/blob/main/process/Technical_Initiative_Lifecycle.md>

0of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20OpenAI%3A%20Cybersecurity%20Grant%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Grants%20in%20increments%20of%20USD%2010%2C000%20as%20API%20credits%2C%20direct%20funding%20and%2For%20equivalents%3B%20USD%2010M%20in%20API%20credits%20committed%20in%20Feb%202026%20alongside%20Trusted%20Access%20for%20Cyber%20%28per%20secondary%20reporting%29%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20per-grant%20maximum%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Defensive%20cybersecurity%20projects%20using%20AI%3B%20recent%20emphasis%20%28Feb%202026%20update%29%20on%20teams%20with%20a%20record%20of%20finding%20and%20fixing%20vulnerabilities%20in%20open-source%20software%20and%20critical%20infrastructure.%20unconfirmed%3A%20entity%20restrictions%20%28program%20has%20funded%20companies%2C%20researchers%20and%20nonprofits%29.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20Worldwide%20%28subject%20to%20OpenAI%20service%20availability%20and%20sanctions%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20for-profit%2C%20nonprofit%2C%20academic%2C%20individuals.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopenai.com%2Findex%2Fopenai-cybersecurity-grant-program%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopenai.com%2Fform%2Fcybersecurity-grant-program%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20the%20online%20application%20form%20%28choose%20full%20research%20grant%20or%20API%20credits%29.%202.%20Rolling%20review%20by%20OpenAI.%203.%20Shortlisted%20teams%20contacted%3B%20recent%20recipients%20also%20pass%20through%20Trusted%20Access%20for%20Cyber%20identity%20verification.%204.%20Grant%20or%20credit%20award.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20form%3A%20project%20proposal%20and%20defensive%20security%20goal%0A-%20Choice%20between%20research%20grant%20or%20API%20credits%20only%0A-%20Team%20background%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20plan%20for%20open%20release%20of%20results%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20published%3B%20program%20reports%20%3E1%2C000%20applications%20reviewed%20and%20few%20funded%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20progress%20reporting%3B%20OpenAI%20has%20favoured%20projects%20whose%20results%20are%20shared%20publicly.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20AI%20agents%20%28already%20run%20by%20the%20ForkMesh%20desktop%20node%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%29%20that%20triage%20and%20patch%20vulnerabilities%20across%20mirrored%20repositories%20and%20verify%20mirror%20integrity%2C%20i.e.%20AI-driven%20defense%20for%20the%20open-source%20supply%20chain.%20Credits%20would%20cover%20agent%20compute%20for%20the%20mirror%20fleet.%0ARisks%3A%20Official%20page%20could%20not%20be%20fetched%20today%20%28HTTP%20403%29%2C%20so%20status%20is%20based%20on%20search%20snippets%3A%20program%20described%20as%20%27evolving%27%20toward%20large-scale%20deployment%20of%20OpenAI%20models%2C%20and%20recent%20awards%20look%20invite%2Frelationship%20driven.%20Very%20low%20acceptance%20rate.%20Heavy%20dependence%20on%20OpenAI%20APIs.)** · [Program page](https://openai.com/index/openai-cybersecurity-grant-program/) · [Apply](https://openai.com/form/cybersecurity-grant-program/)

- **Funder:** OpenAI
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** Grants in increments of USD 10,000 as API credits, direct funding and/or equivalents; USD 10M in API credits committed in Feb 2026 alongside Trusted Access for Cyber (per secondary reporting); unconfirmed: per-grant maximum (about $10,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 40/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Defensive cybersecurity projects using AI; recent emphasis (Feb 2026 update) on teams with a record of finding and fixing vulnerabilities in open-source software and critical infrastructure. unconfirmed: entity restrictions (program has funded companies, researchers and nonprofits).
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: Worldwide (subject to OpenAI service availability and sanctions) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: for-profit, nonprofit, academic, individuals
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not published; program reports >1,000 applications reviewed and few funded
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: progress reporting; OpenAI has favoured projects whose results are shared publicly.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose AI agents (already run by the ForkMesh desktop node in isolated worktrees) that triage and patch vulnerabilities across mirrored repositories and verify mirror integrity, i.e. AI-driven defense for the open-source supply chain. Credits would cover agent compute for the mirror fleet.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit the online application form (choose full research grant or API credits).
2. Rolling review by OpenAI.
3. Shortlisted teams contacted; recent recipients also pass through Trusted Access for Cyber identity verification.
4. Grant or credit award.

**What the application asks for:**

- Online form: project proposal and defensive security goal
- Choice between research grant or API credits only
- Team background
- unconfirmed: plan for open release of results

**Risks:** Official page could not be fetched today (HTTP 403), so status is based on search snippets: program described as 'evolving' toward large-scale deployment of OpenAI models, and recent awards look invite/relationship driven. Very low acceptance rate. Heavy dependence on OpenAI APIs.

**Sources:** <https://openai.com/index/openai-cybersecurity-grant-program/>, <https://openai.com/index/trusted-access-for-cyber/>, <https://openai.com/index/accelerating-cyber-defense-ecosystem/>, <https://www.securityweek.com/openai-unveils-million-dollar-cybersecurity-grant-program/amp/>

%20start-ups.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.gov.uk%2Fgovernment%2Fnews%2F100-million-competition-to-back-british-ai-companies-to-fix-public-services%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sovereignai.gov.uk%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Watch%20sovereignai.gov.uk%20and%20UK%20tender%20notices%20%28Find%20a%20Tender%20%2F%20Contracts%20Finder%29%20for%20the%20next%20challenge%20competitions.%202.%20Attend%20the%20supplier%20briefing.%203.%20Submit%20a%20proposal%20through%20the%20streamlined%20procurement%20process%20by%20the%20batch%20deadline%20%28documents%20unconfirmed%29.%204.%20Win%20a%20pre-commercial%20R%26D%20contract%20with%20the%20challenge-owning%20department%20%28develop%2C%20test%2C%20pilot%2C%20validate%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20tender%20response%20in%20the%20format%20set%20by%20each%20competition%20notice%0A-%20Evidence%20that%20the%20technology%20is%20at%20demonstrator%20stage%20and%20ready%20for%20real-world%20testing%0A-%20Proposed%20approach%20for%20the%20challenge%20%28e.g.%2C%20evidence-based%2C%20ideally%20automated%20agent%20risk%20management%20for%20CISOs%20covering%20resilience%20and%20cyber%20risk%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20Pre-commercial%20R%26D%20contract%20deliverables%20and%20validation%20with%20the%20department%3B%20the%20supplier%20keeps%20its%20IP%20and%20is%20encouraged%20to%20commercialise%20it.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20If%20the%20NCSC%20agent-security%20challenge%20reruns%2C%20propose%20ForkMesh%27s%20isolated-worktree%20agent%20runner%20with%20signed%2C%20reviewable%20PR%20trails%20as%20an%20open-source%20control%20architecture%20that%20lets%20CISOs%20constrain%2C%20monitor%20and%20audit%20coding%20agents.%20The%20challenge%20text%20prefers%20solutions%20built%20on%20open-source%20frameworks%20for%20wide%20adoption.%0ARisks%3A%20This%20is%20procurement%2C%20not%20a%20grant.%20It%20targets%20British%20AI%20companies.%20The%20first%20batch%20has%20closed%20and%20future%20challenges%20may%20change.%20No%20dedicated%20UKRI%20sovereign%20or%20open-source%20software%20fund%20is%20open%3A%20the%20Research%20Software%20Maintenance%20Fund%20round%202%20closed%20in%20February%202026%20and%20targets%20software%20used%20in%20UK%20research%2C%20and%20the%20AIRR%20open%20access%20call%20%28compute%20only%29%20closed%20in%20July%202026.)** · [Program page](https://www.gov.uk/government/news/100-million-competition-to-back-british-ai-companies-to-fix-public-services) · [Apply](https://www.sovereignai.gov.uk/)

- **Funder:** UK Government Sovereign AI Unit (Department for Science, Innovation and Technology) with the National Cyber Security Centre
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Contracts GBP 250,000 - 10 million, most around GBP 1-3 million; scheme budget up to GBP 100 million to March 2030; upfront payments possible (about $332,500 - $13,300,000)
- **Deadline:** First batch closed 2026-10-01; further challenge competitions expected in later phases (dates unconfirmed) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 38/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** British AI companies, including start-ups, with demonstrator-stage technology. There is no minimum turnover, net asset or cash reserve requirement. The formal UK establishment requirement is unconfirmed.
- **Geography:** UK · **Entity types:** UK AI companies (for-profit), including start-ups
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** Pre-commercial R&D contract deliverables and validation with the department; the supplier keeps its IP and is encouraged to commercialise it.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** If the NCSC agent-security challenge reruns, propose ForkMesh's isolated-worktree agent runner with signed, reviewable PR trails as an open-source control architecture that lets CISOs constrain, monitor and audit coding agents. The challenge text prefers solutions built on open-source frameworks for wide adoption.

**How to apply:**

1. Watch sovereignai.gov.uk and UK tender notices (Find a Tender / Contracts Finder) for the next challenge competitions.
2. Attend the supplier briefing.
3. Submit a proposal through the streamlined procurement process by the batch deadline (documents unconfirmed).
4. Win a pre-commercial R&D contract with the challenge-owning department (develop, test, pilot, validate).

**What the application asks for:**

- unconfirmed: tender response in the format set by each competition notice
- Evidence that the technology is at demonstrator stage and ready for real-world testing
- Proposed approach for the challenge (e.g., evidence-based, ideally automated agent risk management for CISOs covering resilience and cyber risk)

**Risks:** This is procurement, not a grant. It targets British AI companies. The first batch has closed and future challenges may change. No dedicated UKRI sovereign or open-source software fund is open: the Research Software Maintenance Fund round 2 closed in February 2026 and targets software used in UK research, and the AIRR open access call (compute only) closed in July 2026.

**Sources:** <https://www.gov.uk/government/news/100-million-competition-to-back-british-ai-companies-to-fix-public-services>, <https://www.sovereignai.gov.uk/post/making-procurement-work-for-britains-ai-startups>, <https://www.techmarketview.com/news/archive/2026/09/01/ukhotviewsextra-100m-sovereign-ai-rd-scheme-opens-to-uk-startups>, <https://www.ukri.org/opportunity/ssi-research-software-maintenance-fund-round-two/>, <https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/ai-research-resource/ai-open-access-call>

0collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20National%20Research%20Council%20Canada%3A%20NRC%20Industrial%20Research%20Assistance%20Program%2C%20financial%20support%20for%20technology%20innovation%0AAward%3A%20Not%20stated%20on%20the%20NRC%20page%3B%20secondary%20summaries%20cite%20CAD%2050%2C000%20-%201%2C000%2C000%20for%20regular%20IRAP%20projects%20%28unconfirmed%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Incorporated%2C%20for-profit%20businesses%20operating%20in%20Canada%20with%20up%20to%20500%20full-time%20equivalent%20employees%20that%20develop%20and%20commercialise%20technology-driven%20products%20or%20services%20and%20will%20create%20economic%20benefits%20in%20Canada.%20ULCs%2C%20LLCs%2C%20sole%20proprietorships%2C%20partnerships%20and%20cooperatives%20are%20not%20eligible.%20Work%20outside%20Canada%20and%20day-to-day%20operating%20costs%20are%20not%20funded.%20Geography%3A%20Canada%20only.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Canadian%20incorporated%20for-profit%20SMEs%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fnrc.canada.ca%2Fen%2Fsupport-technology-innovation%2Ffinancial-support-technology-innovation%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fnrc.canada.ca%2Fen%2Fsupport-technology-innovation%2Ffinancial-support-technology-innovation%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20A%20senior%20executive%20calls%20the%20NRC%20contact%20centre%20%281-877-994-4727%29%20to%20check%20readiness.%202.%20Meet%20a%20client%20engagement%20advisor%2C%20who%20may%20refer%20you%20on.%203.%20Work%20with%20an%20industrial%20technology%20advisor%20%28ITA%29%2C%20who%20assesses%20vision%2C%20team%20and%20innovation%20potential.%204.%20If%20invited%2C%20submit%20a%20project%20proposal%3B%20an%20invitation%20does%20not%20guarantee%20funding.%205.%20Funding%20agreement.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20CRA%20business%20number%0A-%20Business%20plan%0A-%20Recent%20financial%20statements%0A-%20Business%20ownership%20structure%20details%0A-%20R%C3%A9sum%C3%A9s%20or%20profiles%20of%20the%20management%20and%20technical%20team%0A-%20Project%20proposal%20when%20invited%20by%20the%20ITA%20%28format%20unconfirmed%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20the%20page%20does%20not%20describe%20post-award%20reporting%20or%20claims%20terms%3B%20work%20must%20happen%20in%20Canada.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20If%20ForkMesh%20is%20or%20becomes%20a%20Canadian%20corporation%2C%20an%20ITA%20could%20co-fund%20Canadian%20developer%20salaries%20for%20R%26D%20on%20the%20mirror%20fleet%2C%20signed-record%20protocol%20and%20desktop%20node.%20IRAP%20backs%20technology%20R%26D%20with%20commercial%20potential%20and%20benefits%20to%20Canada.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20a%20Canadian%20corporation%20operating%20in%20Canada.%20Funding%20is%20advisor-led%20and%20discretionary%2C%20with%20no%20published%20amounts.%20Only%20Canadian%20work%20is%20eligible.)** · [Program page](https://nrc.canada.ca/en/support-technology-innovation/financial-support-technology-innovation) · [Apply](https://nrc.canada.ca/en/support-technology-innovation/financial-support-technology-innovation)

- **Funder:** National Research Council Canada
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Not stated on the NRC page; secondary summaries cite CAD 50,000 - 1,000,000 for regular IRAP projects (unconfirmed) (about $730,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 38/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Incorporated, for-profit businesses operating in Canada with up to 500 full-time equivalent employees that develop and commercialise technology-driven products or services and will create economic benefits in Canada. ULCs, LLCs, sole proprietorships, partnerships and cooperatives are not eligible. Work outside Canada and day-to-day operating costs are not funded.
- **Geography:** Canada only · **Entity types:** Canadian incorporated for-profit SMEs only
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: the page does not describe post-award reporting or claims terms; work must happen in Canada.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** If ForkMesh is or becomes a Canadian corporation, an ITA could co-fund Canadian developer salaries for R&D on the mirror fleet, signed-record protocol and desktop node. IRAP backs technology R&D with commercial potential and benefits to Canada.

**How to apply:**

1. A senior executive calls the NRC contact centre (1-877-994-4727) to check readiness.
2. Meet a client engagement advisor, who may refer you on.
3. Work with an industrial technology advisor (ITA), who assesses vision, team and innovation potential.
4. If invited, submit a project proposal; an invitation does not guarantee funding.
5. Funding agreement.

**What the application asks for:**

- CRA business number
- Business plan
- Recent financial statements
- Business ownership structure details
- Résumés or profiles of the management and technical team
- Project proposal when invited by the ITA (format unconfirmed)

**Risks:** Needs a Canadian corporation operating in Canada. Funding is advisor-led and discretionary, with no published amounts. Only Canadian work is eligible.

**Sources:** <https://nrc.canada.ca/en/support-technology-innovation/financial-support-technology-innovation>
