onprofit%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdfinity.org%2Fgrants%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdfinity.submittable.com%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20the%20program%20terms%20and%20the%20Guide%20to%20Writing%20Effective%20Grant%20Milestones.%202.%20Submit%20the%20Developer%20Grant%20Application%20Form%20on%20DFINITY%27s%20Submittable%20portal.%203.%20Grant%20team%20reviews%20%28poor-quality%20applications%20are%20rejected%20immediately%29%3B%20possible%20follow-up%20questions%20or%20call.%204.%20Sign%20the%20grant%20agreement.%205.%20Deliver%20milestones%3B%20ICP%20paid%20on%20milestone%20completion.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20description%20and%20how%20it%20uses%20the%20Internet%20Computer%0A-%20Team%20background%20and%20links%20%28GitHub%2C%20prior%20work%29%0A-%20Requested%20tier%20and%20budget%0A-%20Defined%20milestones%20with%20deliverables%2C%20timelines%20and%20amounts%20%28per%20DFINITY%20milestone-writing%20guide%29%0A-%20Open-source%20repository%20link%20%2F%20licensing%20plan%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20form%20fields%20not%20fetched%29%0A-%20KYC%20%2F%20grant%20agreement%20acceptance%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20typically%20several%20weeks%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone-based%20payout%20in%20ICP%20tokens%20%28no%20up-front%20payment%29%3B%20deliverables%20reviewed%20before%20each%20payment%3B%20terms%20and%20conditions%20agreement%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20an%20Internet%20Computer%20canister%20backend%20that%20acts%20as%20a%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20node%3A%20storing%20signed%20Git%20packs%20and%20ForkMesh%20issue%2FPR%20records%20on-chain%20so%20any%20repo%20can%20have%20a%20tamper-evident%2C%20always-online%20ICP%20replica%2C%20plus%20an%20ICP%20integration%20in%20the%20desktop%20app.%20This%20fits%20DFINITY%27s%20developer%20tooling%20%2F%20open%20internet%20services%20focus%20and%20demonstrates%20ICP%20as%20decentralized%20code%20hosting.%0ARisks%3A%20Could%20not%20confirm%20the%20program%20is%20accepting%20applications%20in%202026%3A%20dfinity.org%20grant%20pages%20returned%20HTTP%20503%20and%20the%20Submittable%20portal%20404%20today%3B%20amounts%20and%20tiers%20conflict%20between%20sources%3B%20payment%20in%20volatile%20ICP%20after%20milestones%3B%20requires%20building%20a%20real%20ICP%20integration%3B%20hiring%20and%20marketing%20costs%20not%20fundable.)** · [Program page](https://dfinity.org/grants) · [Apply](https://dfinity.submittable.com)

- **Funder:** DFINITY Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Tiers of $5,000, $25,000 and $100,000 (one source also lists $10,000; $100,000 generally follows a successful smaller grant, case by case), paid in ICP after milestones (about $5,000 - $100,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (unconfirmed: no 2026 status confirmed; official pages returned errors today) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 38/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Individuals over 18 (or age of majority) or businesses building developer tooling, infrastructure, integrations/APIs, CDKs and agents, or open internet apps on the Internet Computer. Not for token sales/ICOs, marketing, legal, hiring, end-user rewards or mockup-only projects; not venture funding (Beacon Fund handles fundraising).
- **Geography:** Worldwide except sanctioned jurisdictions · **Entity types:** Individuals and businesses (for-profit or nonprofit)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: typically several weeks
- **Obligations:** Milestone-based payout in ICP tokens (no up-front payment); deliverables reviewed before each payment; terms and conditions agreement; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose an Internet Computer canister backend that acts as a ForkMesh mirror node: storing signed Git packs and ForkMesh issue/PR records on-chain so any repo can have a tamper-evident, always-online ICP replica, plus an ICP integration in the desktop app. This fits DFINITY's developer tooling / open internet services focus and demonstrates ICP as decentralized code hosting.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the program terms and the Guide to Writing Effective Grant Milestones.
2. Submit the Developer Grant Application Form on DFINITY's Submittable portal.
3. Grant team reviews (poor-quality applications are rejected immediately); possible follow-up questions or call.
4. Sign the grant agreement.
5. Deliver milestones; ICP paid on milestone completion.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project description and how it uses the Internet Computer
- Team background and links (GitHub, prior work)
- Requested tier and budget
- Defined milestones with deliverables, timelines and amounts (per DFINITY milestone-writing guide)
- Open-source repository link / licensing plan (unconfirmed: exact form fields not fetched)
- KYC / grant agreement acceptance

**Risks:** Could not confirm the program is accepting applications in 2026: dfinity.org grant pages returned HTTP 503 and the Submittable portal 404 today; amounts and tiers conflict between sources; payment in volatile ICP after milestones; requires building a real ICP integration; hiring and marketing costs not fundable.

**Sources:** <https://dfinity.org/grants>, <https://dfinity.org/grants-overview>, <https://dfinity.submittable.com>, <https://support.dfinity.org/hc/en-us/articles/4401932915860-DFINITY-DEVELOPER-GRANT-PROGRAM-TERMS-AND-CONDITIONS>, <https://lgvez-vaaaa-aaaak-quk3q-cai.icp0.io/grants.html>

%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20European%20Cybersecurity%20Competence%20Centre%2C%20Digital%20Europe%20Programme%3A%20DIGITAL-ECCC-2027-DEPLOY-CYBER-11-EULEG%3A%20Strengthening%20EU%20cybersecurity%20capacities%20%26%20capabilities%20in%20line%20with%20legislative%20requirements%0AAward%3A%20Requested%20EU%20contribution%20expected%20EUR%203-5%20million%20per%20project%20%28topic%20budget%20EUR%2020%20million%3B%20call%20total%20up%20to%20EUR%2096%20million%29%3B%20Simple%20Grant%20at%2050%25%20funding%20rate%0ADeadline%3A%202027-01-14%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Legal%20entities%20%28public%20or%20private%29%20established%20in%20EU%20Member%20States%20%28including%20OCTs%29%20or%20EEA%20countries%20%28Norway%2C%20Iceland%2C%20Liechtenstein%29.%20They%20must%20not%20be%20directly%20or%20indirectly%20controlled%20from%20a%20non-eligible%20country%20%28security%20restrictions%20under%20Article%2012%285%29%29.%20Single%20applicants%20are%20allowed%3B%20multi-country%20consortia%20are%20not%20required%20but%20help%20the%20impact%20score.%20All%20work%2C%20including%20subcontracting%2C%20must%20take%20place%20in%20eligible%20countries.%20Geography%3A%20EU%20Member%20States%20and%20EEA%20only%3B%20no%20control%20from%20outside.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Companies%2C%20non-profits%2C%20public%20bodies%2C%20universities%20%28legal%20entities%20only%3B%20no%20individuals%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcybersecurity-centre.europa.eu%2Fnews%2Fnew-eccc-call-proposals-under-digital-europe-programme-open-applications-2026-09-01_en%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fec.europa.eu%2Finfo%2Ffunding-tenders%2Fopportunities%2Fportal%2Fscreen%2Fopportunities%2Fcalls-for-proposals%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Register%20every%20beneficiary%20in%20the%20Participant%20Register%20%28PIC%29%20and%20pass%20validation.%202.%20Optionally%20build%20a%20consortium%20%28e.g.%2C%20with%20a%20National%20Coordination%20Centre%2C%20CSIRT%20or%20conformity%20assessment%20body%29.%203.%20Write%20Part%20B%20%28max%2070%20pages%29%20with%20the%20topic-mandatory%20KPIs.%204.%20Submit%20on%20the%20EU%20Funding%20%26%20Tenders%20Portal%20by%2014%20January%202027%2C%2017%3A00%20CET.%205.%20Evaluation%20February-March%202027%3B%20results%20April%202027.%206.%20Grant%20agreement%20signature%20around%20October%202027.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Application%20Form%20Part%20A%20%28administrative%20data%2C%20budget%29%0A-%20Application%20Form%20Part%20B%20technical%20description%2C%20max%2070%20pages%0A-%20Topic-mandatory%20KPIs%20on%20the%20KPI%20template%20%28e.g.%2C%20stakeholders%20supported%20in%20implementing%20EU%20cybersecurity%20law%2C%20broken%20down%20by%20SMEs%20and%20start-ups%2C%20authorities%2C%20conformity%20assessment%20bodies%29%0A-%20Milestones%20and%20deliverables%20per%20the%20call%20guidance%0A-%20Declaration%20on%20ownership%20and%20control%20for%20every%20entity%0A-%20Legal%20status%20and%20origin%20documents%20for%20PIC%20validation%0A-%20Budget%20at%20the%2050%25%20funding%20rate%20%28equipment%20by%20depreciation%20only%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Results%20about%203%20months%20after%20the%20deadline%20%28April%202027%29%3B%20grant%20agreement%20around%20October%202027%0AObligations%3A%2050%25%20co-financing%3B%20KPI%20reporting%3B%20security%20and%20possible%20IPR%20restrictions%3B%20all%20activities%20in%20eligible%20countries.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20open%2C%20low-cost%20Cyber%20Resilience%20Act%20compliance%20tooling%20built%20on%20ForkMesh%20for%20EU%20SMEs%20and%20open-source%20stewards%3A%20signed%20release%20provenance%20with%20SBOMs%2C%20signed%20vulnerability-handling%20records%20kept%20in%20the%20repository%2C%20and%20mirrored%20source%20availability%20for%20coordinated%20vulnerability%20disclosure.%20The%20topic%20text%20explicitly%20asks%20for%20SBOM%2C%20CVD%20and%20CSAF%20automation%20and%20user-friendly%20CRA%20compliance%20tools.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20an%20EU%20or%20EEA%20entity%20not%20controlled%20from%20outside.%2050%25%20co-funding%20of%20a%20EUR%203-5M%20project%20is%20very%20large%20for%20a%20small%20team.%20Competition%20from%20established%20security%20vendors%20and%20national%20bodies%20is%20strong.%20Fits%20best%20as%20a%20partner%20in%20a%20consortium%20led%20by%20a%20CRA-focused%20organisation.)** · [Program page](https://cybersecurity-centre.europa.eu/news/new-eccc-call-proposals-under-digital-europe-programme-open-applications-2026-09-01_en) · [Apply](https://ec.europa.eu/info/funding-tenders/opportunities/portal/screen/opportunities/calls-for-proposals)

- **Funder:** European Cybersecurity Competence Centre (ECCC), Digital Europe Programme (European Commission)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Requested EU contribution expected EUR 3-5 million per project (topic budget EUR 20 million; call total up to EUR 96 million); Simple Grant at 50% funding rate (about $3,450,000 - $5,750,000)
- **Deadline:** 2027-01-14 (open)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Legal entities (public or private) established in EU Member States (including OCTs) or EEA countries (Norway, Iceland, Liechtenstein). They must not be directly or indirectly controlled from a non-eligible country (security restrictions under Article 12(5)). Single applicants are allowed; multi-country consortia are not required but help the impact score. All work, including subcontracting, must take place in eligible countries.
- **Geography:** EU Member States and EEA only; no control from outside · **Entity types:** Companies, non-profits, public bodies, universities (legal entities only; no individuals)
- **Decision time:** Results about 3 months after the deadline (April 2027); grant agreement around October 2027
- **Obligations:** 50% co-financing; KPI reporting; security and possible IPR restrictions; all activities in eligible countries.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose open, low-cost Cyber Resilience Act compliance tooling built on ForkMesh for EU SMEs and open-source stewards: signed release provenance with SBOMs, signed vulnerability-handling records kept in the repository, and mirrored source availability for coordinated vulnerability disclosure. The topic text explicitly asks for SBOM, CVD and CSAF automation and user-friendly CRA compliance tools.

**How to apply:**

1. Register every beneficiary in the Participant Register (PIC) and pass validation.
2. Optionally build a consortium (e.g., with a National Coordination Centre, CSIRT or conformity assessment body).
3. Write Part B (max 70 pages) with the topic-mandatory KPIs.
4. Submit on the EU Funding & Tenders Portal by 14 January 2027, 17:00 CET.
5. Evaluation February-March 2027; results April 2027.
6. Grant agreement signature around October 2027.

**What the application asks for:**

- Application Form Part A (administrative data, budget)
- Application Form Part B technical description, max 70 pages
- Topic-mandatory KPIs on the KPI template (e.g., stakeholders supported in implementing EU cybersecurity law, broken down by SMEs and start-ups, authorities, conformity assessment bodies)
- Milestones and deliverables per the call guidance
- Declaration on ownership and control for every entity
- Legal status and origin documents for PIC validation
- Budget at the 50% funding rate (equipment by depreciation only)

**Risks:** Needs an EU or EEA entity not controlled from outside. 50% co-funding of a EUR 3-5M project is very large for a small team. Competition from established security vendors and national bodies is strong. Fits best as a partner in a consortium led by a CRA-focused organisation.

**Sources:** <https://cybersecurity-centre.europa.eu/news/new-eccc-call-proposals-under-digital-europe-programme-open-applications-2026-09-01_en>, <https://cybersecurity-centre.europa.eu/document/8e1d6074-95ed-4b23-8bd3-2ca42b096411_en>, <https://island.is/en/news/new-eccc-call-for-proposals-under-the-digital-europe-programme-is-open-for-applications>

endent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Open%20Technology%20Fund%3A%20Free%20and%20Open%20Source%20Software%20Sustainability%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20Single-maintainer%20projects%20about%20USD%207%2C000%20per%20month%20for%206-24%20months%3B%20large%20multi-technology%20initiatives%20up%20to%20USD%20400%2C000%20%28guidebook%3A%20ideal%20USD%20150%2C000%20-%20400%2C000%20over%20~24%20months%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20call%20expected%20spring%202027%20%282026%20call%20ran%2026%20Mar%20-%207%20May%202026%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%202027%20dates%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Internet-freedom%20FOSS%20tools%2C%20apps%2C%20protocols%20or%20libraries%20that%20are%20core%20dependencies%20of%20internet%20freedom%20technologies%20or%20user-facing%20tools%20with%20substantial%20users%20in%20repressive%20contexts%2C%20with%20active%20development%20in%20the%20last%20two%20years.%20The%20guidebook%20additionally%20lists%20public%20release%20for%20at%20least%203%20years%20and%20at%20least%204%20updates%20a%20year.%20Individuals%2018%2B%20and%20for-profit%20or%20nonprofit%20organizations%20may%20apply.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28except%20U.S.-sanctioned%20countries%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%2C%20nonprofit%2C%20individuals.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.opentech.fund%2Ffunds%2Ffree-and-open-source-software-sustainability-fund%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.opentech.fund%2Ffunds%2Ffree-and-open-source-software-sustainability-fund%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Watch%20the%20fund%20page%20for%20the%20annual%20solicitation.%202.%20Submit%20a%20Concept%20Note%20through%20OTF%27s%20online%20system%20before%20the%20call%20deadline.%203.%20OTF%20may%20ask%20clarifying%20questions%2C%20then%20invites%20competitive%20applicants%20to%20submit%20a%20full%20Proposal%20by%20a%20set%20deadline.%204.%20Final%20review%20by%20the%20FOSS%20Sustainability%20Fund%20Advisory%20Council.%205.%20Performance-based%20contract.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Concept%20Note%20via%20OTF%20system%20%28same%20core%20fields%20as%20the%20Internet%20Freedom%20Fund%20concept%20note%29%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20dependency%20or%20user%20base%20in%20internet%20freedom%20ecosystem%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20active%20development%20%28release%20history%2C%20update%20cadence%29%0A-%20If%20invited%3A%20full%20Proposal%20with%20detailed%20activities%20and%20budget%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%202026%20round%20expected%20contracts%20in%20late%20summer%20%2F%20early%20fall%2C%20about%204-6%20months%20after%20the%20call%20closed%0AObligations%3A%20Performance-based%20payments%20on%20deliverables%3B%20OTF%20program%20manager%20monitoring%3B%20possible%20security%20audits%20with%20time%20allotted%20for%20fixes%3B%20OTF%20standard%20contract%20terms%20incl.%20termination%20for%20convenience%20and%20work-product%20ownership%20clause%3B%20no%20publicity%20without%20OTF%20written%20approval.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20maintenance%20and%20hardening%20of%20the%20ForkMesh%20mirroring%20protocol%20as%20a%20keystone%20dependency%20that%20keeps%20internet-freedom%20repositories%20reachable.%20Only%20realistic%20once%20ForkMesh%20can%20show%20multi-year%20release%20history%20and%20adoption%20by%20internet%20freedom%20tool%20projects.%0ARisks%3A%20Fund%20page%20currently%20says%20%27Not%20currently%20accepting%20applications%27.%20Guidebook%20requires%203%2B%20years%20of%20public%20release%20and%20keystone%20status%2C%20which%20an%20early-stage%20project%20likely%20fails.%20Work%20product%20becomes%20OTF%20property%20per%20page%20terms%3B%20needs%20legal%20review%20against%20the%20MIT%20licence%20model.)** · [Program page](https://www.opentech.fund/funds/free-and-open-source-software-sustainability-fund/) · [Apply](https://www.opentech.fund/funds/free-and-open-source-software-sustainability-fund/)

- **Funder:** Open Technology Fund
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** Single-maintainer projects about USD 7,000 per month for 6-24 months; large multi-technology initiatives up to USD 400,000 (guidebook: ideal USD 150,000 - 400,000 over ~24 months) (about $400,000)
- **Deadline:** Next call expected spring 2027 (2026 call ran 26 Mar - 7 May 2026; unconfirmed: 2027 dates) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Internet-freedom FOSS tools, apps, protocols or libraries that are core dependencies of internet freedom technologies or user-facing tools with substantial users in repressive contexts, with active development in the last two years. The guidebook additionally lists public release for at least 3 years and at least 4 updates a year. Individuals 18+ and for-profit or nonprofit organizations may apply.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (except U.S.-sanctioned countries) · **Entity types:** For-profit, nonprofit, individuals
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: 2026 round expected contracts in late summer / early fall, about 4-6 months after the call closed
- **Obligations:** Performance-based payments on deliverables; OTF program manager monitoring; possible security audits with time allotted for fixes; OTF standard contract terms incl. termination for convenience and work-product ownership clause; no publicity without OTF written approval.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch maintenance and hardening of the ForkMesh mirroring protocol as a keystone dependency that keeps internet-freedom repositories reachable. Only realistic once ForkMesh can show multi-year release history and adoption by internet freedom tool projects.

**How to apply:**

1. Watch the fund page for the annual solicitation.
2. Submit a Concept Note through OTF's online system before the call deadline.
3. OTF may ask clarifying questions, then invites competitive applicants to submit a full Proposal by a set deadline.
4. Final review by the FOSS Sustainability Fund Advisory Council.
5. Performance-based contract.

**What the application asks for:**

- Concept Note via OTF system (same core fields as the Internet Freedom Fund concept note)
- Evidence of dependency or user base in internet freedom ecosystem
- Evidence of active development (release history, update cadence)
- If invited: full Proposal with detailed activities and budget

**Risks:** Fund page currently says 'Not currently accepting applications'. Guidebook requires 3+ years of public release and keystone status, which an early-stage project likely fails. Work product becomes OTF property per page terms; needs legal review against the MIT licence model.

**Sources:** <https://www.opentech.fund/funds/free-and-open-source-software-sustainability-fund/>, <https://docs.opentech.fund/otf-application-guidebook/our-funds-and-fellowships/free-and-open-source-software-foss-sustainability-fund>

## 52. Cloudflare for Startups

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Cloudflare%20for%20Startups.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.cloudflare.com%2Fforstartups%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.cloudflare.com%2Flp%2Fstartups%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Cloudflare%20for%20Startups%0AAward%3A%20Tier%203%3A%20USD%2010k%20%28bootstrapped%2Fself-funded%2C%20raised%20under%20USD%201M%29%3B%20Tier%202%3A%20USD%20100k%20%28raised%20under%20USD%205M%2C%20funded%20by%20an%20affiliated%20partner%29%3B%20Tier%201%3A%20USD%20350k%20%28raised%20USD%205M%2B%2C%20funded%20by%20an%20affiliated%20partner%29%3B%20credits%20valid%20one%20year%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20For-profit%20companies%20incorporated%20no%20more%20than%2010%20years%20ago%2C%20funded%20up%20to%20Series%20B%2C%20actively%20building%20a%20technology%20product%2C%20with%20a%20live%20publicly%20verifiable%20website%2C%20a%20public%20presence%20on%20LinkedIn%2FX%2FGitHub%2C%20and%20a%20business%20email%20matching%20the%20domain%3B%20first-time%20applicants%20only.%20Tiers%201-2%20require%20an%20affiliated%20accelerator%2FVC%20partner%3B%20Tier%203%20is%20for%20self-funded%20companies.%20Excludes%20educational%20institutions%2C%20personal%20blogs%2C%20consultancies%2C%20agencies%2C%20MSPs%2C%20resellers%20and%20existing%20enterprise%20customers.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20sanctioned-country%20exclusions%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20companies%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.cloudflare.com%2Fforstartups%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.cloudflare.com%2Flp%2Fstartups%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20a%20Cloudflare%20account%20and%20add%20a%20valid%20payment%20method.%202.%20Apply%20via%20the%20Apply%20here%20form%20at%20cloudflare.com%2Flp%2Fstartups%20with%20a%20business%20email.%203.%20Review%20typically%20within%2048%20hours%3B%20approval%20by%20email%20%28contact%20startups%40cloudflare.com%20after%20a%20week%29.%204.%20If%20rejected%2C%20a%20reason%20is%20given%20and%20re-evaluation%20can%20be%20requested%20with%20supporting%20information.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Cloudflare%20account%20with%20valid%20payment%20method%0A-%20Business%20email%20matching%20company%20domain%0A-%20Live%20public%20company%20website%0A-%20Public%20LinkedIn%2C%20X%20or%20GitHub%20presence%0A-%20Incorporation%20date%0A-%20Funding%20raised%20and%20stage%3B%20affiliated%20partner%20%28accelerator%2FVC%29%20details%20for%20Tiers%201-2%0A-%20Product%20description%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Up%20to%2048%20hours%20typically%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20last%20one%20year%20or%20until%20used%2C%20no%20extensions.%20Exclusions%3A%20AI%20Gateway%2C%20registrar%20fees%2C%20enterprise%20add-ons%2C%20Magic%20Transit%2C%20Spectrum%2C%20dedicated%20IPs%2C%20China%20Network%3B%20R2%20capped%20at%20USD%2010%2C000%3B%20Workers%20AI%20caps%20by%20tier%20%28USD%202%2C500%20for%20Tier%203%29.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Use%20Tier%203%20credits%20for%20edge%20caching%20and%20R2%20object%20storage%20of%20release%20artifacts%20and%20signed%20mirror%20snapshots%2C%20plus%20DDoS%20protection%20in%20front%20of%20forkmesh.com%20and%20the%20public%20clone%20endpoints.%20Cloudflare%20backs%20developer-infrastructure%20startups%20and%20its%20R2%2FWorkers%20fit%20serving%20immutable%20signed%20objects.%0ARisks%3A%20ForkMesh%20Next%20deliberately%20moved%20off%20Cloudflare%20Workers%2FD1%2FDurable%20Objects%2C%20so%20adopting%20Cloudflare%20again%20conflicts%20with%20the%20self-hosted%20architecture.%20Self-funded%20tier%20is%20only%20USD%2010k%3B%20larger%20tiers%20need%20an%20affiliated%20investor.%20Credits%20expire%20after%20one%20year%20with%20no%20extension.)** · [Program page](https://www.cloudflare.com/forstartups/) · [Apply](https://www.cloudflare.com/lp/startups)

- **Funder:** Cloudflare
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Tier 3: USD 10k (bootstrapped/self-funded, raised under USD 1M); Tier 2: USD 100k (raised under USD 5M, funded by an affiliated partner); Tier 1: USD 350k (raised USD 5M+, funded by an affiliated partner); credits valid one year (about $10,000 - $350,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** For-profit companies incorporated no more than 10 years ago, funded up to Series B, actively building a technology product, with a live publicly verifiable website, a public presence on LinkedIn/X/GitHub, and a business email matching the domain; first-time applicants only. Tiers 1-2 require an affiliated accelerator/VC partner; Tier 3 is for self-funded companies. Excludes educational institutions, personal blogs, consultancies, agencies, MSPs, resellers and existing enterprise customers.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: sanctioned-country exclusions) · **Entity types:** For-profit companies only
- **Decision time:** Up to 48 hours typically
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits last one year or until used, no extensions. Exclusions: AI Gateway, registrar fees, enterprise add-ons, Magic Transit, Spectrum, dedicated IPs, China Network; R2 capped at USD 10,000; Workers AI caps by tier (USD 2,500 for Tier 3).

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Use Tier 3 credits for edge caching and R2 object storage of release artifacts and signed mirror snapshots, plus DDoS protection in front of forkmesh.com and the public clone endpoints. Cloudflare backs developer-infrastructure startups and its R2/Workers fit serving immutable signed objects.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a Cloudflare account and add a valid payment method.
2. Apply via the Apply here form at cloudflare.com/lp/startups with a business email.
3. Review typically within 48 hours; approval by email (contact startups@cloudflare.com after a week).
4. If rejected, a reason is given and re-evaluation can be requested with supporting information.

**What the application asks for:**

- Cloudflare account with valid payment method
- Business email matching company domain
- Live public company website
- Public LinkedIn, X or GitHub presence
- Incorporation date
- Funding raised and stage; affiliated partner (accelerator/VC) details for Tiers 1-2
- Product description

**Risks:** ForkMesh Next deliberately moved off Cloudflare Workers/D1/Durable Objects, so adopting Cloudflare again conflicts with the self-hosted architecture. Self-funded tier is only USD 10k; larger tiers need an affiliated investor. Credits expire after one year with no extension.

**Sources:** <https://www.cloudflare.com/forstartups/>

rx.com%2Fget-funded%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.dodsbirsttr.mil%2Fsubmissions%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20topics%20at%20pre-release%20%28first%20Wednesday%20of%20the%20month%29%20in%20the%20DoW%20SBIR%2FSTTR%20Innovation%20Portal%20%28DSIP%29.%202.%20Contact%20topic%20authors%20during%20pre-release.%203.%20Write%20the%20proposal%20on%20the%20required%20template.%204.%20Submit%20in%20DSIP%20before%20the%20release%20closes.%205.%20Proposals%20are%20scored%20on%20defense%20need%2C%20technical%20merit%20and%20commercialisation.%206.%20Phase%20I%20award%3B%20Phase%20II%2FD2P2%20needs%20a%20signed%20customer%20memorandum.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20DSIP%20proposal%20following%20the%20topic%27s%20required%20proposal%20template%20%28exact%20volumes%20and%20page%20limits%20unconfirmed%20for%20FY27%20Release%201%29%0A-%20Cost%20proposal%20within%20the%20Phase%20I%20cap%20and%20duration%0A-%20Active%20SAM.gov%20registration%20%28an%20inactive%20registration%20is%20a%20common%20reason%20for%20rejection%29%0A-%20Company%20commercialisation%20information%20%28unconfirmed%29%0A-%20For%20Phase%20II%20or%20D2P2%3A%20customer%20memorandum%20on%20the%20mandatory%20template%20signed%20by%20an%20end%20user%2C%20customer%20and%20technical%20point%20of%20contact%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20Phase%20I%20feasibility%20deliverables%20and%20final%20report%20%28unconfirmed%29%3B%20SBIR%20data%20rights%3B%20Phase%20II%20needs%20a%20DAF%20end%20user.%20FY26%20SBIR%20budget%20proration%20may%20cut%20the%20number%20of%20awards.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Look%20for%20a%20Release%201%20FY27%20topic%20on%20software%20supply-chain%20assurance%2C%20software%20factories%2FDevSecOps%20or%20contested-environment%20code%20distribution.%20Offer%20ForkMesh%20mirrors%20as%20a%20signed%2C%20offline-capable%20code%20distribution%20and%20review%20layer%20for%20Air%20Force%20software%20factories.%20AFWERX%20funds%20dual-use%20tech%20that%20can%20transition%20to%20a%20DAF%20customer.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20majority%20U.S.%20ownership%20and%20a%20matching%20topic%2C%20and%20broad%20open%20topics%20have%20been%20replaced%20by%20focused%20topics.%20Needs%20a%20defense%20end%20user%20for%20Phase%20II.%20A%20military%20context%20may%20conflict%20with%20the%20censorship-resistance%20and%20community%20positioning.%20The%20crypto%20features%20are%20irrelevant%20here.%20Release%206%20closes%20in%205%20days%2C%20which%20is%20too%20soon%20for%20a%20new%20proposal.)** · [Program page](https://afwerx.com/get-funded/) · [Apply](https://www.dodsbirsttr.mil/submissions)

- **Funder:** U.S. Department of the Air Force (AFWERX / SpaceWERX), Department of War SBIR/STTR
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Phase I: up to $323,000, up to six months (about $323,000)
- **Deadline:** 2026-11-25 (FY27 Release 1: pre-release 2026-10-07, opens 2026-10-28; dates subject to change). Release 6 closes 2026-10-21 (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** For-profit U.S. small businesses with 500 or fewer employees including affiliates, more than 50% owned and controlled by U.S. citizens, permanent resident aliens or qualifying U.S. entities. The work must be dual-use and match a Department of the Air Force need. SAM registration is required before award.
- **Geography:** US only · **Entity types:** U.S. for-profit small businesses only
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** Phase I feasibility deliverables and final report (unconfirmed); SBIR data rights; Phase II needs a DAF end user. FY26 SBIR budget proration may cut the number of awards.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Look for a Release 1 FY27 topic on software supply-chain assurance, software factories/DevSecOps or contested-environment code distribution. Offer ForkMesh mirrors as a signed, offline-capable code distribution and review layer for Air Force software factories. AFWERX funds dual-use tech that can transition to a DAF customer.

**How to apply:**

1. Read topics at pre-release (first Wednesday of the month) in the DoW SBIR/STTR Innovation Portal (DSIP).
2. Contact topic authors during pre-release.
3. Write the proposal on the required template.
4. Submit in DSIP before the release closes.
5. Proposals are scored on defense need, technical merit and commercialisation.
6. Phase I award; Phase II/D2P2 needs a signed customer memorandum.

**What the application asks for:**

- DSIP proposal following the topic's required proposal template (exact volumes and page limits unconfirmed for FY27 Release 1)
- Cost proposal within the Phase I cap and duration
- Active SAM.gov registration (an inactive registration is a common reason for rejection)
- Company commercialisation information (unconfirmed)
- For Phase II or D2P2: customer memorandum on the mandatory template signed by an end user, customer and technical point of contact

**Risks:** Needs majority U.S. ownership and a matching topic, and broad open topics have been replaced by focused topics. Needs a defense end user for Phase II. A military context may conflict with the censorship-resistance and community positioning. The crypto features are irrelevant here. Release 6 closes in 5 days, which is too soon for a new proposal.

**Sources:** <https://afwerx.com/>, <https://afwerx.com/get-funded/>, <https://afwerx.com/wp-content/uploads/26-0423-SBIR_STTR-Reauthorization-Webinar-QA-_Final-1.pdf>

## 54. Swarm Association / Swarm Foundation: Swarm Grants Programme

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Swarm%20Association%20%2F%20Swarm%20Foundation%3A%20Swarm%20Grants%20Programme.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.ethswarm.org%2Fgrants%2Fswarm-grants-programme%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fmy.ethswarm.org%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Swarm%20Association%20%2F%20Swarm%20Foundation%3A%20Swarm%20Grants%20Programme%0AAward%3A%20Starter%20Microgrants%20500%20-%202%2C000%20USD%20in%20BZZ%3B%20Builder%20Grants%202%2C000%20-%205%2C000%20USD%20in%20BZZ%3B%20Booster%20Grants%20up%20to%2010%2C000%20USD%20in%20BZZ%20%28exceptional%2Fendorsed%20cases%29%3B%20most%20recent%20wave%20pool%20300%2C000%20BZZ%0ADeadline%3A%20Not%20open%20on%202026-10-16%20%28page%20states%20grants%20are%20not%20open%20for%20applications%20at%20this%20time%29%3B%20next%20wave%20date%20not%20announced%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Builders%20integrating%20Swarm%20decentralised%20storage.%20Starter%20Microgrants%20are%20for%20simple%20storage%20integrations%20or%20proofs%20of%20concept%20by%20projects%20with%20no%20prior%20grants%3B%20Builder%20Grants%20require%20a%20functional%20prototype%20and%20defined%20next%20steps%3B%20Booster%20Grants%20are%20for%20exceptional%20or%20Swarm%20Association-endorsed%20projects.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20worldwide%20%28no%20restriction%20stated%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20individuals%20and%20teams%20%28not%20stated%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.ethswarm.org%2Fgrants%2Fswarm-grants-programme%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fmy.ethswarm.org%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Follow%20Swarm%27s%20official%20channels%20for%20the%20next%20wave%20announcement.%202.%20When%20a%20wave%20opens%2C%20submit%20the%20application%20form%20at%20my.ethswarm.org%20choosing%20the%20tier.%203.%20Review%20by%20the%20Swarm%20Association.%204.%20Grant%20paid%20in%20BZZ.%20unconfirmed%3A%20review%20time%2C%20milestone%20and%20reporting%20steps%20not%20published%20on%20the%20page.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Application%20form%20at%20my.ethswarm.org%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20fields%20not%20published%29%0A-%20Description%20of%20the%20Swarm%20storage%20integration%20and%20target%20focus%20area%0A-%20For%20Builder%20Grants%3A%20functional%20prototype%20and%20clear%20future%20development%20steps%0A-%20BZZ-capable%20%28Gnosis%20Chain%29%20wallet%20address%20for%20payout%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20reporting%20and%20licensing%20terms%20not%20stated%20on%20the%20official%20page%3B%20paid%20in%20BZZ%20tokens%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20Swarm%20storage%20backend%20for%20ForkMesh%3A%20mirror%20nodes%20push%20signed%20Git%20pack%20snapshots%20and%20in-repo%20issue%2FPR%20records%20to%20Swarm%20so%20any%20repository%20can%20be%20restored%20from%20decentralised%20storage%20when%20every%20mirror%20is%20offline.%20This%20fits%20the%20wave%27s%20listed%20impact%20areas%20of%20Web2%20migration%2C%20developer%20tooling%20and%20infrastructure%2C%20and%20decentralised%20data%20control.%0ARisks%3A%20Program%20confirmed%20as%20existing%20but%20closed%20today%3B%20the%20most%20recent%20wave%27s%20dates%20conflict%20across%20Swarm%20pages%20and%20give%20no%20year.%20Small%20grants%20%28max%20~USD%2010k%29%20paid%20in%20BZZ%20tokens%20with%20price%20risk.%20Focus%20areas%20emphasise%20blobs%2Fdata%20availability%2C%20so%20the%20proposal%20must%20be%20framed%20as%20Swarm%20storage%20usage.)** · [Program page](https://www.ethswarm.org/grants/swarm-grants-programme) · [Apply](https://my.ethswarm.org/)

- **Funder:** Swarm Association / Swarm Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Starter Microgrants 500 - 2,000 USD in BZZ; Builder Grants 2,000 - 5,000 USD in BZZ; Booster Grants up to 10,000 USD in BZZ (exceptional/endorsed cases); most recent wave pool 300,000 BZZ (about $500 - $10,000)
- **Deadline:** Not open on 2026-10-16 (page states grants are not open for applications at this time); next wave date not announced (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Builders integrating Swarm decentralised storage. Starter Microgrants are for simple storage integrations or proofs of concept by projects with no prior grants; Builder Grants require a functional prototype and defined next steps; Booster Grants are for exceptional or Swarm Association-endorsed projects.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: worldwide (no restriction stated) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: individuals and teams (not stated)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: reporting and licensing terms not stated on the official page; paid in BZZ tokens

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a Swarm storage backend for ForkMesh: mirror nodes push signed Git pack snapshots and in-repo issue/PR records to Swarm so any repository can be restored from decentralised storage when every mirror is offline. This fits the wave's listed impact areas of Web2 migration, developer tooling and infrastructure, and decentralised data control.

**How to apply:**

1. Follow Swarm's official channels for the next wave announcement.
2. When a wave opens, submit the application form at my.ethswarm.org choosing the tier.
3. Review by the Swarm Association.
4. Grant paid in BZZ. unconfirmed: review time, milestone and reporting steps not published on the page.

**What the application asks for:**

- Application form at my.ethswarm.org (unconfirmed: exact fields not published)
- Description of the Swarm storage integration and target focus area
- For Builder Grants: functional prototype and clear future development steps
- BZZ-capable (Gnosis Chain) wallet address for payout

**Risks:** Program confirmed as existing but closed today; the most recent wave's dates conflict across Swarm pages and give no year. Small grants (max ~USD 10k) paid in BZZ tokens with price risk. Focus areas emphasise blobs/data availability, so the proposal must be framed as Swarm storage usage.

**Sources:** <https://www.ethswarm.org/grants/swarm-grants-programme>, <https://docs.ethswarm.org/docs/learn/ecosystem/grants-bounties>, <https://my.ethswarm.org/>

hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Drips%20Network%3A%20Drips%20Wave%20maintainer%20programs%2C%20Drip%20List%20%2F%20RetroPGF%20ecosystem%20funding%20and%20claimable%20repo%20funding%0AAward%3A%20No%20fixed%20grant%20amount%3A%20funds%20arrive%20as%20streams%2Fsplits%20from%20Drip%20Lists%20and%20ecosystem%20rounds%20%28e.g.%20ENS%20streamed%20%2450%2C000%20USDC%20over%20six%20months%20to%20seven%20projects%29%20or%20as%20per-merged-PR%20Wave%20rewards%20set%20by%20the%20sponsoring%20ecosystem%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20per-repo%20amounts%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28repo%20claiming%20any%20time%3B%20Stellar%20Wave%20active%20until%202026-10-22%20with%20recurring%20one-week%20Waves%20thereafter%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Any%20open-source%20repository%20can%20be%20claimed%20on%20Drips%20%28by%20adding%20a%20FUNDING.json%20with%20the%20maintainer%27s%20wallet%29%20and%20then%20be%20included%20in%20Drip%20Lists%20or%20ecosystem%20RetroPGF%20rounds.%20Drips%20Wave%20includes%20repos%20approved%20by%20the%20sponsoring%20ecosystem%20%28currently%20Stellar%29%2C%20with%20a%20repo%20application%20and%20appeal%20process.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28on-chain%20payouts%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20sanctions%20or%20KYC%20requirements%20for%20Wave%20rewards%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Any%20open-source%20project%2C%20individual%20maintainers%20or%20organisations%20%28wallet-based%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.drips.network%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fdrips.network%2Fwave%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Claim%20the%20ForkMesh%20GitHub%20repository%20on%20Drips%20by%20committing%20a%20FUNDING.json%20with%20the%20receiving%20Ethereum%20address.%202.%20Create%20a%20project%20profile%20and%20list%20ForkMesh%27s%20own%20dependencies%20%28optional%20Drip%20List%29%20to%20signal%20good%20citizenship.%203.%20Apply%20the%20repo%20to%20a%20Drips%20Wave%20program%20as%20a%20maintainer%20%28currently%20the%20Stellar%20Wave%29%20and%20publish%20scoped%2C%20labelled%20issues%20for%20one-week%20contribution%20sprints.%204.%20Seek%20inclusion%20in%20ecosystem%20Drip%20Lists%20%2F%20RetroPGF%20rounds%20run%20on%20Drips%20%28e.g.%20Radworks%2C%20Filecoin%2C%20ENS%29.%205.%20Collect%20streamed%20funds%20from%20the%20Drips%20app.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20FUNDING.json%20in%20the%20repository%20root%20with%20the%20maintainer%20wallet%20address%0A-%20Drips%20project%20profile%20%28description%2C%20avatar%2C%20links%29%0A-%20Wave%20repo%20application%20%28repository%2C%20maintainers%2C%20scoped%20issues%20with%20point%20values%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20fields%29%0A-%20Public%20GitHub%20repository%20under%20an%20open-source%20licence%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Immediate%20for%20repo%20claiming%3B%20Wave%20repo%20applications%20reviewed%20before%20each%20Wave%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20days%20to%20weeks%29%0AObligations%3A%20Maintainers%20in%20a%20Wave%20must%20review%20and%20merge%20contributor%20PRs%20during%20sprints%3B%20funds%20are%20on-chain%20%28ERC-20%29%20and%20public%3B%20no%20equity%20or%20reporting%20beyond%20program%20rules.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Claim%20the%20ForkMesh%20repo%20on%20Drips%20so%20dependency-funding%20rounds%20%28Radworks%20funds%20FOSS%20dependencies%20through%20Drips%29%20can%20reach%20it%2C%20and%20position%20ForkMesh%20as%20a%20natural%20partner%3A%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed%20PR%20records%20and%20contribution%20pool%20could%20route%20payouts%20into%20Drips%20splits.%20If%20ForkMesh%20adds%20a%20Stellar%20payout%20integration%2C%20it%20can%20join%20the%20Stellar%20Wave%20to%20get%20contributors%20paid%20for%20merged%20PRs%20at%20no%20cost%20to%20the%20team.%0ARisks%3A%20Not%20a%20classic%20grant%3A%20income%20depends%20on%20third%20parties%20choosing%20to%20fund%20ForkMesh%3B%20the%20only%20active%20Wave%20is%20Stellar-specific%3B%20Radicle%20%28peer-to-peer%20Git%2C%20funded%20by%20the%20same%20Radworks%20DAO%29%20is%20a%20direct%20alternative%20to%20ForkMesh%2C%20so%20Radworks%20itself%20may%20not%20prioritise%20funding%20a%20competitor%3B%20payouts%20in%20crypto.)** · [Program page](https://www.drips.network/) · [Apply](https://drips.network/wave)

- **Funder:** Drips (Radworks ecosystem)
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** No fixed grant amount: funds arrive as streams/splits from Drip Lists and ecosystem rounds (e.g. ENS streamed $50,000 USDC over six months to seven projects) or as per-merged-PR Wave rewards set by the sponsoring ecosystem (unconfirmed: per-repo amounts) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (repo claiming any time; Stellar Wave active until 2026-10-22 with recurring one-week Waves thereafter) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Any open-source repository can be claimed on Drips (by adding a FUNDING.json with the maintainer's wallet) and then be included in Drip Lists or ecosystem RetroPGF rounds. Drips Wave includes repos approved by the sponsoring ecosystem (currently Stellar), with a repo application and appeal process.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (on-chain payouts; unconfirmed: sanctions or KYC requirements for Wave rewards) · **Entity types:** Any open-source project, individual maintainers or organisations (wallet-based)
- **Decision time:** Immediate for repo claiming; Wave repo applications reviewed before each Wave (unconfirmed: days to weeks)
- **Obligations:** Maintainers in a Wave must review and merge contributor PRs during sprints; funds are on-chain (ERC-20) and public; no equity or reporting beyond program rules.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Claim the ForkMesh repo on Drips so dependency-funding rounds (Radworks funds FOSS dependencies through Drips) can reach it, and position ForkMesh as a natural partner: ForkMesh's signed PR records and contribution pool could route payouts into Drips splits. If ForkMesh adds a Stellar payout integration, it can join the Stellar Wave to get contributors paid for merged PRs at no cost to the team.

**How to apply:**

1. Claim the ForkMesh GitHub repository on Drips by committing a FUNDING.json with the receiving Ethereum address.
2. Create a project profile and list ForkMesh's own dependencies (optional Drip List) to signal good citizenship.
3. Apply the repo to a Drips Wave program as a maintainer (currently the Stellar Wave) and publish scoped, labelled issues for one-week contribution sprints.
4. Seek inclusion in ecosystem Drip Lists / RetroPGF rounds run on Drips (e.g. Radworks, Filecoin, ENS).
5. Collect streamed funds from the Drips app.

**What the application asks for:**

- FUNDING.json in the repository root with the maintainer wallet address
- Drips project profile (description, avatar, links)
- Wave repo application (repository, maintainers, scoped issues with point values; unconfirmed: exact fields)
- Public GitHub repository under an open-source licence

**Risks:** Not a classic grant: income depends on third parties choosing to fund ForkMesh; the only active Wave is Stellar-specific; Radicle (peer-to-peer Git, funded by the same Radworks DAO) is a direct alternative to ForkMesh, so Radworks itself may not prioritise funding a competitor; payouts in crypto.

**Sources:** <https://www.drips.network/>, <https://drips.network/wave>, <https://www.drips.network/blog/posts/radworks-gives-1m-to-foss-dependencies-with-drips>, <https://community.radworks.org/t/formal-review-rgp-28-multi-org-annual-proposal-for-2026/3701>, <https://community.radworks.org/t/drips-org-q1-2026-update/3721>

<a id="eurostars-call-12"></a>
## 56. Eureka network: Eurostars

**[Draft this application in ure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Eureka%20network%3A%20Eurostars%0AAward%3A%20Each%20participant%20is%20funded%20by%20its%20National%20Funding%20Body%20under%20national%20rules%3B%20budgets%20and%20funding%20rates%20are%20set%20nationally%20and%20not%20stated%20on%20the%20Eureka%20page%0ADeadline%3A%202027-03-04%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20The%20consortium%20must%20be%20led%20by%20an%20innovative%20SME%20from%20a%20Eurostars%20country%2C%20with%20at%20least%20two%20independent%20entities%20from%20at%20least%20two%20Eurostars%20countries%2C%20one%20of%20them%20an%20EU%20or%20Horizon%20Europe%20associated%20country.%20SMEs%20must%20carry%20at%20least%2050%25%20of%20total%20project%20cost%2C%20and%20no%20participant%20or%20country%20may%20exceed%2070%25%20of%20the%20budget.%20Civil%20purposes%20only%3B%20max%2036%20months.%20Geography%3A%20Eurostars%20countries%20%28at%20least%20one%20EU%20or%20Horizon%20Europe%20associated%20country%20in%20the%20consortium%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20SME%20lead%20%28for-profit%29%3B%20partners%20can%20be%20companies%2C%20universities%2C%20research%20organisations.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Feurekanetwork.org%2Fprogrammes-and-calls%2Feurostars%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Feurekanetwork.org%2Fprogrammes-and-calls%2Feurostars%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Build%20an%20international%20consortium%20with%20an%20innovative%20SME%20as%20lead.%202.%20Check%20each%20partner%27s%20National%20Funding%20Body%20rules%20%28some%20need%20national%20forms%29.%203.%20Submit%20the%20Eurostars%20application%20by%20the%204%20March%202027%20cut-off%20%28Call%2012%20opens%2017%20Dec%202026%20per%20secondary%20source%29.%204.%20Independent%20expert%20evaluation.%205.%20National%20funding%20bodies%20decide%20and%20sign%20contracts%20with%20each%20partner.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Eurostars%20application%20form%20on%20the%20Eureka%20platform%20%28sections%20and%20limits%20per%20Call%2012%20guidelines%3A%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Per-participant%20budget%20and%20effort%20split%20meeting%20the%2050%25%20SME%20and%2070%25%20maximum%20rules%0A-%20National%20eligibility%20documents%20for%20each%20participant%27s%20funding%20body%20%28unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Consortium%20agreement%20%28unconfirmed%20timing%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20Set%20by%20each%20National%20Funding%20Body%20%28reporting%2C%20cost%20claims%29%3B%20the%20project%20must%20be%20civilian%20and%20finish%20within%2036%20months.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Team%20up%20with%20a%20European%20SME%20or%20research%20group%20%28e.g.%2C%20a%20forge%2C%20CI%20or%20supply-chain%20security%20company%29%20to%20co-develop%20interoperable%20signed-mirror%20and%20federation%20features%20between%20ForkMesh%20and%20their%20product.%20Eurostars%20funds%20market-oriented%20cross-border%20SME%20R%26D%2C%20which%20suits%20a%20protocol%20meant%20to%20link%20independent%20hosts.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20a%20foreign%20partner%20and%20an%20SME%20lead.%20National%20budgets%20and%20rules%20vary%20and%20can%20run%20out.%20ForkMesh%27s%20entity%20country%20is%20unknown%2C%20so%20its%20own%20national%20funder%20is%20unknown.%20The%202027%20cut-off%20date%20comes%20from%20the%20official%20page%2C%20but%20the%20opening%20date%20is%20from%20a%20secondary%20source.)** · [Program page](https://eurekanetwork.org/programmes-and-calls/eurostars/) · [Apply](https://eurekanetwork.org/programmes-and-calls/eurostars/)

- **Funder:** Eureka network (co-funded by Horizon Europe and national funding bodies)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Each participant is funded by its National Funding Body under national rules; budgets and funding rates are set nationally and not stated on the Eureka page (about varies)
- **Deadline:** 2027-03-04 (open)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** The consortium must be led by an innovative SME from a Eurostars country, with at least two independent entities from at least two Eurostars countries, one of them an EU or Horizon Europe associated country. SMEs must carry at least 50% of total project cost, and no participant or country may exceed 70% of the budget. Civil purposes only; max 36 months.
- **Geography:** Eurostars countries (at least one EU or Horizon Europe associated country in the consortium) · **Entity types:** SME lead (for-profit); partners can be companies, universities, research organisations
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** Set by each National Funding Body (reporting, cost claims); the project must be civilian and finish within 36 months.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Team up with a European SME or research group (e.g., a forge, CI or supply-chain security company) to co-develop interoperable signed-mirror and federation features between ForkMesh and their product. Eurostars funds market-oriented cross-border SME R&D, which suits a protocol meant to link independent hosts.

**How to apply:**

1. Build an international consortium with an innovative SME as lead.
2. Check each partner's National Funding Body rules (some need national forms).
3. Submit the Eurostars application by the 4 March 2027 cut-off (Call 12 opens 17 Dec 2026 per secondary source).
4. Independent expert evaluation.
5. National funding bodies decide and sign contracts with each partner.

**What the application asks for:**

- Eurostars application form on the Eureka platform (sections and limits per Call 12 guidelines: unconfirmed)
- Per-participant budget and effort split meeting the 50% SME and 70% maximum rules
- National eligibility documents for each participant's funding body (unconfirmed)
- Consortium agreement (unconfirmed timing)

**Risks:** Needs a foreign partner and an SME lead. National budgets and rules vary and can run out. ForkMesh's entity country is unknown, so its own national funder is unknown. The 2027 cut-off date comes from the official page, but the opening date is from a secondary source.

**Sources:** <https://eurekanetwork.org/programmes-and-calls/eurostars/>, <https://www.hezelburcht.com/en/news/eurostars-call-12-opens-on-17-december-2026-for-international-sme-collaborations/>, <https://www.ffg.at/europa/ausschreibungen/eurostars3-11>

s%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20GitHub%20with%20M12%3A%20GitHub%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20Equity%20investment%3B%20fund%20started%20with%20a%20USD%2010%20million%20commitment%20to%20invest%20in%20eight%20to%20ten%20companies%20per%20year%20at%20pre-seed%20and%20seed%3B%20typically%20does%20not%20lead%20rounds.%20Per-company%20cheque%20size%20unconfirmed.%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Early-stage%2C%20open-source-first%20companies%20building%20developer%20tools%2C%20AI%2C%20cloud%20infrastructure%2C%20security%20and%20DevOps%2C%20raising%20pre-seed%20or%20seed%20venture%20capital.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20restricted%20on%20the%20official%20page.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%20startups%20%28equity%20investment%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fopen-source%2Fgithub-fund%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fopen-source%2Fgithub-fund%2Fcontact%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20interest%20via%20the%20GitHub%20Fund%20contact%20page%20%28%27Contact%20us%20to%20apply%27%29.%202.%20Introductory%20call% · [Program page](https://github.com/open-source/github-fund) · [Apply](https://github.com/open-source/github-fund/contact)

- **Funder:** GitHub with M12 (Microsoft's venture fund)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** Equity investment; fund started with a USD 10 million commitment to invest in eight to ten companies per year at pre-seed and seed; typically does not lead rounds. Per-company cheque size unconfirmed. (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Early-stage, open-source-first companies building developer tools, AI, cloud infrastructure, security and DevOps, raising pre-seed or seed venture capital.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: not restricted on the official page · **Entity types:** For-profit startups (equity investment)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: weeks, tied to the round's timeline
- **Obligations:** Equity stake; investor rights per the round's terms. Not a grant.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** ForkMesh is an open-source-first developer-tools company (Git collaboration, supply-chain-resilient mirroring, AI coding agents in worktrees) squarely inside GitHub Fund's stated focus areas. Pitch it when raising a pre-seed/seed round with a lead investor.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit interest via the GitHub Fund contact page ('Contact us to apply').
2. Introductory call with the fund team.
3. Diligence alongside the lead investor of the round.
4. Term sheet / participation in the round.

**What the application asks for:**

- Contact form submission (unconfirmed: fields)
- Pitch deck (standard for venture diligence; unconfirmed)
- Round details: size, lead investor, valuation (unconfirmed)
- Open source project traction (GitHub stars, contributors, adoption)

**Risks:** Dilutive equity, not a grant, and the fund rarely leads, so a lead investor is needed first. ForkMesh positions itself partly as an alternative to centralised hosts like GitHub, which may be a strategic conflict for a GitHub/Microsoft-backed fund. GitHub Accelerator (the requested program) appears dormant: no cohort or application call since 2024.

**Sources:** <https://github.com/open-source/accelerator>, <https://github.com/open-source/github-fund>, <https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/github-fund-2024-and-beyond-looking-to-the-future/>, <https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/powering-advancements-of-ai-in-the-open-apply-now-to-github-accelerator/>

0mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Software%20in%20the%20Public%20Interest%3A%20SPI%20Associated%20Project%20-%20chosen%20over%20Software%20Freedom%20Conservancy%2C%20which%20is%20selective%20and%20gives%20no%20current%20intake%20status%0AAward%3A%20No%20grant%3B%20free%20to%20join%20and%20remain.%20SPI%20deducts%20attributable%20transaction%20costs%20and%20then%205%25%20overhead%20from%20donations%3B%20the%20rest%20is%20held%20for%20the%20project.%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28vote%20at%20the%20monthly%20public%20SPI%20board%20meeting%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Genuine%2C%20substantial%20and%20significant%20free%20and%20open%20source%20community%20projects%3A%20an%20established%20development%20community%20and%20release%20history%2C%20plus%20a%20broad%20user%20base%20or%20political%2Fmarket%20importance.%20Board%20judges%20whether%20it%20is%20a%20genuine%20Free%20Software%20community%20project.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28SPI%20is%20a%20US%20501%28c%29%283%29%3B%20hosts%20international%20projects%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Free%20software%20community%20projects%20%28unincorporated%29%3B%20not%20a%20company%20itself.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.spi-inc.org%2Fprojects%2Fassociated-project-howto%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.spi-inc.org%2Fprojects%2Fassociated-project-howto%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Informal%20discussion%20with%20one%20or%20more%20SPI%20board%20members.%202.%20A%20satisfied%20board%20member%20agrees%20to%20sponsor%20a%20formal%20application.%203.%20Send%20the%20formal%20application%20to%20the%20sponsor.%204.%20Sponsor%20drafts%20a%20board%20resolution.%205.%20Discussion%20by%20board%20and%20contributing%20members%20%28at%20least%20three%20days%29.%206.%20Vote%20at%20the%20monthly%20public%20board%20meeting%20on%20IRC%20%28OFTC%20%23spi%29.%207.%20Project%20liaison%20accepts%20within%20the%20stated%20period%20%28usually%2060%20days%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20name%20and%20details%20%28community%2C%20release%20history%2C%20users%29%0A-%20List%20of%20SPI%20services%20the%20project%20needs%20%28donations%2C%20asset%20holding%2C%20trademarks%2C%20etc.%29%0A-%20Named%20liaison%20and%20the%20rules%20for%20selecting%20or%20electing%20replacements%0A-%20Disclosure%20of%20any%20existing%20contracts%2C%20liabilities%20or%20pending%20litigation%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%20one%20board%20cycle%20%28monthly%29%20after%20sponsor%20support%3B%20at%20least%20three%20days%20of%20discussion%20before%20the%20vote%0AObligations%3A%205%25%20overhead%20on%20donations%3B%20expenses%20approved%20via%20the%20SPI%20Treasurer%20request%20tracker%20with%20reimbursement%20paperwork%3B%20monthly%20public%20treasurer%20reports%3B%20liaison%20is%20the%20decision%20maker%20for%20project%20assets%3B%20project%20deemed%20defunct%20if%20no%20liaison%20for%20two%20years.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Make%20the%20open%20ForkMesh%20protocol%20and%20public%20mirror%20network%20an%20SPI%20associated%20project%20so%20its%20assets%20%28domain%2C%20trademark%20for%20the%20protocol%2C%20donations%20for%20the%20mirror%20fleet%20that%20preserves%20source%20code%29%20are%20held%20by%20a%20neutral%20nonprofit%2C%20independent%20of%20the%20startup.%20This%20gives%20donors%20and%20other%20funders%20a%20tax-deductible%2C%20community-governed%20home%20for%20the%20preservation%20commons.%0ARisks%3A%20SPI%20requires%20a%20substantial%2C%20established%20community%20and%20release%20history%3B%20a%20young%20startup-led%20project%20may%20be%20judged%20not%20yet%20significant.%20Commercial%20hosted%20service%20and%20crypto%20payout%20pool%20must%20stay%20outside%20SPI.%20The%20page%20does%20not%20state%20whether%20new%20projects%20are%20currently%20being%20accepted%20%28no%20pause%20announced%29.%20Software%20Freedom%20Conservancy%20reviews%20rolling%20but%20is%20selective%20and%20often%20refers%20projects%20elsewhere.)** · [Program page](https://www.spi-inc.org/projects/associated-project-howto/) · [Apply](https://www.spi-inc.org/projects/associated-project-howto/)

- **Funder:** Software in the Public Interest (SPI)
- **Category:** Preservation and digital commons
- **Amount:** No grant; free to join and remain. SPI deducts attributable transaction costs and then 5% overhead from donations; the rest is held for the project. (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (vote at the monthly public SPI board meeting) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Genuine, substantial and significant free and open source community projects: an established development community and release history, plus a broad user base or political/market importance. Board judges whether it is a genuine Free Software community project.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (SPI is a US 501(c)(3); hosts international projects) · **Entity types:** Free software community projects (unincorporated); not a company itself
- **Decision time:** About one board cycle (monthly) after sponsor support; at least three days of discussion before the vote
- **Obligations:** 5% overhead on donations; expenses approved via the SPI Treasurer request tracker with reimbursement paperwork; monthly public treasurer reports; liaison is the decision maker for project assets; project deemed defunct if no liaison for two years.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Make the open ForkMesh protocol and public mirror network an SPI associated project so its assets (domain, trademark for the protocol, donations for the mirror fleet that preserves source code) are held by a neutral nonprofit, independent of the startup. This gives donors and other funders a tax-deductible, community-governed home for the preservation commons.

**How to apply:**

1. Informal discussion with one or more SPI board members.
2. A satisfied board member agrees to sponsor a formal application.
3. Send the formal application to the sponsor.
4. Sponsor drafts a board resolution.
5. Discussion by board and contributing members (at least three days).
6. Vote at the monthly public board meeting on IRC (OFTC #spi).
7. Project liaison accepts within the stated period (usually 60 days).

**What the application asks for:**

- Project name and details (community, release history, users)
- List of SPI services the project needs (donations, asset holding, trademarks, etc.)
- Named liaison and the rules for selecting or electing replacements
- Disclosure of any existing contracts, liabilities or pending litigation

**Risks:** SPI requires a substantial, established community and release history; a young startup-led project may be judged not yet significant. Commercial hosted service and crypto payout pool must stay outside SPI. The page does not state whether new projects are currently being accepted (no pause announced). Software Freedom Conservancy reviews rolling but is selective and often refers projects elsewhere.

**Sources:** <https://www.spi-inc.org/projects/associated-project-howto/>, <https://sfconservancy.org/members/apply/>
