hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Zcash%20Community%20Grants%2C%20administered%20by%20the%20Financial%20Privacy%20Foundation%3A%20Zcash%20Community%20Grants%0AAward%3A%20No%20per-grant%20range%20stated%3B%20pool%20of%20about%20100%2C000%20ZEC%20%28stated%20as%20roughly%20%2411M%20USD%20at%20%24110%20ZECUSD%29%3B%20requests%20over%20%2450%2C000%20USD%20trigger%20KYC%20and%20more%20milestones%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28applications%20reviewed%20at%20bi-weekly%20committee%20meetings%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Individuals%2C%20teams%20and%20organisations%20proposing%20work%20that%20benefits%20the%20Zcash%20ecosystem%20in%20wallets%2C%20SDK%2C%20core%2C%20interoperability%2C%20Zapps%2C%20ongoing%20services%20or%20education%2C%20or%20answering%20an%20open%20RFP.%20FPF%20checks%20eligibility%20first%3B%20detailed%20eligibility%20criteria%20not%20published%20on%20the%20process%20page.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20sanctioned%20jurisdictions%20excluded%20via%20FPF%20KYC%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20%28may%20stay%20publicly%20pseudonymous%29%2C%20teams%2C%20for-profit%20and%20nonprofit%20organisations.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fzcashcommunitygrants.org%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fzcashcommunitygrants.org%2Fselection%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Complete%20the%20ZCG%20grant%20application%20GitHub%20issue%20form%20%28grant_application.yaml%29%20with%20all%20required%20fields.%202.%20Post%20a%20link%20to%20it%20on%20the%20Zcash%20Community%20Forum%20grants%20category%20%28required%29.%203.%20FPF%20eligibility%20review.%204.%20At%20least%20one%20week%20of%20community%20review%20and%20due%20diligence.%205.%20Grant%20Committee%20review%20at%20its%20bi-weekly%20meeting%20%28may%20consult%20outside%20experts%29.%206.%20FPF%20works%20with%20the%20applicant%20to%20refine%20milestones%20and%20KPIs.%207.%20Committee%20approval%20by%20simple%20majority%20%283%20of%205%29%2C%20noted%20in%20published%20minutes.%208.%20FPF%20handles%20KYC%20%28required%20over%20%2450%2C000%29%20and%20the%20grant%20agreement.%209.%20Milestone%20payouts%20after%20forum%20updates.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20GitHub%20issue%20grant%20application%20form%20%28project%20summary%2C%20problem%2C%20solution%2C%20team%2C%20budget%2C%20milestones%20and%20deliverables%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20field%20list%29%0A-%20Zcash%20Community%20Forum%20post%20linking%20the%20application%0A-%20Milestones%20with%20KPIs%20%28more%20detail%20for%20larger%20requests%29%0A-%20KYC%20documents%20for%20fiduciaries%20of%20grants%20over%20%2450%2C000%20%28handled%20by%20FPF%29%0A-%20Signed%20grant%20agreement%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Usually%20a%20few%20weeks%3A%20minimum%20one%20week%20community%20review%2C%20then%20bi-weekly%20committee%20meetings%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone-based%20payouts%20released%20only%20after%20forum%20updates%3B%20monthly%20forum%20status%20updates%20appreciated%3B%20KYC%20over%20%2450%2C000%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20payment%20currency%20%28ZEC%20or%20USD-equivalent%29%20and%20licensing%20terms.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20%28a%29%20a%20resilient%2C%20signed%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20set%20for%20the%20Zcash%20ecosystem%27s%20core%20repositories%20%28zcashd%2FZebra%2C%20librustzcash%2C%20wallets%29%20so%20Zcash%20source%20stays%20reachable%20if%20a%20host%20censors%20it%2C%20and%20%28b%29%20shielded-ZEC%20payouts%20in%20the%20ForkMesh%20contribution%20pool%20so%20contributors%20can%20be%20paid%20privately.%20The%20ongoing-services%20and%20interoperability%20categories%20match%20this.%0ARisks%3A%20Committee%20funds%20Zcash-specific%20work%2C%20so%20a%20generic%20Git-hosting%20project%20must%20show%20clear%20Zcash%20benefit%3B%20public%20forum%20review%20exposes%20the%20proposal%20to%20community%20scrutiny%3B%20payment%20currency%20unconfirmed%20%28ZEC%20volatility%20possible%29%3B%20ForkMesh%20uses%20Solana%20today%2C%20so%20a%20ZEC%20payout%20integration%20would%20be%20new%20scope.)** · [Program page](https://zcashcommunitygrants.org/) · [Apply](https://zcashcommunitygrants.org/selection/)

- **Funder:** Zcash Community Grants (ZCG), administered by the Financial Privacy Foundation (FPF)
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** No per-grant range stated; pool of about 100,000 ZEC (stated as roughly $11M USD at $110 ZECUSD); requests over $50,000 USD trigger KYC and more milestones (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (applications reviewed at bi-weekly committee meetings) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Individuals, teams and organisations proposing work that benefits the Zcash ecosystem in wallets, SDK, core, interoperability, Zapps, ongoing services or education, or answering an open RFP. FPF checks eligibility first; detailed eligibility criteria not published on the process page.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: sanctioned jurisdictions excluded via FPF KYC) · **Entity types:** Individuals (may stay publicly pseudonymous), teams, for-profit and nonprofit organisations
- **Decision time:** Usually a few weeks: minimum one week community review, then bi-weekly committee meetings
- **Obligations:** Milestone-based payouts released only after forum updates; monthly forum status updates appreciated; KYC over $50,000; unconfirmed: payment currency (ZEC or USD-equivalent) and licensing terms.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose (a) a resilient, signed ForkMesh mirror set for the Zcash ecosystem's core repositories (zcashd/Zebra, librustzcash, wallets) so Zcash source stays reachable if a host censors it, and (b) shielded-ZEC payouts in the ForkMesh contribution pool so contributors can be paid privately. The ongoing-services and interoperability categories match this.

**How to apply:**

1. Complete the ZCG grant application GitHub issue form (grant_application.yaml) with all required fields.
2. Post a link to it on the Zcash Community Forum grants category (required).
3. FPF eligibility review.
4. At least one week of community review and due diligence.
5. Grant Committee review at its bi-weekly meeting (may consult outside experts).
6. FPF works with the applicant to refine milestones and KPIs.
7. Committee approval by simple majority (3 of 5), noted in published minutes.
8. FPF handles KYC (required over $50,000) and the grant agreement.
9. Milestone payouts after forum updates.

**What the application asks for:**

- GitHub issue grant application form (project summary, problem, solution, team, budget, milestones and deliverables; unconfirmed: exact field list)
- Zcash Community Forum post linking the application
- Milestones with KPIs (more detail for larger requests)
- KYC documents for fiduciaries of grants over $50,000 (handled by FPF)
- Signed grant agreement

**Risks:** Committee funds Zcash-specific work, so a generic Git-hosting project must show clear Zcash benefit; public forum review exposes the proposal to community scrutiny; payment currency unconfirmed (ZEC volatility possible); ForkMesh uses Solana today, so a ZEC payout integration would be new scope.

**Sources:** <https://zcashcommunitygrants.org/>, <https://zcashcommunitygrants.org/selection/>, <https://forum.zcashcommunity.com/c/grants/33>

<a id="sloan-technology-open-source-science"></a>
## 60. Alfred P. Sloan Foundation: Technology program: Open Source in Science

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Alfred%20P.%20Sloan%20Foundation%3A%20Technology%20program%3A%20Open%20Source%20in%20Science.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fsloan.org%2Fprograms%2Fdigital-technology%2Fopen-source-in-science%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fsloan.org%2Fprograms%2Fdigital-technology%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Alfred%20P.%20Sloan%20Foundation%3A%20Technology%20program%3A%20Open%20Source%20in%20Science%0AAward%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20most%20Technology%20program%20grants%20USD%2050K%20-%20600K%20%28per%20a%202026%20university%20funding%20listing%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28letters%20of%20inquiry%20accepted%20year-round%3B%20unconfirmed%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Projects%20building%20tools%2C%20norms%20and%20institutions%20for%20open-source%20research%20software%2C%20with%20stated%20focus%20on%20publication%20and%20archiving%20of%20research%20software%20and%20linking%20it%20to%20articles%20and%20data%3B%20mainly%20not%20individual%20project%20development.%20unconfirmed%3A%20Sloan%20generally%20grants%20to%20nonprofits%2Funiversities.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20primarily%20US%2C%20international%20possible.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20nonprofits%20and%20universities%20%28for-profit%20rarely%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsloan.org%2Fprograms%2Fdigital-technology%2Fopen-source-in-science%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsloan.org%2Fprograms%2Fdigital-technology%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Email%20a%20two-page%20letter%20of%20inquiry%20to%20the%20Technology%20program.%202.%20If%20invited%2C%20submit%20a%20full%20proposal%20with%20budget.%203.%20Program%20staff%20and%20board%20review.%204.%20Grant%20agreement.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Two-page%20letter%20of%20inquiry%0A-%20If%20invited%3A%20full%20proposal%20following%20Sloan%20proposal%20guidelines%0A-%20If%20invited%3A%20budget%20and%20budget%20justification%0A-%20If%20invited%3A%20organizational%20documents%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20LOIs%20reviewed%20about%20quarterly%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20narrative%20and%20financial%20reports%3B%20Sloan%20typically%20expects%20open%20outputs.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%2C%20with%20an%20academic%20or%20archive%20partner%2C%20ForkMesh-based%20preservation%20of%20research%20software%20together%20with%20its%20review%20and%20issue%20history%20as%20signed%20in-repo%20records%2C%20enabling%20citable%2C%20durable%20archives%20that%20complement%20Software%20Heritage.%20Fits%20Sloan%27s%20interest%20in%20software%20as%20a%20first-class%2C%20archived%20research%20output.%0ARisks%3A%20sloan.org%20blocked%20automated%20fetch%20today%20%28403%29%2C%20so%20current%20terms%20are%20unverified.%20Software%20Heritage%20itself%20offers%20mirror-hosting%20partnerships%20but%20no%20funding.%20Science%20focus%20may%20be%20a%20stretch%20for%20a%20general%20developer%20tool%3B%20likely%20needs%20a%20nonprofit%20or%20university%20lead.)** · [Program page](https://sloan.org/programs/digital-technology/open-source-in-science) · [Apply](https://sloan.org/programs/digital-technology)

- **Funder:** Alfred P. Sloan Foundation
- **Category:** Preservation and digital commons
- **Amount:** unconfirmed: most Technology program grants USD 50K - 600K (per a 2026 university funding listing) (about $50,000 - $600,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (letters of inquiry accepted year-round; unconfirmed) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 35/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Projects building tools, norms and institutions for open-source research software, with stated focus on publication and archiving of research software and linking it to articles and data; mainly not individual project development. unconfirmed: Sloan generally grants to nonprofits/universities.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: primarily US, international possible · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: nonprofits and universities (for-profit rarely)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: LOIs reviewed about quarterly
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: narrative and financial reports; Sloan typically expects open outputs.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose, with an academic or archive partner, ForkMesh-based preservation of research software together with its review and issue history as signed in-repo records, enabling citable, durable archives that complement Software Heritage. Fits Sloan's interest in software as a first-class, archived research output.

**How to apply:**

1. Email a two-page letter of inquiry to the Technology program.
2. If invited, submit a full proposal with budget.
3. Program staff and board review.
4. Grant agreement.

**What the application asks for:**

- Two-page letter of inquiry
- If invited: full proposal following Sloan proposal guidelines
- If invited: budget and budget justification
- If invited: organizational documents

**Risks:** sloan.org blocked automated fetch today (403), so current terms are unverified. Software Heritage itself offers mirror-hosting partnerships but no funding. Science focus may be a stretch for a general developer tool; likely needs a nonprofit or university lead.

**Sources:** <https://sloan.org/programs/digital-technology/open-source-in-science>, <https://csde.washington.edu/news-events/new-alfred-p-sloan-foundation-technology-program-rolling/>, <https://www.softwareheritage.org/mirrors>

o%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Institute%20of%20Museum%20and%20Library%20Services%3A%20National%20Leadership%20Grants%20for%20Libraries%20FY2027%0AAward%3A%20Planning%20USD%2075%2C000-200%2C000%3B%20Forum%20USD%2075%2C000-200%2C000%3B%20Community-Centered%20Implementation%20USD%2025%2C000-100%2C000%3B%20National%20Implementation%20USD%2075%2C000-1%2C000%2C000%20%281%3A1%20cost%20share%29%3B%20Applied%20Research%20USD%2075%2C000-750%2C000.%20~USD%2011.5M%20total%2C%20~45%20awards%0ADeadline%3A%202026-11-13%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20U.S.%20libraries%2C%20archives%2C%20digital%20libraries%2C%20library%2Farchives%20consortia%20and%20associations%2C%20academic%20units%20%28e.g.%20LIS%20schools%29%20and%20affiliated%20nonprofits%20that%20are%20a%20unit%20of%20state%2Flocal%2Ftribal%20government%20or%20a%20private%20nonprofit.%20For-profits%20cannot%20apply%20but%20can%20be%20partners%2Fcontractors.%20Geography%3A%20US%20only%20%28incl.%20territories%20and%20Freely%20Associated%20States%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Nonprofit%20or%20government%20libraries%2Farchives%20and%20affiliated%20nonprofits%3B%20for-profit%20only%20as%20partner%2Fsubcontractor.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fsimpler.grants.gov%2Fopportunity%2F363766%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.grants.gov%2Fsearch-results-detail%2F363766%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Partner%20with%20an%20eligible%20U.S.%20library%2Farchive%20as%20lead%20applicant.%202.%20Ensure%20SAM.gov%2FUEI%20and%20Grants.gov%20registration.%203.%20Choose%20one%20project%20type.%204.%20Submit%20full%20application%20through%20Grants.gov%20by%2011%3A59%20p.m.%20ET%20on%2013%20Nov%202026.%205.%20Peer%20review.%206.%20Notification%20expected%20July%202027%3B%20projects%20start%201%20Aug%202027.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20SF-424S%20Application%20for%20Federal%20Domestic%20Assistance%20%28short%20form%29%0A-%20IMLS%20Library%20Discretionary%20Program%20Information%20Form%0A-%20Abstract%0A-%20Narrative%20%28max%2010%20pages%29%0A-%20Schedule%20of%20Completion%20%281%20page%20per%20year%20recommended%29%0A-%20IMLS%20Budget%20Form%0A-%20Budget%20Justification%0A-%20Resumes%20of%20key%20staff%20and%20consultants%20%282%20pages%20each%20recommended%2C%20with%20ORCID%29%0A-%20Digital%20Products%20Plan%20%282%20pages%20recommended%29%0A-%20Performance%20Measurement%20Plan%20%282%20pages%20recommended%29%0A-%20Proof%20of%20nonprofit%20status%20%28IRS%20letter%29%20if%20applicable%0A-%20Federally%20negotiated%20indirect%20cost%20rate%20agreement%20if%20used%0A-%20Public%20Access%20Plan%20for%20research%20projects%0A-%20Optional%3A%20letters%20of%20participation%2Fsupport%2C%20planning%20reports%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%208%20months%20%28notification%20July%202027%29%0AObligations%3A%20Federal%20award%20terms%3A%20performance%20and%20financial%20reporting%2C%20cost%20share%20for%20National%20Implementation%20%281%3A1%29%2C%20openly%20available%20digital%20products%20per%20the%20Digital%20Products%20Plan%2C%20IMLS%20assurances%20and%20certifications.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20With%20a%20university%20library%20or%20software-preservation%20partner%20as%20lead%2C%20propose%20a%20Planning%20or%20Applied%20Research%20project%20on%20using%20signed%2C%20decentralized%20Git%20mirrors%20%28ForkMesh%29%20to%20preserve%20research%20and%20cultural-heritage%20software%20and%20its%20collaboration%20history%20%28%27preserving%20the%20national%20information%20infrastructure%27%29.%20ForkMesh%20supplies%20the%20open-source%20tooling%20as%20a%20subcontractor.%0ARisks%3A%20ForkMesh%20cannot%20be%20lead%20applicant%3B%20needs%20a%20committed%20U.S.%20library%20partner%20within%20four%20weeks.%20The%20President%27s%20FY2027%20budget%20request%20proposes%20zero%20for%20IMLS%2C%20so%20awards%20are%20subject%20to%20appropriations.%20Chosen%20over%20Mellon%2C%20whose%20Public%20Knowledge%20program%20funds%20nonprofits%20and%20is%20understood%20to%20be%20invitation-driven.)** · [Program page](https://simpler.grants.gov/opportunity/363766) · [Apply](https://www.grants.gov/search-results-detail/363766)

- **Funder:** Institute of Museum and Library Services (IMLS)
- **Category:** Preservation and digital commons
- **Amount:** Planning USD 75,000-200,000; Forum USD 75,000-200,000; Community-Centered Implementation USD 25,000-100,000; National Implementation USD 75,000-1,000,000 (1:1 cost share); Applied Research USD 75,000-750,000. ~USD 11.5M total, ~45 awards (about $25,000 - $1,000,000)
- **Deadline:** 2026-11-13 (open)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** U.S. libraries, archives, digital libraries, library/archives consortia and associations, academic units (e.g. LIS schools) and affiliated nonprofits that are a unit of state/local/tribal government or a private nonprofit. For-profits cannot apply but can be partners/contractors.
- **Geography:** US only (incl. territories and Freely Associated States) · **Entity types:** Nonprofit or government libraries/archives and affiliated nonprofits; for-profit only as partner/subcontractor
- **Decision time:** About 8 months (notification July 2027)
- **Obligations:** Federal award terms: performance and financial reporting, cost share for National Implementation (1:1), openly available digital products per the Digital Products Plan, IMLS assurances and certifications.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** With a university library or software-preservation partner as lead, propose a Planning or Applied Research project on using signed, decentralized Git mirrors (ForkMesh) to preserve research and cultural-heritage software and its collaboration history ('preserving the national information infrastructure'). ForkMesh supplies the open-source tooling as a subcontractor.

**How to apply:**

1. Partner with an eligible U.S. library/archive as lead applicant.
2. Ensure SAM.gov/UEI and Grants.gov registration.
3. Choose one project type.
4. Submit full application through Grants.gov by 11:59 p.m. ET on 13 Nov 2026.
5. Peer review.
6. Notification expected July 2027; projects start 1 Aug 2027.

**What the application asks for:**

- SF-424S Application for Federal Domestic Assistance (short form)
- IMLS Library Discretionary Program Information Form
- Abstract
- Narrative (max 10 pages)
- Schedule of Completion (1 page per year recommended)
- IMLS Budget Form
- Budget Justification
- Resumes of key staff and consultants (2 pages each recommended, with ORCID)
- Digital Products Plan (2 pages recommended)
- Performance Measurement Plan (2 pages recommended)
- Proof of nonprofit status (IRS letter) if applicable
- Federally negotiated indirect cost rate agreement if used
- Public Access Plan for research projects
- Optional: letters of participation/support, planning reports

**Risks:** ForkMesh cannot be lead applicant; needs a committed U.S. library partner within four weeks. The President's FY2027 budget request proposes zero for IMLS, so awards are subject to appropriations. Chosen over Mellon, whose Public Knowledge program funds nonprofits and is understood to be invitation-driven.

**Sources:** <https://simpler.grants.gov/opportunity/363766>, <https://files.simpler.grants.gov/opportunities/1bbe7a77-aeff-4653-8602-b864c237ba91/attachments/e01ef773-7dab-4523-9317-d2a651350746/fy27-ols-nlgl-NOFO.pdf>

der%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Internet%20Society%20Foundation%3A%20Research%20Grant%20Programme%0AAward%3A%20Independent%20researchers%20up%20to%20US%24200%2C000%3B%20organizations%20and%20institutions%20up%20to%20US%24500%2C000%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20call%20expected%20~April-May%202027%20%282026%20window%20was%207%20Apr%20-%2022%20May%202026%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%202027%20dates%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Independent%20researchers%20with%20a%20postgraduate%20degree%20%28PhD%20or%20Masters%29%20and%20peer-reviewed%20publications%2Fpatents%2C%20or%20public%20research%20institutions%20and%20public%20universities%20that%20are%20501%28c%29%283%29%20or%20equivalent.%20Private%20institutions%20are%20not%20eligible%3B%20research%20must%20be%20applied%20and%20published%20openly.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Independent%20researchers%20%28individuals%29%2C%20public%20universities%20%2F%20public%20research%20institutions%3B%20not%20for-profit%20companies.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.isocfoundation.org%2Fgrant-programme%2Fresearch%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.isocfoundation.org%2Fgrant-programme%2Fresearch%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Register%20in%20the%20Fluxx%20grants%20system%20at%20least%2024%20hours%20before%20the%20deadline.%202.%20Attend%20info%20sessions%20%28optional%29.%203.%20Submit%20full%20application%20in%20Fluxx%20%28English%2C%20French%20or%20Spanish%29.%204.%20External%20review%20against%20thematic%20fit%2C%20novelty%2C%20applied%20relevance%2C%20collaboration%20and%20intersectional%20approach.%205.%20Award%20and%20grant%20agreement.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Fluxx%20registration%20and%20organization%2Fresearcher%20profile%0A-%20Full%20application%20form%20%28example%20form%20published%20on%20the%20page%29%0A-%20Research%20proposal%20aligned%20to%20one%20theme%0A-%20Budget%0A-%20CV%2Fpublications%20for%20independent%20researchers%0A-%20Human-subjects%20ethics%20certification%20if%20applicable%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20several%20months%20after%20the%20May%20deadline%0AObligations%3A%20Principal%20investigator%20manages%20the%20grant%20and%20all%20reporting%3B%20research%20outputs%20published%20openly%20at%20no%20cost.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Partner%20with%20a%20public%20university%20or%20an%20independent%20researcher%20to%20measure%20how%20decentralized%20Git%20mirroring%20affects%20availability%20of%20critical%20open-source%20code%20during%20outages%2C%20takedowns%20and%20censorship%20events%2C%20under%20the%20%27Trustworthy%20Internet%27%20theme.%20The%20Foundation%20cares%20about%20resilient%2C%20trustworthy%20internet%20infrastructure%20and%20open%20data.%0ARisks%3A%20ForkMesh%20as%20a%20company%20cannot%20apply%3B%20needs%20a%20public-university%20partner%20or%20an%20independent%20researcher%20lead.%20Program%20is%20research%2C%20not%20product%20development.%20The%20Common%20Good%20Cyber%20Fund%20%282026%20window%2023%20Jun%20-%204%20Aug%29%20is%20nonprofit-only.)** · [Program page](https://www.isocfoundation.org/grant-programme/research/) · [Apply](https://www.isocfoundation.org/grant-programme/research/)

- **Funder:** Internet Society Foundation
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** Independent researchers up to US$200,000; organizations and institutions up to US$500,000 (about $500,000)
- **Deadline:** Next call expected ~April-May 2027 (2026 window was 7 Apr - 22 May 2026; unconfirmed: 2027 dates) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Independent researchers with a postgraduate degree (PhD or Masters) and peer-reviewed publications/patents, or public research institutions and public universities that are 501(c)(3) or equivalent. Private institutions are not eligible; research must be applied and published openly.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Independent researchers (individuals), public universities / public research institutions; not for-profit companies
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: several months after the May deadline
- **Obligations:** Principal investigator manages the grant and all reporting; research outputs published openly at no cost.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Partner with a public university or an independent researcher to measure how decentralized Git mirroring affects availability of critical open-source code during outages, takedowns and censorship events, under the 'Trustworthy Internet' theme. The Foundation cares about resilient, trustworthy internet infrastructure and open data.

**How to apply:**

1. Register in the Fluxx grants system at least 24 hours before the deadline.
2. Attend info sessions (optional).
3. Submit full application in Fluxx (English, French or Spanish).
4. External review against thematic fit, novelty, applied relevance, collaboration and intersectional approach.
5. Award and grant agreement.

**What the application asks for:**

- Fluxx registration and organization/researcher profile
- Full application form (example form published on the page)
- Research proposal aligned to one theme
- Budget
- CV/publications for independent researchers
- Human-subjects ethics certification if applicable

**Risks:** ForkMesh as a company cannot apply; needs a public-university partner or an independent researcher lead. Program is research, not product development. The Common Good Cyber Fund (2026 window 23 Jun - 4 Aug) is nonprofit-only.

**Sources:** <https://www.isocfoundation.org/grant-programme/research/>, <https://www.isocfoundation.org/grant-programme/>, <https://www.isocfoundation.org/grant-programme/common-good-cyber-fund/>

working%20prototype%2C%20open-source%20frontier%20tech%20benefiting%20children%2C%20and%20publicly%20accessible%20real-time%20data.%20Geography%3A%20DPG%3A%20Worldwide.%20Venture%20Fund%3A%20companies%20registered%20in%20UNICEF%20programme%20countries%20%28emerging%20economies%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20DPG%3A%20any%20owner.%20Venture%20Fund%3A%20private%20for-profit%20companies%20only%20%28no%20NGOs%2C%20universities%2C%20individuals%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.unicefventurefund.org%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.digitalpublicgoods.net%2Fsubmission-guide%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Take%20the%20DPG%20eligibility%20test%20%28~5%20min%29.%202.%20Submit%20DPG%20application%20with%20evidence%20for%20all%209%20indicators%3B%20DPGA%20reviews%20within%20~30%20days%3B%20renew%20annually.%203.%20For%20UNICEF%3A%20watch%20for%20a%20matching%20open%20call%2C%20submit%20online%20Expression%20of%20Interest.%204.%20Shortlisted%20companies%20submit%20a%20Request%20for%20Proposal%20%28months%202-3%29.%205.%2030-minute%20demo%20interview%20%28months%204-5%29.%206.%20Investment%20decision%20%28month%206%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20DPG%3A%20SDG%20relevance%20with%20specific%20targets%0A-%20DPG%3A%20public%20link%20to%20approved%20open%20licence%0A-%20DPG%3A%20ownership%2Fcopyright%2Ftrademark%20evidence%0A-%20DPG%3A%20platform%20independence%20statement%0A-%20DPG%3A%20documentation%20links%0A-%20DPG%3A%20data%20extraction%2Fexport%20mechanisms%0A-%20DPG%3A%20privacy%20policy%20and%20terms%20%28if%20personal%20data%29%0A-%20DPG%3A%20standards%20and%20best%20practices%20list%0A-%20DPG%3A%20do-no-harm%20safeguards%0A-%20UNICEF%3A%20Expression%20of%20Interest%20form%2C%20then%20RFP%20with%20prototype%20evidence%20and%20plan%2C%20demo%20interview%2C%20vendor%20registration%0ADecision%20time%3A%20DPG%20~30%20days%3B%20UNICEF%20Venture%20Fund%20about%206%20months%20from%20EOI%20to%20decision%0AObligations%3A%20DPG%3A%20annual%20renewal.%20UNICEF%3A%20open-source%20licence%20by%20month%206%2C%20publicly%20share%20real-time%20data%2C%20institutional%20contract%20and%20UNICEF%20vendor%20registration%3B%20no%20equity%20taken.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Register%20the%20ForkMesh%20protocol%20and%20self-hosting%20node%20as%20a%20Digital%20Public%20Good%20%28SDG%209%20resilient%20infrastructure%20%2F%20SDG%2016%20access%20to%20information%29%2C%20which%20strengthens%20every%20later%20commons%20grant.%20A%20UNICEF%20call%20would%20only%20fit%20if%20framed%20around%20preserving%20and%20serving%20open-source%20child-focused%20digital%20public%20goods%20in%20low-connectivity%20countries.%0ARisks%3A%20UNICEF%20funding%20requires%20a%20for-profit%20company%20registered%20in%20a%20programme%20country%20and%20a%20child-impact%20focus%3B%20ForkMesh%27s%20entity%20country%20is%20unknown%20and%20the%20child%20angle%20is%20weak.%20DPG%20listing%20gives%20recognition%2C%20not%20money%3B%20the%20hosted%20paid%20tier%20must%20not%20undermine%20the%20platform-independence%20indicator.)** · [Program page](https://www.unicefventurefund.org/) · [Apply](https://www.digitalpublicgoods.net/submission-guide)

- **Funder:** Digital Public Goods Alliance / UNICEF
- **Category:** Preservation and digital commons
- **Amount:** UNICEF Venture Fund early-stage: up to USD 100,000 equity-free seed funding (USD and/or cryptocurrency); growth-stage up to USD 400,000 by invitation. DPG registry listing is free recognition (no funding). (about $50,000 - $100,000)
- **Deadline:** DPG registry: Rolling. UNICEF Venture Fund: thematic calls several times a year (current calls are femtech, health tech, climate; AI/blockchain for data integrity call closed) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** DPG: any open-source solution meeting the 9 DPG Standard indicators, submitted by an authorised representative. UNICEF Venture Fund early stage: private for-profit companies registered in a UNICEF programme country with a working prototype, open-source frontier tech benefiting children, and publicly accessible real-time data.
- **Geography:** DPG: Worldwide. Venture Fund: companies registered in UNICEF programme countries (emerging economies) · **Entity types:** DPG: any owner. Venture Fund: private for-profit companies only (no NGOs, universities, individuals)
- **Decision time:** DPG ~30 days; UNICEF Venture Fund about 6 months from EOI to decision
- **Obligations:** DPG: annual renewal. UNICEF: open-source licence by month 6, publicly share real-time data, institutional contract and UNICEF vendor registration; no equity taken.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Register the ForkMesh protocol and self-hosting node as a Digital Public Good (SDG 9 resilient infrastructure / SDG 16 access to information), which strengthens every later commons grant. A UNICEF call would only fit if framed around preserving and serving open-source child-focused digital public goods in low-connectivity countries.

**How to apply:**

1. Take the DPG eligibility test (~5 min).
2. Submit DPG application with evidence for all 9 indicators; DPGA reviews within ~30 days; renew annually.
3. For UNICEF: watch for a matching open call, submit online Expression of Interest.
4. Shortlisted companies submit a Request for Proposal (months 2-3).
5. 30-minute demo interview (months 4-5).
6. Investment decision (month 6).

**What the application asks for:**

- DPG: SDG relevance with specific targets
- DPG: public link to approved open licence
- DPG: ownership/copyright/trademark evidence
- DPG: platform independence statement
- DPG: documentation links
- DPG: data extraction/export mechanisms
- DPG: privacy policy and terms (if personal data)
- DPG: standards and best practices list
- DPG: do-no-harm safeguards
- UNICEF: Expression of Interest form, then RFP with prototype evidence and plan, demo interview, vendor registration

**Risks:** UNICEF funding requires a for-profit company registered in a programme country and a child-impact focus; ForkMesh's entity country is unknown and the child angle is weak. DPG listing gives recognition, not money; the hosted paid tier must not undermine the platform-independence indicator.

**Sources:** <https://www.digitalpublicgoods.net/submission-guide>, <https://www.unicefventurefund.org/>, <https://www.unicef.org/innovation/venturefund>, <https://www.unicefventurefund.org/call/ai-and-blockchain-data-and-trust>

## 64. Vinnova: Innovative Startups 2026

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Vinnova%3A%20Innovative%20Startups%202026.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.vinnova.se%2Fen%2Fcalls-for-proposals%2Finnovative-startups%2Finnovative-startups-2026%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.vinnova.se%2Fen%2Fcalls-for-proposals%2Finnovative-startups%2Finnovative-startups-2026%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Vinnova%3A%20Innovative%20Startups%202026%0AAward%3A%20Maximum%20SEK%20500%2C000%2C%20up%20to%20100%25%20of%20project%20costs%2C%20max%209%20months%0ADeadline%3A%202027-01-12%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Swedish%20limited%20companies%20%28aktiebolag%29%20operating%20in%20Sweden%3A%20unlisted%2C%20no%20profit%20distributed%20last%20year%2C%20net%20turnover%20or%20balance%20sheet%20total%20of%20SEK%2010%20million%20or%20less%20%28only%20one%20may%20be%20exceeded%29%2C%20max%2010%20FTE%20employees%2C%20at%20most%2010%20years%20old%20on%2023%20March%202027.%20No%20funding%20from%20Innovative%20Startups%202025.%20One%20proposal%20per%20company.%20Geography%3A%20Sweden.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Swedish%20limited%20companies%20%28aktiebolag%29%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.vinnova.se%2Fen%2Fcalls-for-proposals%2Finnovative-startups%2Finnovative-startups-2026%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.vinnova.se%2Fen%2Fcalls-for-proposals%2Finnovative-startups%2Finnovative-startups-2026%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Attend%20an%20info%20meeting%20%282%20or%209%20November%202026%29.%202.%20Apply%20in%20Vinnova%27s%20portal%20in%20Swedish%20or%20English%20by%2012%20January%202027%2C%2014%3A00.%203.%20Three%20experts%20score%20potential%2C%20implementability%20and%20team.%204.%20Proposals%20are%20rejected%20or%20placed%20in%20a%20randomised%20group%20when%20the%20budget%20is%20short.%205.%20Decision%20on%2023%20March%202027%3B%20project%20starts%20by%201%20April%202027.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Vinnova%20application%20form%20covering%20the%20project%2C%20participants%20and%20budget%0A-%20Budget%20by%20cost%20type%20%28personnel%2C%20equipment%2C%20consultancy%20and%20licensing%20with%20justification%2C%20other%20direct%20costs%2C%20indirect%20costs%20at%20most%2030%25%20of%20wage%20costs%29%0A-%20Company%20eligibility%20information%20%28size%2C%20turnover%2C%20age%2C%20ownership%29%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20de%20minimis%20declaration%20for%20companies%20aged%205-10%20years%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Decision%20on%2023%20March%202027%20%28about%2010%20weeks%20after%20close%29%0AObligations%3A%20Sign%20the%20grant%20agreement%3B%20report%20progress%2C%20costs%20and%20results%3B%20follow%20the%20rules%20on%20use%20of%20results%3B%20repay%20if%20paid%20too%20much%20or%20if%20terms%20are%20breached%3B%20GBER%20Article%2022%20or%20de%20minimis%20state-aid%20rules%20apply.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20If%20ForkMesh%20is%20a%20young%20Swedish%20AB%2C%20test%20customer%20needs%20and%20willingness%20to%20pay%20for%20the%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20%28managed%20mirrors%2C%20runner%20tier%29%20with%20European%20development%20teams.%20Vinnova%20funds%20early%20market%20validation%20of%20innovations%20that%20can%20compete%20globally.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20a%20small%2C%20young%20Swedish%20AB.%20Selection%20is%20partly%20by%20lottery.%20Small%20amount.)** · [Program page](https://www.vinnova.se/en/calls-for-proposals/innovative-startups/innovative-startups-2026) · [Apply](https://www.vinnova.se/en/calls-for-proposals/innovative-startups/innovative-startups-2026)

- **Funder:** Vinnova (Sweden's Innovation Agency)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Maximum SEK 500,000, up to 100% of project costs, max 9 months (about $52,000)
- **Deadline:** 2027-01-12 (open)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Swedish limited companies (aktiebolag) operating in Sweden: unlisted, no profit distributed last year, net turnover or balance sheet total of SEK 10 million or less (only one may be exceeded), max 10 FTE employees, at most 10 years old on 23 March 2027. No funding from Innovative Startups 2025. One proposal per company.
- **Geography:** Sweden · **Entity types:** Swedish limited companies (aktiebolag) only
- **Decision time:** Decision on 23 March 2027 (about 10 weeks after close)
- **Obligations:** Sign the grant agreement; report progress, costs and results; follow the rules on use of results; repay if paid too much or if terms are breached; GBER Article 22 or de minimis state-aid rules apply.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** If ForkMesh is a young Swedish AB, test customer needs and willingness to pay for the bounded hosted services (managed mirrors, runner tier) with European development teams. Vinnova funds early market validation of innovations that can compete globally.

**How to apply:**

1. Attend an info meeting (2 or 9 November 2026).
2. Apply in Vinnova's portal in Swedish or English by 12 January 2027, 14:00.
3. Three experts score potential, implementability and team.
4. Proposals are rejected or placed in a randomised group when the budget is short.
5. Decision on 23 March 2027; project starts by 1 April 2027.

**What the application asks for:**

- Vinnova application form covering the project, participants and budget
- Budget by cost type (personnel, equipment, consultancy and licensing with justification, other direct costs, indirect costs at most 30% of wage costs)
- Company eligibility information (size, turnover, age, ownership)
- unconfirmed: de minimis declaration for companies aged 5-10 years

**Risks:** Needs a small, young Swedish AB. Selection is partly by lottery. Small amount.

**Sources:** <https://www.vinnova.se/en/calls-for-proposals/innovative-startups/innovative-startups-2026>, <https://indiantelevision.com/regulators/meity-allocates-rs-1-81-crore-under-samridh-programme/>, <https://rigzen.in/startup-india-seed-fund-scheme-deadline-extended-to-june-30-2026/>

.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20ARIN%20Community%20Grant%20Program%0AAward%3A%20US%241%2C000%20-%20US%2420%2C000%20per%20award%3B%20US%2450%2C000%20total%20budget%20in%202026%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20window%20expected%20~April-June%202027%20%282026%20window%20was%2016%20Apr%20-%2014%20Jun%202026%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%202027%20dates%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Organizations%20%28not%20individuals%29%2C%20with%20nonprofits%2C%20NGOs%2C%20academic%2Fresearch%20institutions%20and%20open-source%20groups%20encouraged.%20Projects%20must%20be%20noncommercial%2C%20serve%20the%20public%20good%2C%20and%20broadly%20benefit%20the%20ARIN%20service%20region%20in%20one%20of%20four%20categories%3A%20Internet%20technical%20improvements%2C%20registry%20processes%2Ftechnology%2C%20informational%20outreach%2C%20or%20research.%20Geography%3A%20ARIN%20region%20%28United%20States%2C%20Canada%2C%20parts%20of%20the%20Caribbean%29%3B%20project%20must%20benefit%20this%20region.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Organizations%20incl.%20open-source%20groups%20and%20nonprofits%3B%20noncommercial%20projects%20only%3B%20not%20individuals.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.arin.net%2Fabout%2Fcommunity_grants%2Fprogram%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.arin.net%2Fabout%2Fcommunity_grants%2Fprogram%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20a%20SurveyMonkey%20Apply%20account.%202.%20Submit%20the%20application%20by%20the%20deadline.%203.%20Grant%20Selection%20Committee%20scores%20applications%20%2850-point%20scale%2C%20July-August%29.%204.%20Board%20of%20Trustees%20approves.%205.%20Notification%20Aug%2FSep%3B%20funds%20disbursed%20in%20September%20after%20Grant%20Recipient%20Agreement.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Applicant%20details%20%28contact%2C%20organization%2C%20application%20history%2C%20referral%20source%29%0A-%20Project%20summary%20of%20200%20words%20or%20less%20with%201-3%20objectives%0A-%20Project%20description%2C%20team%20experience%2C%20senior%20participant%20bios%2C%20timeline%0A-%20Budget%20by%20category%20plus%20funding%20sources%20and%20financial%20controls%20%28max%205%25%20on%20promotion%2C%20overhead%2C%20travel%20or%20equipment%29%0A-%20Outcomes%2C%20impact%20and%20sustainability%0A-%20Compliance%3A%20category%20fit%2C%20regional%20benefit%2C%2012-month%20timeline%2C%20recognition%20plan%2C%20conflicts%20of%20interest%0A-%20For%20software%3A%20requirements%20document%20or%20mockup%20%28optional%20upload%29%0A-%20Data%20management%20plan%20for%20projects%20generating%20code%20or%20data%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%202-3%20months%20after%20the%20June%20deadline%0AObligations%3A%20Sign%20Grant%20Recipient%20Agreement%3B%20update%20report%20%28March%20following%20year%29%3B%20present%20at%20the%20spring%20ARIN%20meeting%3B%20final%20written%20report%20%28Sept%2FOct%20following%20year%29%3B%20survey%3B%20acknowledge%20ARIN%3B%20allow%20results%20in%20an%20ARIN%20blog%20post.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Small%20noncommercial%20grant%20to%20build%20an%20open-source%20%27resilient%20source%20mirror%27%20kit%20for%20North%20American%20network%20operators%20and%20research%20networks%2C%20documented%20for%20the%20ARIN%20community%20as%20an%20Internet%20technical%20improvement.%20ARIN%20cares%20about%20operational%20resilience%20of%20internet%20infrastructure%20in%20its%20region.%0ARisks%3A%20Commercial%20projects%20are%20ineligible%3B%20must%20frame%20the%20open-source%20protocol%20work%20separately%20from%20paid%20hosted%20services%2C%20ideally%20through%20a%20nonprofit%20or%20open-source%20group.%20Small%20amounts.%20LACNIC%20FRIDA%20was%20not%20selected%20because%20ARIN%27s%20program%20is%20confirmed%20running%20with%20a%20published%202026%20cycle.)** · [Program page](https://www.arin.net/about/community_grants/program/) · [Apply](https://www.arin.net/about/community_grants/program/)

- **Funder:** ARIN (American Registry for Internet Numbers)
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** US$1,000 - US$20,000 per award; US$50,000 total budget in 2026 (about $1,000 - $20,000)
- **Deadline:** Next window expected ~April-June 2027 (2026 window was 16 Apr - 14 Jun 2026; unconfirmed: 2027 dates) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Organizations (not individuals), with nonprofits, NGOs, academic/research institutions and open-source groups encouraged. Projects must be noncommercial, serve the public good, and broadly benefit the ARIN service region in one of four categories: Internet technical improvements, registry processes/technology, informational outreach, or research.
- **Geography:** ARIN region (United States, Canada, parts of the Caribbean); project must benefit this region · **Entity types:** Organizations incl. open-source groups and nonprofits; noncommercial projects only; not individuals
- **Decision time:** About 2-3 months after the June deadline
- **Obligations:** Sign Grant Recipient Agreement; update report (March following year); present at the spring ARIN meeting; final written report (Sept/Oct following year); survey; acknowledge ARIN; allow results in an ARIN blog post.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Small noncommercial grant to build an open-source 'resilient source mirror' kit for North American network operators and research networks, documented for the ARIN community as an Internet technical improvement. ARIN cares about operational resilience of internet infrastructure in its region.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a SurveyMonkey Apply account.
2. Submit the application by the deadline.
3. Grant Selection Committee scores applications (50-point scale, July-August).
4. Board of Trustees approves.
5. Notification Aug/Sep; funds disbursed in September after Grant Recipient Agreement.

**What the application asks for:**

- Applicant details (contact, organization, application history, referral source)
- Project summary of 200 words or less with 1-3 objectives
- Project description, team experience, senior participant bios, timeline
- Budget by category plus funding sources and financial controls (max 5% on promotion, overhead, travel or equipment)
- Outcomes, impact and sustainability
- Compliance: category fit, regional benefit, 12-month timeline, recognition plan, conflicts of interest
- For software: requirements document or mockup (optional upload)
- Data management plan for projects generating code or data

**Risks:** Commercial projects are ineligible; must frame the open-source protocol work separately from paid hosted services, ideally through a nonprofit or open-source group. Small amounts. LACNIC FRIDA was not selected because ARIN's program is confirmed running with a published 2026 cycle.

**Sources:** <https://www.arin.net/about/community_grants/program/>, <https://www.arin.net/about/community_grants/>

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20SIDN%20fonds%3A%20Pioniers%20open%20application.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sidnfonds.nl%2Faanvragen%2Fpioniers%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sidnfonds.nl%2Faanvragen%2Fpioniers%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20SIDN%20fonds%3A%20Pioniers%20open%20application%0AAward%3A%20Up%20to%20EUR%2010%2C000%20per%20Pioniers%20project%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Natural%20or%20legal%20persons%3B%20individuals%20and%20students%20can%20apply.%20For%202026%20the%20project%20must%20contribute%20to%20a%20%27Sterk%20internet%27%20and%20be%20relevant%20to%20Dutch%20internet%20infrastructure%20while%20being%20broadly%20applicable.%20Impact%20should%20start%20in%20the%20Netherlands%2C%20and%20collaboration%20with%20Dutch%20internet-sector%20partners%20is%20a%20plus.%20Purely%20B2B%20projects%2C%20projects%20benefiting%20a%20single%20company%20or%20person%2C%20sponsorship%2Fevents%20and%20structural%20overhead%20are%20excluded.%20Geography%3A%20Netherlands-focused%20%28impact%20in%20the%20Netherlands%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20students%2C%20non-profits%2C%20companies%20%28requests%20above%20EUR%2010%2C000%20need%20a%20legal%20entity%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sidnfonds.nl%2Faanvragen%2Fpioniers%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sidnfonds.nl%2Faanvragen%2Fpioniers%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Optionally%20test%20the%20idea%20with%20the%20%27Toets%20je%20idee%27%20web%20form%20or%20the%20Pioniers%20Quickscan.%202.%20Submit%20the%20Pioniersaanvraag%20in%20FundPro.%203.%20Staff%20and%20an%20advisory%20board%20from%20the%20Dutch%20internet%20community%20review%20it.%204.%20Answer%20within%20six%20weeks.%205.%20Run%20the%20project%20within%20six%20months%20and%20share%20the%20results.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20questionnaire%20in%20FundPro%0A-%20Short%20video%20pitch%0A-%20Chamber%20of%20Commerce%20%28KvK%29%20extract%20%28organisations%29%0A-%20Annual%20accounts%20%28organisations%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Within%20six%20weeks%20of%20submission%0AObligations%3A%20Results%20and%20knowledge%20must%20be%20shared%20openly%20and%20be%20available%20to%20everyone%3B%20the%20project%20must%20finish%20within%20six%20months%3B%20general%20SIDN%20fonds%20terms%20apply.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pilot%20ForkMesh%20mirrors%20for%20Dutch%20open-source%20projects%20or%20public-sector%20Git%20repositories%20so%20their%20code%20stays%20reachable%20if%20a%20foreign%20host%20fails%20or%20blocks%20access.%20That%20fits%20the%20fund%27s%20strong-internet%20and%20digital-autonomy%20focus.%0ARisks%3A%20Small%20grant.%20Needs%20clear%20relevance%20to%20the%20Netherlands%20and%20ideally%20Dutch%20partners.%20Projects%20that%20mainly%20benefit%20one%20company%20are%20excluded.%20Larger%20themed%20calls%20%28e.g.%2C%20the%20past%20%27transitie%20naar%20digitale%20autonomie%27%20call%29%20come%201-2%20times%20a%20year%2C%20and%20none%20is%20open%20now.)** · [Program page](https://www.sidnfonds.nl/aanvragen/pioniers) · [Apply](https://www.sidnfonds.nl/aanvragen/pioniers)

- **Funder:** SIDN fonds
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Up to EUR 10,000 per Pioniers project (about $11,500)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Natural or legal persons; individuals and students can apply. For 2026 the project must contribute to a 'Sterk internet' and be relevant to Dutch internet infrastructure while being broadly applicable. Impact should start in the Netherlands, and collaboration with Dutch internet-sector partners is a plus. Purely B2B projects, projects benefiting a single company or person, sponsorship/events and structural overhead are excluded.
- **Geography:** Netherlands-focused (impact in the Netherlands) · **Entity types:** Individuals, students, non-profits, companies (requests above EUR 10,000 need a legal entity)
- **Decision time:** Within six weeks of submission
- **Obligations:** Results and knowledge must be shared openly and be available to everyone; the project must finish within six months; general SIDN fonds terms apply.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pilot ForkMesh mirrors for Dutch open-source projects or public-sector Git repositories so their code stays reachable if a foreign host fails or blocks access. That fits the fund's strong-internet and digital-autonomy focus.

**How to apply:**

1. Optionally test the idea with the 'Toets je idee' web form or the Pioniers Quickscan.
2. Submit the Pioniersaanvraag in FundPro.
3. Staff and an advisory board from the Dutch internet community review it.
4. Answer within six weeks.
5. Run the project within six months and share the results.

**What the application asks for:**

- Online questionnaire in FundPro
- Short video pitch
- Chamber of Commerce (KvK) extract (organisations)
- Annual accounts (organisations)

**Risks:** Small grant. Needs clear relevance to the Netherlands and ideally Dutch partners. Projects that mainly benefit one company are excluded. Larger themed calls (e.g., the past 'transitie naar digitale autonomie' call) come 1-2 times a year, and none is open now.

**Sources:** <https://www.sidnfonds.nl/>, <https://www.sidnfonds.nl/aanvragen>, <https://www.sidnfonds.nl/aanvragen/pioniers>

## 67. LF Decentralized Trust: LFDT Mentorship Program

20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20LF%20Decentralized%20Trust%3A%20LFDT%20Mentorship%20Program%0AAward%3A%20Paid%20mentee%20stipends%20via%20LFX%20Mentorship%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20from%20July%202026%20LF%20baseline%20USD%206%2C000%20adjusted%20by%20country%20%28USD%201%2C000%20-%206%2C600%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20proposal%20call%20expected%20~February%202027%20%282026%20call%20opened%2010%20Feb%202026%3B%20mentee%20applications%20closed%208%20May%202026%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%202027%20dates%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Maintainers%2C%20contributors%20and%20community%20members%20propose%20projects%20tied%20to%20an%20LFDT%20project%2C%20Lab%20or%20the%20wider%20community%3B%20if%20not%20already%20an%20LFDT%20project%20or%20Lab%2C%20mentors%20must%20be%20ready%20to%20sponsor%20the%20work%20as%20an%20LFDT%20Lab.%20Proposers%20serve%20as%20mentors%20%28co-mentoring%20preferred%29.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20%28mentors%29%20on%20behalf%20of%20LFDT%20projects%2FLabs.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.lfdecentralizedtrust.org%2Fblog%2Fthe-2026-lfdt-mentorship-program-is-officially-open%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Flf-hyperledger.atlassian.net%2Fwiki%2Fspaces%2FINTERN%2Fpages%2F21954932%2FProject%2BProposal%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20the%20proposal%20guidelines.%202.%20Submit%20the%20proposal%20as%20an%20issue%20on%20the%20LFDT%20mentorship%20GitHub%20repository%20using%20the%20template.%203.%20TAC%20Mentorship%20Task%20Force%20selects%20projects.%204.%20Mentees%20apply%20via%20LFX%20Mentorship%20%28from%20early%20April%29.%205.%20~6%20months%20part-time%20mentorship.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20proposal%20issue%20%28template%29%3A%20description%2C%20expected%20outcomes%2C%20required%20skills%2C%20mentors%2C%20related%20LFDT%20project%20or%20Lab%0A-%20Commitment%20to%20~15%20hours%2Fweek%20mentee%20scope%20for%20~6%20months%0A-%20Willingness%20to%20sponsor%20an%20LFDT%20Lab%20if%20not%20already%20in%20LFDT%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20about%201-2%20months%0AObligations%3A%20Mentor%20supervision%20and%20evaluations%3B%20contribute%20work%20under%20the%20LFDT%20project%2FLab%3B%20LF%20code%20of%20conduct.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Sponsor%20an%20LFDT%20Lab%20for%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed%2C%20decentralized%20collaboration%20records%20%28verifiable%20issue%2FPR%20history%29%20and%20propose%20a%20mentee%20project%20on%20it%2C%20gaining%20a%20paid%20contributor%20and%20an%20LF%20home%20for%20the%20protocol.%0ARisks%3A%20Requires%20placing%20work%20in%20an%20LFDT%20Lab%20%28Apache-2.0%20and%20LF%20governance%20norms%29%3B%20LFDT%20prioritises%20ledger%2Fidentity%20projects%3B%20stipend%20goes%20to%20mentee%2C%20not%20ForkMesh.)** · [Program page](https://www.lfdecentralizedtrust.org/blog/the-2026-lfdt-mentorship-program-is-officially-open) · [Apply](https://lf-hyperledger.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/INTERN/pages/21954932/Project+Proposal)

- **Funder:** LF Decentralized Trust (Linux Foundation)
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** Paid mentee stipends via LFX Mentorship; unconfirmed: from July 2026 LF baseline USD 6,000 adjusted by country (USD 1,000 - 6,600) (about $6,600)
- **Deadline:** Next proposal call expected ~February 2027 (2026 call opened 10 Feb 2026; mentee applications closed 8 May 2026; unconfirmed: 2027 dates) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Maintainers, contributors and community members propose projects tied to an LFDT project, Lab or the wider community; if not already an LFDT project or Lab, mentors must be ready to sponsor the work as an LFDT Lab. Proposers serve as mentors (co-mentoring preferred).
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Individuals (mentors) on behalf of LFDT projects/Labs
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: about 1-2 months
- **Obligations:** Mentor supervision and evaluations; contribute work under the LFDT project/Lab; LF code of conduct.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Sponsor an LFDT Lab for ForkMesh's signed, decentralized collaboration records (verifiable issue/PR history) and propose a mentee project on it, gaining a paid contributor and an LF home for the protocol.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the proposal guidelines.
2. Submit the proposal as an issue on the LFDT mentorship GitHub repository using the template.
3. TAC Mentorship Task Force selects projects.
4. Mentees apply via LFX Mentorship (from early April).
5. ~6 months part-time mentorship.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project proposal issue (template): description, expected outcomes, required skills, mentors, related LFDT project or Lab
- Commitment to ~15 hours/week mentee scope for ~6 months
- Willingness to sponsor an LFDT Lab if not already in LFDT

**Risks:** Requires placing work in an LFDT Lab (Apache-2.0 and LF governance norms); LFDT prioritises ledger/identity projects; stipend goes to mentee, not ForkMesh.

**Sources:** <https://www.lfdecentralizedtrust.org/blog/the-2026-lfdt-mentorship-program-is-officially-open>, <https://lf-hyperledger.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/INTERN/pages/21954932/Project+Proposal>, <https://techbeatly.com/lfx-mentorship-how-to-apply/>

20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Amazon%20Web%20Services%3A%20AWS%20Cloud%20Credits%20for%20Open%20Source%20Projects%0AAward%3A%20AWS%20Promotional%20Credits%3B%20amount%20set%20per%20request%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20no%20published%20range%29%3B%20credits%20expire%20after%20one%20year%20per%202019%20launch%20terms%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28reviewed%20monthly%3B%20no%20credits%20issued%20in%20December%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open-source%20projects%20under%20an%20OSI-approved%20license%20that%20are%20actively%20maintained%20and%20engaged%20with%20their%20community%2C%20not%20dominated%20by%20a%20single%20vendor%20and%20not%20funded%20by%20venture%20capital.%20Requires%20an%20active%20AWS%20account%20with%20no%20outstanding%20invoices%3B%20projects%20owned%20by%20nonprofits%2Ffoundations%20with%20multi-organisation%20maintainers%20have%20the%20best%20chances%20and%20AWS%20favours%20projects%20relevant%20to%20AWS%20customers.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open-source%20projects%20%28foundation%2Fnonprofit-owned%20preferred%29%3B%20single-vendor%20or%20VC-funded%20projects%20excluded.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Faws.amazon.com%2Fblogs%2Fopensource%2Faws-cloud-credits-for-open-source-projects-affirming-our-commitment%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Faws.amazon.com%2Fblogs%2Fopensource%2Faws-cloud-credits-for-open-source-projects-affirming-our-commitment%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Download%20the%20AWS%20Open%20Source%20Credits%20Application%20form%20%28.xlsx%29%20linked%20from%20the%20AWS%20Open%20Source%20blog%20post.%202.%20Fill%20in%20the%20project%2C%20AWS%20account%20number%2C%20credit%20amount%20and%20intended%20use.%203.%20Email%20it%20to%20awsopensourcecredits%40amazon.com.%204.%20AWS%20reviews%20monthly%20and%20replies%20by%20email%20%28about%2010-15%20business%20days%20per%20launch%20terms%29.%205.%20Credits%20are%20applied%20to%20the%20AWS%20account%20%28not%20retroactive%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20AWS%20Open%20Source%20Credits%20Application%20form%20%28.xlsx%29%0A-%20AWS%20account%20number%20%28active%2C%20no%20outstanding%20invoices%29%0A-%20Amount%20of%20credits%20requested%0A-%20Intended%20use%20of%20credits%20%28e.g.%20CI%2FCD%2C%20testing%2C%20artifact%20storage%2C%20compute%29%0A-%20Project%20details%3A%20OSI%20license%2C%20repository%2C%20maintainers%20and%20community%20activity%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%2010-15%20business%20days%20%282019%20launch%20terms%29%3B%20issuance%20monthly%0AObligations%3A%20Keep%20the%20project%20actively%20maintained%20and%20engage%20with%20the%20developer%20community%3B%20credits%20are%20non-retroactive%20and%20expire%20after%20one%20year%20%282019%20terms%29%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Request%20credits%20for%20ForkMesh%27s%20runner%20tier%20and%20CI%20%28multi-OS%20desktop%20builds%2C%20integrity-check%20and%20mirror-failover%20tests%29%20and%20for%20an%20S3-backed%20archival%20mirror.%20AWS%20cares%20about%20supply-chain%20integrity%20tooling%20used%20by%20its%20customers.%0ARisks%3A%20Excludes%20single-vendor-dominated%20and%20VC-funded%20projects%3B%20ForkMesh%20as%20a%20startup-controlled%20project%20may%20be%20judged%20single-vendor.%20The%20served%20stack%20is%20self-hosted%2C%20so%20AWS%20credits%20only%20help%20CI%2Frunner%20or%20archive%20workloads.%20Amount%20not%20published.)** · [Program page](https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/opensource/aws-cloud-credits-for-open-source-projects-affirming-our-commitment/) · [Apply](https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/opensource/aws-cloud-credits-for-open-source-projects-affirming-our-commitment/)

- **Funder:** Amazon Web Services
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** AWS Promotional Credits; amount set per request (unconfirmed: no published range); credits expire after one year per 2019 launch terms (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (reviewed monthly; no credits issued in December) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open-source projects under an OSI-approved license that are actively maintained and engaged with their community, not dominated by a single vendor and not funded by venture capital. Requires an active AWS account with no outstanding invoices; projects owned by nonprofits/foundations with multi-organisation maintainers have the best chances and AWS favours projects relevant to AWS customers.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Open-source projects (foundation/nonprofit-owned preferred); single-vendor or VC-funded projects excluded
- **Decision time:** About 10-15 business days (2019 launch terms); issuance monthly
- **Obligations:** Keep the project actively maintained and engage with the developer community; credits are non-retroactive and expire after one year (2019 terms); no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Request credits for ForkMesh's runner tier and CI (multi-OS desktop builds, integrity-check and mirror-failover tests) and for an S3-backed archival mirror. AWS cares about supply-chain integrity tooling used by its customers.

**How to apply:**

1. Download the AWS Open Source Credits Application form (.xlsx) linked from the AWS Open Source blog post.
2. Fill in the project, AWS account number, credit amount and intended use.
3. Email it to awsopensourcecredits@amazon.com.
4. AWS reviews monthly and replies by email (about 10-15 business days per launch terms).
5. Credits are applied to the AWS account (not retroactive).

**What the application asks for:**

- AWS Open Source Credits Application form (.xlsx)
- AWS account number (active, no outstanding invoices)
- Amount of credits requested
- Intended use of credits (e.g. CI/CD, testing, artifact storage, compute)
- Project details: OSI license, repository, maintainers and community activity

**Risks:** Excludes single-vendor-dominated and VC-funded projects; ForkMesh as a startup-controlled project may be judged single-vendor. The served stack is self-hosted, so AWS credits only help CI/runner or archive workloads. Amount not published.

**Sources:** <https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/opensource/aws-cloud-credits-for-open-source-projects-affirming-our-commitment/>, <https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/opensource/aws-promotional-credits-open-source-projects/>, <https://devclass.com/2019/10/15/aws-tempts-open-source-projects-with-promo-credit-sweeteners/>
