nvidia.com%2Fphoenix%2Fapplication%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20the%20application%20at%20programs.nvidia.com%2Fphoenix%2Fapplication%20with%20business%20and%20product%20details%2C%20incorporation%20date%2C%20pitch%20deck%20and%20funding%20status.%202.%20NVIDIA%20reviews%20and%20may%20contact%20the%20applicant%20for%20more%20information.%203.%20On%20acceptance%2C%20access%20the%20member%20portal%20for%20credits%2C%20partner%20offers%20and%20training%3B%20keep%20the%20profile%20current.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Company%20website%0A-%20Incorporation%20date%0A-%20Business%20and%20product%20details%0A-%20Clear%20description%20of%20the%20NVIDIA%20technology%20use%20case%20%28GPUs%2C%20CUDA%2C%20NIM%2C%20etc.%29%0A-%20Company%20pitch%20deck%0A-%20Funding%20status%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20Free%2C%20no%20equity%20or%20membership%20fees.%20Keep%20product%20and%20company%20details%20current%20in%20the%20portal%20to%20retain%20benefits.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20the%20desktop%20app%27s%20AI%20coding%20agents%20%28Codex%20%2F%20Claude%20Code%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%29%20and%20runner%20tier%20as%20GPU-backed%20workloads%2C%20e.g.%20self-hosted%20open%20models%20via%20NVIDIA%20NIM%20on%20runner%20nodes%20for%20code%20review%20and%20agent%20tasks.%20Inception%20mainly%20unlocks%20partner%20GPU%20cloud%20credits%20and%20VC%20introductions%20rather%20than%20direct%20funding.%0ARisks%3A%20Excludes%20cryptocurrency-associated%20companies%20and%20cloud%20service%20providers%3A%20the%20Solana%20payout%20pool%20and%20paid%20hosted%20service%20could%20disqualify%20ForkMesh.%20GPU%20use%20case%20is%20secondary%20to%20ForkMesh%27s%20core.%20Credits%20come%20from%20partners%20with%20unstated%20amounts.%20Requires%20incorporation.)** · [Program page](https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/startups/) · [Apply](https://programs.nvidia.com/phoenix/application)

- **Funder:** NVIDIA
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Free cloud credits from NVIDIA and partners (amounts not stated), partner discounts, preferred pricing on select NVIDIA products, free self-paced training, VC introductions; no cash (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Officially incorporated companies under 10 years old with a working website, a clear use case for NVIDIA technology, and at least one actively operating founder; any funding stage, no revenue required. Excludes consulting/outsourced development firms, cryptocurrency-associated companies, cloud service providers, resellers/distributors and public companies.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (unconfirmed: sanctioned-country exclusions) · **Entity types:** Incorporated companies (for-profit startups)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** Free, no equity or membership fees. Keep product and company details current in the portal to retain benefits.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch the desktop app's AI coding agents (Codex / Claude Code in isolated worktrees) and runner tier as GPU-backed workloads, e.g. self-hosted open models via NVIDIA NIM on runner nodes for code review and agent tasks. Inception mainly unlocks partner GPU cloud credits and VC introductions rather than direct funding.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit the application at programs.nvidia.com/phoenix/application with business and product details, incorporation date, pitch deck and funding status.
2. NVIDIA reviews and may contact the applicant for more information.
3. On acceptance, access the member portal for credits, partner offers and training; keep the profile current.

**What the application asks for:**

- Company website
- Incorporation date
- Business and product details
- Clear description of the NVIDIA technology use case (GPUs, CUDA, NIM, etc.)
- Company pitch deck
- Funding status

**Risks:** Excludes cryptocurrency-associated companies and cloud service providers: the Solana payout pool and paid hosted service could disqualify ForkMesh. GPU use case is secondary to ForkMesh's core. Credits come from partners with unstated amounts. Requires incorporation.

**Sources:** <https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/startups/>

0no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Open%20Source%20Pledge%3A%20companies%20pay%20USD%202%2C000%2B%20per%20developer%20per%20year%20to%20open%20source%20maintainers%20of%20their%20choosing%0AAward%3A%20Member%20companies%20pay%20at%20least%20USD%202%2C000%20per%20year%20per%20full-time-equivalent%20developer%20to%20open%20source%20maintainers%20and%20foundations%20of%20their%20choosing%3B%20cash%20only.%20Pledge%20reports%20USD%207%2C421%2C535%20raised%20in%20total%2C%20over%20USD%203M%20in%20the%20past%20year.%20Per-project%20amounts%20are%20at%20each%20member%20company%27s%20discretion.%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28member%20companies%20choose%20recipients%2C%20typically%20annually%29%20%28invite-only%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20No%20application%20for%20maintainers%3B%20member%20companies%20choose%20which%20maintainers%2Fprojects%20to%20pay.%20A%20project%20needs%20a%20public%20way%20to%20receive%20money%20%28e.g.%20GitHub%20Sponsors%2C%20Open%20Collective%2C%20thanks.dev%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20channels%20accepted%29.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%28depends%20on%20the%20paying%20company%20and%20payout%20channel%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open%20source%20maintainers%2C%20projects%20and%20foundations%20%28recipient%20chosen%20by%20companies%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopensourcepledge.com%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopensourcepledge.com%2Fabout%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Set%20up%20public%20funding%20channels%20%28GitHub%20Sponsors%20and%2For%20an%20Open%20Source%20Collective%20page%29%20and%20a%20FUNDING.yml.%202.%20Identify%20Pledge%20member%20companies%20listed%20on%20opensourcepledge.com%20that%20use%20Git%20hosting%20%2F%20supply-chain%20tooling.%203.%20Contact%20them%20with%20a%20short%20case%20for%20funding%20ForkMesh%27s%20open%20protocol%20and%20mirror%20network.%204.%20Companies%20pay%20directly%20and%20report%20the%20payment%20in%20their%20annual%20Pledge%20report.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Public%20funding%20channel%20%28GitHub%20Sponsors%2C%20Open%20Collective%2C%20etc.%29%0A-%20FUNDING.yml%20in%20the%20repository%0A-%20Short%20funding%20pitch%20for%20member%20companies%20%28what%20the%20money%20pays%20for%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20At%20each%20member%20company%27s%20discretion%20%28annual%20cycles%29%0AObligations%3A%20None%20formal%20for%20recipients%3B%20payments%20are%20intended%20to%20be%20no-strings-attached.%20Member%20companies%20publish%20an%20annual%20report%20of%20their%20payments.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20Pledge%20member%20companies%20%28many%20are%20developer-tool%20and%20infrastructure%20companies%29%20to%20fund%20ForkMesh%27s%20independent%20mirror%20network%20as%20a%20supply-chain%20resilience%20measure%20for%20the%20Git%20dependencies%20they%20rely%20on.%20Works%20best%20once%20ForkMesh%20has%20an%20Open%20Source%20Collective%20page%20and%20GitHub%20Sponsors%20as%20receiving%20channels.%0ARisks%3A%20No%20application%20route%3A%20income%20depends%20on%20companies%20choosing%20ForkMesh%2C%20which%20favours%20widely%20used%20dependencies%20over%20a%20young%20project.%20Payments%20to%20a%20for-profit%20startup%20may%20not%20count%20as%20a%20payment%20to%20an%20open%20source%20maintainer%20under%20Pledge%20norms%3B%20receive%20via%20a%20fiscal%20host%20for%20the%20open-source%20part.)** · [Program page](https://opensourcepledge.com/) · [Apply](https://opensourcepledge.com/about)

- **Funder:** Open Source Pledge (member companies)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** Member companies pay at least USD 2,000 per year per full-time-equivalent developer to open source maintainers and foundations of their choosing; cash only. Pledge reports USD 7,421,535 raised in total, over USD 3M in the past year. Per-project amounts are at each member company's discretion. (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (member companies choose recipients, typically annually) (invite-only)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** No application for maintainers; member companies choose which maintainers/projects to pay. A project needs a public way to receive money (e.g. GitHub Sponsors, Open Collective, thanks.dev; unconfirmed: exact channels accepted).
- **Geography:** Worldwide (depends on the paying company and payout channel) · **Entity types:** Open source maintainers, projects and foundations (recipient chosen by companies)
- **Decision time:** At each member company's discretion (annual cycles)
- **Obligations:** None formal for recipients; payments are intended to be no-strings-attached. Member companies publish an annual report of their payments.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch Pledge member companies (many are developer-tool and infrastructure companies) to fund ForkMesh's independent mirror network as a supply-chain resilience measure for the Git dependencies they rely on. Works best once ForkMesh has an Open Source Collective page and GitHub Sponsors as receiving channels.

**How to apply:**

1. Set up public funding channels (GitHub Sponsors and/or an Open Source Collective page) and a FUNDING.yml.
2. Identify Pledge member companies listed on opensourcepledge.com that use Git hosting / supply-chain tooling.
3. Contact them with a short case for funding ForkMesh's open protocol and mirror network.
4. Companies pay directly and report the payment in their annual Pledge report.

**What the application asks for:**

- Public funding channel (GitHub Sponsors, Open Collective, etc.)
- FUNDING.yml in the repository
- Short funding pitch for member companies (what the money pays for)

**Risks:** No application route: income depends on companies choosing ForkMesh, which favours widely used dependencies over a young project. Payments to a for-profit startup may not count as a payment to an open source maintainer under Pledge norms; receive via a fiscal host for the open-source part.

**Sources:** <https://opensourcepledge.com/>, <https://opensourcepledge.com/about>, <https://httptoolkit.com/blog/open-source-funding-pledge/>, <https://lwn.net/Articles/993073>

3A%2F%2Fwww.sovereign.tech%2Fprograms%2Fresilience%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fapply.sovereigntechfund.de%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Read%20the%20criteria%20and%20participation%20rules.%202.%20Create%20an%20account%20on%20the%20application%20portal%20and%20submit%20the%20application%20%28preview%20PDF%20available%29.%203.%20Ongoing%20review%3B%20ineligible%20projects%20are%20declined%2C%20eligible%20ones%20invited%20to%20join.%204.%20Work%20is%20scoped%20individually%20with%20the%20project%20and%20an%20implementation%20partner.%205.%20Start%20depends%20on%20partner%20capacity%20and%20demand.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Portal%20application%20form%20%28preview%20PDF%20on%20the%20program%20page%29%0A-%20Project%20description%20and%20evidence%20against%20the%20program%20criteria%0A-%20Requested%20services%20%28e.g.%20security%20audit%2C%20supply%20chain%20security%2C%20CRA%20compliance%2C%20technical%20debt%29%0A-%20Confirmation%20of%20the%20participation%20rules%0A-%20Maintainer%20agreement%20to%20the%20work%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20applications%20reviewed%20on%20an%20ongoing%20basis%3B%20start%20depends%20on%20partner%20capacity%0AObligations%3A%20Follow%20the%20participation%20rules%3B%20all%20work%20is%20done%20in%20agreement%20with%20the%20project%27s%20maintainers%3B%20no%20cash%2C%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Request%20a%20security%20audit%20and%20supply-chain%20security%20review%20of%20the%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20signing%20and%20integrity-check%20code%20paths.%20A%20third-party%20audit%20would%20strengthen%20the%20product%27s%20core%20trust%20claim%2C%20and%20the%20program%20exists%20to%20harden%20open%20source%20infrastructure.%0ARisks%3A%20Services%20only%2C%20no%20money.%20Criteria%20favour%20critical%2C%20widely%20used%20dependencies%3B%20an%20early-stage%20project%20is%20unlikely%20to%20be%20accepted.%20Bug%20bounty%20platform%20paused%20for%20new%20reports%20%28status%20Sept%202026%2C%20backlog%20ending%20fall%202026%29.%20Start%20times%20depend%20on%20partner%20capacity.)** · [Program page](https://www.sovereign.tech/programs/resilience) · [Apply](https://apply.sovereigntechfund.de)

- **Funder:** Sovereign Tech Agency
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** In-kind services, no cash amount stated: technical debt management, memory safety transition, post-quantum readiness, Cyber Resilience Act compliance, security audits, supply chain security, bug and fix bounties (bounties paused as of Sept 2026) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 30/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source projects meeting the program criteria (critical, widely used open digital infrastructure) whose maintainers agree to the participation rules; projects can also be invited after another Sovereign Tech Agency program or recommended by a service provider.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: no geographic restriction stated on the page · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: project maintainers / communities; entity type not stated
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: applications reviewed on an ongoing basis; start depends on partner capacity
- **Obligations:** Follow the participation rules; all work is done in agreement with the project's maintainers; no cash, no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Request a security audit and supply-chain security review of the ForkMesh mirror signing and integrity-check code paths. A third-party audit would strengthen the product's core trust claim, and the program exists to harden open source infrastructure.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the criteria and participation rules.
2. Create an account on the application portal and submit the application (preview PDF available).
3. Ongoing review; ineligible projects are declined, eligible ones invited to join.
4. Work is scoped individually with the project and an implementation partner.
5. Start depends on partner capacity and demand.

**What the application asks for:**

- Portal application form (preview PDF on the program page)
- Project description and evidence against the program criteria
- Requested services (e.g. security audit, supply chain security, CRA compliance, technical debt)
- Confirmation of the participation rules
- Maintainer agreement to the work

**Risks:** Services only, no money. Criteria favour critical, widely used dependencies; an early-stage project is unlikely to be accepted. Bug bounty platform paused for new reports (status Sept 2026, backlog ending fall 2026). Start times depend on partner capacity.

**Sources:** <https://www.sovereign.tech/programs/resilience>, <https://www.sovereign.tech/programs/fund>

erated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20U.S.%20National%20Science%20Foundation%3A%20Security%2C%20Privacy%2C%20and%20Trust%20in%20Cyberspace%0AAward%3A%20RES%3A%20up%20to%20%241%2C200%2C000%20for%20up%20to%204%20years%3B%20EDU%3A%20up%20to%20%24500%2C000%20for%20up%20to%203%20years%3B%20SEED%3A%20up%20to%20%24300%2C000%20for%20up%20to%202%20years%20%28topics%20set%20by%20Dear%20Colleague%20Letters%29%3B%20about%20%2460%2C000%2C000%20per%20year%2C%20about%2075%20awards%0ADeadline%3A%202027-01-25%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Only%20accredited%20U.S.%20institutions%20of%20higher%20education%20and%20U.S.%20non-profit%2C%20non-academic%20research%20organisations%20may%20submit.%20PIs%20need%20a%20tenured%2Ftenure-track%20or%20primary%20full-time%20research%20or%20teaching%20appointment%20at%20an%20eligible%20U.S.%20organisation.%20People%20whose%20primary%20appointment%20is%20at%20a%20for-profit%20cannot%20be%20PIs.%20A%20company%20could%20only%20join%20as%20a%20collaborator%20under%20an%20academic%20lead.%20Geography%3A%20US%20only.%20Entity%20types%3A%20U.S.%20universities%20and%20U.S.%20non-profit%20research%20organisations%20%28for-profits%20and%20individuals%20cannot%20submit%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.nsf.gov%2Ffunding%2Fopportunities%2Fsatc-20-security-privacy-trust-cyberspace%2Fnsf25-515%2Fsolicitation%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.research.gov%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Find%20a%20U.S.%20university%20security%20research%20group%20willing%20to%20lead%20as%20PI.%202.%20Choose%20RES%2C%20EDU%20or%20SEED.%203.%20Prepare%20the%20proposal%20per%20the%20PAPPG%20and%20NSF%2025-515.%204.%20Submit%20in%20Research.gov%20or%20Grants.gov%20by%20a%20target%20date%3A%20last%20Monday%20of%20January%20%2825%20Jan%202027%29%20or%20last%20Monday%20of%20September.%205.%20Panel%20review.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20Summary%20with%201-6%20prioritised%20keywords%20%28RES%20and%20SEED%29%0A-%20Project%20Description%3A%20max%2015%20pages%20%28RES%2C%20EDU%29%20or%2010%20pages%20%28SEED%29%0A-%20List%20of%20Project%20Personnel%20and%20Partner%20Institutions%0A-%20Data%20Management%20and%20Sharing%20Plan%20%28max%202%20pages%29%0A-%20Collaboration%20Plan%20%28max%202%20pages%29%20for%20multi-PI%20proposals%20over%20%24600%2C000%0A-%20Broadening%20Participation%20in%20Computing%20plan%20for%20projects%20over%20%24600%2C000%20%28Standalone%20max%203%20pages%20or%20Connected%20max%202%20pages%29%0A-%20Letters%20of%20collaboration%20%28optional%2C%20max%201%20page%20each%29%0A-%20Optional%20Transition%20to%20Education%20plan%20for%20RES%20%28max%202%20pages%2C%20up%20to%20%2450%2C000%29%0A-%20Mentoring%20plan%20where%20applicable%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20NSF%20generally%20aims%20to%20notify%20within%20about%206%20months%0AObligations%3A%20NSF%20annual%20and%20final%20project%20reports%20and%20a%20project%20outcomes%20report%3B%20data%20sharing%20as%20set%20out%20in%20the%20plan.%20A%20person%20may%20be%20on%20at%20most%20four%20SaTC%20proposals%20in%20any%2012%20months%20%28max%20two%20RES%2C%20one%20EDU%2C%20one%20SEED%29.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Co-design%20research%20with%20a%20U.S.%20university%20security%20lab%20on%20tamper-evident%2C%20decentralised%20distribution%20of%20source%20code%20%28threat%20models%20and%20verification%20for%20signed%20mirrors%20and%20signed%20review%20records%29%2C%20using%20ForkMesh%20as%20the%20open-source%20testbed.%20SaTC%20funds%20basic%20trust%20and%20supply-chain%20security%20research%20and%20would%20get%20a%20real%20deployment%20to%20evaluate.%0ARisks%3A%20ForkMesh%20cannot%20lead%20and%20depends%20on%20finding%20an%20academic%20PI.%20Whether%20a%20for-profit%20subaward%20is%20acceptable%20is%20unconfirmed.%20NSF%20CSSI%20shows%20%27Waiting%20for%20new%20publication%27%20with%20no%20due%20dates.%20NSF%20CICI%20%28deadline%20the%20third%20Wednesday%20of%20January%29%20also%20bars%20for-profit%20submitters.%20The%2025%20January%202027%20date%20is%20a%20target%20date%2C%20not%20a%20hard%20deadline.)** · [Program page](https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/satc-20-security-privacy-trust-cyberspace/nsf25-515/solicitation) · [Apply](https://www.research.gov/)

- **Funder:** U.S. National Science Foundation (CISE, with SBE, EDU, ENG, MPS)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** RES: up to $1,200,000 for up to 4 years; EDU: up to $500,000 for up to 3 years; SEED: up to $300,000 for up to 2 years (topics set by Dear Colleague Letters); about $60,000,000 per year, about 75 awards (about $1,200,000)
- **Deadline:** 2027-01-25 (open)
- **Fit score:** 28/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Only accredited U.S. institutions of higher education and U.S. non-profit, non-academic research organisations may submit. PIs need a tenured/tenure-track or primary full-time research or teaching appointment at an eligible U.S. organisation. People whose primary appointment is at a for-profit cannot be PIs. A company could only join as a collaborator under an academic lead.
- **Geography:** US only · **Entity types:** U.S. universities and U.S. non-profit research organisations (for-profits and individuals cannot submit)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: NSF generally aims to notify within about 6 months
- **Obligations:** NSF annual and final project reports and a project outcomes report; data sharing as set out in the plan. A person may be on at most four SaTC proposals in any 12 months (max two RES, one EDU, one SEED).

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Co-design research with a U.S. university security lab on tamper-evident, decentralised distribution of source code (threat models and verification for signed mirrors and signed review records), using ForkMesh as the open-source testbed. SaTC funds basic trust and supply-chain security research and would get a real deployment to evaluate.

**How to apply:**

1. Find a U.S. university security research group willing to lead as PI.
2. Choose RES, EDU or SEED.
3. Prepare the proposal per the PAPPG and NSF 25-515.
4. Submit in Research.gov or Grants.gov by a target date: last Monday of January (25 Jan 2027) or last Monday of September.
5. Panel review.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project Summary with 1-6 prioritised keywords (RES and SEED)
- Project Description: max 15 pages (RES, EDU) or 10 pages (SEED)
- List of Project Personnel and Partner Institutions
- Data Management and Sharing Plan (max 2 pages)
- Collaboration Plan (max 2 pages) for multi-PI proposals over $600,000
- Broadening Participation in Computing plan for projects over $600,000 (Standalone max 3 pages or Connected max 2 pages)
- Letters of collaboration (optional, max 1 page each)
- Optional Transition to Education plan for RES (max 2 pages, up to $50,000)
- Mentoring plan where applicable

**Risks:** ForkMesh cannot lead and depends on finding an academic PI. Whether a for-profit subaward is acceptable is unconfirmed. NSF CSSI shows 'Waiting for new publication' with no due dates. NSF CICI (deadline the third Wednesday of January) also bars for-profit submitters. The 25 January 2027 date is a target date, not a hard deadline.

**Sources:** <https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/satc-20-security-privacy-trust-cyberspace/nsf25-515/solicitation>, <https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/cssi-cyberinfrastructure-sustained-scientific-innovation>, <https://www.nsf.gov/funding/opportunities/cici-cybersecurity-innovation-cyberinfrastructure/nsf25-531/solicitation>

f%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Bpifrance%3A%20Bourse%20French%20Tech%0AAward%3A%20Grant%20%28subvention%29%20of%20up%20to%20EUR%2030%2C000%20%27sous%20condition%27%2C%20covering%20up%20to%2050%25%20of%20eligible%20expenses%20and%2C%20depending%20on%20company%20category%2C%20up%20to%2070%25%3B%20programme%20up%20to%2024%20months%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20SMEs%20under%20the%20European%20definition%20with%20a%20high-technology-risk%20innovation%20project%20at%20maturation%20or%20feasibility%20stage.%20Expenses%20incurred%20before%20the%20application%20is%20filed%20are%20not%20eligible.%20Secondary%20sources%20add%3A%20registered%20in%20France%20for%20under%20one%20year%2C%20fewer%20than%2050%20employees%2C%20and%20at%20least%2030%25%20self-financing%20%28unconfirmed%20on%20the%20official%20page%29.%20Geography%3A%20France.%20Entity%20types%3A%20French%20SMEs%20%28for-profit%20start-ups%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.bpifrance.fr%2Fcatalogue-offres%2Fbourse-french-tech%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.bpifrance.fr%2Fcatalogue-offres%2Fbourse-french-tech%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Contact%20Bpifrance%20through%20the%20offer%20page%20or%20a%20regional%20office.%202.%20File%20the%20aid%20request%20before%20incurring%20the%20project%20expenses.%203.%20Bpifrance%20reviews%20the%20request.%204.%20Sign%20the%20contract%3B%20the%20first%20tranche%20is%20paid%20at%20signature%20%28after%20any%20conditions%20are%20lifted%29.%205.%20The%20balance%20is%20paid%20at%20completion%20after%20an%20end-of-programme%20report%20and%20a%20statement%20of%20paid%20expenses.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Aid%20request%20to%20Bpifrance%20describing%20the%20innovation%20project%2C%20its%20technical%20risks%20and%20maturation%2Ffeasibility%20work%20%28form%20contents%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Budget%20of%20internal%20and%20external%20R%26D%20expenses%0A-%20Company%20registration%20and%20SME%20status%20documents%20%28unconfirmed%29%0A-%20At%20completion%3A%20end-of-programme%20report%20and%20summary%20statement%20of%20expenses%20paid%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20Two-tranche%20payment%2C%20final%20technical%20report%20and%20expense%20statement%3B%20the%20company%20co-finances%20the%20rest.%20No%20application%20or%20processing%20fees.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20If%20ForkMesh%20incorporates%20in%20France%2C%20use%20it%20for%20the%20maturation%20and%20feasibility%20phase%20of%20the%20hosted%20mirror%20fleet%20and%20the%20desktop%20node%27s%20isolated%20agent%20runner.%20Bpifrance%20funds%20early%20innovation%20projects%20with%20high%20technology%20risk.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20a%20young%20French%20company.%20Small%20amount.%20The%20rule%20against%20costs%20incurred%20before%20filing%20means%20timing%20matters.%20The%20i-Nov%20competition%20%28also%20listed%20in%20the%20brief%29%20was%20not%20checked%20and%20runs%20in%20themed%20waves.)** · [Program page](https://www.bpifrance.fr/catalogue-offres/bourse-french-tech) · [Apply](https://www.bpifrance.fr/catalogue-offres/bourse-french-tech)

- **Funder:** Bpifrance
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Grant (subvention) of up to EUR 30,000 'sous condition', covering up to 50% of eligible expenses and, depending on company category, up to 70%; programme up to 24 months (about $34,500)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 28/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** SMEs under the European definition with a high-technology-risk innovation project at maturation or feasibility stage. Expenses incurred before the application is filed are not eligible. Secondary sources add: registered in France for under one year, fewer than 50 employees, and at least 30% self-financing (unconfirmed on the official page).
- **Geography:** France · **Entity types:** French SMEs (for-profit start-ups)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** Two-tranche payment, final technical report and expense statement; the company co-finances the rest. No application or processing fees.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** If ForkMesh incorporates in France, use it for the maturation and feasibility phase of the hosted mirror fleet and the desktop node's isolated agent runner. Bpifrance funds early innovation projects with high technology risk.

**How to apply:**

1. Contact Bpifrance through the offer page or a regional office.
2. File the aid request before incurring the project expenses.
3. Bpifrance reviews the request.
4. Sign the contract; the first tranche is paid at signature (after any conditions are lifted).
5. The balance is paid at completion after an end-of-programme report and a statement of paid expenses.

**What the application asks for:**

- Aid request to Bpifrance describing the innovation project, its technical risks and maturation/feasibility work (form contents unconfirmed)
- Budget of internal and external R&D expenses
- Company registration and SME status documents (unconfirmed)
- At completion: end-of-programme report and summary statement of expenses paid

**Risks:** Needs a young French company. Small amount. The rule against costs incurred before filing means timing matters. The i-Nov competition (also listed in the brief) was not checked and runs in themed waves.

**Sources:** <https://www.bpifrance.fr/catalogue-offres/bourse-french-tech>, <https://hayot-expertise.fr/en/blog/french-tech-grant-bpifrance>, <https://subventions.fr/insights/bourse-french-tech-guide/>

## 74. Sovereign Tech Agency: Sovereign Tech Fellowship

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Agency%3A%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Fellowship.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sovereign.tech%2Fprograms%2Ffellowship%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fapply.sovereign.tech%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Agency%3A%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Fellowship%0AAward%3A%20Freelance%3A%20hourly%20rate%20negotiated%20at%20market%20rates%2C%203-12%20months%2C%206-32%20h%2Fweek.%20Employment%3A%20TVoeD-Bund%20based%2C%20EUR%2064%2C000%20to%2082%2C000%20per%20year%20full-time%2C%202-year%20contracts%2C%2020-40%20h%2Fweek%20%28up%20to%20three%20positions%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20call%20expected%202027%20%28unconfirmed%3B%202026%20round%20closed%202026-04-06%2C%20next%20round%20to%20be%20announced%20via%20newsletter%20and%20social%20media%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Individual%20open%20source%20maintainers%2C%20community%20managers%20or%20technical%20writers%20with%20verifiable%20open%20source%20work%20on%20suitably%20licensed%20projects.%20Freelancers%20must%20not%20be%20paid%20otherwise%20for%20the%20same%20work%20and%20cannot%20apply%20on%20behalf%20of%20an%20organisation%20employing%20them%3B%20employment%20track%20requires%20being%20located%20in%20and%20authorised%20to%20work%20in%20Germany.%20Geography%3A%20Freelance%3A%20unconfirmed%2C%20apparently%20worldwide%3B%20Employment%3A%20Germany%20only.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20only%20%28organisations%20are%20pointed%20to%20the%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Fund%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.sovereign.tech%2Fprograms%2Ffellowship%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fapply.sovereign.tech%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Apply%20via%20the%20online%20portal%20in%20English%20during%20the%20open%20window%20%28one%20application%20per%20person%3B%20no%20email%20materials%29.%202.%20Shortlist%20and%20video%20interviews.%203.%20Decisions%20and%20offers%20%282026%20round%3A%20interviews%20from%20late%20April%2C%20offers%20by%20mid-May%29.%204.%20Contract%20start%20%28freelance%20contract%20or%20employment%20with%20Sovereign%20Tech%20Agency%20GmbH%29.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20portal%20application%20in%20English%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20verifiable%20open%20source%20maintainer%20%2F%20community%20%2F%20technical%20writing%20work%0A-%20Projects%20worked%20on%20and%20their%20licences%0A-%20Proposed%20fellowship%20work%20%28sustainability%2C%20mentoring%2C%20onboarding%2C%20technical%20improvements%29%0A-%20Track%20choice%20%28freelance%20or%20employment%29%20and%20availability%20in%20hours%2Fweek%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%205-6%20weeks%20after%20the%20deadline%20%282026%3A%20deadline%20Apr%206%2C%20offers%20by%20May%2015%29%0AObligations%3A%20Work%20on%20project%20sustainability%3B%20take%20part%20in%20events%2C%20communications%2C%20interviews%20and%20evaluation%20reporting%3B%20join%20the%20maintainers%20community%3B%20full-time%20fellows%20also%20advise%20on%20Agency%20program%20design.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20A%20ForkMesh%20maintainer%20could%20apply%20as%20a%20freelance%20fellow%20to%20work%20on%20the%20open%20mirror%20protocol%2C%20contributor%20onboarding%20and%20documentation.%20The%20fellowship%20pays%20maintainers%20of%20open%20infrastructure%2C%20but%20it%20funds%20the%20person%2C%20not%20the%20company.%0ARisks%3A%20Closed%20until%20a%202027%20round%20is%20announced%20%28dates%20unconfirmed%29.%20Individual-only%20and%20cannot%20be%20used%20by%20a%20company%20to%20fund%20its%20employees%3B%20requires%20an%20established%20maintainer%20track%20record%20on%20widely%20used%20infrastructure.%20Employment%20track%20Germany-only.)** · [Program page](https://www.sovereign.tech/programs/fellowship) · [Apply](https://apply.sovereign.tech)

- **Funder:** Sovereign Tech Agency
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** Freelance: hourly rate negotiated at market rates, 3-12 months, 6-32 h/week. Employment: TVoeD-Bund based, EUR 64,000 to 82,000 per year full-time, 2-year contracts, 20-40 h/week (up to three positions) (about $95,000)
- **Deadline:** Next call expected 2027 (unconfirmed; 2026 round closed 2026-04-06, next round to be announced via newsletter and social media) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 25/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Individual open source maintainers, community managers or technical writers with verifiable open source work on suitably licensed projects. Freelancers must not be paid otherwise for the same work and cannot apply on behalf of an organisation employing them; employment track requires being located in and authorised to work in Germany.
- **Geography:** Freelance: unconfirmed, apparently worldwide; Employment: Germany only · **Entity types:** Individuals only (organisations are pointed to the Sovereign Tech Fund)
- **Decision time:** About 5-6 weeks after the deadline (2026: deadline Apr 6, offers by May 15)
- **Obligations:** Work on project sustainability; take part in events, communications, interviews and evaluation reporting; join the maintainers community; full-time fellows also advise on Agency program design.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** A ForkMesh maintainer could apply as a freelance fellow to work on the open mirror protocol, contributor onboarding and documentation. The fellowship pays maintainers of open infrastructure, but it funds the person, not the company.

**How to apply:**

1. Apply via the online portal in English during the open window (one application per person; no email materials).
2. Shortlist and video interviews.
3. Decisions and offers (2026 round: interviews from late April, offers by mid-May).
4. Contract start (freelance contract or employment with Sovereign Tech Agency GmbH).

**What the application asks for:**

- Online portal application in English
- Evidence of verifiable open source maintainer / community / technical writing work
- Projects worked on and their licences
- Proposed fellowship work (sustainability, mentoring, onboarding, technical improvements)
- Track choice (freelance or employment) and availability in hours/week

**Risks:** Closed until a 2027 round is announced (dates unconfirmed). Individual-only and cannot be used by a company to fund its employees; requires an established maintainer track record on widely used infrastructure. Employment track Germany-only.

**Sources:** <https://www.sovereign.tech/programs/fellowship>

ys%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Avalanche%20Team1%3A%20Team1%20Mini%20Grants%20for%20Avalanche%20builders%0AAward%3A%20Mini%20Grants%3A%20up%20to%20%2410%2C000%20%28open%20application%29.%20Accelerator%20Grants%3A%20up%20to%20%2430%2C000%20%28no%20open%20applications%3B%20committee-selected%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20New%20and%20established%20projects%2C%20community%20builders%2C%20developers%20and%20people%20with%20unique%20ideas%20building%20on%20Avalanche%20%28smart%20contracts%2C%20applications%20or%20ecosystem%20contributions%29.%20Accelerator%20Grants%20are%20for%20startups%20and%20established%20projects%20and%20can%20follow%20a%20Mini%20Grant%20or%20be%20fast-tracked%20by%20the%20committee.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20worldwide%20%28no%20regional%20restriction%20stated%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2C%20startups%2C%20established%20projects%20%28for-profit%20or%20community%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fteam1.network%2Fgrants%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgo.team1.network%2Fmini-grants%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Apply%20through%20the%20Mini%20Grants%20form%20%28go.team1.network%2Fmini-grants%29.%202.%20Team1%20reviews%3B%20questions%20via%20the%20Team1%20Discord.%203.%20Funded%20projects%20may%20later%20be%20proposed%20for%20an%20Accelerator%20Grant%2C%20voted%20on%20by%20a%20committee%20of%20Avalanche%20team%20members%20and%20community%20representatives.%20unconfirmed%3A%20review%20time%2C%20payment%20and%20milestone%20terms.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Mini%20Grants%20application%20form%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20fields%20not%20shown%20on%20the%20official%20page%29%0A-%20Project%20description%20and%20Avalanche%20integration%20plan%0A-%20Team%2Fbuilder%20details%0A-%20Wallet%20or%20payment%20details%20%28unconfirmed%20currency%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20milestones%2C%20reporting%20and%20payment%20currency%20not%20stated%20on%20the%20official%20pages.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20small%20Avalanche%20integration%3A%20ForkMesh%20mirrors%20and%20signs%20Avalanche%20L1%20and%20tooling%20repositories%20and%20offers%20an%20Avalanche%20%28C-Chain%2FUSDC%29%20payout%20option%20in%20the%20contribution%20pool%2C%20giving%20Avalanche%20builders%20a%20censorship-resistant%20code-hosting%20fallback.%20Fast%2C%20low-effort%20funding%3B%20only%20worth%20it%20if%20the%20integration%20also%20serves%20ForkMesh%27s%20multi-chain%20payout%20plans.%0ARisks%3A%20Arbitrum%20Foundation%20grants%20are%20not%20an%20option%20now%3A%20the%20Foundation%20Grant%20Program%20is%20marked%20inactive%20with%20an%20undated%20%27applications%20will%20open%20soon%27%2C%20and%20the%20DAO%27s%20D.A.O.%20Season%203%20ran%20only%20to%20March%202026%20with%20no%20Season%204%20found.%20Avalanche%20Retro9000%20and%20infraBUIDL%28%29%20were%20also%20checked%3A%20Retro9000%20Round%202%20closed%20April%202026%20and%20infraBUIDL%28%29%20is%20no%20longer%20listed%20on%20the%20Builder%20Hub.%20Team1%20Mini%20Grants%20are%20small%20and%20high-volume%20%28%27does%20not%20guarantee%20funding%27%29%2C%20and%20require%20a%20genuine%20Avalanche%20integration%20that%20ForkMesh%20does%20not%20have%20today.)** · [Program page](https://team1.network/grants) · [Apply](https://go.team1.network/mini-grants)

- **Funder:** Avalanche Team1 (Avalanche ecosystem / Ava Labs Builder Hub)
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Mini Grants: up to $10,000 (open application). Accelerator Grants: up to $30,000 (no open applications; committee-selected) (about $10,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (open)
- **Fit score:** 25/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** New and established projects, community builders, developers and people with unique ideas building on Avalanche (smart contracts, applications or ecosystem contributions). Accelerator Grants are for startups and established projects and can follow a Mini Grant or be fast-tracked by the committee.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: worldwide (no regional restriction stated) · **Entity types:** Individuals, startups, established projects (for-profit or community)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: milestones, reporting and payment currency not stated on the official pages.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a small Avalanche integration: ForkMesh mirrors and signs Avalanche L1 and tooling repositories and offers an Avalanche (C-Chain/USDC) payout option in the contribution pool, giving Avalanche builders a censorship-resistant code-hosting fallback. Fast, low-effort funding; only worth it if the integration also serves ForkMesh's multi-chain payout plans.

**How to apply:**

1. Apply through the Mini Grants form (go.team1.network/mini-grants).
2. Team1 reviews; questions via the Team1 Discord.
3. Funded projects may later be proposed for an Accelerator Grant, voted on by a committee of Avalanche team members and community representatives. unconfirmed: review time, payment and milestone terms.

**What the application asks for:**

- Mini Grants application form (unconfirmed: fields not shown on the official page)
- Project description and Avalanche integration plan
- Team/builder details
- Wallet or payment details (unconfirmed currency)

**Risks:** Arbitrum Foundation grants are not an option now: the Foundation Grant Program is marked inactive with an undated 'applications will open soon', and the DAO's D.A.O. Season 3 ran only to March 2026 with no Season 4 found. Avalanche Retro9000 and infraBUIDL() were also checked: Retro9000 Round 2 closed April 2026 and infraBUIDL() is no longer listed on the Builder Hub. Team1 Mini Grants are small and high-volume ('does not guarantee funding'), and require a genuine Avalanche integration that ForkMesh does not have today.

**Sources:** <https://team1.network/grants>, <https://build.avax.network/grants/team1-mini-grants>, <https://build.avax.network/grants/infrabuidl>, <https://arbitrum.foundation/grants>

served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20OpenSats%20General%20Grant%0AAward%3A%20Not%20published%3A%20grant%20amounts%20are%20private%20by%20default%3B%20paid%20only%20in%20bitcoin%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28applications%20evaluated%20on%20an%20ongoing%20basis%3B%20intake%20closed%20during%20March%2C%20June%2C%20September%20and%20December%2C%20so%20it%20pauses%20again%20in%20December%202026%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Any%20free%20and%20open%20source%20project%20aligned%20with%20the%20OpenSats%20mission%2C%20related%20to%20Bitcoin%20or%20an%20ancillary%20technology%20that%20helps%20Bitcoin%20use%20%28e.g.%20Tor%29%3B%20OpenSats%20also%20runs%20a%20Nostr%20Fund.%20All%20programs%20are%20open%20to%20applicants%20worldwide.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20projects%20and%20developers%3B%20whether%20companies%20can%20be%20grantees%20is%20not%20stated.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopensats.org%2Fapply%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopensats.org%2Fapply%2Fgrant%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20the%20General%20Grant%20form%20at%20opensats.org%2Fapply%2Fgrant%20%28ideally%20a%20concise%2C%20one-page-length%20application%29%20outside%20the%20closed%20months.%202.%20Board%20vets%20the%20application%20on%20technical%20merit%2C%20potential%20impact%20and%20ability%20to%20execute%2C%20with%20possible%20due%20diligence%20contact.%203.%20If%20approved%2C%20provide%20tax%20information%20depending%20on%20location%20and%20bitcoin%20%2F%20lightning%20payout%20addresses.%204.%20Progress%20reports%20reviewed%20by%20committees%20during%20the%20grant.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20General%20Grant%20application%20form%20%28concise%2C%20ideally%20about%20one%20page%3B%20exact%20fields%20not%20public%29%0A-%20Accurate%20contact%20information%20for%20due%20diligence%0A-%20Proof%20of%20a%20proper%20open%20source%20licence%20and%20public%20source%20code%0A-%20After%20approval%3A%20tax%20information%20depending%20on%20location%2C%20bitcoin%20%2F%20lightning%20payout%20addresses%0A-%20Progress%20reports%20during%20the%20grant%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20ongoing%20review%2C%20no%20timeframe%20published%0AObligations%3A%20Proper%20open%20source%20licence%20with%20source%20and%20docs%20publicly%20available%20free%20of%20charge%20and%20without%20restrictions%3B%20educational%20material%20under%20an%20open%20licence%3B%20progress%20reports%3B%20share%20experience%20and%20preferably%20mentor%3B%20payment%20in%20bitcoin%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20signed%2C%20live%20ForkMesh%20mirrors%20for%20Bitcoin%20and%20Nostr%20open%20source%20repositories%20plus%20interoperability%20with%20Nostr%20Git%20collaboration%20%28in-repo%20signed%20issues%2FPRs%20mapped%20to%20Nostr%20events%29%2C%20so%20those%20projects%20stay%20reachable%20and%20reviewable%20if%20a%20central%20code%20host%20blocks%20them.%20OpenSats%20funds%20censorship-resistant%20FOSS%20that%20the%20Bitcoin%20and%20Nostr%20ecosystems%20depend%20on.%0ARisks%3A%20OpenSats%20requires%20a%20Bitcoin%20%28or%20ancillary%29%20connection%2C%20which%20ForkMesh%20has%20only%20through%20this%20proposed%20work%3B%20ForkMesh%27s%20Solana-referenced%20payout%20pool%20may%20conflict%20with%20OpenSats%27%20Bitcoin-only%20mission.%20Amounts%20unpublished%3B%20paid%20in%20bitcoin.)** · [Program page](https://opensats.org/apply) · [Apply](https://opensats.org/apply/grant)

- **Funder:** OpenSats
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** Not published: grant amounts are private by default; paid only in bitcoin (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (applications evaluated on an ongoing basis; intake closed during March, June, September and December, so it pauses again in December 2026) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 25/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Any free and open source project aligned with the OpenSats mission, related to Bitcoin or an ancillary technology that helps Bitcoin use (e.g. Tor); OpenSats also runs a Nostr Fund. All programs are open to applicants worldwide.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: projects and developers; whether companies can be grantees is not stated
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: ongoing review, no timeframe published
- **Obligations:** Proper open source licence with source and docs publicly available free of charge and without restrictions; educational material under an open licence; progress reports; share experience and preferably mentor; payment in bitcoin; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose signed, live ForkMesh mirrors for Bitcoin and Nostr open source repositories plus interoperability with Nostr Git collaboration (in-repo signed issues/PRs mapped to Nostr events), so those projects stay reachable and reviewable if a central code host blocks them. OpenSats funds censorship-resistant FOSS that the Bitcoin and Nostr ecosystems depend on.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit the General Grant form at opensats.org/apply/grant (ideally a concise, one-page-length application) outside the closed months.
2. Board vets the application on technical merit, potential impact and ability to execute, with possible due diligence contact.
3. If approved, provide tax information depending on location and bitcoin / lightning payout addresses.
4. Progress reports reviewed by committees during the grant.

**What the application asks for:**

- General Grant application form (concise, ideally about one page; exact fields not public)
- Accurate contact information for due diligence
- Proof of a proper open source licence and public source code
- After approval: tax information depending on location, bitcoin / lightning payout addresses
- Progress reports during the grant

**Risks:** OpenSats requires a Bitcoin (or ancillary) connection, which ForkMesh has only through this proposed work; ForkMesh's Solana-referenced payout pool may conflict with OpenSats' Bitcoin-only mission. Amounts unpublished; paid in bitcoin.

**Sources:** <https://opensats.org/apply>, <https://opensats.org/apply/grant>, <https://opensats.org/faq>, <https://chanzuckerberg.com/eoss/>, <https://grantedai.com/grants/open-source-for-science-fund-renaissance-philanthropy-with-biohub-wel-87f228ff>

Flp%2Fproject-alexandria%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.cloudflare.com%2Flp%2Fproject-alexandria%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Confirm%20the%20project%20needs%20more%20than%20Cloudflare%27s%20free%20tiers.%202.%20Submit%20the%20application%20form%20on%20the%20Project%20Alexandria%20landing%20page.%203.%20Cloudflare%20reviews%20and%20tailors%20a%20credit%20package.%204.%20Accepted%20projects%20receive%20annual%20recurring%20credits%20and%20access%20to%20a%20dedicated%20Discord%20support%20channel.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20Alexandria%20application%20form%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20fields%20not%20visible%3B%20landing%20page%20returned%20403%20to%20automated%20fetch%29%0A-%20Link%20to%20repository%20with%20recognised%20OSS%20license%0A-%20Description%20of%20the%20project%27s%20non-profit%20basis%20or%20mission%20alignment%0A-%20Description%20of%20Cloudflare%20usage%20needs%20%28Workers%2FPages%20traffic%2C%20R2%20storage%2C%20security%2C%20Zero%20Trust%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20none%20stated%20beyond%20annual%20recurring%20credits%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Use%20R2%20for%20an%20off-fleet%20archival%20copy%20of%20signed%20mirror%20bundles%20and%20release%20artifacts%2C%20and%20WAF%2FDDoS%20protection%20in%20front%20of%20public%20clone%20endpoints%2C%20strengthening%20ForkMesh%27s%20resilience%20story.%20The%20served%20stack%20deliberately%20avoids%20Cloudflare%20Workers%2FD1%2FDurable%20Objects%2C%20so%20the%20ask%20should%20be%20storage%20and%20edge%20security%20only.%0ARisks%3A%20Landing%20page%20could%20not%20be%20fetched%20today%20%28HTTP%20403%29%2C%20so%20current%20status%20is%20confirmed%20only%20via%20the%20September%202024%20announcement%20and%20search%20snippets.%20Non-profit%20wording%20may%20exclude%20a%20startup%20that%20sells%20hosted%20services.%20ForkMesh%20Next%20deliberately%20moved%20off%20Cloudflare%2C%20so%20taking%20credits%20reintroduces%20a%20single-provider%20dependency%20the%20project%20is%20designed%20to%20avoid.)** · [Program page](https://www.cloudflare.com/lp/project-alexandria/) · [Apply](https://www.cloudflare.com/lp/project-alexandria/)

- **Funder:** Cloudflare, Inc.
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Recurring annual credits tailored to project size and needs (plan upgrade to Pro/Business/Enterprise, higher Workers and Pages request limits, more R2 storage, expanded Zero Trust); no dollar amount published (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 25/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Open-source projects with a recognised OSI license that 'operate solely on a non-profit basis and/or otherwise align with the project mission' (the blog says and/or; the landing page reportedly says and). Small projects that fit within free tiers are told they do not need credits.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Open-source projects; non-profit basis or mission-aligned (unconfirmed whether for-profit-backed projects qualify)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: none stated beyond annual recurring credits; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Use R2 for an off-fleet archival copy of signed mirror bundles and release artifacts, and WAF/DDoS protection in front of public clone endpoints, strengthening ForkMesh's resilience story. The served stack deliberately avoids Cloudflare Workers/D1/Durable Objects, so the ask should be storage and edge security only.

**How to apply:**

1. Confirm the project needs more than Cloudflare's free tiers.
2. Submit the application form on the Project Alexandria landing page.
3. Cloudflare reviews and tailors a credit package.
4. Accepted projects receive annual recurring credits and access to a dedicated Discord support channel.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project Alexandria application form (unconfirmed: fields not visible; landing page returned 403 to automated fetch)
- Link to repository with recognised OSS license
- Description of the project's non-profit basis or mission alignment
- Description of Cloudflare usage needs (Workers/Pages traffic, R2 storage, security, Zero Trust)

**Risks:** Landing page could not be fetched today (HTTP 403), so current status is confirmed only via the September 2024 announcement and search snippets. Non-profit wording may exclude a startup that sells hosted services. ForkMesh Next deliberately moved off Cloudflare, so taking credits reintroduces a single-provider dependency the project is designed to avoid.

**Sources:** <https://blog.cloudflare.com/expanding-our-support-for-oss-projects-with-project-alexandria/>, <https://www.cloudflare.com/lp/project-alexandria/>

he%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20OpenAI%20for%20Startups%0AAward%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20free%20API%20credits%2C%20rate-limit%20upgrades%20and%20time%20with%20solutions%20engineers%3B%20amount%20not%20published%20%28regional%20variant%3A%20OpenAI%20for%20Australia%20startup%20program%20up%20to%20USD%2015%2C000%2C%20reported%20raised%20to%20USD%2050%2C000%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28via%20VC%20referral%29%20%28invite-only%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Startups%20backed%20by%20an%20eligible%20OpenAI%20VC%20partner%3B%20a%20referral%20code%20from%20the%20VC%20is%20required%20to%20apply.%20Third-party%20reports%20conflict%20on%20whether%20any%20direct%20self-serve%20route%20remains%20in%202026.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20worldwide%20via%20partner%20VCs%3B%20separate%20Australia-specific%20program.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%2C%20VC-backed%20startups.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopenai.com%2Fbusiness%2Fwhy-openai%2Fstartups%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fopenai.com%2Fbusiness%2Fwhy-openai%2Fstartups%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Obtain%20backing%20from%20%28or%20a%20relationship%20with%29%20a%20VC%20in%20OpenAI%27s%20partner%20network.%202.%20Request%20a%20referral%20code%20from%20the%20VC.%203.%20Submit%20the%20startup%20application%20on%20OpenAI%27s%20startups%20page%20with%20the%20referral%20code.%204.%20OpenAI%20reviews%20and%20applies%20credits%20and%20rate-limit%20upgrades%20to%20the%20API%20organization.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20VC%20referral%20code%0A-%20OpenAI%20API%20organization%20ID%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20company%20name%2C%20website%2C%20funding%20details%20and%20use%20case%20description%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity%20to%20OpenAI.%20Credits%20subject%20to%20OpenAI%20terms%20and%20expiry%20%28unconfirmed%20duration%29.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Credits%20would%20fund%20Codex%20agent%20runs%20in%20ForkMesh%20worktrees%20and%20the%20runner%20tier%2C%20where%20agents%20open%20signed%20pull%20requests%20in%20decentralized%20repositories.%20OpenAI%20wants%20startups%20building%20agentic%20coding%20products%20on%20its%20models%2C%20and%20ForkMesh%20ships%20Codex%20as%20a%20first-class%20agent%20runtime.%0ARisks%3A%20Official%20page%20returned%20HTTP%20403%20to%20fetch%2C%20so%20not%20verified%20today%3B%20search%20snippet%20of%20the%20official%20page%20says%20credits%20require%20a%20VC%20referral%20code.%20A%20third-party%20guide%20claims%20OpenAI%20stopped%20offering%20startup%20credits%20entirely.%20Without%20a%20partner-VC%20backer%20ForkMesh%20likely%20cannot%20access%20it.%20Distinct%20from%20Codex%20for%20Open%20Source%20%28covered%20elsewhere%29%3B%20OpenAI%20Grove%20%28USD%2050K%20credits%2C%20individual%20founder%20cohort%29%20is%20an%20alternative%20worth%20checking.)** · [Program page](https://openai.com/business/why-openai/startups/) · [Apply](https://openai.com/business/why-openai/startups/)

- **Funder:** OpenAI
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** unconfirmed: free API credits, rate-limit upgrades and time with solutions engineers; amount not published (regional variant: OpenAI for Australia startup program up to USD 15,000, reported raised to USD 50,000) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (via VC referral) (invite-only)
- **Fit score:** 25/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Startups backed by an eligible OpenAI VC partner; a referral code from the VC is required to apply. Third-party reports conflict on whether any direct self-serve route remains in 2026.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: worldwide via partner VCs; separate Australia-specific program · **Entity types:** For-profit, VC-backed startups
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** No equity to OpenAI. Credits subject to OpenAI terms and expiry (unconfirmed duration).

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Credits would fund Codex agent runs in ForkMesh worktrees and the runner tier, where agents open signed pull requests in decentralized repositories. OpenAI wants startups building agentic coding products on its models, and ForkMesh ships Codex as a first-class agent runtime.

**How to apply:**

1. Obtain backing from (or a relationship with) a VC in OpenAI's partner network.
2. Request a referral code from the VC.
3. Submit the startup application on OpenAI's startups page with the referral code.
4. OpenAI reviews and applies credits and rate-limit upgrades to the API organization.

**What the application asks for:**

- VC referral code
- OpenAI API organization ID
- unconfirmed: company name, website, funding details and use case description

**Risks:** Official page returned HTTP 403 to fetch, so not verified today; search snippet of the official page says credits require a VC referral code. A third-party guide claims OpenAI stopped offering startup credits entirely. Without a partner-VC backer ForkMesh likely cannot access it. Distinct from Codex for Open Source (covered elsewhere); OpenAI Grove (USD 50K credits, individual founder cohort) is an alternative worth checking.

**Sources:** <https://openai.com/business/why-openai/startups/>, <https://openai.com/global-affairs/openai-for-australia/>, <https://guptadeepak.com/startup-offers/guides/openai-for-startups>, <https://itbrief.com.au/story/openai-boosts-australian-startups-credits-to-usd-50-000>

- **Funder:** thanks.dev
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** unconfirmed: no fixed amount; donations are distributed to dependencies according to usage/popularity by donors' configuration (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 25/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** unconfirmed: open source projects that appear in donors' dependency trees can receive funds; a community report indicates funds go to the owning organisation/account rather than to individual maintainers per sub-project.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: Worldwide · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: open source projects / organisations / maintainers (payout via the project's linked account)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: no review; funds accrue automatically based on donor dependency usage
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: platform terms and any fees; no reporting or equity known

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Publish ForkMesh's reusable libraries (signed-record format, mirror verification client, CLI) as packages so companies funding their dependency trees through thanks.dev route money to ForkMesh. Pairs naturally with an Open Source Collective host as the receiving account.

**How to apply:**

unconfirmed:
1. Make the repository and its packages discoverable as a dependency (published packages).
2. Sign in to thanks.dev with the code host account that owns the project.
3. Claim/accept accrued donations and configure a payout channel.
4. Receive periodic distributions as donors' dependency trees include the project.

**What the application asks for:**

- unconfirmed: code-host (GitHub/GitLab) account owning the project
- unconfirmed: payout details or linked fiscal host (e.g. Open Source Collective)

**Risks:** Program details could not be confirmed: the official docs host (docs.thanks.dev) did not resolve today and the homepage shows only a tagline. Income depends on ForkMesh being a widely used dependency, which an end-user application with few published packages is not; expected amounts are small.

**Sources:** <https://thanks.dev/>, <https://discuss.python.org/t/distributing-pypa-funds-to-project-maintainers/108119>

%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Interledger%20Foundation%3A%20Innovation%20Grants%3A%20Open%20Payments%20SDK%20grant%0AAward%3A%20USD%205%2C000%20-%2050%2C000%20per%20award%20%28per%20the%20Foundation%27s%20March%202026%20community%20newsletter%3B%20not%20shown%20on%20the%20closed%20program%20page%29.%20Sibling%20Open%20Payments%20Accelerator%3A%20USD%2050%2C000%20plus%20Summit%20travel%20funding.%0ADeadline%3A%202026%20cycle%20closed%3B%20next%20cycle%20expected%20around%20April%202027%20%28unconfirmed%3A%202026%20call%20opened%201%20April%202026%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Individual%20developers%20or%20organisations%20with%20the%20technical%20capacity%20to%20build%20Open%20Payments%20SDK%20tooling%2C%20not%20subject%20to%20US%20sanctions.%20Work%20must%20advance%20Open%20Payments%20developer%20tooling%20%28SDKs%2C%20security%20scheme%20support%2C%20HTTP%20Message%20Signatures%20libraries%2C%20specification%20docs%29%3B%202026%20themes%20were%20GNAP%20in%20OpenAPI%2C%20Kiota%20GNAP%2FRuby%2FArazzo%20support.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20except%20US-sanctioned%20persons%2Fcountries.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20and%20organisations%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20for-profit%20status%20not%20restricted%20on%20the%20page%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Finterledger.org%2Fgrant%2Finnovation%2Fsdk%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Finterledger.org%2Fgrants%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Subscribe%20to%20Interledger%20grant%20alerts%20and%20watch%20for%20the%20next%20SDK%20call%20%282026%20opened%201%20April%29.%202.%20Read%20the%20call%20themes%20and%20the%20Grantmaking%20FAQs.%203.%20Submit%20the%20application%20through%20the%20Foundation%27s%20Submittable%20portal%20%28submit.interledger.org%29.%204.%20Review%20by%20the%20program%20team.%205.%20Award%20agreement%20and%20milestone-based%20delivery%20of%20open-source%20tooling.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Submittable%20application%20form%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20questions%20not%20published%20while%20closed%29%0A-%20Project%20proposal%20addressing%20one%20of%20the%20call%27s%20technical%20themes%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20technical%20capacity%20%28prior%20open-source%20work%2C%20team%29%0A-%20Budget%20and%20timeline%20%28unconfirmed%20format%29%0A-%20Sanctions%20eligibility%20confirmation%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20page%20does%20not%20list%20them%3B%20Interledger%20software%20is%20generally%20Apache-2.0%20licensed%20and%20grants%20typically%20carry%20milestones%20and%20reporting%20%28see%20Grantmaking%20FAQs%29.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20integrating%20Open%20Payments%20as%20the%20payout%20rail%20for%20ForkMesh%27s%20community%20contribution%20pool%2C%20delivered%20as%20a%20reusable%20open-source%20Open%20Payments%20client%20library%20%28e.g.%20for%20C%2B%2B%2FQt%20or%20Go%29%20with%20GNAP%20and%20HTTP%20Message%20Signatures%20support.%20This%20gives%20Interledger%20new%20SDK%20coverage%20plus%20a%20real%20deployment%20paying%20open-source%20contributors.%0ARisks%3A%202026%20SDK%20and%20Accelerator%20cycles%20are%20closed%20and%20next-year%20themes%20are%20unknown%3B%20the%20SDK%20grant%20funds%20narrowly%20scoped%20Interledger%20tooling%2C%20not%20ForkMesh%20itself.%20ForkMesh%27s%20payout%20pool%20references%20Solana%3B%20Interledger%20reviewers%20may%20want%20Open%20Payments%20to%20be%20the%20main%20rail.%20The%20Accelerator%20targets%20financial%20inclusion%20for%20underserved%20communities%2C%20which%20ForkMesh%20does%20not%20fit.)** · [Program page](https://interledger.org/grant/innovation/sdk/) · [Apply](https://interledger.org/grants)

- **Funder:** Interledger Foundation
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** USD 5,000 - 50,000 per award (per the Foundation's March 2026 community newsletter; not shown on the closed program page). Sibling Open Payments Accelerator: USD 50,000 plus Summit travel funding. (about $5,000 - $50,000)
- **Deadline:** 2026 cycle closed; next cycle expected around April 2027 (unconfirmed: 2026 call opened 1 April 2026) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 24/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Individual developers or organisations with the technical capacity to build Open Payments SDK tooling, not subject to US sanctions. Work must advance Open Payments developer tooling (SDKs, security scheme support, HTTP Message Signatures libraries, specification docs); 2026 themes were GNAP in OpenAPI, Kiota GNAP/Ruby/Arazzo support.
- **Geography:** Worldwide except US-sanctioned persons/countries · **Entity types:** Individuals and organisations (unconfirmed: for-profit status not restricted on the page)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: page does not list them; Interledger software is generally Apache-2.0 licensed and grants typically carry milestones and reporting (see Grantmaking FAQs).

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose integrating Open Payments as the payout rail for ForkMesh's community contribution pool, delivered as a reusable open-source Open Payments client library (e.g. for C++/Qt or Go) with GNAP and HTTP Message Signatures support. This gives Interledger new SDK coverage plus a real deployment paying open-source contributors.

**How to apply:**

1. Subscribe to Interledger grant alerts and watch for the next SDK call (2026 opened 1 April).
2. Read the call themes and the Grantmaking FAQs.
3. Submit the application through the Foundation's Submittable portal (submit.interledger.org).
4. Review by the program team.
5. Award agreement and milestone-based delivery of open-source tooling.

**What the application asks for:**

- Submittable application form (unconfirmed: exact questions not published while closed)
- Project proposal addressing one of the call's technical themes
- Evidence of technical capacity (prior open-source work, team)
- Budget and timeline (unconfirmed format)
- Sanctions eligibility confirmation

**Risks:** 2026 SDK and Accelerator cycles are closed and next-year themes are unknown; the SDK grant funds narrowly scoped Interledger tooling, not ForkMesh itself. ForkMesh's payout pool references Solana; Interledger reviewers may want Open Payments to be the main rail. The Accelerator targets financial inclusion for underserved communities, which ForkMesh does not fit.

**Sources:** <https://interledger.org/grants>, <https://interledger.org/grant/innovation/>, <https://interledger.org/grant/innovation/sdk/>, <https://interledger.org/grant/innovation/accelerator/>, <https://community.interledger.org/interledger/march-2026-discover-new-grant-opportunities-with-interledger-4e92>
