0source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Aptos%20Foundation%3A%20Aptos%20Ecosystem%20Grants%0AAward%3A%20Grants%20typically%20range%20from%20%245%2C000%20to%20%2450%2C000%20and%20are%20milestone-based%20%28Foundation%20also%20cites%20up%20to%20%24150K%20in%20non-dilutive%20grants%20across%20programs%3B%20SecCreds%20audit%20grants%20up%20to%20%2425%2C000%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Founders%20and%20startups%20building%20applications%2C%20infrastructure%20and%20tools%20that%20expand%20the%20Aptos%20network%3B%20projects%20should%20be%20Aptos-native%20or%20open%20source%2C%20or%20have%20a%20clear%20plan%20to%20open-source%20over%20time.%20A%20live%20product%20with%20traction%20is%20expected%20%28per%20search%20summaries%20of%20the%20official%20page%29.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20worldwide%20%28no%20restriction%20found%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Startups%2Ffor-profit%20teams%2C%20open-source%20projects%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20individuals%20and%20nonprofits%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Faptosnetwork.com%2Fgrants%2Fecosystem%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Faptosfoundation.org%2Fgrants%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20the%20Ecosystem%20Grants%20application%20with%20roadmap%2C%20milestones%20and%20budget%2C%20explaining%20how%20the%20grant%20supports%20growth.%202.%20Rolling%20review%20by%20the%20Foundation%20grants%20team.%203.%20Agree%20milestones%20and%20sign%20the%20grant%20agreement.%204.%20Funds%20released%20in%20stages%20as%20milestones%20are%20met.%20unconfirmed%3A%20KYC%20and%20review%20time.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20roadmap%0A-%20Milestones%0A-%20Budget%0A-%20Explanation%20of%20how%20the%20grant%20supports%20growth%20and%20expands%20the%20Aptos%20network%0A-%20Open-source%20status%20or%20open-sourcing%20plan%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20a%20live%20product%20and%20traction%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20application%20form%20fields%20%28official%20page%20blocked%20to%20automated%20fetch%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%20%28rolling%20review%29%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone-based%20staged%20payments%3B%20open-source%20preference.%20unconfirmed%3A%20reporting%2C%20KYC%20and%20payment%20currency.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20an%20Aptos%20integration%20of%20ForkMesh%27s%20contribution%20pool%20and%20a%20signed%20mirror%20service%20for%20Move%2FAptos%20framework%20and%20tooling%20repositories%2C%20giving%20Aptos%20builders%20a%20resilient%2C%20censorship-resistant%20code%20host.%20Aptos%20funds%20infrastructure%20and%20tools%20that%20expand%20the%20network%2C%20but%20ForkMesh%20would%20need%20a%20genuine%20Aptos-native%20component.%0ARisks%3A%20Web3%20Foundation%20%28Polkadot%29%20grants%20are%20not%20an%20option%20now%3A%20the%20W3F%20Grants-Program%20repo%20says%20the%20general%20Grants%20Program%20is%20discontinued%20and%20no%20new%20applications%20are%20accepted.%20The%20Aptos%20official%20pages%20are%20behind%20a%20bot%20checkpoint%20%28HTTP%20429%20%2F%20Vercel%20challenge%29%2C%20so%20the%20details%20above%20come%20from%20search%20summaries%20of%20the%20official%20pages%2C%20not%20a%20fetch%3B%20check%20manually%20before%20applying.%20The%20program%20expects%20a%20live%20product%20with%20traction%20on%20Aptos%2C%20which%20ForkMesh%20does%20not%20have.)** · [Program page](https://aptosnetwork.com/grants/ecosystem) · [Apply](https://aptosfoundation.org/grants)

- **Funder:** Aptos Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Grants typically range from $5,000 to $50,000 and are milestone-based (Foundation also cites up to $150K in non-dilutive grants across programs; SecCreds audit grants up to $25,000) (about $5,000 - $50,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 24/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Founders and startups building applications, infrastructure and tools that expand the Aptos network; projects should be Aptos-native or open source, or have a clear plan to open-source over time. A live product with traction is expected (per search summaries of the official page).
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: worldwide (no restriction found) · **Entity types:** Startups/for-profit teams, open-source projects (unconfirmed: individuals and nonprofits)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed (rolling review)
- **Obligations:** Milestone-based staged payments; open-source preference. unconfirmed: reporting, KYC and payment currency.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose an Aptos integration of ForkMesh's contribution pool and a signed mirror service for Move/Aptos framework and tooling repositories, giving Aptos builders a resilient, censorship-resistant code host. Aptos funds infrastructure and tools that expand the network, but ForkMesh would need a genuine Aptos-native component.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit the Ecosystem Grants application with roadmap, milestones and budget, explaining how the grant supports growth.
2. Rolling review by the Foundation grants team.
3. Agree milestones and sign the grant agreement.
4. Funds released in stages as milestones are met. unconfirmed: KYC and review time.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project roadmap
- Milestones
- Budget
- Explanation of how the grant supports growth and expands the Aptos network
- Open-source status or open-sourcing plan
- Evidence of a live product and traction
- unconfirmed: exact application form fields (official page blocked to automated fetch)

**Risks:** Web3 Foundation (Polkadot) grants are not an option now: the W3F Grants-Program repo says the general Grants Program is discontinued and no new applications are accepted. The Aptos official pages are behind a bot checkpoint (HTTP 429 / Vercel challenge), so the details above come from search summaries of the official pages, not a fetch; check manually before applying. The program expects a live product with traction on Aptos, which ForkMesh does not have.

**Sources:** <https://aptosnetwork.com/grants/ecosystem>, <https://aptosfoundation.org/grants>, <https://aptosfoundation.org/grants/registry>, <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/w3f/Grants-Program/master/README.md>

t%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Enterprise%20Singapore%3A%20Startup%20SG%20Tech%0AAward%3A%20POC%20grant%20cap%20SGD%20400%2C000%3B%20POV%20grant%20cap%20SGD%20800%2C000%20%28raised%20on%202%20Jan%202025%29%3B%20paid%20on%20milestones%3B%20Enterprise%20Singapore%20holds%20a%20right%20to%20subscribe%20for%20shares%20%28equity%20component%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Singapore%20private%20limited%20start-ups%20registered%20within%20the%20past%2010%20years%2C%20with%20at%20least%2030%25%20local%20equity%20held%20by%20Singaporeans%2FPRs%2C%20not%20a%20corporate%20subsidiary%20at%20incorporation%2C%20group%20turnover%20at%20most%20SGD%20100%20million%20or%20at%20most%20200%20workers%2C%20and%20core%20R%26D%20in%20Singapore.%20Must%20have%20proprietary%20technology%20%28integrating%20off-the-shelf%20components%20does%20not%20count%29.%20Paid-up%20capital%20must%20be%20at%20least%2010%25%20%28POC%29%20or%2020%25%20%28POV%29%20of%20the%20grant.%20Geography%3A%20Singapore.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Singapore-incorporated%20private%20limited%20companies%20%28public-sector%20research%20PIs%20for%20POC%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.startupsg.gov.sg%2Fprogrammes%2F4897%2Fstartup-sg-tech%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgo.gov.sg%2Faskenterprisesg%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Download%20and%20fill%20in%20the%20Startup%20SG%20Tech%20Registration%20of%20Interest%20form.%202.%20Submit%20it%20via%20go.gov.sg%2Faskenterprisesg.%203.%20Discuss%20scope%20with%20an%20Enterprise%20Singapore%20officer.%204.%20Submit%20the%20formal%20Stage%202%20application%20%28POC%20or%20POV%29.%205.%20Milestone-based%20grant%20with%20an%20equity%20subscription%20right.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Startup%20SG%20Tech%20Registration%20of%20Interest%20form%0A-%20Formal%20Stage%202%20application%20%28contents%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20paid-up%20capital%20%28at%20least%2010%25%20of%20a%20POC%20grant%20or%2020%25%20of%20a%20POV%20grant%29%0A-%20Explanation%20of%20the%20science%2Ftechnology%20and%20proprietary%20IP%20or%20intellectual%20assets%20owned%20by%20the%20startup%0A-%20For%20POV%3A%20proof%20of%20interest%20from%20a%20potential%20customer%20or%20third-party%20investor%20and%20evidence%20of%20business%20competencies%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone-based%20payments%3B%20Enterprise%20Singapore%27s%20share-subscription%20right%3B%20one%20project%20at%20a%20time%3B%20core%20R%26D%20in%20Singapore.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20With%20a%20Singapore%20entity%2C%20a%20POC%20grant%20could%20fund%20the%20proprietary%20layer%20around%20the%20open%20protocol%2C%20such%20as%20hosted%20mirror-fleet%20orchestration%20and%20the%20runner%20tier.%20The%20scheme%20funds%20commercialisation%20of%20proprietary%2C%20scalable%20technology.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%2030%25%20local%20shareholding%20and%20R%26D%20in%20Singapore.%20The%20equity%20component%20dilutes%20ownership.%20An%20MIT-licensed%20core%20may%20not%20count%20as%20proprietary%20IP.%20IMDA%20alternatives%20were%20not%20checked.)** · [Program page](https://www.startupsg.gov.sg/programmes/4897/startup-sg-tech) · [Apply](https://go.gov.sg/askenterprisesg)

- **Funder:** Enterprise Singapore
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** POC grant cap SGD 400,000; POV grant cap SGD 800,000 (raised on 2 Jan 2025); paid on milestones; Enterprise Singapore holds a right to subscribe for shares (equity component) (about $620,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 22/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Singapore private limited start-ups registered within the past 10 years, with at least 30% local equity held by Singaporeans/PRs, not a corporate subsidiary at incorporation, group turnover at most SGD 100 million or at most 200 workers, and core R&D in Singapore. Must have proprietary technology (integrating off-the-shelf components does not count). Paid-up capital must be at least 10% (POC) or 20% (POV) of the grant.
- **Geography:** Singapore · **Entity types:** Singapore-incorporated private limited companies (public-sector research PIs for POC)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** Milestone-based payments; Enterprise Singapore's share-subscription right; one project at a time; core R&D in Singapore.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** With a Singapore entity, a POC grant could fund the proprietary layer around the open protocol, such as hosted mirror-fleet orchestration and the runner tier. The scheme funds commercialisation of proprietary, scalable technology.

**How to apply:**

1. Download and fill in the Startup SG Tech Registration of Interest form.
2. Submit it via go.gov.sg/askenterprisesg.
3. Discuss scope with an Enterprise Singapore officer.
4. Submit the formal Stage 2 application (POC or POV).
5. Milestone-based grant with an equity subscription right.

**What the application asks for:**

- Startup SG Tech Registration of Interest form
- Formal Stage 2 application (contents unconfirmed)
- Evidence of paid-up capital (at least 10% of a POC grant or 20% of a POV grant)
- Explanation of the science/technology and proprietary IP or intellectual assets owned by the startup
- For POV: proof of interest from a potential customer or third-party investor and evidence of business competencies

**Risks:** Needs 30% local shareholding and R&D in Singapore. The equity component dilutes ownership. An MIT-licensed core may not count as proprietary IP. IMDA alternatives were not checked.

**Sources:** <https://www.startupsg.gov.sg/programmes/4897/startup-sg-tech>, <https://www.meti.gov.sg/newsroom/written-reply-to-pq-on-data-on-startup-sg-tech-proof-of-concept-and-proof-of-value-grant-awards--and-breakdown-by-industry-and-research-institution-origins-in-last-10-years/>

## 83. Innovate UK: Frontier AI: SME Champions, Phase 1

reserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Innovate%20UK%3A%20Frontier%20AI%3A%20SME%20Champions%2C%20Phase%201%0AAward%3A%20Total%20eligible%20project%20costs%20GBP%20150%2C000%20-%20250%2C000%3B%20grant%20up%20to%2070%25%20%28micro%2Fsmall%29%2C%2060%25%20%28medium%29%3B%20competition%20pot%20up%20to%20GBP%203.1%20million%3B%20Phase%202%20%28invite%20only%29%20up%20to%20GBP%201%20million%0ADeadline%3A%202026-11-11%20%28open%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20UK-registered%20micro%2C%20small%20or%20medium%20enterprises%20applying%20alone%3B%20no%20subcontractors%20and%20one%20application%20per%20SME.%20Work%20must%20be%20done%20and%20exploited%20in%20the%20UK.%20The%20project%20must%20start%20at%20TRL%201-3%2C%20with%20AI%2FML%20as%20the%20core%20technical%20contribution%3B%20routine%20integration%20of%20existing%20tools%20or%20third-party%20models%20is%20excluded.%20It%20must%20fit%20one%20theme%20and%20one%20priority%2C%20e.g.%2C%20Theme%204%20AI%20safety%20and%20assurance%2C%20%27agentic%20runtime%20control%20and%20action%20assurance%27.%20Geography%3A%20UK%20only.%20Entity%20types%3A%20UK-registered%20SMEs%20%28for-profit%29%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fapply-for-innovation-funding.service.gov.uk%2Fcompetition%2F2584%2Foverview%2F4c7afb5d-4572-4ef2-9507-73ef2dec8ec8%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fapply-for-innovation-funding.service.gov.uk%2Fcompetition%2F2584%2Foverview%2F4c7afb5d-4572-4ef2-9507-73ef2dec8ec8%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Confirm%20the%20fit%20to%20Theme%204%20and%20one%20priority.%202.%20Fill%20in%20the%20Innovation%20Funding%20Service%20application%20%28questions%201-17%2C%20scope%2C%20summary%29.%203.%20Upload%20appendices.%204.%20Submit%20by%2011%20November%202026%2C%2011%3A00%20UK%20time.%205.%20Assessor%20scoring%3B%20about%20a%202%25%20success%20chance.%206.%20Decision%20on%2026%20January%202027%3B%20project%20setup%20and%20Grant%20Offer%20Letter%3B%20project%20runs%201-3%20months%20starting%20by%201%20April%202027.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Project%20summary%20%28400%20words%29%20and%20public%20description%20%28400%20words%29%0A-%20Scope%20%28800%20words%29%3A%20market%2C%20IP%20and%20defensibility%2C%20technical%20novelty%20and%20benchmarked%20validation%2C%20new-to-world%20capability%2C%20one%20key%20uncertainty%0A-%20Q1-Q7%20unscored%3A%20themes%2C%20applicant%20location%2C%20animal%20testing%2C%20permits%20and%20licences%2C%20international%20collaboration%2C%20export%20licence%2C%20Trusted%20Research%20and%20Innovation%0A-%20Q8%20IP%20position%2C%20freedom%20to%20operate%20and%20defensibility%20%28400%20words%29%0A-%20Q9%20Need%2C%20technical%20development%2C%20validation%20and%20outputs%20%28800%20words%29%20plus%20appendix%20of%20max%202%20A4%20pages%20with%20architecture%20diagram%20and%20validation%2Fbenchmark%20matrix%0A-%20Q10%20Team%20and%20resources%20%28600%20words%29%20plus%20one-page%20CV%20per%20known%20team%20member%0A-%20Q11%20Market%20awareness%2C%20Q12%20Outcomes%20and%20route%20to%20market%2C%20Q13%20Wider%20impacts%20%28400%20words%20each%29%0A-%20Q14%20Project%20management%20%28400%20words%29%20plus%20Gantt%20chart%20appendix%20of%20max%202%20A4%20pages%0A-%20Q15%20Risks%20%28400%20words%29%20plus%20risk%20register%20appendix%20of%20max%202%20A4%20pages%0A-%20Q16%20Added%20value%20and%20Q17%20Costs%20and%20value%20for%20money%20%28400%20words%20each%29%0A-%20Project%20costs%20within%20GBP%20150%2C000-250%2C000%20total%20eligible%20costs%3B%20no%20URLs%20allowed%20in%20answers%0ADecision%20time%3A%20Decision%20on%2026%20January%202027%20%28about%2011%20weeks%20after%20close%29%0AObligations%3A%20Technical%20whitepaper%20at%20the%20end%20%28progress%2C%20validation%2C%20IP%20defensibility%2C%20Phase%202%20readiness%29.%20Subsidy-control%20compliance%20%28Streamlined%20Subsidy%20Scheme%20SC10780%29.%20Project%20setup%20checks%3B%20no%20work%20before%20the%20Grant%20Offer%20Letter.%20Project%20must%20end%20by%2030%20June%202027.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Under%20Theme%204%20%27agentic%20runtime%20control%20and%20action%20assurance%27%2C%20propose%20new%20ML%20methods%20that%20constrain%20and%20verify%20the%20actions%20of%20autonomous%20coding%20agents%20running%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%2C%20e.g.%2C%20learned%20gating%20of%20agent%20commits%20and%20PRs%20with%20signed%20audit%20trails.%20This%20only%20fits%20if%20ForkMesh%20builds%20genuinely%20new%20AI%2FML%20rather%20than%20integrating%20Codex%20or%20Claude%20Code.%0ARisks%3A%20Replaces%20Smart%20grants%2C%20paused%20since%20January%202025%20with%20no%20any-sector%20successor%20grant.%20Of%2030%20open%20Innovation%20Funding%20Service%20competitions%2C%20none%20is%20about%20open-source%20or%20software%20infrastructure.%20About%20a%202%25%20success%20rate.%20AI%2FML%20must%20be%20the%20core%20novelty.%20UK%20SME%20only.%20Projects%20are%20very%20short%20%281-3%20months%29.%20The%20UK-Germany%20Bila%E2%80%A6)** · [Program page](https://apply-for-innovation-funding.service.gov.uk/competition/2584/overview/4c7afb5d-4572-4ef2-9507-73ef2dec8ec8) · [Apply](https://apply-for-innovation-funding.service.gov.uk/competition/2584/overview/4c7afb5d-4572-4ef2-9507-73ef2dec8ec8)

- **Funder:** Innovate UK (UK Research and Innovation)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** Total eligible project costs GBP 150,000 - 250,000; grant up to 70% (micro/small), 60% (medium); competition pot up to GBP 3.1 million; Phase 2 (invite only) up to GBP 1 million (about $140,000 - $233,000)
- **Deadline:** 2026-11-11 (open)
- **Fit score:** 22/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** UK-registered micro, small or medium enterprises applying alone; no subcontractors and one application per SME. Work must be done and exploited in the UK. The project must start at TRL 1-3, with AI/ML as the core technical contribution; routine integration of existing tools or third-party models is excluded. It must fit one theme and one priority, e.g., Theme 4 AI safety and assurance, 'agentic runtime control and action assurance'.
- **Geography:** UK only · **Entity types:** UK-registered SMEs (for-profit) only
- **Decision time:** Decision on 26 January 2027 (about 11 weeks after close)
- **Obligations:** Technical whitepaper at the end (progress, validation, IP defensibility, Phase 2 readiness). Subsidy-control compliance (Streamlined Subsidy Scheme SC10780). Project setup checks; no work before the Grant Offer Letter. Project must end by 30 June 2027.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Under Theme 4 'agentic runtime control and action assurance', propose new ML methods that constrain and verify the actions of autonomous coding agents running in isolated worktrees, e.g., learned gating of agent commits and PRs with signed audit trails. This only fits if ForkMesh builds genuinely new AI/ML rather than integrating Codex or Claude Code.

**How to apply:**

1. Confirm the fit to Theme 4 and one priority.
2. Fill in the Innovation Funding Service application (questions 1-17, scope, summary).
3. Upload appendices.
4. Submit by 11 November 2026, 11:00 UK time.
5. Assessor scoring; about a 2% success chance.
6. Decision on 26 January 2027; project setup and Grant Offer Letter; project runs 1-3 months starting by 1 April 2027.

**What the application asks for:**

- Project summary (400 words) and public description (400 words)
- Scope (800 words): market, IP and defensibility, technical novelty and benchmarked validation, new-to-world capability, one key uncertainty
- Q1-Q7 unscored: themes, applicant location, animal testing, permits and licences, international collaboration, export licence, Trusted Research and Innovation
- Q8 IP position, freedom to operate and defensibility (400 words)
- Q9 Need, technical development, validation and outputs (800 words) plus appendix of max 2 A4 pages with architecture diagram and validation/benchmark matrix
- Q10 Team and resources (600 words) plus one-page CV per known team member
- Q11 Market awareness, Q12 Outcomes and route to market, Q13 Wider impacts (400 words each)
- Q14 Project management (400 words) plus Gantt chart appendix of max 2 A4 pages
- Q15 Risks (400 words) plus risk register appendix of max 2 A4 pages
- Q16 Added value and Q17 Costs and value for money (400 words each)
- Project costs within GBP 150,000-250,000 total eligible costs; no URLs allowed in answers

**Risks:** Replaces Smart grants, paused since January 2025 with no any-sector successor grant. Of 30 open Innovation Funding Service competitions, none is about open-source or software infrastructure. About a 2% success rate. AI/ML must be the core novelty. UK SME only. Projects are very short (1-3 months). The UK-Germany Bilateral CR&D Round 5 (any sector, closes 1 Dec 2026) is an alternative if there is a German SME partner.

**Sources:** <https://apply-for-innovation-funding.service.gov.uk/competition/2584/overview/4c7afb5d-4572-4ef2-9507-73ef2dec8ec8>, <https://iuk-business-connect.org.uk/opportunities/frontier-ai-sme-champions-phase-1/>, <https://apply-for-innovation-funding.service.gov.uk/competition/search>, <https://echogrant.com/resources/innovate-uk-smart-grants-alternatives>

## 84. Opendata.ch: Prototype Fund Switzerland

20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Opendata.ch%3A%20Prototype%20Fund%20Switzerland%0AAward%3A%20Up%20to%20CHF%2050%2C000%20per%20team%0ADeadline%3A%202026%20round%20closed%202026-09-06%3B%20next%20call%20expected%20around%20July-September%202027%20%28unconfirmed%3A%202027%20dates%20not%20yet%20announced%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Individuals%20and%20small%20teams%20over%2018%20who%20hold%20a%20valid%20Swiss%20work%20permit%20%28Swiss%20nationality%20not%20required%29.%20Funding%20is%20paid%20to%20a%20legal%20entity%20with%20its%20own%20bank%20account%20after%20approval%20%28association%2C%20sole%20proprietorship%20or%20LLC%29%3B%20public%20institutions%20and%20universities%20cannot%20apply%20directly%20but%20their%20staff%20may%20join%20a%20team.%20Geography%3A%20Switzerland%20%28team%20members%20must%20be%20eligible%20to%20work%20in%20Switzerland%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%2Fsmall%20teams%20paid%20via%20an%20association%2C%20sole%20proprietorship%20or%20LLC%3B%20no%20public%20institutions%20or%20universities.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fprototypefund.opendata.ch%2Fen%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fprototypefund.opendata.ch%2Fen%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Watch%20for%20the%20annual%20call%20announcement%20%282026%20window%20ran%2028%20July%20-%206%20September%29.%202.%20Submit%20the%20idea%20through%20the%20online%20application%20portal%20linked%20from%20the%20program%20site.%203.%20Jury%20evaluates%20against%20the%20year%27s%20criteria%20%282026%3A%20responsible%20and%20sustainable%20AI%2C%20public-interest%20impact%29.%204.%20Selected%20teams%20are%20notified%20on%20a%20rolling%20basis%20in%20August-September.%205.%20Four-month%20prototyping%20phase%20%28mid-October%20to%20mid-February%29%20with%20kick-off%20workshop%20and%20cohort%20events.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20portal%20application%20describing%20an%20early-stage%20but%20well-thought-out%20concept%0A-%20Clear%20statement%20of%20what%20will%20be%20built%20during%20the%20four-month%20prototyping%20period%20%28and%20novelty%20if%20reusing%20an%20existing%20project%29%0A-%20Team%20description%20showing%20members%20hold%20Swiss%20work%20permits%0A-%20Legal%20entity%20with%20bank%20account%20for%20payment%20after%20approval%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20form%20fields%2C%20word%20limits%20and%20budget%20format%20are%20only%20visible%20in%20the%20portal%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%201-2%20months%20%28jury%20selection%20August-September%2C%20rolling%29%0AObligations%3A%202-3%20team%20members%20must%20attend%20each%20prototyping-phase%20event%20including%20kick-off%3B%20share%20learnings%20for%20programme%20reporting%20and%20interim%20insights%3B%20prototype%20must%20include%20open-source%20elements%20in%20full%20or%20in%20part%3B%20travel%20costs%20covered%20from%20the%20grant.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20four-month%20prototype%20of%20ForkMesh%27s%20AI-agent%20worktree%20workflow%20with%20signed%2C%20auditable%20provenance%20for%20agent-authored%20pull%20requests%2C%20framed%20as%20transparent%20and%20accountable%20AI%20in%20software%20development%2C%20the%202026%20theme.%20Only%20viable%20if%20a%20team%20member%20holds%20a%20Swiss%20work%20permit.%0ARisks%3A%20Requires%20Swiss%20work%20permit%20for%20team%20members%2C%20which%20ForkMesh%20likely%20lacks.%202026%20round%20is%20closed%20and%20the%20annual%20theme%20changes%20%282026%3A%20responsible%20and%20sustainable%20AI%29%2C%20so%20the%202027%20theme%20may%20not%20fit.%20Supports%20new%20prototypes%2C%20not%20general%20development%20of%20an%20existing%20product.)** · [Program page](https://prototypefund.opendata.ch/en) · [Apply](https://prototypefund.opendata.ch/en)

- **Funder:** Opendata.ch (funded by Mercator Foundation Switzerland)
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** Up to CHF 50,000 per team (about $62,000)
- **Deadline:** 2026 round closed 2026-09-06; next call expected around July-September 2027 (unconfirmed: 2027 dates not yet announced) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 22/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Individuals and small teams over 18 who hold a valid Swiss work permit (Swiss nationality not required). Funding is paid to a legal entity with its own bank account after approval (association, sole proprietorship or LLC); public institutions and universities cannot apply directly but their staff may join a team.
- **Geography:** Switzerland (team members must be eligible to work in Switzerland) · **Entity types:** Individuals/small teams paid via an association, sole proprietorship or LLC; no public institutions or universities
- **Decision time:** About 1-2 months (jury selection August-September, rolling)
- **Obligations:** 2-3 team members must attend each prototyping-phase event including kick-off; share learnings for programme reporting and interim insights; prototype must include open-source elements in full or in part; travel costs covered from the grant.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a four-month prototype of ForkMesh's AI-agent worktree workflow with signed, auditable provenance for agent-authored pull requests, framed as transparent and accountable AI in software development, the 2026 theme. Only viable if a team member holds a Swiss work permit.

**How to apply:**

1. Watch for the annual call announcement (2026 window ran 28 July - 6 September).
2. Submit the idea through the online application portal linked from the program site.
3. Jury evaluates against the year's criteria (2026: responsible and sustainable AI, public-interest impact).
4. Selected teams are notified on a rolling basis in August-September.
5. Four-month prototyping phase (mid-October to mid-February) with kick-off workshop and cohort events.

**What the application asks for:**

- Online portal application describing an early-stage but well-thought-out concept
- Clear statement of what will be built during the four-month prototyping period (and novelty if reusing an existing project)
- Team description showing members hold Swiss work permits
- Legal entity with bank account for payment after approval
- unconfirmed: exact form fields, word limits and budget format are only visible in the portal

**Risks:** Requires Swiss work permit for team members, which ForkMesh likely lacks. 2026 round is closed and the annual theme changes (2026: responsible and sustainable AI), so the 2027 theme may not fit. Supports new prototypes, not general development of an existing product.

**Sources:** <https://prototypefund.opendata.ch/en>, <https://prototypefund.opendata.ch/en/application/faq-2/>, <https://opendata.ch/news/apply-to-prototype-your-public-interest-tech-idea/>

20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20GitHub%20for%20Startups%0AAward%3A%20USD%2010%2C000%20in%20flexible%20platform%20credits%20for%20GitHub%20Enterprise%20licences%20and%20eligible%20add-ons%20%28Copilot%2C%20Advanced%20Security%2C%20other%20metered%20services%29%2C%2050k%20included%20Actions%20minutes%20on%20Enterprise%2C%20onboarding%20and%20office%20hours%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Startups%20affiliated%20with%20an%20approved%20GitHub%20for%20Startups%20partner%20%28investor%2C%20incubator%20or%20accelerator%20referral%29%2C%20with%20outside%20equity%20funding%20of%20Series%20B%20or%20earlier%20%28non-equity%20credits%20do%20not%20count%29%2C%20new%20to%20GitHub%20Enterprise%20%28not%20on%20an%20Enterprise%20plan%20in%20the%20past%20six%20months%20and%20never%20received%20GitHub%20credits%20or%20licences%29.%20Enterprise%20Cloud%20on%20GitHub.com%20only.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20geographic%20restriction%20stated.%20Entity%20types%3A%20For-profit%2C%20equity-funded%20startups.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fenterprise%2Fstartups%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fenterprise%2Fstartups%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Obtain%20a%20referral%20from%20%28or%20be%20affiliated%20with%29%20an%20approved%20GitHub%20for%20Startups%20partner%2C%20or%20use%20the%20partner%20application%20route%20on%20the%20page.%202.%20Submit%20the%20application.%203.%20Response%20to%20the%20applicant%27s%20primary%20email%20within%202-3%20business%20days.%204.%20Onboarding%20to%20GitHub%20Enterprise%20Cloud%20with%20a%20valid%20payment%20method%3B%20authorization%20charge%20within%20a%20few%20weeks.%205.%20Credits%20applied%20for%20up%20to%2012%20months.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Partner%20affiliation%20%2F%20referral%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20outside%20equity%20funding%20%28Series%20B%20or%20earlier%29%0A-%20GitHub%20organization%20%2F%20Enterprise%20Cloud%20account%0A-%20Valid%20payment%20method%20on%20file%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20form%20fields%20not%20shown%20on%20the%20page%0ADecision%20time%3A%202-3%20business%20days%0AObligations%3A%20No%20equity.%20Credits%20valid%20up%20to%2012%20months%2C%20non-transferable%2C%20redeemable%20once%3B%20auto-billing%20to%20the%20payment%20method%20when%20credits%20run%20out%3B%20downgrading%20early%20forfeits%20credits%20and%20future%20eligibility.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Use%20Enterprise%20credits%20for%20Copilot%2C%20Advanced%20Security%20%28code%20scanning%2C%20secret%20scanning%29%20and%20Actions%20minutes%20on%20ForkMesh%27s%20GitHub-hosted%20mirror%20of%20its%20own%20source%2C%20and%20to%20build%20and%20test%20the%20GitHub%20import%2Fmirroring%20bridge%20that%20lets%20GitHub%20repositories%20be%20mirrored%20onto%20the%20ForkMesh%20fleet.%20GitHub%20benefits%20from%20tooling%20that%20interoperates%20with%20its%20platform%3B%20ForkMesh%20benefits%20from%20security%20scanning%20on%20supply-chain-sensitive%20code.%0ARisks%3A%20Requires%20partner%20affiliation%20and%20outside%20equity%20funding%2C%20which%20ForkMesh%20may%20lack.%20Credits%20only%20cover%20GitHub%20Enterprise%20products%3B%20ForkMesh%20positions%20itself%20as%20reducing%20dependence%20on%20a%20single%20hosting%20provider%20such%20as%20GitHub%2C%20so%20the%20strategic%20fit%20and%20messaging%20are%20weak.%20Payment%20method%20and%20auto-billing%20after%20credits%20expire.)** · [Program page](https://github.com/enterprise/startups) · [Apply](https://github.com/enterprise/startups)

- **Funder:** GitHub (Microsoft)
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** USD 10,000 in flexible platform credits for GitHub Enterprise licences and eligible add-ons (Copilot, Advanced Security, other metered services), 50k included Actions minutes on Enterprise, onboarding and office hours (about $10,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 22/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Startups affiliated with an approved GitHub for Startups partner (investor, incubator or accelerator referral), with outside equity funding of Series B or earlier (non-equity credits do not count), new to GitHub Enterprise (not on an Enterprise plan in the past six months and never received GitHub credits or licences). Enterprise Cloud on GitHub.com only.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: no geographic restriction stated · **Entity types:** For-profit, equity-funded startups
- **Decision time:** 2-3 business days
- **Obligations:** No equity. Credits valid up to 12 months, non-transferable, redeemable once; auto-billing to the payment method when credits run out; downgrading early forfeits credits and future eligibility.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Use Enterprise credits for Copilot, Advanced Security (code scanning, secret scanning) and Actions minutes on ForkMesh's GitHub-hosted mirror of its own source, and to build and test the GitHub import/mirroring bridge that lets GitHub repositories be mirrored onto the ForkMesh fleet. GitHub benefits from tooling that interoperates with its platform; ForkMesh benefits from security scanning on supply-chain-sensitive code.

**How to apply:**

1. Obtain a referral from (or be affiliated with) an approved GitHub for Startups partner, or use the partner application route on the page.
2. Submit the application.
3. Response to the applicant's primary email within 2-3 business days.
4. Onboarding to GitHub Enterprise Cloud with a valid payment method; authorization charge within a few weeks.
5. Credits applied for up to 12 months.

**What the application asks for:**

- Partner affiliation / referral
- Evidence of outside equity funding (Series B or earlier)
- GitHub organization / Enterprise Cloud account
- Valid payment method on file
- unconfirmed: exact form fields not shown on the page

**Risks:** Requires partner affiliation and outside equity funding, which ForkMesh may lack. Credits only cover GitHub Enterprise products; ForkMesh positions itself as reducing dependence on a single hosting provider such as GitHub, so the strategic fit and messaging are weak. Payment method and auto-billing after credits expire.

**Sources:** <https://github.com/enterprise/startups>

3A%20Worldwide%2C%20subject%20to%20KYC%20and%20legal-jurisdiction%20checks%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20excluded%20countries%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20teams%20and%20companies%3B%20KYC%20and%20legal%20jurisdiction%20collected%20from%20selected%20grantees.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforum.celo.org%2Ft%2Fprezenti-season-3-is-open-frontier-anchor-and-boost-grants-for-celo%2F13714%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Ffrontier.prezenti.xyz%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Deploy%20a%20working%20integration%20on%20Celo%20mainnet%20and%20create%20a%20KarmaGAP%20project%20profile.%202.%20Apply%20through%20frontier.prezenti.xyz%20%28Frontier%29%20or%20anchor.prezenti.xyz%20%28Anchor%29%3B%20Boost%20needs%20a%20referral%20from%20Prezenti%2C%20Celo%20core%20contributors%20or%20Celo%20DevRel.%203.%20Prezenti%20reviews%20applications%20%28applications%20moved%20to%20Tally%20forms%20in%20May%202026%29.%204.%20Selected%20grantees%20accept%20grant%20policies%2C%20complete%20KYC%20and%20provide%20payment%20details.%205.%20Deliver%20milestones%3B%20payment%20against%20verified%20milestones.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Current%20traction%20and%20on-chain%20transaction%20evidence%20with%20contracts%20and%20addresses%20used%0A-%20Credible%20architecture%20description%0A-%20Plan%20for%20first%2010-100%20users%20or%20agents%20%2F%20distribution%20plan%0A-%20ERC-8004%20registration%20for%20agent%20projects%3B%20Self%20Protocol%20Agent%20ID%20use%20or%20integration%0A-%20Revenue%20projections%20and%20assumptions%0A-%20Previous%20Celo%20ecosystem%20funding%20and%20outcomes%0A-%20Team%20experience%0A-%20KarmaGAP%20profile%0A-%20Working%20demo%20%28strongly%20encouraged%29%0A-%20If%20selected%3A%20grant%20policy%20agreement%2C%20KYC%2C%20legal%20jurisdiction%2C%20lead%20contact%20and%20private%20delivery%20email%2C%20Celo%20L2%20payment%20address%2C%20contract%20addresses%2C%20high-resolution%20logo%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20Milestone-based%20delivery%20with%20verifiable%20proof%20%28contract%20addresses%2C%20dashboards%29%3B%20Anchor%20milestones%20include%20TVL%2C%20daily-transaction%20and%20Celo%20revenue%20targets%3B%20KYC%3B%20no%20equity%20stated.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Propose%20a%20Frontier%20grant%20for%20agent-to-agent%20payments%20in%20ForkMesh%3A%20the%20AI%20coding%20agents%20ForkMesh%20runs%20could%20hold%20Celo%20identities%20and%20be%20paid%20%28or%20pay%20human%20reviewers%29%20in%20stablecoins%20on%20Celo%20when%20their%20signed%20pull%20requests%20are%20merged%2C%20extending%20the%20contribution%20pool%20to%20Celo.%20This%20is%20a%20concrete%20developer-tooling%20use%20of%20the%20agent%20economy%20Prezenti%20wants.%0ARisks%3A%20Requires%20a%20live%20Celo%20mainnet%20deployment%20with%20on-chain%20activity%20before%20applying%2C%20which%20ForkMesh%20does%20not%20have%3B%20amounts%20unconfirmed%3B%20focus%20is%20AI%20agent%20economy%20and%20DeFi%20traction%20metrics%20rather%20than%20open-source%20infrastructure%3B%20ERC-8004%20%2F%20Self%20Protocol%20integration%20expected%3B%20the%20older%20Celo%20Public%20Goods%20rounds%20appear%20superseded.)** · [Program page](https://forum.celo.org/t/prezenti-season-3-is-open-frontier-anchor-and-boost-grants-for-celo/13714) · [Apply](https://frontier.prezenti.xyz)

- **Funder:** Prezenti (Celo ecosystem grants operator, Celo Community Treasury)
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** unconfirmed: per-grant amounts and currency not stated on the Season 3 announcement (Anchor paid 20% on signing, 80% on verified milestones) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** 2026-12-29 (open)
- **Fit score:** 22/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Frontier: projects building AI and agent-economy infrastructure on Celo (agent identity, agent-to-agent payments, developer tooling, verification, interoperability) with a working Celo mainnet deployment, verifiable on-chain activity, credible architecture and a plan for the first 10-100 users or agents; agent projects need an ERC-8004 registration and Self Protocol Agent ID integration is expected. Anchor needs 5,000+ daily transactions; Boost is referral-only.
- **Geography:** Worldwide, subject to KYC and legal-jurisdiction checks (unconfirmed: excluded countries) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: teams and companies; KYC and legal jurisdiction collected from selected grantees
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** Milestone-based delivery with verifiable proof (contract addresses, dashboards); Anchor milestones include TVL, daily-transaction and Celo revenue targets; KYC; no equity stated.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Propose a Frontier grant for agent-to-agent payments in ForkMesh: the AI coding agents ForkMesh runs could hold Celo identities and be paid (or pay human reviewers) in stablecoins on Celo when their signed pull requests are merged, extending the contribution pool to Celo. This is a concrete developer-tooling use of the agent economy Prezenti wants.

**How to apply:**

1. Deploy a working integration on Celo mainnet and create a KarmaGAP project profile.
2. Apply through frontier.prezenti.xyz (Frontier) or anchor.prezenti.xyz (Anchor); Boost needs a referral from Prezenti, Celo core contributors or Celo DevRel.
3. Prezenti reviews applications (applications moved to Tally forms in May 2026).
4. Selected grantees accept grant policies, complete KYC and provide payment details.
5. Deliver milestones; payment against verified milestones.

**What the application asks for:**

- Current traction and on-chain transaction evidence with contracts and addresses used
- Credible architecture description
- Plan for first 10-100 users or agents / distribution plan
- ERC-8004 registration for agent projects; Self Protocol Agent ID use or integration
- Revenue projections and assumptions
- Previous Celo ecosystem funding and outcomes
- Team experience
- KarmaGAP profile
- Working demo (strongly encouraged)
- If selected: grant policy agreement, KYC, legal jurisdiction, lead contact and private delivery email, Celo L2 payment address, contract addresses, high-resolution logo

**Risks:** Requires a live Celo mainnet deployment with on-chain activity before applying, which ForkMesh does not have; amounts unconfirmed; focus is AI agent economy and DeFi traction metrics rather than open-source infrastructure; ERC-8004 / Self Protocol integration expected; the older Celo Public Goods rounds appear superseded.

**Sources:** <https://forum.celo.org/t/prezenti-season-3-is-open-frontier-anchor-and-boost-grants-for-celo/13714>, <https://forum.celo.org/c/ecosystem/grants/27>, <https://celopg.notion.site/Celo-Public-Goods-Funding-772d4f3dcc3e45cb977cb2b8fc5e6392>

## 87. APNIC Foundation: ISIF Asia grants

sif-asia%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Submit%20a%20concept%20note%20during%20the%20call%20window%20%28Mar-Apr%29.%202.%20Review%20May-June.%203.%20Selected%20applicants%20invited%20in%20June%20to%20submit%20a%20full%20application%20%282026%20deadline%206%20July%29.%204.%20Announcement%20in%20October.%205.%20Projects%20start%20in%20Q4.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Concept%20note%3A%20Introduction%20%28short%20summary%29%0A-%20Alignment%20with%20APNIC%20Foundation%20and%20ISIF%20Asia%20aims%0A-%20Challenge%0A-%20Approach%20%28planned%20activities%29%0A-%20Impact%0A-%20Monitoring%20and%20Evaluation%0A-%20Organisational%20Capability%0A-%20Estimated%20budget%20in%20USD%0A-%20If%20invited%3A%20full%20application%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%206-7%20months%20from%20concept%20note%20to%20announcement%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20grant%20agreement%20with%20reporting%20and%20monitoring%2Fevaluation%3B%20not%20detailed%20on%20the%20page.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Pitch%20ForkMesh%20mirror%20nodes%20hosted%20by%20Asia-Pacific%20universities%20or%20network%20operators%20so%20regional%20developers%20keep%20access%20to%20source%20code%20during%20disasters%2C%20cable%20cuts%20or%20blocking%20%28fits%20%27disaster%20risk%20mitigation%27%20and%20%27essential%20services%27%20priorities%29.%20Requires%20an%20APAC-registered%20lead%20partner.%0ARisks%3A%20ForkMesh%20is%20eligible%20only%20if%20its%20entity%20%28or%20a%20lead%20partner%29%20is%20registered%20in%20an%20APNIC%20economy.%20Priorities%20lean%20to%20connectivity%2C%20IPv6%2C%20health%20and%20climate%3B%20developer%20tooling%20is%20a%20stretch.)** · [Program page](https://apnic.foundation/projects/isif-asia/) · [Apply](https://apnic.foundation/projects/isif-asia/)

- **Funder:** APNIC Foundation
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** Three tiers: up to USD 20,000, up to USD 50,000, or up to USD 75,000 (about $20,000 - $75,000)
- **Deadline:** Next concept-note round opens in 2027 (2026 call ran 23 Mar - 24 Apr 2026; unconfirmed: 2027 dates) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 20/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Initiatives conducted in the Asia Pacific and led by organisations legally registered in one of the 56 economies of the APNIC Foundation service region. Any kind of organisation (government, private company, NGO) may apply; individuals are not eligible.
- **Geography:** Asia Pacific (APNIC service region, 56 economies) · **Entity types:** For-profit companies, nonprofits/NGOs, government bodies; not individuals
- **Decision time:** About 6-7 months from concept note to announcement
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: grant agreement with reporting and monitoring/evaluation; not detailed on the page.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Pitch ForkMesh mirror nodes hosted by Asia-Pacific universities or network operators so regional developers keep access to source code during disasters, cable cuts or blocking (fits 'disaster risk mitigation' and 'essential services' priorities). Requires an APAC-registered lead partner.

**How to apply:**

1. Submit a concept note during the call window (Mar-Apr).
2. Review May-June.
3. Selected applicants invited in June to submit a full application (2026 deadline 6 July).
4. Announcement in October.
5. Projects start in Q4.

**What the application asks for:**

- Concept note: Introduction (short summary)
- Alignment with APNIC Foundation and ISIF Asia aims
- Challenge
- Approach (planned activities)
- Impact
- Monitoring and Evaluation
- Organisational Capability
- Estimated budget in USD
- If invited: full application

**Risks:** ForkMesh is eligible only if its entity (or a lead partner) is registered in an APNIC economy. Priorities lean to connectivity, IPv6, health and climate; developer tooling is a stretch.

**Sources:** <https://apnic.foundation/projects/isif-asia/>, <https://apnic.foundation/isif-asia-2026-open-for-concept-notes/>

## 88. Outreachy internships

**[Draft this application in ChatGPT](https://chatgpt.com/?q=You%20are%20helping%20the%20ForkMesh%20team%20write%20a%20complete%20draft%20of%20our%20application%20to%20Outreachy%20internships.%0A%0AHow%20to%20write%20it%3A%0A-%20First%20open%20the%20program%20page%20and%20the%20application%20form%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fwww.outreachy.org%2Fdocs%2Fcommunity%2F%20and%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.outreachy.org%2Fcommunities%2Fcfp%2F%29%20and%20use%20their%20current%20questions%2C%20limits%2C%20deadline%20and%20criteria%3B%20say%20so%20if%20anything%20below%20is%20out%20of%20date.%0A-%20Answer%20each%20question%20under%20its%20own%20heading%2C%20in%20their%20order%2C%20with%20its%20word%20or%20character%20count%20after%20it.%0A-%20Propose%20one%20concrete%2C%20fundable%20piece%20of%20ForkMesh%20work%20that%20fits%20what%20they%20fund%2C%20with%20milestones%2C%20dates%2C%20deliverables%20and%20a%20budget%20in%20their%20currency%20inside%20their%20range.%0A-%20Plain%2C%20specific%20words%2C%20no%20hype%2C%20no%20emoji.%20Use%20only%20facts%20given%20here%20or%20found%20on%20the%20linked%20pages%3B%20never%20invent%20traction%2C%20users%2C%20partners%2C%20numbers%20or%20names.%0A-%20Where%20the%20form%20needs%20something%20only%20we%20can%20supply%20%28legal%20entity%2C%20registration%20numbers%2C%20bank%20details%2C%20CVs%2C%20letters%20of%20support%29%2C%20never%20invent%20it%3A%20list%20it%20at%20the%20end.%0A-%20Reply%20with%20the%20finished%20application%2C%20ready%20to%20paste%2C%20then%20a%20checklist%20of%20every%20document%20and%20fact%20we%20still%20have%20to%20supply%2C%20then%20three%20ways%20to%20make%20it%20stronger.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20us%3A%20ForkMesh%0AForkMesh%20%28https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%29%20is%20MIT-licensed%20open-source%20software%20that%20lets%20developers%20collaborate%20on%2C%20mirror%20and%20preserve%20Git%20repositories%20across%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Outreachy%20internships%0AAward%3A%20Intern%20stipend%20USD%207%2C000%20for%203%20months%3B%20communities%20donate%20at%20least%20USD%208%2C000%20per%20intern%20%28USD%207%2C000%20stipend%20%2B%20USD%201%2C000-3%2C000%20activities%29.%20General%20fund%20currently%20not%20able%20to%20fund%20internships.%0ADeadline%3A%20May%202027%20cohort%3A%20community%20call%20expected%20to%20open%20early%20January%202027%20%28unconfirmed%20exact%20date%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open%20source%2C%20open%20science%20or%20nonprofit%20communities%20doing%20public%20work%20under%20OSI%2FFSF-approved%20or%20free-culture%20licences%2C%20advancing%20free%20software%2C%20not%20tightly%20controlled%20by%20a%20single%20company%2C%20with%20public%20channels%20and%20at%20least%20one%20experienced%20mentor.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20FOSS%20communities%20%28sponsoring%20company%20or%20foundation%20pays%20per%20intern%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.outreachy.org%2Fdocs%2Fcommunity%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.outreachy.org%2Fcommunities%2Fcfp%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Community%20coordinator%20submits%20participation%20application%20on%20the%20CFP%20page.%202.%20Organizers%2FProject%20Leadership%20Committee%20approve.%203.%20Mentors%20submit%20project%20descriptions%3B%20coordinator%20approves%20projects%20and%20co-mentors.%204.%20Applicants%20make%20contributions%20during%20the%20contribution%20period.%205.%20Mentors%20select%20interns%3B%203-month%20internship.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Community%20participation%20application%0A-%20Funding%20commitment%20%28USD%208%2C000%20per%20intern%29%20or%20sponsor%0A-%20Project%20descriptions%20per%20mentor%0A-%20Mentor%20and%20co-mentor%20sign-ups%0A-%20Contribution%20tasks%20for%20applicants%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%202-3%20months%20from%20community%20sign-up%20to%20intern%20announcement%0AObligations%3A%20Pay%20the%20per-intern%20sponsorship%3B%20mentors%20guide%20interns%20for%203%20months%20and%20complete%20evaluations.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Low%20priority%3A%20only%20if%20a%20sponsor%20covers%20the%20USD%208%2C000%2C%20offer%20internships%20on%20documentation%2C%20accessibility%20of%20the%20desktop%20app%20and%20mobile%20clients.%0ARisks%3A%20Costs%20ForkMesh%20money%20rather%20than%20providing%20it%3B%20%27not%20tightly%20tied%20to%20one%20company%27%20rule%20may%20be%20an%20issue%20for%20a%20startup-led%20project.)** · [Program page](https://www.outreachy.org/docs/community/) · [Apply](https://www.outreachy.org/communities/cfp/)

- **Funder:** Outreachy (Software Freedom Conservancy)
- **Category:** AI and developer tools
- **Amount:** Intern stipend USD 7,000 for 3 months; communities donate at least USD 8,000 per intern (USD 7,000 stipend + USD 1,000-3,000 activities). General fund currently not able to fund internships. (about $7,000)
- **Deadline:** May 2027 cohort: community call expected to open early January 2027 (unconfirmed exact date) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 20/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open source, open science or nonprofit communities doing public work under OSI/FSF-approved or free-culture licences, advancing free software, not tightly controlled by a single company, with public channels and at least one experienced mentor.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** FOSS communities (sponsoring company or foundation pays per intern)
- **Decision time:** About 2-3 months from community sign-up to intern announcement
- **Obligations:** Pay the per-intern sponsorship; mentors guide interns for 3 months and complete evaluations.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Low priority: only if a sponsor covers the USD 8,000, offer internships on documentation, accessibility of the desktop app and mobile clients.

**How to apply:**

1. Community coordinator submits participation application on the CFP page.
2. Organizers/Project Leadership Committee approve.
3. Mentors submit project descriptions; coordinator approves projects and co-mentors.
4. Applicants make contributions during the contribution period.
5. Mentors select interns; 3-month internship.

**What the application asks for:**

- Community participation application
- Funding commitment (USD 8,000 per intern) or sponsor
- Project descriptions per mentor
- Mentor and co-mentor sign-ups
- Contribution tasks for applicants

**Risks:** Costs ForkMesh money rather than providing it; 'not tightly tied to one company' rule may be an issue for a startup-led project.

**Sources:** <https://www.outreachy.org/docs/community/>, <https://www.outreachy.org/communities/cfp/>

0a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Craig%20Newmark%20Philanthropies%3A%20Cybersecurity%20%2F%20%23CyberCivilDefense%20grants%0AAward%3A%20No%20minimum%2C%20maximum%20or%20typical%20range%20stated%3B%20applicant%20states%20the%20amount%20requested%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Public%20charities%20%28IRS%20501%28c%29%283%29%20with%20509%28a%29%281%29%2F%28a%29%282%29%20status%29%20or%20projects%20under%20a%20fiscal%20sponsor%2C%20with%20priority%20on%20cybersecurity%20protecting%20critical%20infrastructure%20and%20communities%20%28hospitals%2C%20water%20utilities%2C%20K-12%2C%20local%20government%29%20and%20cyber%20civil%20defense.%20Geography%3A%20US%20%28requires%20IRS%20public%20charity%20status%20or%20US%20fiscal%20sponsor%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20US%20nonprofits%20%2F%20fiscally%20sponsored%20projects%20only.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcraignewmarkphilanthropies.org%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcraignewmarkphilanthropies.org%2Fapply%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Download%2C%20complete%20and%20sign%20the%20Information%20Request%20and%20Checklist%20Form%20%28PDF%29.%202.%20Fill%20in%20the%20web%20form%20%28name%2C%20email%2C%20organization%2C%20amount%20requested%2C%20referral%20source%29.%203.%20Upload%20the%20signed%20form%20and%20supporting%20documents.%204.%20Rolling%20review%3B%20CNP%20follows%20up%20only%20if%20it%20intends%20to%20fund.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Signed%20Information%20Request%20and%20Checklist%20Form%0A-%20Short%20overview%3A%20funding%20request%2C%20problem%2C%20plan%2C%20how%20the%20amount%20was%20calculated%2C%20success%20evaluation%2C%20communications%20plan%20%28incl.%20response%20to%20criticism%29%2C%20collaboration%20plan%20%28incl.%20with%20cyber%20civil%20defense%20groups%29%0A-%20IRS%20determination%20letter%20%28501%28c%29%283%29%2C%20509%28a%29%281%29%2F%28a%29%282%29%29%0A-%20IRS%20active%20status%20confirmation%0A-%20Secretary%20of%20State%20certificate%20of%20good%20standing%0A-%20Fiscal%20sponsorship%20agreement%20if%20applicable%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20rolling%3B%20no%20response%20unless%20funded%0AObligations%3A%20Multi-year%20funding%20released%20annually%20based%20on%20progress%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20reporting%20details.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Via%20a%20US%20fiscal%20sponsor%2C%20propose%20resilient%20source-code%20mirrors%20for%20open-source%20tools%20that%20local%20governments%2C%20utilities%20and%20schools%20depend%20on%2C%20so%20patches%20and%20code%20remain%20available%20during%20an%20attack%20on%20a%20hosting%20provider.%20Only%20worth%20it%20if%20paired%20with%20a%20cyber%20civil%20defense%20partner.%0ARisks%3A%20Requires%20US%20public%20charity%20status%20or%20a%20fiscal%20sponsor%3B%20focus%20on%20direct%20protection%20of%20critical%20infrastructure%20rather%20than%20developer%20tooling%3B%20no%20feedback%20unless%20funded.)** · [Program page](https://craignewmarkphilanthropies.org/) · [Apply](https://craignewmarkphilanthropies.org/apply/)

- **Funder:** Craig Newmark Philanthropies
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** No minimum, maximum or typical range stated; applicant states the amount requested (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 20/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Public charities (IRS 501(c)(3) with 509(a)(1)/(a)(2) status) or projects under a fiscal sponsor, with priority on cybersecurity protecting critical infrastructure and communities (hospitals, water utilities, K-12, local government) and cyber civil defense.
- **Geography:** US (requires IRS public charity status or US fiscal sponsor) · **Entity types:** US nonprofits / fiscally sponsored projects only
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: rolling; no response unless funded
- **Obligations:** Multi-year funding released annually based on progress; unconfirmed: reporting details.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Via a US fiscal sponsor, propose resilient source-code mirrors for open-source tools that local governments, utilities and schools depend on, so patches and code remain available during an attack on a hosting provider. Only worth it if paired with a cyber civil defense partner.

**How to apply:**

1. Download, complete and sign the Information Request and Checklist Form (PDF).
2. Fill in the web form (name, email, organization, amount requested, referral source).
3. Upload the signed form and supporting documents.
4. Rolling review; CNP follows up only if it intends to fund.

**What the application asks for:**

- Signed Information Request and Checklist Form
- Short overview: funding request, problem, plan, how the amount was calculated, success evaluation, communications plan (incl. response to criticism), collaboration plan (incl. with cyber civil defense groups)
- IRS determination letter (501(c)(3), 509(a)(1)/(a)(2))
- IRS active status confirmation
- Secretary of State certificate of good standing
- Fiscal sponsorship agreement if applicable

**Risks:** Requires US public charity status or a fiscal sponsor; focus on direct protection of critical infrastructure rather than developer tooling; no feedback unless funded.

**Sources:** <https://craignewmarkphilanthropies.org/apply/>, <https://craignewmarkphilanthropies.org/>

no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Mozilla%20Foundation%20Incubator%20thematic%20open%20calls%0AAward%3A%202026%20Democracy%20x%20AI%20Cohort%3A%20USD%2050%2C000%20per%20project%20over%2012%20months%20for%2010%20projects%2C%20plus%20mentorship%20and%20cohort%20support%20%28next%20call%27s%20theme%20and%20amounts%20unconfirmed%29%0ADeadline%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20next%20thematic%20call%20not%20announced%3B%202026%20call%20closed%202026-03-16%20%28a%20third-party%20listing%20mentions%20a%202027%20cohort%20opening%20in%20late%202026%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Projects%20matching%20the%20theme%20of%20each%20open%20call%3B%20the%202026%20Democracy%20x%20AI%20call%20required%20projects%20past%20the%20prototype%20stage%20with%20real%20users%2C%20building%20AI-based%20tools%20for%20democratic%20goals.%20unconfirmed%3A%20eligibility%20of%20for-profit%20startups%20and%20geography%20for%20future%20calls.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%202026%20call%20was%20international.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20nonprofits%2C%20for-profits%20and%20individuals%20have%20been%20eligible%20in%20past%20Mozilla%20open%20calls%3B%20check%20each%20call.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.mozillafoundation.org%2Fen%2Fwhat-we-do%2Fgrantmaking%2Fincubator%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.mozillafoundation.org%2Fen%2Fwhat-we-do%2Fgrantmaking%2Fincubator%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Sign%20up%20on%20the%20Incubator%20page%20to%20be%20notified%20when%20the%20next%20thematic%20call%20opens.%202.%20Submit%20the%20proposal%20during%20the%20call%20window.%203.%20Shortlisting%20and%20review.%204.%20Selection%20announced%20%282026%3A%20deadline%20Mar%2016%2C%20selections%20Jun%201%29.%205.%2012-month%20cohort%20with%20mentorship.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20online%20proposal%20form%20per%20call%20%28project%20description%2C%20theme%20fit%2C%20users%20and%20traction%20past%20prototype%20stage%2C%20team%2C%2012-month%20plan%20and%20budget%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20About%202.5%20months%20in%202026%20%28deadline%202026-03-16%2C%20selections%202026-06-01%29%0AObligations%3A%20Participation%20in%20a%2012-month%20cohort%20with%20mentorship%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20reporting%20and%20open%20licensing%20requirements%20per%20call.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Only%20worth%20applying%20if%20a%20future%20call%27s%20theme%20covers%20open%2C%20decentralised%20infrastructure%20or%20trustworthy%20AI%20agents%3B%20then%20pitch%20ForkMesh%27s%20isolated%2C%20auditable%20AI%20coding%20agents%20that%20open%20signed%20pull%20requests%20on%20independent%20hosts.%20Mozilla%20funds%20open-web%20alternatives%20to%20concentrated%20platforms%2C%20which%20matches%20ForkMesh%27s%20mission.%0ARisks%3A%20Mozilla%20Foundation%20pages%20returned%20403%2C%20so%20status%20comes%20from%20search%20results%3B%20no%20Mozilla%20call%20is%20confirmed%20open%20now.%20Mozilla%20Open%20Source%20Support%20%28MOSS%29%20is%20on%20hiatus%20and%20the%20Fellows%202026%20deadline%20%28Aug%2031%2C%202026%29%20has%20passed.%20Calls%20are%20thematic%20and%20the%202026%20theme%20%28Democracy%20x%20AI%29%20does%20not%20fit%20ForkMesh.%20Mozilla%20Ventures%20%28equity%20investment%29%20is%20the%20other%20running%20channel%20but%20is%20not%20a%20grant.)** · [Program page](https://www.mozillafoundation.org/en/what-we-do/grantmaking/incubator/) · [Apply](https://www.mozillafoundation.org/en/what-we-do/grantmaking/incubator/)

- **Funder:** Mozilla Foundation
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** 2026 Democracy x AI Cohort: USD 50,000 per project over 12 months for 10 projects, plus mentorship and cohort support (next call's theme and amounts unconfirmed) (about $50,000)
- **Deadline:** unconfirmed: next thematic call not announced; 2026 call closed 2026-03-16 (a third-party listing mentions a 2027 cohort opening in late 2026) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 20/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Projects matching the theme of each open call; the 2026 Democracy x AI call required projects past the prototype stage with real users, building AI-based tools for democratic goals. unconfirmed: eligibility of for-profit startups and geography for future calls.
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: 2026 call was international · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: nonprofits, for-profits and individuals have been eligible in past Mozilla open calls; check each call
- **Decision time:** About 2.5 months in 2026 (deadline 2026-03-16, selections 2026-06-01)
- **Obligations:** Participation in a 12-month cohort with mentorship; unconfirmed: reporting and open licensing requirements per call.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Only worth applying if a future call's theme covers open, decentralised infrastructure or trustworthy AI agents; then pitch ForkMesh's isolated, auditable AI coding agents that open signed pull requests on independent hosts. Mozilla funds open-web alternatives to concentrated platforms, which matches ForkMesh's mission.

**How to apply:**

1. Sign up on the Incubator page to be notified when the next thematic call opens.
2. Submit the proposal during the call window.
3. Shortlisting and review.
4. Selection announced (2026: deadline Mar 16, selections Jun 1).
5. 12-month cohort with mentorship.

**What the application asks for:**

- unconfirmed: online proposal form per call (project description, theme fit, users and traction past prototype stage, team, 12-month plan and budget)

**Risks:** Mozilla Foundation pages returned 403, so status comes from search results; no Mozilla call is confirmed open now. Mozilla Open Source Support (MOSS) is on hiatus and the Fellows 2026 deadline (Aug 31, 2026) has passed. Calls are thematic and the 2026 theme (Democracy x AI) does not fit ForkMesh. Mozilla Ventures (equity investment) is the other running channel but is not a grant.

**Sources:** <https://www.mozillafoundation.org/en/what-we-do/grantmaking/incubator/>, <https://www.mozillafoundation.org/en/what-we-do/grantmaking/incubator/democracy-ai-cohort/>, <https://grantedai.com/blog/mozilla-democracy-ai-cohort-50k-grants-2026>, <https://streamlyne.com/funders/mozilla-foundation/>

20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Algorand%20Foundation%3A%20xGov%20retroactive%20public-goods%20grants%0AAward%3A%20Minimum%202%2C500%20ALGO%2C%20maximum%20300%2C000%20ALGO%20per%20proposal%20%28USD%20figures%20are%20a%20rough%20approximation%20at%20about%20USD%200.20%2FALGO%2C%20unconfirmed%29%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28continuous%20proposals%20and%20votes%20in%20xGov%20Beta%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20KYC-approved%20proposers%20requesting%20retroactive%20funding%20for%20fully%20built%2C%20working%2C%20open-source%20public%20goods%20that%20have%20already%20benefited%20the%20Algorand%20ecosystem%3B%20the%20repo%20must%20be%20open%20source%20when%20applying%20and%20is%20submitted%20to%20Electric%20Capital.%20Council%20sponsorship%20and%20an%20open-source-upfront%20commitment%20for%20smaller%20asks%20were%20discussed%20in%202026%20council%20meetings%20%28unconfirmed%20whether%20adopted%29.%20Geography%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20worldwide%20subject%20to%20KYC%20%28no%20restriction%20stated%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20or%20teams%20passing%20KYC%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20entity%20types%20not%20stated%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fxgov.algorand.co%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fxgov.algorand.co%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Connect%20an%20Algorand%20wallet%20on%20the%20xGov%20platform%20and%20accept%20the%20Terms%20%26%20Conditions.%202.%20Pay%20the%20100%20ALGO%20one-time%20proposer%20fee.%203.%20Complete%20manual%20KYC%20%28up%20to%20one%20business%20day%20to%20sync%29.%204.%20Pay%20the%20proposal%20creation%20fee%20and%20fill%20in%20the%20proposal%20form%2C%20paying%20a%203%25%20anti-spam%20deposit.%205.%20Open%20the%20forum%20discussion%20thread%20and%20link%20it.%206.%20Discuss%20for%20at%20least%20three%20weeks%2C%20then%20submit%20for%20an%20on-chain%20vote%20by%20xGovs%20%28block-producing%20node%20runners%29.%207.%20If%20approved%2C%20receive%20the%20grant%3B%20deposit%20refunded%20after%20voting%20unless%20the%20terms%20were%20breached.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Title%0A-%20Forum%20discussion%20link%0A-%20Description%20of%20what%20was%20built%20and%20how%20the%20community%20benefits%0A-%20About%20the%20team%2C%20including%20how%20ecosystem%20projects%20can%20use%20the%20work%0A-%20Open-source%20licence%20%28repo%20must%20already%20be%20open%20source%29%0A-%20Focus%20area%0A-%20Funding%20type%20%28retroactive%20public%20good%29%0A-%20Adoption%20metrics%20%28e.g.%20download%20counts%2C%20usage%20data%29%0A-%20Amount%20requested%20%282%2C500%20-%20300%2C000%20ALGO%29%0A-%20KYC%20documents%0A-%20100%20ALGO%20proposer%20fee%2C%20proposal%20fee%20and%203%25%20refundable%20deposit%0ADecision%20time%3A%20At%20least%203%20weeks%20of%20forum%20discussion%20plus%20the%20voting%20period%20%28voting%20duration%20unconfirmed%29%0AObligations%3A%20Comply%20with%20xGov%20Terms%20%26%20Conditions%20%28breach%20means%20no%20payment%20and%20slashed%20deposit%29%3B%20keep%20work%20open%20source.%20No%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Only%20viable%20after%20ForkMesh%20ships%20something%20Algorand-specific%3A%20e.g.%20an%20open-source%20module%20that%20mirrors%20and%20signs%20Algorand%20ecosystem%20repositories%20or%20pays%20ForkMesh%20contributors%20in%20ALGO%2C%20then%20requests%20retroactive%20funding%20with%20adoption%20metrics.%20The%20program%20rewards%20working%20open-source%20public%20goods%20that%20already%20serve%20Algorand%20builders.%0ARisks%3A%20Retroactive%20only%3A%20ForkMesh%20has%20no%20existing%20Algorand%20integration%2C%20so%20it%20cannot%20apply%20now.%20Paid%20in%20ALGO%20with%20price%20risk%3B%20non-refundable%20fees%20and%20a%20deposit%20that%20can%20be%20slashed.%20The%20program%27s%20funding%20model%20and%20council%20rules%20were%20being%20reworked%20in%20August%202026.)** · [Program page](https://xgov.algorand.co/) · [Apply](https://xgov.algorand.co/)

- **Funder:** Algorand Foundation
- **Category:** Web3 ecosystem
- **Amount:** Minimum 2,500 ALGO, maximum 300,000 ALGO per proposal (USD figures are a rough approximation at about USD 0.20/ALGO, unconfirmed) (about $500 - $60,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (continuous proposals and votes in xGov Beta) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 18/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** KYC-approved proposers requesting retroactive funding for fully built, working, open-source public goods that have already benefited the Algorand ecosystem; the repo must be open source when applying and is submitted to Electric Capital. Council sponsorship and an open-source-upfront commitment for smaller asks were discussed in 2026 council meetings (unconfirmed whether adopted).
- **Geography:** unconfirmed: worldwide subject to KYC (no restriction stated) · **Entity types:** Individuals or teams passing KYC (unconfirmed: entity types not stated)
- **Decision time:** At least 3 weeks of forum discussion plus the voting period (voting duration unconfirmed)
- **Obligations:** Comply with xGov Terms & Conditions (breach means no payment and slashed deposit); keep work open source. No equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Only viable after ForkMesh ships something Algorand-specific: e.g. an open-source module that mirrors and signs Algorand ecosystem repositories or pays ForkMesh contributors in ALGO, then requests retroactive funding with adoption metrics. The program rewards working open-source public goods that already serve Algorand builders.

**How to apply:**

1. Connect an Algorand wallet on the xGov platform and accept the Terms & Conditions.
2. Pay the 100 ALGO one-time proposer fee.
3. Complete manual KYC (up to one business day to sync).
4. Pay the proposal creation fee and fill in the proposal form, paying a 3% anti-spam deposit.
5. Open the forum discussion thread and link it.
6. Discuss for at least three weeks, then submit for an on-chain vote by xGovs (block-producing node runners).
7. If approved, receive the grant; deposit refunded after voting unless the terms were breached.

**What the application asks for:**

- Title
- Forum discussion link
- Description of what was built and how the community benefits
- About the team, including how ecosystem projects can use the work
- Open-source licence (repo must already be open source)
- Focus area
- Funding type (retroactive public good)
- Adoption metrics (e.g. download counts, usage data)
- Amount requested (2,500 - 300,000 ALGO)
- KYC documents
- 100 ALGO proposer fee, proposal fee and 3% refundable deposit

**Risks:** Retroactive only: ForkMesh has no existing Algorand integration, so it cannot apply now. Paid in ALGO with price risk; non-refundable fees and a deposit that can be slashed. The program's funding model and council rules were being reworked in August 2026.

**Sources:** <https://xgov.algorand.co/>, <https://forum.algorand.co/t/xgov-beta-becoming-a-proposer-and-creating-proposals/14809>, <https://forum.algorand.co/t/xgov-mainnet-announcement/14812>, <https://forum.algorand.co/t/xgov-council-algorand-foundation-meeting-august-5th-2026/15401>

## 92. European Innovation Council: EIC Pathfinder Open

**[Draft this application in
