ndependent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20European%20Innovation%20Council%3A%20EIC%20Pathfinder%20Open%0AAward%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20up%20to%20EUR%204%20million%20per%20project%20in%20the%202026%20call%20%28secondary%20sources%3B%20earlier%20calls%20EUR%203%20million%29%2C%20100%25%20of%20eligible%20costs%2C%20lump%20sum%0ADeadline%3A%20Next%20call%20expected%20early%202027%20%28draft%20Work%20Programme%202027%20reportedly%20two-stage%3A%20Stage%201%202027-02-25%2C%20Stage%202%202027-09-16%3B%20unconfirmed%20until%20adoption%29%20%28upcoming%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Normally%20a%20consortium%20of%20at%20least%20three%20independent%20legal%20entities%20in%20three%20different%20countries%3A%20at%20least%20one%20in%20an%20EU%20Member%20State%2C%20the%20others%20in%20Member%20States%20or%20associated%20countries.%20Single%20applicants%20are%20possible%20only%20as%20an%20exception%20%28unconfirmed%20for%202027%29.%20Funds%20early-stage%20science-to-technology%20research%20with%20no%20set%20themes.%20Geography%3A%20EU%20Member%20States%20and%20Horizon%20Europe%20associated%20countries.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Universities%2C%20research%20organisations%2C%20companies%20%28consortium%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Feic.ec.europa.eu%2Feic-funding-opportunities%2Feic-pathfinder%2Feic-pathfinder-open_en%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fec.europa.eu%2Finfo%2Ffunding-tenders%2Fopportunities%2Fportal%2Fscreen%2Fopportunities%2Fcalls-for-proposals%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Watch%20for%20adoption%20of%20the%20EIC%20Work%20Programme%202027%20%28usually%20November-December%29.%202.%20Build%20a%20research-led%20consortium%20across%20three%20countries.%203.%20Submit%20Stage%201%3B%20the%20draft%202027%20rules%20require%20commitment%20letters%20from%20all%20beneficiaries.%204.%20If%20successful%2C%20submit%20the%20Stage%202%20full%20proposal.%205.%20Interview%20%28new%20in%20the%202027%20draft%29.%206.%20Grant%20agreement.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Part%20A%20administrative%20forms%20on%20the%20Funding%20%26%20Tenders%20Portal%0A-%20Part%20B%20technical%20description%20%28page%20limit%20unconfirmed%3B%20secondary%20sources%20cite%2017-20%20A4%20pages%29%0A-%20Commitment%20letters%20from%20every%20intended%20beneficiary%20at%20Stage%201%20%28draft%202027%2C%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Interview%20pitch%20of%20up%20to%2010%20minutes%20after%20Stage%202%20%28draft%202027%2C%20unconfirmed%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%202026%20call%3A%20deadline%2012%20May%202026%2C%20results%20expected%20October%202026%20%28about%205%20months%29%3B%20the%202027%20draft%20puts%20an%20interview%2010-14%20weeks%20after%20Stage%202%20%28unconfirmed%29%0AObligations%3A%20Horizon%20Europe%20lump-sum%20grant%20reporting%2C%20open-access%20publication%2C%20and%20exploitation%2FIP%20duties%20under%20the%20grant%20agreement.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Only%20with%20university%20partners%3A%20a%20visionary%20research%20project%20on%20provably%20available%2C%20censorship-resistant%20software%20distribution%20and%20verifiable%20collaboration%20records%20at%20internet%20scale%2C%20with%20ForkMesh%20as%20the%20industrial%20testbed%20and%20exploitation%20partner.%20Pathfinder%20funds%20radically%20new%20science-to-technology%20ideas%2C%20so%20the%20bid%20must%20be%20research-led.%0ARisks%3A%20Extremely%20competitive%20%282%2C103%20proposals%20in%202026%29.%20Needs%20a%20three-country%20research%20consortium%20and%20a%20breakthrough-science%20novelty%20bar%20that%20a%20software%20product%20struggles%20to%20meet.%20The%202027%20dates%20and%20rules%20are%20still%20draft%2C%20and%20the%20official%20page%20fetched%20still%20cites%20the%202025%20Work%20Programme.)** · [Program page](https://eic.ec.europa.eu/eic-funding-opportunities/eic-pathfinder/eic-pathfinder-open_en) · [Apply](https://ec.europa.eu/info/funding-tenders/opportunities/portal/screen/opportunities/calls-for-proposals)

- **Funder:** European Innovation Council (European Commission, Horizon Europe)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** unconfirmed: up to EUR 4 million per project in the 2026 call (secondary sources; earlier calls EUR 3 million), 100% of eligible costs, lump sum (about $4,600,000)
- **Deadline:** Next call expected early 2027 (draft Work Programme 2027 reportedly two-stage: Stage 1 2027-02-25, Stage 2 2027-09-16; unconfirmed until adoption) (upcoming)
- **Fit score:** 15/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Normally a consortium of at least three independent legal entities in three different countries: at least one in an EU Member State, the others in Member States or associated countries. Single applicants are possible only as an exception (unconfirmed for 2027). Funds early-stage science-to-technology research with no set themes.
- **Geography:** EU Member States and Horizon Europe associated countries · **Entity types:** Universities, research organisations, companies (consortium)
- **Decision time:** 2026 call: deadline 12 May 2026, results expected October 2026 (about 5 months); the 2027 draft puts an interview 10-14 weeks after Stage 2 (unconfirmed)
- **Obligations:** Horizon Europe lump-sum grant reporting, open-access publication, and exploitation/IP duties under the grant agreement.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Only with university partners: a visionary research project on provably available, censorship-resistant software distribution and verifiable collaboration records at internet scale, with ForkMesh as the industrial testbed and exploitation partner. Pathfinder funds radically new science-to-technology ideas, so the bid must be research-led.

**How to apply:**

1. Watch for adoption of the EIC Work Programme 2027 (usually November-December).
2. Build a research-led consortium across three countries.
3. Submit Stage 1; the draft 2027 rules require commitment letters from all beneficiaries.
4. If successful, submit the Stage 2 full proposal.
5. Interview (new in the 2027 draft).
6. Grant agreement.

**What the application asks for:**

- Part A administrative forms on the Funding & Tenders Portal
- Part B technical description (page limit unconfirmed; secondary sources cite 17-20 A4 pages)
- Commitment letters from every intended beneficiary at Stage 1 (draft 2027, unconfirmed)
- Interview pitch of up to 10 minutes after Stage 2 (draft 2027, unconfirmed)

**Risks:** Extremely competitive (2,103 proposals in 2026). Needs a three-country research consortium and a breakthrough-science novelty bar that a software product struggles to meet. The 2027 dates and rules are still draft, and the official page fetched still cites the 2025 Work Programme.

**Sources:** <https://eic.ec.europa.eu/eic-funding-opportunities/eic-pathfinder/eic-pathfinder-open_en>, <https://accelopment.com/blog/funding/eic-pathfinder-open-2026-limited-funding-high-competition-and-next-steps/>, <https://www.rasph.com/eic-pathfinder-2027>, <https://www.grantbite.com/en/funding/eic-pathfinder-open-horizon-europe>

%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20CSIRO%20Kick-Start%0AAward%3A%20AUD%2020%2C000%20-%2060%2C000%20dollar-matched%20funding%20for%20R%26D%20with%20CSIRO%20researchers%2C%20projects%20up%20to%2012%20months%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Australian%20start-ups%20or%20SMEs%20with%20annual%20turnover%20and%20operating%20expenditure%20under%20AUD%2010%20million%2C%20or%20trading%20for%20less%20than%20three%20years.%20Must%20hold%20an%20ACN%20or%20ICN%2C%20be%20registered%20for%20GST%2C%20and%20be%20able%20to%20provide%20matching%20funding.%20Geography%3A%20Australia%20only.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Australian%20companies%20%28ACN%29%20or%20incorporated%20bodies%20%28ICN%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.csiro.au%2Fen%2Fwork-with-us%2Ffunding-programs%2Fsme%2Fcsiro-kick-start%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.csiro.au%2Fen%2Fwork-with-us%2Ffunding-programs%2Fsme%2Fcsiro-kick-start%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Check%20eligibility.%202.%20Email%20the%20expression-of-interest%20form%20%28DOCX%29%20to%20the%20Kick-Start%20team.%203.%20CSIRO%20assesses%20the%20EOI%2C%20helps%20shape%20the%20project%20and%20matches%20a%20research%20team.%204.%20Scope%2C%20deliver%20and%20evaluate%20the%20project%20with%20the%20CSIRO%20team.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Kick-Start%20expression%20of%20interest%20form%20%28DOCX%29%0A-%20ACN%20or%20ICN%0A-%20GST%20registration%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20the%20business%27s%20matching%20cash%20contribution%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20Matched%20funding%20from%20the%20business%3B%20IP%2C%20reporting%20and%20contract%20terms%20not%20stated%20%28ask%20the%20Kick-Start%20team%29.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Commission%20CSIRO%20cyber-security%20researchers%20%28team%20matched%20by%20CSIRO%29%20to%20formally%20analyse%20or%20benchmark%20ForkMesh%27s%20signed-mirror%20protocol%20and%20integrity%20checks.%20Kick-Start%20exists%20to%20connect%20young%20Australian%20tech%20firms%20with%20CSIRO%20research%20capability.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20an%20Australian%20entity.%20Small%2C%20matched%20funding%20that%20pays%20for%20CSIRO%20research%20rather%20than%20ForkMesh%20salaries.%20The%20Industry%20Growth%20Program%20is%20paused%20to%20new%20applications%2C%20AEA%20Ignite%20is%20for%20university%20researchers%20and%20closed%20in%20March%202026%2C%20and%20no%20open%20federal%20cyber-security%20business%20grant%20was%20found.)** · [Program page](https://www.csiro.au/en/work-with-us/funding-programs/sme/csiro-kick-start) · [Apply](https://www.csiro.au/en/work-with-us/funding-programs/sme/csiro-kick-start)

- **Funder:** CSIRO (Commonwealth Scientific and Industrial Research Organisation, Australian Government)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** AUD 20,000 - 60,000 dollar-matched funding for R&D with CSIRO researchers, projects up to 12 months (about $13,000 - $39,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 15/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Australian start-ups or SMEs with annual turnover and operating expenditure under AUD 10 million, or trading for less than three years. Must hold an ACN or ICN, be registered for GST, and be able to provide matching funding.
- **Geography:** Australia only · **Entity types:** Australian companies (ACN) or incorporated bodies (ICN)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** Matched funding from the business; IP, reporting and contract terms not stated (ask the Kick-Start team).

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Commission CSIRO cyber-security researchers (team matched by CSIRO) to formally analyse or benchmark ForkMesh's signed-mirror protocol and integrity checks. Kick-Start exists to connect young Australian tech firms with CSIRO research capability.

**How to apply:**

1. Check eligibility.
2. Email the expression-of-interest form (DOCX) to the Kick-Start team.
3. CSIRO assesses the EOI, helps shape the project and matches a research team.
4. Scope, deliver and evaluate the project with the CSIRO team.

**What the application asks for:**

- Kick-Start expression of interest form (DOCX)
- ACN or ICN
- GST registration
- Evidence of the business's matching cash contribution

**Risks:** Needs an Australian entity. Small, matched funding that pays for CSIRO research rather than ForkMesh salaries. The Industry Growth Program is paused to new applications, AEA Ignite is for university researchers and closed in March 2026, and no open federal cyber-security business grant was found.

**Sources:** <https://www.csiro.au/en/work-with-us/funding-programs/sme/csiro-kick-start>, <https://www.business.gov.au/IGP>, <https://www.fundingcentre.com.au/grants/gold-coast/R00123>

20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Microsoft%20FOSS%20Fund%0AAward%3A%20Up%20to%20USD%2012%2C500%20per%20quarterly%20fund%2C%20distributed%20across%20one%20or%20more%20projects%0ADeadline%3A%20Nomination-based%3B%20rounds%20roughly%20every%20few%20months%20%28latest%20listed%20FOSS%20Fund%2036%2C%20March%202026%29%20%28invite-only%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Open-source%20projects%20used%20by%20Microsoft%2C%20under%20an%20OSI-approved%20licence%2C%20able%20to%20receive%20funds%20in%20a%20way%20Microsoft%20procurement%20accepts%20%28GitHub%20Sponsors%20preferred%29%2C%20and%20not%20owned%20by%20a%20Microsoft%20employee.%20Must%20be%20nominated%20by%20a%20Microsoft%20employee%20or%20intern.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open-source%20projects%20%28receiving%20via%20GitHub%20Sponsors%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fmicrosoft%2Ffoss-fund%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fmicrosoft%2Ffoss-fund%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Set%20up%20GitHub%20Sponsors%20for%20the%20project.%202.%20A%20Microsoft%20employee%20nominates%20the%20project.%203.%20Open%20Source%20Champs%20curate%205-20%20nominees%20per%20round.%204.%20Eligible%20Microsoft%20open-source%20contributors%20vote.%205.%20Winner%28s%29%20receive%20sponsorship.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20GitHub%20Sponsors%20profile%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20Microsoft%20usage%20%28nominator%20provides%29%0A-%20OSI%20licence%0ADecision%20time%3A%20One%20round%20cycle%20%28roughly%20quarterly%29%0AObligations%3A%20None%20stated%20beyond%20accepting%20sponsorship.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Only%20relevant%20once%20Microsoft%20engineers%20use%20ForkMesh%20%28e.g.%20for%20mirroring%20dependencies%29%3B%20enabling%20GitHub%20Sponsors%20costs%20nothing%20and%20keeps%20the%20door%20open.%0ARisks%3A%20Cannot%20apply%20directly%3B%20requires%20Microsoft%20usage%20and%20nomination.%20Other%20corporate%20FOSS%20funds%20%28Bloomberg%2C%20Indeed%2C%20Salesforce%2C%20Spotify%29%20could%20not%20be%20confirmed%20active%20in%202026%20from%20official%20pages.)** · [Program page](https://github.com/microsoft/foss-fund) · [Apply](https://github.com/microsoft/foss-fund)

- **Funder:** Microsoft
- **Category:** Preservation and digital commons
- **Amount:** Up to USD 12,500 per quarterly fund, distributed across one or more projects (about $12,500)
- **Deadline:** Nomination-based; rounds roughly every few months (latest listed FOSS Fund 36, March 2026) (invite-only)
- **Fit score:** 15/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Open-source projects used by Microsoft, under an OSI-approved licence, able to receive funds in a way Microsoft procurement accepts (GitHub Sponsors preferred), and not owned by a Microsoft employee. Must be nominated by a Microsoft employee or intern.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Open-source projects (receiving via GitHub Sponsors)
- **Decision time:** One round cycle (roughly quarterly)
- **Obligations:** None stated beyond accepting sponsorship.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Only relevant once Microsoft engineers use ForkMesh (e.g. for mirroring dependencies); enabling GitHub Sponsors costs nothing and keeps the door open.

**How to apply:**

1. Set up GitHub Sponsors for the project.
2. A Microsoft employee nominates the project.
3. Open Source Champs curate 5-20 nominees per round.
4. Eligible Microsoft open-source contributors vote.
5. Winner(s) receive sponsorship.

**What the application asks for:**

- GitHub Sponsors profile
- Evidence of Microsoft usage (nominator provides)
- OSI licence

**Risks:** Cannot apply directly; requires Microsoft usage and nomination. Other corporate FOSS funds (Bloomberg, Indeed, Salesforce, Spotify) could not be confirmed active in 2026 from official pages.

**Sources:** <https://github.com/microsoft/foss-fund>, <https://www.bloomberg.com/company/stories/celebrating-q1-2025-bloomberg-foss-fund-recipients/>

0Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%201Password%20for%20Open%20Source%0AAward%3A%20In-kind%3A%20free%201Password%20Teams%20account%20that%20does%20not%20expire%20while%20the%20project%20stays%20eligible%20%28includes%20SSH%20key%2C%20Git%20commit%20signing%2C%20CLI%20and%20secrets-management%20integrations%29%3B%20no%20cash%20value%20stated%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Core%20contributors%20of%20an%20active%20open-source%20project%20at%20least%2030%20days%20old%20under%20a%20permissive%20open-source%20license%3B%20teams%20working%20primarily%20on%20open%20source%20not%20supported%20by%20commercial%20activity%3B%20or%20organisers%20of%20open-source%20meetups%2Fevents.%20The%20team%20account%20must%20not%20be%20tied%20to%20commercial%20activity%20%28GitHub%20Sponsors%2FOpen%20Collective%20funding%20is%20allowed%29.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open-source%20projects%2Fteams%20and%20event%20organisers%3B%20no%20personal%2C%20family%20or%20business%20accounts%3B%20no%20commercially%20supported%20teams.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2F1Password%2Ffor-open-source%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2F1Password%2Ffor-open-source%2Fissues%2Fnew%2Fchoose%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Create%20a%201Password%20Teams%20account%20%2814-day%20trial%20available%29%20and%20be%20its%20owner.%202.%20Invite%20at%20least%20one%20other%20person%20and%20add%20them%20to%20the%20Owners%20group.%203.%20Open%20a%20new%20issue%20in%20the%201Password%2Ffor-open-source%20GitHub%20repository%20using%20the%20application%20template.%204.%201Password%20reviews%20%28label%20%27applicationstatus%3A%20reviewing%27%29%20and%20approves%20or%20rejects%20at%20its%20discretion.%205.%20Teams%20account%20converted%20to%20a%20free%20open-source%20membership.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20GitHub%20issue%20using%20the%20application%20template%20%28project%20name%2C%20repository%20URL%2C%20license%2C%20team%20account%20details%29%0A-%20Existing%201Password%20Teams%20account%20with%20at%20least%20two%20owners%0A-%20Evidence%20the%20applicant%20is%20a%20core%20contributor%20and%20the%20project%20is%20active%20and%20at%20least%2030%20days%20old%0A-%20Statement%20that%20the%20account%20is%20not%20tied%20to%20commercial%20activity%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%3B%20October%202026%20applications%20are%20being%20labelled%20and%20decided%20within%20days%20to%20weeks%0AObligations%3A%20Remain%20eligible%20%28membership%20can%20be%20cancelled%20if%20eligibility%20lapses%29%3B%20membership%20is%20non-transferable%3B%20participation%20may%20be%20recorded%20publicly%20and%20used%20in%201Password%20promotion.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Use%201Password%20Teams%20to%20manage%20the%20mirror-fleet%20SSH%20keys%2C%20release-signing%20and%20Git%20commit-signing%20keys%20and%20operator%20secrets%20for%20ForkMesh%27s%20open-source%20project%2C%20which%20supports%20its%20signed-record%20and%20supply-chain-integrity%20model.%20Low%20effort%2C%20small%20in-kind%20value.%0ARisks%3A%20Account%20must%20not%20be%20tied%20to%20commercial%20activity%3B%20ForkMesh%27s%20paid%20hosted%20services%20and%20startup%20entity%20may%20disqualify%20it%20unless%20applied%20for%20as%20the%20open-source%20project%20team%20only.%20Holding%20fleet%20operator%20secrets%20in%20a%20third-party%20vault%20adds%20a%20dependency%20to%20evaluate.)** · [Program page](https://github.com/1Password/for-open-source) · [Apply](https://github.com/1Password/for-open-source/issues/new/choose)

- **Funder:** 1Password (AgileBits Inc.)
- **Category:** Security, privacy and internet freedom
- **Amount:** In-kind: free 1Password Teams account that does not expire while the project stays eligible (includes SSH key, Git commit signing, CLI and secrets-management integrations); no cash value stated (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 15/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Core contributors of an active open-source project at least 30 days old under a permissive open-source license; teams working primarily on open source not supported by commercial activity; or organisers of open-source meetups/events. The team account must not be tied to commercial activity (GitHub Sponsors/Open Collective funding is allowed).
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Open-source projects/teams and event organisers; no personal, family or business accounts; no commercially supported teams
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated; October 2026 applications are being labelled and decided within days to weeks
- **Obligations:** Remain eligible (membership can be cancelled if eligibility lapses); membership is non-transferable; participation may be recorded publicly and used in 1Password promotion.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Use 1Password Teams to manage the mirror-fleet SSH keys, release-signing and Git commit-signing keys and operator secrets for ForkMesh's open-source project, which supports its signed-record and supply-chain-integrity model. Low effort, small in-kind value.

**How to apply:**

1. Create a 1Password Teams account (14-day trial available) and be its owner.
2. Invite at least one other person and add them to the Owners group.
3. Open a new issue in the 1Password/for-open-source GitHub repository using the application template.
4. 1Password reviews (label 'applicationstatus: reviewing') and approves or rejects at its discretion.
5. Teams account converted to a free open-source membership.

**What the application asks for:**

- GitHub issue using the application template (project name, repository URL, license, team account details)
- Existing 1Password Teams account with at least two owners
- Evidence the applicant is a core contributor and the project is active and at least 30 days old
- Statement that the account is not tied to commercial activity

**Risks:** Account must not be tied to commercial activity; ForkMesh's paid hosted services and startup entity may disqualify it unless applied for as the open-source project team only. Holding fleet operator secrets in a third-party vault adds a dependency to evaluate.

**Sources:** <https://github.com/1Password/for-open-source>, <https://github.com/1Password/for-open-source/issues?q=is%3Aissue>

rce%20%28Arm%20GitHub%20runners%20or%20a%20partner%20cloud%29.%202.%20For%20runner%20credits%2C%20open%20a%20%27Credits%20for%20Arm-based%20GitHub%20Runners%27%20issue%20in%20the%20WorksOnArm%2FGitHub-Runners%20repository%20using%20the%20issue%20template.%203.%20For%20partner%20clouds%2C%20use%20that%20provider%27s%20linked%20request%20page.%204.%20Arm%20or%20the%20provider%20follows%20up%20on%20the%20request.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20GitHub%20issue%20using%20the%20WorksOnArm%2FGitHub-Runners%20template%20%28unconfirmed%3A%20template%20fields%20not%20visible%29%0A-%20Project%20name%2C%20repository%20URL%20and%20OSI%20license%0A-%20Description%20of%20Arm64%20build%2Ftest%20needs%20%28e.g.%20CI%20workloads%2C%20expected%20runner%20minutes%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20several%202024-2026%20request%20issues%20remain%20open%20with%20at%20most%20one%20reply%2C%20so%20responses%20may%20be%20slow%0AObligations%3A%20None%20stated%3B%20implied%20expectation%20to%20support%20and%20test%20the%20project%20on%20Arm64.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Request%20Arm64%20runner%20credits%20to%20build%20and%20test%20ForkMesh%27s%20desktop%20node%20%28Linux%20arm64%2C%20Apple%20Silicon%20parity%29%20and%20the%20self-hosted%20node%20on%20Arm%20servers%2C%20so%20independent%20operators%20can%20run%20mirrors%20on%20cheap%20Arm%20hardware.%20It%20strengthens%20the%20multi-architecture%2C%20many-host%20resilience%20story.%0ARisks%3A%20Program%20looks%20semi-dormant%3A%20the%20official%20page%20still%20lists%20Equinix%20Metal%20%28shut%20down%29%20and%20an%20expired%20AWS%20trial%2C%20and%20runner-credit%20request%20issues%20from%202024-2026%20sit%20open%20with%20little%20response.%20GitHub%20already%20provides%20free%20Arm64%20hosted%20runners%20for%20public%20repositories%2C%20which%20may%20make%20this%20redundant.%20In-kind%20only.)** · [Program page](https://www.arm.com/solutions/infrastructure/works-on-arm) · [Apply](https://github.com/WorksOnArm/GitHub-Runners/issues/new/choose)

- **Funder:** Arm Ltd.
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** Free-of-cost access to Arm64 developer platforms, cloud instances and CI/CD (Arm-based GitHub-hosted runner credits; partner cloud offers via AWS, Google Cloud, Azure, OCI, Tencent); no amount stated (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 14/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** no, confirm first
- **Who can apply:** Open-source developers and projects building, testing or optimising software on Arm64. unconfirmed: no written eligibility criteria; commercial status not addressed.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: open-source projects and developers (no entity restriction stated)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: several 2024-2026 request issues remain open with at most one reply, so responses may be slow
- **Obligations:** None stated; implied expectation to support and test the project on Arm64.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Request Arm64 runner credits to build and test ForkMesh's desktop node (Linux arm64, Apple Silicon parity) and the self-hosted node on Arm servers, so independent operators can run mirrors on cheap Arm hardware. It strengthens the multi-architecture, many-host resilience story.

**How to apply:**

1. Read the Works on Arm page and pick the resource (Arm GitHub runners or a partner cloud).
2. For runner credits, open a 'Credits for Arm-based GitHub Runners' issue in the WorksOnArm/GitHub-Runners repository using the issue template.
3. For partner clouds, use that provider's linked request page.
4. Arm or the provider follows up on the request.

**What the application asks for:**

- GitHub issue using the WorksOnArm/GitHub-Runners template (unconfirmed: template fields not visible)
- Project name, repository URL and OSI license
- Description of Arm64 build/test needs (e.g. CI workloads, expected runner minutes)

**Risks:** Program looks semi-dormant: the official page still lists Equinix Metal (shut down) and an expired AWS trial, and runner-credit request issues from 2024-2026 sit open with little response. GitHub already provides free Arm64 hosted runners for public repositories, which may make this redundant. In-kind only.

**Sources:** <https://www.arm.com/solutions/infrastructure/works-on-arm>, <https://github.com/WorksOnArm/GitHub-Runners>, <https://github.com/WorksOnArm/GitHub-Runners/issues>, <https://newsroom.arm.com/news/works-on-arm-developer-access-to-arm-based-instances>

ss%20independent%2C%20developer-operated%20hosts%2C%20so%20no%20single%20hosting%20provider%20is%20a%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20German%20federal%20government%20EXIST%20programme%2C%20administered%20by%20Projekttraeger%20Juelich%3A%20EXIST-Gruendungsstipendium%0AAward%3A%2012-month%20personal%20stipend%3A%20EUR%203%2C000%2Fmonth%20%28doctoral%29%2C%20EUR%202%2C500%20%28graduates%29%2C%20EUR%202%2C000%20%28vocational%20qualification%29%2C%20EUR%201%2C000%20%28students%29%2C%20plus%20EUR%20150%20per%20child%3B%20material%20costs%20up%20to%20EUR%2010%2C000%20%28individual%29%20or%20EUR%2030%2C000%20%28team%29%3B%20EUR%205%2C000%20coaching%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28programme%20open%20until%202029-12-31%29%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Founders%20from%20German%20universities%20and%20research%20institutions%3A%20scientists%2C%20graduates%20or%20former%20academic%20staff%20up%20to%20five%20years%20after%20graduating%20or%20leaving%2C%20and%20students%20at%20least%20halfway%20through%20their%20studies.%20Teams%20of%20up%20to%20three%20people.%20Applications%20go%20through%20the%20university%20or%20research%20institution%27s%20start-up%20network.%20Secondary%20sources%20say%20the%20company%20must%20not%20yet%20be%20founded%20%28unconfirmed%20in%20the%20official%20text%20fetched%29.%20Geography%3A%20Germany.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Individuals%20and%20founding%20teams%20via%20a%20German%20university%20or%20research%20institution%3B%20not%20existing%20companies.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.ptj.de%2Ffoerdermoeglichkeiten%2Fexist%2Fgruendungsstipendium%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.ptj.de%2Ffoerdermoeglichkeiten%2Fexist%2Fgruendungsstipendium%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Join%20a%20German%20university%20or%20research%20institution%27s%20start-up%20service%20%28Gruendungsnetzwerk%29%20and%20find%20an%20academic%20mentor.%202.%20Write%20the%20idea%20paper%20with%20the%20start-up%20office.%203.%20The%20institution%20submits%20the%20application%20to%20PtJ.%204.%20Review%20and%20decision.%205.%2012-month%20stipend%20with%20a%20workspace%20at%20the%20institution.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Idea%20paper%20on%20the%20technology-based%20business%20idea%20%28length%20unconfirmed%29%0A-%20CVs%20of%20all%20team%20members%20%28unconfirmed%29%0A-%20Named%20academic%20mentor%20at%20the%20host%20institution%20%28secondary%20source%29%0A-%20Host%20institution%27s%20commitment%20to%20provide%20a%20workspace%20and%20founding-network%20support%20%28secondary%20source%29%0A-%20Application%20forms%20per%20the%20EXIST%20guideline%20%28unconfirmed%29%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20reporting%20under%20the%20EXIST%20guideline%3B%20the%20host%20institution%20supervises%20the%2012%20months.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Only%20if%20team%20members%20are%20recent%20graduates%20or%20researchers%20at%20a%20German%20university%3A%20a%20pre-founding%20stipend%20year%20to%20build%20a%20German%20entity%20around%20ForkMesh%27s%20hosted%20services%20and%20mirror%20fleet.%20EXIST%20funds%20tech-based%20founders%20coming%20out%20of%20academia.%0ARisks%3A%20Needs%20a%20German%20academic%20affiliation%20and%20likely%20a%20not-yet-founded%20company.%20Pays%20personal%20stipends%2C%20not%20company%20funding.%20DATIpilot%20no%20longer%20takes%20submissions%2C%20and%20its%202026%20%27Transfer%20Scale-up%27%20call%20is%20only%20for%20already-funded%20Innovationssprints.)** · [Program page](https://www.ptj.de/foerdermoeglichkeiten/exist/gruendungsstipendium) · [Apply](https://www.ptj.de/foerdermoeglichkeiten/exist/gruendungsstipendium)

- **Funder:** German federal government EXIST programme, administered by Projekttraeger Juelich (PtJ)
- **Category:** Government and research
- **Amount:** 12-month personal stipend: EUR 3,000/month (doctoral), EUR 2,500 (graduates), EUR 2,000 (vocational qualification), EUR 1,000 (students), plus EUR 150 per child; material costs up to EUR 10,000 (individual) or EUR 30,000 (team); EUR 5,000 coaching (about $164,000)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (programme open until 2029-12-31) (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 12/100 · **Effort:** medium · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Founders from German universities and research institutions: scientists, graduates or former academic staff up to five years after graduating or leaving, and students at least halfway through their studies. Teams of up to three people. Applications go through the university or research institution's start-up network. Secondary sources say the company must not yet be founded (unconfirmed in the official text fetched).
- **Geography:** Germany · **Entity types:** Individuals and founding teams via a German university or research institution; not existing companies
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: reporting under the EXIST guideline; the host institution supervises the 12 months.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Only if team members are recent graduates or researchers at a German university: a pre-founding stipend year to build a German entity around ForkMesh's hosted services and mirror fleet. EXIST funds tech-based founders coming out of academia.

**How to apply:**

1. Join a German university or research institution's start-up service (Gruendungsnetzwerk) and find an academic mentor.
2. Write the idea paper with the start-up office.
3. The institution submits the application to PtJ.
4. Review and decision.
5. 12-month stipend with a workspace at the institution.

**What the application asks for:**

- Idea paper on the technology-based business idea (length unconfirmed)
- CVs of all team members (unconfirmed)
- Named academic mentor at the host institution (secondary source)
- Host institution's commitment to provide a workspace and founding-network support (secondary source)
- Application forms per the EXIST guideline (unconfirmed)

**Risks:** Needs a German academic affiliation and likely a not-yet-founded company. Pays personal stipends, not company funding. DATIpilot no longer takes submissions, and its 2026 'Transfer Scale-up' call is only for already-funded Innovationssprints.

**Sources:** <https://www.ptj.de/foerdermoeglichkeiten/exist/gruendungsstipendium>, <https://www.ptj.de/foerdermoeglichkeiten/datipilot>, <https://www.deutsche-startups.de/lexikon/exist-grunderstipendium/>

t%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20GitLab%20Inc.%3A%20GitLab%20for%20Open%20Source%20Program%0AAward%3A%20In-kind%3A%20GitLab%20Ultimate%20features%20%28SaaS%20or%20self-managed%29%20for%20a%20chosen%20number%20of%20concurrent%20seats%20plus%2050%2C000%20compute%20minutes%20per%20year%3B%20no%20cash%20value%20stated%0ADeadline%3A%20Rolling%20%28rolling%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Every%20project%20in%20the%20GitLab%20group%20or%20self-managed%20instance%20must%20use%20an%20OSI-approved%20license%20and%20be%20publicly%20visible.%20The%20organisation%20may%20accept%20donations%20but%20must%20not%20seek%20profit%20by%20selling%20services%2C%20charging%20for%20enhancements%20or%20add-ons%2C%20or%20other%20means%3B%20personal%20namespaces%20must%20be%20converted%20to%20a%20group%20first.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide.%20Entity%20types%3A%20Open-source%20projects%2Forganisations%20of%20any%20form%20that%20do%20not%20seek%20profit%20%28nonprofits%2C%20foundations%2C%20community%20groups%29.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fabout.gitlab.com%2Fsolutions%2Fopen-source%2Fjoin%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fcustomers.gitlab.com%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Check%20the%20full%20program%20requirements%20in%20the%20GitLab%20handbook.%202.%20Create%20a%20Free-tier%20or%20Ultimate-trial%20GitLab%20account%20and%20group%20%28migration%20need%20not%20be%20finished%29.%203.%20Apply%20via%20the%20GitLab%20Customers%20Portal%20form%2C%20verifying%20the%20group%20meets%20the%20requirements.%204.%20Receive%20an%20email%20with%20a%20coupon%20code%20and%20provisioning%20instructions%20%28self-managed%20requests%20are%20reviewed%20manually%29.%205.%20Renew%20annually%20by%20re-applying%201-3%20months%20before%20expiry.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Customers%20Portal%20application%20form%20verifying%20OSI%20license%2C%20public%20visibility%20and%20non-profit%20status%20of%20the%20group%0A-%20GitLab.com%20group%20path%20or%20self-managed%20instance%20details%0A-%20Requested%20number%20of%20concurrent%20seats%0A-%20For%20self-managed%3A%20specific%20project%20details%20and%20a%20clear%20explanation%20of%20why%20self-managed%20is%20necessary%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20no%20timeline%20stated%3B%20coupon%20emailed%20after%20GitLab.com%20form%20submission%2C%20self-managed%20reviewed%20manually%20%28slower%20around%20holidays%29%0AObligations%3A%20Annual%20renewal%20via%20new%20application%3B%20license%20only%20covers%20approved%20public%20open-source%20projects%20%28private%20projects%20only%20with%20written%20permission%29%3B%20no%20support%20included%3B%20downgrade%20if%20not%20renewed.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20ForkMesh%20could%20use%20GitLab%20Ultimate%20CI%20minutes%20and%20security%20scanning%20for%20a%20public%20mirror%20of%20its%20repositories%2C%20which%20also%20demonstrates%20ForkMesh%20mirroring%20a%20GitLab-hosted%20repo%20as%20one%20independent%20host%20among%20many.%20The%20benefit%20is%20tooling%2C%20not%20money%2C%20and%20only%20helps%20if%20the%20project%20can%20show%20it%20is%20not%20run%20for%20profit.%0ARisks%3A%20ForkMesh%20sells%20bounded%20hosted%20services%2C%20which%20conflicts%20with%20the%20rule%20that%20the%20organisation%20must%20not%20seek%20profit%20by%20selling%20services%3B%20likely%20ineligible%20unless%20a%20separate%20non-profit%20entity%20holds%20the%20open-source%20project.%20GitLab%20is%20a%20competing%20forge.%20In-kind%20only%2C%20no%20cash.)** · [Program page](https://about.gitlab.com/solutions/open-source/join/) · [Apply](https://customers.gitlab.com/)

- **Funder:** GitLab Inc.
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** In-kind: GitLab Ultimate features (SaaS or self-managed) for a chosen number of concurrent seats plus 50,000 compute minutes per year; no cash value stated (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 12/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Every project in the GitLab group or self-managed instance must use an OSI-approved license and be publicly visible. The organisation may accept donations but must not seek profit by selling services, charging for enhancements or add-ons, or other means; personal namespaces must be converted to a group first.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Open-source projects/organisations of any form that do not seek profit (nonprofits, foundations, community groups)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: no timeline stated; coupon emailed after GitLab.com form submission, self-managed reviewed manually (slower around holidays)
- **Obligations:** Annual renewal via new application; license only covers approved public open-source projects (private projects only with written permission); no support included; downgrade if not renewed.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** ForkMesh could use GitLab Ultimate CI minutes and security scanning for a public mirror of its repositories, which also demonstrates ForkMesh mirroring a GitLab-hosted repo as one independent host among many. The benefit is tooling, not money, and only helps if the project can show it is not run for profit.

**How to apply:**

1. Check the full program requirements in the GitLab handbook.
2. Create a Free-tier or Ultimate-trial GitLab account and group (migration need not be finished).
3. Apply via the GitLab Customers Portal form, verifying the group meets the requirements.
4. Receive an email with a coupon code and provisioning instructions (self-managed requests are reviewed manually).
5. Renew annually by re-applying 1-3 months before expiry.

**What the application asks for:**

- Customers Portal application form verifying OSI license, public visibility and non-profit status of the group
- GitLab.com group path or self-managed instance details
- Requested number of concurrent seats
- For self-managed: specific project details and a clear explanation of why self-managed is necessary

**Risks:** ForkMesh sells bounded hosted services, which conflicts with the rule that the organisation must not seek profit by selling services; likely ineligible unless a separate non-profit entity holds the open-source project. GitLab is a competing forge. In-kind only, no cash.

**Sources:** <https://about.gitlab.com/solutions/open-source/join/>

ww.docker.com%2Fcommunity%2Fopen-source%2Fapplication%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.docker.com%2Fcommunity%2Fopen-source%2Fapplication%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20Publish%20the%20project%27s%20container%20images%20in%20public%20Docker%20Hub%20repositories.%202.%20Complete%20the%20DSOS%20application%20%28Airtable%20form%20linked%20as%20%27Apply%20here%27%29.%203.%20Docker%20reviews%20eligibility.%204.%20If%20accepted%2C%20project%20leads%20and%20core%20committers%20receive%20the%20badge%20and%20Team%20subscriptions%20for%20one%20year.%205.%20Re-apply%20for%20renewal%20after%20one%20year.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20Online%20application%20form%20%28Airtable%29%20with%20Docker%20Hub%20organisation%2Fnamespace%20and%20project%20details%0A-%20Link%20to%20public%20source%20repository%20showing%20an%20OSI%20license%0A-%20Evidence%20of%20active%20development%20%28image%20or%20dependency%20updates%20within%206%20months%29%0A-%20unconfirmed%3A%20exact%20form%20fields%20not%20shown%20on%20the%20public%20page%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20stated%0AObligations%3A%20Benefits%20valid%20one%20year%2C%20only%20for%20project%20leads%20and%20core%20committers%2C%20only%20for%20developing%20the%20non-commercial%20open-source%20project%3B%20licenses%20may%20not%20be%20shared%20with%20third%20parties%3B%20renewal%20requires%20still%20meeting%20requirements.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Publish%20ForkMesh%27s%20self-hostable%20node%20and%20mirror%20images%20on%20Docker%20Hub%20so%20self-hosters%20can%20pull%20without%20rate%20limits%20and%20see%20a%20Verified%20badge%2C%20which%20lowers%20the%20barrier%20for%20independent%20operators%20to%20join%20the%20mirror%20network.%20It%20is%20a%20small%20in-kind%20benefit%20for%20distribution%2C%20not%20funding.%0ARisks%3A%20Non-commercial%20requirement%20conflicts%20with%20ForkMesh%27s%20paid%20hosted%20services%3B%20likely%20ineligible%20unless%20the%20open-source%20project%20is%20held%20by%20a%20separate%20non-commercial%20entity.%20Low%20value.%20DigitalOcean%27s%20Open%20Source%20Credits%20program%20is%20closing%20%28announced%20to%20maintainers%20in%20early%20October%202026%29%2C%20so%20this%20is%20the%20nearest%20in-kind%20alternative.)** · [Program page](https://www.docker.com/community/open-source/application/) · [Apply](https://www.docker.com/community/open-source/application/)

- **Funder:** Docker Inc.
- **Category:** Cloud and in-kind credits
- **Amount:** In-kind: Docker Verified badge, removal of Docker Hub pull rate limits for the project's public images, and one year of Docker Team subscriptions for core contributors; no cash value stated (about varies)
- **Deadline:** Rolling (rolling)
- **Fit score:** 10/100 · **Effort:** low · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Projects meeting the OSI open-source definition, with images in public Docker Hub repositories and public source, actively developed (images or dependencies updated within the past 6 months). The organisation must have no pathway to commercialisation (no paid services or higher tiers); donations are allowed.
- **Geography:** Worldwide · **Entity types:** Non-commercial open-source projects (any form; companies with paid tiers excluded)
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not stated
- **Obligations:** Benefits valid one year, only for project leads and core committers, only for developing the non-commercial open-source project; licenses may not be shared with third parties; renewal requires still meeting requirements.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Publish ForkMesh's self-hostable node and mirror images on Docker Hub so self-hosters can pull without rate limits and see a Verified badge, which lowers the barrier for independent operators to join the mirror network. It is a small in-kind benefit for distribution, not funding.

**How to apply:**

1. Publish the project's container images in public Docker Hub repositories.
2. Complete the DSOS application (Airtable form linked as 'Apply here').
3. Docker reviews eligibility.
4. If accepted, project leads and core committers receive the badge and Team subscriptions for one year.
5. Re-apply for renewal after one year.

**What the application asks for:**

- Online application form (Airtable) with Docker Hub organisation/namespace and project details
- Link to public source repository showing an OSI license
- Evidence of active development (image or dependency updates within 6 months)
- unconfirmed: exact form fields not shown on the public page

**Risks:** Non-commercial requirement conflicts with ForkMesh's paid hosted services; likely ineligible unless the open-source project is held by a separate non-commercial entity. Low value. DigitalOcean's Open Source Credits program is closing (announced to maintainers in early October 2026), so this is the nearest in-kind alternative.

**Sources:** <https://www.docker.com/community/open-source/application/>, <https://docs.docker.com/docker-hub/repos/manage/trusted-content/dsos-program/>, <https://itsfoss.com/news/digitalocean-open-source-credits-end/>, <https://www.digitalocean.com/open-source/credits-for-projects>

%20point%20of%20failure.%20Git%20stays%20the%20source%20of%20truth%3B%20clones%20are%20served%20from%20live%2C%20signed%2C%20integrity-checked%20mirrors%2C%20so%20a%20repository%20stays%20reachable%20when%20its%20source%20host%20goes%20offline.%20Issues%2C%20pull%20requests%2C%20reviews%20and%20releases%20are%20signed%20records%20stored%20in%20the%20repository.%20The%20desktop%20app%20%28Linux%2C%20macOS%2C%20Windows%29%20is%20a%20full%20node%20that%20hosts%20and%20mirrors%20repositories%20and%20runs%20AI%20coding%20agents%20in%20isolated%20worktrees%3B%20iPhone%20and%20Android%20apps%20are%20access%20clients.%20The%20protocol%2C%20self-hosting%20and%20public%20clone%20are%20free%3B%20only%20bounded%20hosted%20services%20are%20paid.%20Source%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fforkmesh%2Fforkmesh.%20Docs%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fdocs%2F.%20Blog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fblog.%20Changelog%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fforkmesh.com%2Fchangelog.%0A%0A%23%23%20About%20Ford%20Foundation%3A%20Technology%20and%20Society%0AAward%3A%20No%20public%20amounts%3B%20grants%20identified%20by%20the%20foundation%20%28example%20past%20award%3A%20USD%20550%2C000%20core%20grant%20over%20three%20years%20to%20a%20technology-policy%20nonprofit%29%0ADeadline%3A%20No%20open%20call%3B%20invitation%20only%20%28invite-only%29%0AWho%20can%20apply%3A%20Organisations%20worldwide%20aligned%20with%20Ford%27s%20programs%2C%20selected%20by%20the%20foundation%3B%20Ford%20does%20not%20accept%20unsolicited%20grant%20proposals%20by%20mail%2C%20email%20or%20phone.%20Technology%20and%20Society%20historically%20funds%20civil%20society%20and%20technically%20skilled%20nonprofits%20working%20on%20digital%20rights%2C%20equity%2C%20privacy%2C%20free%20expression%20and%20public-interest%20technology.%20Geography%3A%20Worldwide%20%2811%20regional%20offices%29.%20Entity%20types%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20in%20practice%20nonprofits%20%2F%20civil%20society%20organisations%3B%20for-profit%20eligibility%20not%20stated.%0AProgram%20page%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.fordfoundation.org%2Fwork%2Four-grants%2F%0AApply%20at%3A%20https%3A%2F%2Fwww.fordfoundation.org%2Fwork%2Four-grants%2Fgrant-opportunities%2F%0AHow%20to%20apply%3A%201.%20No%20application%20route%3A%20the%20majority%20of%20grants%20are%20identified%20by%20the%20foundation.%202.%20Become%20visible%20to%20the%20Technology%20and%20Society%20team%20through%20public-interest%20technology%20networks%2C%20convenings%20and%20grantee%20partners.%203.%20If%20invited%2C%20the%20foundation%20runs%20its%20own%20program-specific%20selection%20and%20due%20diligence.%0AWhat%20the%20application%20asks%20for%3A%0A-%20None%20until%20invited%3B%20unconfirmed%3A%20invited%20applicants%20submit%20an%20organisational%20proposal%2C%20budget%20and%20governance%20documents%20through%20Ford%27s%20grant%20system%0ADecision%20time%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20not%20published%0AObligations%3A%20unconfirmed%3A%20standard%20Ford%20grant%20agreement%20with%20narrative%20and%20financial%20reporting%3B%20no%20equity.%0AWhy%20ForkMesh%20fits%3A%20Long%20shot%20only%3A%20position%20ForkMesh%27s%20censorship-resistant%2C%20provider-independent%20Git%20hosting%20as%20public-interest%20infrastructure%20for%20civil%20society%20and%20digital%20rights%20groups%20whose%20code%20could%20be%20taken%20offline.%20Ford%20cares%20about%20digital%20rights%20and%20free%20expression%2C%20but%20it%20would%20fund%20a%20nonprofit%20steward%2C%20not%20a%20startup.%0ARisks%3A%20Still%20running%2C%20but%20practically%20unreachable%3A%20no%20unsolicited%20proposals%2C%20no%20open%20Technology%20and%20Society%20call%20%28only%20two%20closed%20calls%20listed%2C%20unrelated%29%2C%20grants%20go%20mostly%20to%20nonprofits%20identified%20by%20staff%2C%20and%20the%20Digital%20Infrastructure%20Insights%20Fund%20it%20co-funded%20has%20ended.%20A%20for-profit%20early-stage%20company%20is%20very%20unlikely%20to%20be%20invited.)** · [Program page](https://www.fordfoundation.org/work/our-grants/) · [Apply](https://www.fordfoundation.org/work/our-grants/grant-opportunities/)

- **Funder:** Ford Foundation
- **Category:** Open-source infrastructure
- **Amount:** No public amounts; grants identified by the foundation (example past award: USD 550,000 core grant over three years to a technology-policy nonprofit) (about varies)
- **Deadline:** No open call; invitation only (invite-only)
- **Fit score:** 8/100 · **Effort:** high · **Checked live:** yes
- **Who can apply:** Organisations worldwide aligned with Ford's programs, selected by the foundation; Ford does not accept unsolicited grant proposals by mail, email or phone. Technology and Society historically funds civil society and technically skilled nonprofits working on digital rights, equity, privacy, free expression and public-interest technology.
- **Geography:** Worldwide (11 regional offices) · **Entity types:** unconfirmed: in practice nonprofits / civil society organisations; for-profit eligibility not stated
- **Decision time:** unconfirmed: not published
- **Obligations:** unconfirmed: standard Ford grant agreement with narrative and financial reporting; no equity.

**Why ForkMesh fits:** Long shot only: position ForkMesh's censorship-resistant, provider-independent Git hosting as public-interest infrastructure for civil society and digital rights groups whose code could be taken offline. Ford cares about digital rights and free expression, but it would fund a nonprofit steward, not a startup.

**How to apply:**

1. No application route: the majority of grants are identified by the foundation.
2. Become visible to the Technology and Society team through public-interest technology networks, convenings and grantee partners.
3. If invited, the foundation runs its own program-specific selection and due diligence.

**What the application asks for:**

- None until invited; unconfirmed: invited applicants submit an organisational proposal, budget and governance documents through Ford's grant system

**Risks:** Still running, but practically unreachable: no unsolicited proposals, no open Technology and Society call (only two closed calls listed, unrelated), grants go mostly to nonprofits identified by staff, and the Digital Infrastructure Insights Fund it co-funded has ended. A for-profit early-stage company is very unlikely to be invited.

**Sources:** <https://www.fordfoundation.org/work/our-grants/>, <https://www.fordfoundation.org/work/our-grants/grant-opportunities/>, <https://www.fordfoundation.org/work/our-grants/grants-data>
